import java.security.SecureRandom;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Duration;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.AesGcmSecureContextCodec;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.ConfidentialGatewayService;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.ConfidentialOperationGovernor;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.GatewayPiiDetectorConfiguration;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.PiiDetector;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.SecureContextKeyProvider;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnProperty;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Import;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Profile;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.config.annotation.InterceptorRegistry;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.config.annotation.WebMvcConfigurer;

@Configuration(proxyBeanMethods = false)
@Profile(&quot;azure-cvm &amp; gateway-oidc&quot;)
@ConditionalOnProperty(name = &quot;confidential.gateway.enabled&quot;, havingValue = &quot;true&quot;)
@Import({ConfidentialOperationGovernorConfiguration.class, GatewayPiiDetectorConfiguration.class})
public class AzureOidcGatewayApiConfiguration implements WebMvcConfigurer {
    private final ConfidentialGatewayApiKeyInterceptor apiKeyInterceptor;

    public AzureOidcGatewayApiConfiguration(
            GatewayCredentialAuthenticator credentialAuthenticator) {
        this.apiKeyInterceptor =
                new ConfidentialGatewayApiKeyInterceptor(credentialAuthenticator);
    }

    @Bean
    public AesGcmSecureContextCodec azureSecureContextCodec(
            SecureContextKeyProvider azureSecureContextKeyProvider,
            ObjectMapper objectMapper) {
        return new AesGcmSecureContextCodec(
                azureSecureContextKeyProvider,
                objectMapper,
                new SecureRandom(),
                Clock.systemUTC());
    }

    @Bean
    public ConfidentialGatewayService azureConfidentialGatewayService(
            AesGcmSecureContextCodec azureSecureContextCodec,
            ConfidentialOperationGovernor operationGovernor,
            PiiDetector piiDetector,
            @Value(&quot;${CONFIDENTIAL_GATEWAY_CONTEXT_TTL_MINUTES:10}&quot;) long ttlMinutes) {
        if (ttlMinutes &lt;= 0 || ttlMinutes &gt; 1440) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                    &quot;CONFIDENTIAL_GATEWAY_CONTEXT_TTL_MINUTES must be between 1 and 1440.&quot;);
        }
        return new ConfidentialGatewayService(
                piiDetector,
                azureSecureContextCodec,
                Duration.ofMinutes(ttlMinutes),
                new SecureRandom(),
                Clock.systemUTC(),
                operationGovernor);
    }

    @Bean
    public GatewayQuotaService azureGatewayQuotaService(
            @Value(&quot;${CONFIDENTIAL_GATEWAY_MAX_REQUESTS_PER_MINUTE:60}&quot;)
                    int maximumRequests,
            @Value(&quot;${CONFIDENTIAL_GATEWAY_MAX_CHARACTERS_PER_MINUTE:5000000}&quot;)
                    long maximumCharacters,
            @Value(&quot;${CONFIDENTIAL_GATEWAY_MAX_TENANTS:10000}&quot;)
                    int maximumTenants) {
        return new FixedWindowGatewayQuotaService(
                maximumRequests,
                maximumCharacters,
                maximumTenants,
                Duration.ofMinutes(1),
                Clock.systemUTC());
    }

    @Override
    public void addInterceptors(InterceptorRegistry registry) {
        registry.addInterceptor(apiKeyInterceptor).addPathPatterns(&quot;/v1/confidential/**&quot;);
    }

}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/api/ConfidentialGatewayApiExceptionHandler.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.api;

import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.SecureContextException;
import org.slf4j.Logger;
import org.slf4j.LoggerFactory;
import org.springframework.http.CacheControl;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.core.Ordered;
import org.springframework.core.annotation.Order;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnProperty;
import org.springframework.web.bind.MethodArgumentNotValidException;
import org.springframework.http.converter.HttpMessageNotReadableException;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.ExceptionHandler;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestControllerAdvice;

@RestControllerAdvice(assignableTypes = ConfidentialGatewayController.class)
@ConditionalOnProperty(name = &quot;confidential.gateway.enabled&quot;, havingValue = &quot;true&quot;)
@Order(Ordered.HIGHEST_PRECEDENCE)
public class ConfidentialGatewayApiExceptionHandler {
    private static final Logger logger =
            LoggerFactory.getLogger(ConfidentialGatewayApiExceptionHandler.class);

    @ExceptionHandler(MethodArgumentNotValidException.class)
    public ResponseEntity&lt;GatewayApiError&gt; handleInvalidRequest() {
        return error(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, &quot;INVALID_REQUEST&quot;, &quot;Request validation failed.&quot;);
    }

    @ExceptionHandler(HttpMessageNotReadableException.class)
    public ResponseEntity&lt;GatewayApiError&gt; handleUnreadableRequest() {
        return error(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, &quot;INVALID_REQUEST&quot;, &quot;Request body is invalid.&quot;);
    }

    @ExceptionHandler(NativePromptFormatException.class)
    public ResponseEntity&lt;GatewayApiError&gt; handleInvalidNativePrompt() {
        return error(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, &quot;INVALID_REQUEST&quot;, &quot;Request body is invalid.&quot;);
    }

    @ExceptionHandler(IllegalArgumentException.class)
    public ResponseEntity&lt;GatewayApiError&gt; handleInvalidArgument() {
        return error(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, &quot;INVALID_REQUEST&quot;, &quot;Request parameters are invalid.&quot;);
    }

    @ExceptionHandler(SecureContextException.class)
    public ResponseEntity&lt;GatewayApiError&gt; handleInvalidContext() {
        return error(HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST, &quot;INVALID_CONTEXT&quot;,
                &quot;Secure context is invalid, expired, or unavailable.&quot;);
    }

    @ExceptionHandler(GatewayQuotaExceededException.class)
    public ResponseEntity&lt;GatewayApiError&gt; handleQuotaExceeded() {
        return error(HttpStatus.TOO_MANY_REQUESTS, &quot;QUOTA_EXCEEDED&quot;,
                &quot;Tenant request or text quota has been exceeded.&quot;);
    }

    @ExceptionHandler(Exception.class)
    public ResponseEntity&lt;GatewayApiError&gt; handleUnexpectedFailure(Exception exception) {
        logger.error(&quot;Confidential gateway request failed: {}&quot;,
                exception.getClass().getSimpleName());
        return error(HttpStatus.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR, &quot;INTERNAL_ERROR&quot;,
                &quot;The confidential gateway request could not be completed.&quot;);
    }

    private static ResponseEntity&lt;GatewayApiError&gt; error(
            HttpStatus status, String code, String message) {
        return ResponseEntity.status(status)
                .cacheControl(CacheControl.noStore())
                .body(new GatewayApiError(code, message));
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/api/ConfidentialGatewayApiKeyInterceptor.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.api;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.Optional;

import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;

import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.HttpStatus;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.HandlerInterceptor;

public final class ConfidentialGatewayApiKeyInterceptor implements HandlerInterceptor {
    private static final String BEARER_PREFIX = &quot;Bearer &quot;;

    private final GatewayCredentialAuthenticator credentialAuthenticator;

    public ConfidentialGatewayApiKeyInterceptor(
            GatewayCredentialAuthenticator credentialAuthenticator) {
        this.credentialAuthenticator = credentialAuthenticator;
    }

    @Override
    public boolean preHandle(
            HttpServletRequest request,
            HttpServletResponse response,
            Object handler) throws IOException {
        String requiredScope = requiredScope(request);
        if (requiredScope == null) {
            response.setStatus(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND.value());
            response.setHeader(HttpHeaders.CACHE_CONTROL, &quot;no-store&quot;);
            return false;
        }

        String authorization = request.getHeader(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION);
        if (authorization == null || !authorization.startsWith(BEARER_PREFIX)
                || authorization.length() == BEARER_PREFIX.length()
                || authorization.substring(BEARER_PREFIX.length()).contains(&quot; &quot;)) {
            return reject(response);
        }

        Optional&lt;GatewayPrincipal&gt; principal = credentialAuthenticator.authenticate(
                authorization.substring(BEARER_PREFIX.length()));
        if (principal.isEmpty()) {
            return reject(response);
        }
        if (!principal.get().hasScope(requiredScope)) {
            response.setStatus(HttpStatus.FORBIDDEN.value());
            response.setHeader(HttpHeaders.CACHE_CONTROL, &quot;no-store&quot;);
            response.setHeader(HttpHeaders.CONTENT_TYPE, &quot;application/json&quot;);
            response.getWriter().write(
                    &quot;{\&quot;code\&quot;:\&quot;FORBIDDEN\&quot;,\&quot;message\&quot;:\&quot;Credential is not authorized for this operation.\&quot;}&quot;);
            return false;
        }
        request.setAttribute(GatewayRequestContext.PRINCIPAL_ATTRIBUTE, principal.get());
        return true;
    }

    private static String requiredScope(HttpServletRequest request) {
        if (!&quot;POST&quot;.equalsIgnoreCase(request.getMethod())) {
            return null;
        }
        String path = request.getRequestURI().substring(request.getContextPath().length());
        return switch (path) {
            case &quot;/v1/confidential/sanitize&quot;, &quot;/v1/confidential/sanitize/batch&quot; -&gt;
                &quot;gateway:sanitize&quot;;
            case &quot;/v1/confidential/desanitize&quot;, &quot;/v1/confidential/desanitize/batch&quot; -&gt;
                &quot;gateway:desanitize&quot;;
            default -&gt; null;
        };
    }

    private static boolean reject(HttpServletResponse response) throws IOException {
        response.setStatus(HttpStatus.UNAUTHORIZED.value());
        response.setHeader(HttpHeaders.WWW_AUTHENTICATE, &quot;Bearer&quot;);
        response.setHeader(HttpHeaders.CACHE_CONTROL, &quot;no-store&quot;);
        response.setHeader(HttpHeaders.CONTENT_TYPE, &quot;application/json&quot;);
        response.getWriter().write(
                &quot;{\&quot;code\&quot;:\&quot;UNAUTHORIZED\&quot;,\&quot;message\&quot;:\&quot;Valid bearer credentials are required.\&quot;}&quot;);
        return false;
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/api/ConfidentialGatewayController.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.api;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.util.List;

import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletRequest;
import jakarta.servlet.http.HttpServletResponse;

import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.ConfidentialSegment;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.ConfidentialGatewayService;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.ConfidentialOperationGovernor;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.NativeUtf8CharSequence;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Profile;
import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnProperty;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

@RestController
@RequestMapping(&quot;/v1/confidential&quot;)
@Profile(&quot;(local-untrusted) | ((azure-cvm | gcp-cvm) &amp; gateway-oidc)&quot;)
@ConditionalOnProperty(name = &quot;confidential.gateway.enabled&quot;, havingValue = &quot;true&quot;)
public class ConfidentialGatewayController {
    private final ConfidentialGatewayService gatewayService;
    private final GatewayQuotaService quotaService;

    public ConfidentialGatewayController(
            ConfidentialGatewayService gatewayService,
            GatewayQuotaService quotaService) {
        this.gatewayService = gatewayService;
        this.quotaService = quotaService;
    }

    @PostMapping(path = &quot;/sanitize&quot;, consumes = &quot;application/json&quot;)
    public void sanitize(
            HttpServletRequest httpRequest,
            HttpServletResponse httpResponse) throws IOException {
        GatewayPrincipal principal = GatewayRequestContext.requirePrincipal(httpRequest);
        try (ConfidentialOperationGovernor.Permit permit =
                        gatewayService.acquireOperationPermit();
                ConfidentialSegment prompt = NativePromptJsonReader.readText(
                        httpRequest.getInputStream(), httpRequest.getContentLengthLong())) {
            NativeUtf8CharSequence text = new NativeUtf8CharSequence(prompt);
            quotaService.checkAndConsume(principal, text.length());
            httpResponse.setStatus(HttpServletResponse.SC_OK);
            httpResponse.setContentType(&quot;application/json&quot;);
            httpResponse.setCharacterEncoding(&quot;UTF-8&quot;);
            httpResponse.setHeader(&quot;Cache-Control&quot;, &quot;no-store&quot;);
            gatewayService.writeSanitizeJson(
                    principal.tenantId(), prompt, permit, httpResponse.getOutputStream());
        }
    }

    @PostMapping(&quot;/desanitize&quot;)
    public void desanitize(
            HttpServletRequest httpRequest,
            HttpServletResponse httpResponse) throws IOException {
        GatewayPrincipal principal = GatewayRequestContext.requirePrincipal(httpRequest);
        try (ConfidentialOperationGovernor.Permit permit =
                        gatewayService.acquireOperationPermit();
                NativePromptJsonReader.NativeDesanitizeRequest request =
                        NativePromptJsonReader.readDesanitize(
                                httpRequest.getInputStream(),
                                httpRequest.getContentLengthLong())) {
            int responseLength = new NativeUtf8CharSequence(request.response()).length();
            quotaService.checkAndConsume(
                    principal, Math.addExact(responseLength, request.secureContext().length()));
            httpResponse.setStatus(HttpServletResponse.SC_OK);
            httpResponse.setContentType(&quot;application/json&quot;);
            httpResponse.setCharacterEncoding(&quot;UTF-8&quot;);
            httpResponse.setHeader(&quot;Cache-Control&quot;, &quot;no-store&quot;);
            gatewayService.writeDesanitizeJson(
                    principal.tenantId(),
                    request.response(),
                    request.secureContext(),
                    permit,
                    httpResponse.getOutputStream());
        }
    }

    @PostMapping(path = &quot;/sanitize/batch&quot;, consumes = &quot;application/json&quot;)
    public void sanitizeBatch(
            HttpServletRequest httpRequest,
            HttpServletResponse httpResponse) throws IOException {
        GatewayPrincipal principal = GatewayRequestContext.requirePrincipal(httpRequest);
        try (ConfidentialOperationGovernor.Permit permit =
                        gatewayService.acquireOperationPermit();
                NativePromptJsonReader.NativePromptBatch prompts =
                        NativePromptJsonReader.readSegments(
                                httpRequest.getInputStream(),
                                httpRequest.getContentLengthLong())) {
            long totalCharacters = 0;
            for (ConfidentialSegment segment : prompts.segments()) {
                totalCharacters += new NativeUtf8CharSequence(segment).length();
            }
            quotaService.checkAndConsume(principal, Math.toIntExact(totalCharacters));
            httpResponse.setStatus(HttpServletResponse.SC_OK);
            httpResponse.setContentType(&quot;application/json&quot;);
            httpResponse.setCharacterEncoding(&quot;UTF-8&quot;);
            httpResponse.setHeader(&quot;Cache-Control&quot;, &quot;no-store&quot;);
            gatewayService.writeSanitizeBatchJson(
                    principal.tenantId(),
                    prompts.segments(),
                    permit,
                    httpResponse.getOutputStream());
        }
    }

    @PostMapping(&quot;/desanitize/batch&quot;)
    public void desanitizeBatch(
            HttpServletRequest httpRequest,
            HttpServletResponse httpResponse) throws IOException {
        GatewayPrincipal principal = GatewayRequestContext.requirePrincipal(httpRequest);
        try (ConfidentialOperationGovernor.Permit permit =
                        gatewayService.acquireOperationPermit();
                NativePromptJsonReader.NativeBatchDesanitizeRequest request =
                        NativePromptJsonReader.readBatchDesanitize(
                                httpRequest.getInputStream(),
                                httpRequest.getContentLengthLong())) {
            long responseCharacters = 0;
            for (ConfidentialSegment response : request.responses().segments()) {
                responseCharacters += new NativeUtf8CharSequence(response).length();
            }
            int quotaUnits = Math.toIntExact(
                    responseCharacters + request.secureContext().length());
            quotaService.checkAndConsume(principal, quotaUnits);
            httpResponse.setStatus(HttpServletResponse.SC_OK);
            httpResponse.setContentType(&quot;application/json&quot;);
            httpResponse.setCharacterEncoding(&quot;UTF-8&quot;);
            httpResponse.setHeader(&quot;Cache-Control&quot;, &quot;no-store&quot;);
            gatewayService.writeDesanitizeBatchJson(
                    principal.tenantId(),
                    request.responses().segments(),
                    request.secureContext(),
                    permit,
                    httpResponse.getOutputStream());
        }
    }

    public record DesanitizeResponse(String desanitizedText) {
    }

    public record BatchDesanitizeResponse(List&lt;String&gt; desanitizedResponses) {
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/api/ConfidentialOperationGovernorConfiguration.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.api;

            @Value(&quot;${confidential.gateway.max-concurrent-operations:64}&quot;)

        return new ConfidentialOperationGovernor(maximumConcurrentOperations);
    }

}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/api/FixedWindowGatewayQuotaService.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.api;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;

public final class FixedWindowGatewayQuotaService implements GatewayQuotaService {
    private final int maximumRequests;
    private final long maximumTextCharacters;
    private final int maximumTenants;
    private final Duration window;
    private final Clock clock;
    private final ConcurrentHashMap&lt;String, UsageWindow&gt; usageByTenant =
            new ConcurrentHashMap&lt;&gt;();

    public FixedWindowGatewayQuotaService(
            int maximumRequests,
            long maximumTextCharacters,
            int maximumTenants,
            Duration window,
            Clock clock) {
        if (maximumRequests &lt; 1 || maximumTextCharacters &lt; 1 || maximumTenants &lt; 1
                || window == null || window.isNegative() || window.isZero()
                || window.toMillis() &lt; 1 || clock == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Gateway quota settings are invalid.&quot;);
        }
        this.maximumRequests = maximumRequests;
        this.maximumTextCharacters = maximumTextCharacters;
        this.maximumTenants = maximumTenants;
        this.window = window;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    @Override
    public void checkAndConsume(GatewayPrincipal principal, int textCharacters) {
        if (principal == null || textCharacters &lt; 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Gateway quota request is invalid.&quot;);
        }
        long currentWindow = Math.floorDiv(clock.instant().toEpochMilli(), window.toMillis());
        UsageWindow usage = usageFor(principal.tenantId(), currentWindow);
        synchronized (usage) {
            usage.advance(currentWindow);
            if (usage.requests &gt;= maximumRequests
                    || textCharacters &gt; maximumTextCharacters - usage.textCharacters) {
                throw new GatewayQuotaExceededException(&quot;Gateway tenant quota exceeded.&quot;);
            }
            usage.requests++;
            usage.textCharacters += textCharacters;
        }
    }

    private synchronized UsageWindow usageFor(String tenantId, long currentWindow) {
        UsageWindow existing = usageByTenant.get(tenantId);
        if (existing != null) {
            return existing;
        }
        usageByTenant.entrySet().removeIf(
                entry -&gt; entry.getValue().windowIndex &lt; currentWindow - 1);
        if (usageByTenant.size() &gt;= maximumTenants) {
            throw new GatewayQuotaExceededException(
                    &quot;Gateway tenant capacity quota exceeded.&quot;);
        }
        return usageByTenant.computeIfAbsent(tenantId, ignored -&gt; new UsageWindow(currentWindow));
    }

    private static final class UsageWindow {
        private volatile long windowIndex;
        private int requests;
        private long textCharacters;

        private UsageWindow(long windowIndex) {
            this.windowIndex = windowIndex;
        }

        private void advance(long currentWindow) {
            if (currentWindow &lt; windowIndex) {
                throw new GatewayQuotaExceededException(
                        &quot;Gateway request belongs to an expired quota window.&quot;);
            }
            if (windowIndex &lt; currentWindow) {
                windowIndex = currentWindow;
                requests = 0;
                textCharacters = 0;
            }
        }
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/api/GatewayApiError.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.api;

import java.net.URI;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Profile;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.jwt.JwtDecoder;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.jwt.JwtValidators;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.jwt.NimbusJwtDecoder;

@Configuration(proxyBeanMethods = false)
@Profile(&quot;gateway-oidc&quot;)
public class GatewayOidcAuthenticationConfiguration {
    @Bean
    public JwtDecoder gatewayJwtDecoder(
            @Value(&quot;${confidential.gateway.oidc.issuer}&quot;) String issuer,
            @Value(&quot;${confidential.gateway.oidc.jwks-uri}&quot;) String jwksUri) {
        requireHttpsUri(issuer, &quot;confidential.gateway.oidc.issuer&quot;);
        requireHttpsUri(jwksUri, &quot;confidential.gateway.oidc.jwks-uri&quot;);
        NimbusJwtDecoder decoder = NimbusJwtDecoder.withJwkSetUri(jwksUri).build();
        decoder.setJwtValidator(JwtValidators.createDefaultWithIssuer(issuer));
        return decoder;
    }

    @Bean
    public GatewayCredentialAuthenticator oidcGatewayCredentialAuthenticator(
            JwtDecoder gatewayJwtDecoder,
            @Value(&quot;${confidential.gateway.oidc.issuer}&quot;) String issuer,
            @Value(&quot;${confidential.gateway.oidc.audience}&quot;) String audience,
            @Value(&quot;${confidential.gateway.oidc.tenant-claim:tenant_id}&quot;) String tenantClaim,
            @Value(&quot;${confidential.gateway.oidc.scopes-claim:scope}&quot;) String scopesClaim,
            @Value(&quot;${confidential.gateway.oidc.fixed-scopes:}&quot;) String fixedScopes) {
        return new OidcJwtGatewayCredentialAuthenticator(
                gatewayJwtDecoder,
                issuer,
                audience,
                tenantClaim,
                scopesClaim,
                parseFixedScopes(fixedScopes),
                Clock.systemUTC());
    }

    private static Set&lt;String&gt; parseFixedScopes(String rawScopes) {
        if (rawScopes == null || rawScopes.isBlank()) {
            return Set.of();
        }
        return Arrays.stream(rawScopes.split(&quot;,&quot;, -1))
                .map(String::trim)
                .collect(Collectors.toUnmodifiableSet());
    }

    private static void requireHttpsUri(String raw, String property) {
        final URI uri;
        try {
            uri = URI.create(raw);
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(property + &quot; must be an absolute HTTPS URI.&quot;, e);
        }
        if (!&quot;https&quot;.equalsIgnoreCase(uri.getScheme())
                || uri.getHost() == null
                || uri.getUserInfo() != null
                || uri.getFragment() != null) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(property + &quot; must be an absolute HTTPS URI.&quot;);
        }
    }

import java.util.Set;

public record GatewayPrincipal(String subject, String tenantId, Set&lt;String&gt; scopes) {
    public GatewayPrincipal {
        if (subject == null || subject.isBlank() || subject.length() &gt; 256
                || tenantId == null || tenantId.isBlank() || tenantId.length() &gt; 256
                || scopes == null || scopes.stream().anyMatch(
                        scope -&gt; scope == null || scope.isBlank() || scope.length() &gt; 128)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Gateway principal is invalid.&quot;);
        }
        scopes = Set.copyOf(scopes);
    }

    public boolean hasScope(String scope) {
        return scopes.contains(scope);
    }

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.security.MessageDigest;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.Set;

public final class LocalStaticBearerCredentialAuthenticator
        implements GatewayCredentialAuthenticator {
    private final byte[] expectedToken;
    private final GatewayPrincipal principal;

    public LocalStaticBearerCredentialAuthenticator(String token, String tenantId) {
        if (token == null || token.length() &lt; 32) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    &quot;Gateway API token must contain at least 32 characters.&quot;);
        }
        this.expectedToken = token.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        this.principal = new GatewayPrincipal(
                &quot;local-static-bearer&quot;,
                tenantId,
                Set.of(&quot;gateway:sanitize&quot;, &quot;gateway:desanitize&quot;));
    }

    @Override
    public Optional&lt;GatewayPrincipal&gt; authenticate(String bearerCredential) {
        if (bearerCredential == null) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
        byte[] suppliedToken = bearerCredential.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        return MessageDigest.isEqual(expectedToken, suppliedToken)
                ? Optional.of(principal)
                : Optional.empty();
    }

import org.springframework.boot.autoconfigure.condition.ConditionalOnProperty;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Import;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Profile;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.config.annotation.InterceptorRegistry;
import org.springframework.web.servlet.config.annotation.WebMvcConfigurer;

@Configuration(proxyBeanMethods = false)
@Profile(&quot;local-untrusted&quot;)
@ConditionalOnProperty(name = &quot;confidential.gateway.enabled&quot;, havingValue = &quot;true&quot;)
@Import({ConfidentialOperationGovernorConfiguration.class, GatewayPiiDetectorConfiguration.class})
public class LocalUntrustedGatewayConfiguration implements WebMvcConfigurer {
    private final ConfidentialGatewayApiKeyInterceptor apiKeyInterceptor;

    public LocalUntrustedGatewayConfiguration(
            @Value(&quot;${CONFIDENTIAL_GATEWAY_API_TOKEN:}&quot;) String apiToken,
            @Value(&quot;${CONFIDENTIAL_GATEWAY_TENANT_ID:local-development}&quot;) String tenantId,
            @Value(&quot;${server.address:}&quot;) String serverAddress) {
        requireLoopbackBinding(serverAddress);
        GatewayCredentialAuthenticator authenticator =
                new LocalStaticBearerCredentialAuthenticator(apiToken, tenantId);
        this.apiKeyInterceptor = new ConfidentialGatewayApiKeyInterceptor(authenticator);
    }

    private static void requireLoopbackBinding(String serverAddress) {
        if (serverAddress == null || serverAddress.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                    &quot;The local-untrusted Gateway requires server.address to be explicitly bound to loopback.&quot;);
        }
        try {
            if (!InetAddress.getByName(serverAddress).isLoopbackAddress()) {
                throw new IllegalStateException(
                        &quot;The local-untrusted Gateway may only bind to a loopback address.&quot;);
            }
        } catch (UnknownHostException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                    &quot;The local-untrusted Gateway server.address is not a valid loopback address.&quot;);
        }
    }

    @Bean
    public GatewayQuotaService gatewayQuotaService(
            @Value(&quot;${CONFIDENTIAL_GATEWAY_MAX_REQUESTS_PER_MINUTE:60}&quot;)
                    int maximumRequests,
            @Value(&quot;${CONFIDENTIAL_GATEWAY_MAX_CHARACTERS_PER_MINUTE:5000000}&quot;)
                    long maximumCharacters,
            @Value(&quot;${CONFIDENTIAL_GATEWAY_MAX_TENANTS:10000}&quot;)
                    int maximumTenants) {
        return new FixedWindowGatewayQuotaService(
                maximumRequests,
                maximumCharacters,
                maximumTenants,
                Duration.ofMinutes(1),
                Clock.systemUTC());
    }

    @Bean
    public SecureContextKeyProvider secureContextKeyProvider(
            @Value(&quot;${CONFIDENTIAL_GATEWAY_CONTEXT_KEY:}&quot;) String encodedKey,
            @Value(&quot;${CONFIDENTIAL_GATEWAY_CONTEXT_KEY_ID:local-dev}&quot;) String keyId) {
        byte[] keyBytes;
        try {
            keyBytes = Base64.getDecoder().decode(encodedKey);
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                    &quot;CONFIDENTIAL_GATEWAY_CONTEXT_KEY must be a Base64-encoded 256-bit key.&quot;);
        }
        if (keyBytes.length != 32) {
            Arrays.fill(keyBytes, (byte) 0);
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                    &quot;CONFIDENTIAL_GATEWAY_CONTEXT_KEY must be a Base64-encoded 256-bit key.&quot;);
        }

        SecretKey key = new SecretKeySpec(keyBytes, &quot;AES&quot;);
        Arrays.fill(keyBytes, (byte) 0);
        SecureContextKey contextKey = new SecureContextKey(keyId, key);
        return new SecureContextKeyProvider() {
            @Override
            public SecureContextKey currentKey() {
                return contextKey;
            }

            @Override
            public Optional&lt;SecureContextKey&gt; findKey(String id) {
                return contextKey.id().equals(id) ? Optional.of(contextKey) : Optional.empty();
            }
        };
    }

    @Bean
    public AesGcmSecureContextCodec secureContextCodec(
            SecureContextKeyProvider keyProvider,
            ObjectMapper objectMapper) {
        return new AesGcmSecureContextCodec(
                keyProvider, objectMapper, new SecureRandom(), Clock.systemUTC());
    }

    @Bean
    public ConfidentialGatewayService confidentialGatewayService(
            AesGcmSecureContextCodec contextCodec,
            ConfidentialOperationGovernor operationGovernor,
            PiiDetector piiDetector,
            @Value(&quot;${CONFIDENTIAL_GATEWAY_CONTEXT_TTL_MINUTES:10}&quot;) long ttlMinutes) {
        if (ttlMinutes &lt;= 0 || ttlMinutes &gt; 1440) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                    &quot;CONFIDENTIAL_GATEWAY_CONTEXT_TTL_MINUTES must be between 1 and 1440.&quot;);
        }
        return new ConfidentialGatewayService(
                piiDetector,
                contextCodec,
                Duration.ofMinutes(ttlMinutes),
                new SecureRandom(),
                Clock.systemUTC(),
                operationGovernor);
    }

    public void addInterceptors(InterceptorRegistry registry) {
        registry.addInterceptor(apiKeyInterceptor).addPathPatterns(&quot;/v1/confidential/**&quot;);
    }

import java.io.InputStream;

import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.ConfidentialSegment;

final class NativePromptJsonReader {
    private static final int MAX_PROMPT_CHARS = 1_000_000;
    private static final int MAX_PROMPT_BYTES = MAX_PROMPT_CHARS * 4;
    private static final int MAX_JSON_BYTES = MAX_PROMPT_CHARS * 6 + 64;
    private static final int MAX_BATCH_SEGMENTS = 100;
    private static final int MAX_BATCH_CHARS = 5_000_000;
    private static final int MAX_BATCH_BYTES = MAX_BATCH_CHARS * 4;
    private static final int MAX_SECURE_CONTEXT_CHARS = 2_000_000;
    private static final int MAX_BATCH_JSON_BYTES =
            MAX_BATCH_CHARS * 6 + MAX_SECURE_CONTEXT_CHARS + 1024;

    private NativePromptJsonReader() {
    }

    static ConfidentialSegment readText(InputStream input, long contentLength) throws IOException {
        if (contentLength &gt; MAX_JSON_BYTES) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Request body exceeds the supported size.&quot;);
        }
        int promptCapacity = contentLength &gt;= 0
                ? (int) Math.min(contentLength, MAX_PROMPT_BYTES)
                : MAX_PROMPT_BYTES;
        Parser parser = new Parser(
                Objects.requireNonNull(input, &quot;input&quot;), MAX_JSON_BYTES);
        return parser.readText(promptCapacity);
    }

    static NativePromptBatch readSegments(InputStream input, long contentLength)
            throws IOException {
        if (contentLength &gt; MAX_BATCH_JSON_BYTES) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Request body exceeds the supported size.&quot;);
        }
        int storageCapacity = contentLength &gt;= 0
                ? (int) Math.min(contentLength, MAX_BATCH_BYTES)
                : MAX_BATCH_BYTES;
        return new Parser(
                Objects.requireNonNull(input, &quot;input&quot;), MAX_BATCH_JSON_BYTES)
                .readSegments(storageCapacity);
    }

    static NativeDesanitizeRequest readDesanitize(InputStream input, long contentLength)
            throws IOException {
        if (contentLength &gt; MAX_JSON_BYTES + MAX_SECURE_CONTEXT_CHARS) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Request body exceeds the supported size.&quot;);
        }
        int capacity = contentLength &gt;= 0
                ? (int) Math.min(contentLength, MAX_PROMPT_BYTES)
                : MAX_PROMPT_BYTES;
        return new Parser(
                Objects.requireNonNull(input, &quot;input&quot;),
                MAX_JSON_BYTES + MAX_SECURE_CONTEXT_CHARS)
                .readDesanitize(capacity);
    }

    static NativeBatchDesanitizeRequest readBatchDesanitize(
            InputStream input, long contentLength) throws IOException {
        if (contentLength &gt; MAX_BATCH_JSON_BYTES) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Request body exceeds the supported size.&quot;);
        }
        int capacity = contentLength &gt;= 0
                ? (int) Math.min(contentLength, MAX_BATCH_BYTES)
                : MAX_BATCH_BYTES;
        return new Parser(
                Objects.requireNonNull(input, &quot;input&quot;), MAX_BATCH_JSON_BYTES)
                .readBatchDesanitize(capacity);
    }

    static final class NativePromptBatch implements AutoCloseable {
        private final ConfidentialSegment storage;
        private final List&lt;ConfidentialSegment&gt; segments;

        private NativePromptBatch(
                ConfidentialSegment storage, List&lt;ConfidentialSegment&gt; segments) {
            this.storage = Objects.requireNonNull(storage, &quot;storage&quot;);
            this.segments = List.copyOf(segments);
        }

        List&lt;ConfidentialSegment&gt; segments() {
            return segments;
        }

        @Override
        public void close() {
            try {
                for (ConfidentialSegment segment : segments) {
                    segment.close();
                }
            } finally {
                storage.close();
            }
        }
    }

    static final class NativeDesanitizeRequest implements AutoCloseable {
        private final ConfidentialSegment response;
        private final String secureContext;

        private NativeDesanitizeRequest(
                ConfidentialSegment response, String secureContext) {
            this.response = response;
            this.secureContext = secureContext;
        }

        ConfidentialSegment response() {
            return response;
        }

        String secureContext() {
            return secureContext;
        }

        @Override
        public void close() {
            response.close();
        }
    }

    static final class NativeBatchDesanitizeRequest implements AutoCloseable {
        private final NativePromptBatch responses;
        private final String secureContext;

        private NativeBatchDesanitizeRequest(
                NativePromptBatch responses, String secureContext) {
            this.responses = responses;
            this.secureContext = secureContext;
        }

        NativePromptBatch responses() {
            return responses;
        }

        String secureContext() {
            return secureContext;
        }

        @Override
        public void close() {
            responses.close();
        }
    }

    private static final class Parser {
        private final InputStream input;
        private final int maxJsonBytes;
        private int bytesRead;
        private int pending = -2;

        private Parser(InputStream input, int maxJsonBytes) {
            this.input = input;
            this.maxJsonBytes = maxJsonBytes;
        }

        private ConfidentialSegment readText(int promptCapacity) throws IOException {
            ConfidentialSegment prompt = ConfidentialSegment.allocate(promptCapacity);
            try {
                whitespace();
                expect(&apos;{&apos;);
                boolean textSeen = false;
                while (true) {
                    whitespace();
                    if (peek() != &apos;&quot;&apos;) {
                        throw invalidRequest();
                    }
                    String field = readKey();
                    whitespace();
                    expect(&apos;:&apos;);
                    whitespace();
                    if (&quot;text&quot;.equals(field) &amp;&amp; !textSeen &amp;&amp; peek() == &apos;&quot;&apos;) {
                        readString(prompt);
                        textSeen = true;
                    } else if (&quot;tenantId&quot;.equals(field) &amp;&amp; peek() == &apos;&quot;&apos;) {
                        skipString();
                    } else {
                        throw invalidRequest();
                    }
                    whitespace();
                    int delimiter = read();
                    if (delimiter == &apos;}&apos;) {
                        break;
                    }
                    if (delimiter != &apos;,&apos;) {
                        throw invalidRequest();
                    }
                }
                if (!textSeen) {
                    throw invalidRequest();
                }
                whitespace();
                if (read() != -1) {
                    throw invalidRequest();
                }
                return prompt;
            } catch (RuntimeException | IOException | Error failure) {
                prompt.close();
                throw failure;
            }
        }

        private NativeDesanitizeRequest readDesanitize(int responseCapacity)
                throws IOException {
            ConfidentialSegment response = ConfidentialSegment.allocate(responseCapacity);
            try {
                whitespace();
                expect(&apos;{&apos;);
                boolean responseSeen = false;
                String secureContext = null;
                while (true) {
                    whitespace();
                    if (peek() == &apos;}&apos;) {
                        read();
                        break;
                    }
                    String field = readKey();
                    whitespace();
                    expect(&apos;:&apos;);
                    whitespace();
                    if (&quot;modelResponse&quot;.equals(field) &amp;&amp; !responseSeen &amp;&amp; peek() == &apos;&quot;&apos;) {
                        readString(response);
                        responseSeen = true;
                    } else if (&quot;secureContext&quot;.equals(field)
                            &amp;&amp; secureContext == null &amp;&amp; peek() == &apos;&quot;&apos;) {
                        secureContext = readCapturedString(MAX_SECURE_CONTEXT_CHARS);
                    } else {
                        throw invalidRequest();
                    }
                    whitespace();
                    int delimiter = read();
                    if (delimiter == &apos;}&apos;) {
                        break;
                    }
                    if (delimiter != &apos;,&apos;) {
                        throw invalidRequest();
                    }
                }
                if (!responseSeen || secureContext == null || secureContext.isEmpty()) {
                    throw invalidRequest();
                }
                whitespace();
                if (read() != -1) {
                    throw invalidRequest();
                }
                return new NativeDesanitizeRequest(response, secureContext);
            } catch (RuntimeException | IOException | Error failure) {
                response.close();
                throw failure;
            }
        }

        private NativeBatchDesanitizeRequest readBatchDesanitize(int responseCapacity)
                throws IOException {
            ConfidentialSegment storage = ConfidentialSegment.allocate(responseCapacity);
            List&lt;Span&gt; spans = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;();
            try {
                whitespace();
                expect(&apos;{&apos;);
                boolean responsesSeen = false;
                String secureContext = null;
                while (true) {
                    whitespace();
                    if (peek() == &apos;}&apos;) {
                        read();
                        break;
                    }
                    String field = readKey();
                    whitespace();
                    expect(&apos;:&apos;);
                    whitespace();
                    if (&quot;modelResponses&quot;.equals(field) &amp;&amp; !responsesSeen) {
                        readSegmentArray(storage, spans, 0);
                        responsesSeen = true;
                    } else if (&quot;secureContext&quot;.equals(field)
                            &amp;&amp; secureContext == null &amp;&amp; peek() == &apos;&quot;&apos;) {
                        secureContext = readCapturedString(MAX_SECURE_CONTEXT_CHARS);
                    } else {
                        throw invalidRequest();
                    }
                    whitespace();
                    int delimiter = read();
                    if (delimiter == &apos;}&apos;) {
                        break;
                    }
                    if (delimiter != &apos;,&apos;) {
                        throw invalidRequest();
                    }
                }
                if (!responsesSeen || spans.isEmpty()
                        || secureContext == null || secureContext.isEmpty()) {
                    throw invalidRequest();
                }
                whitespace();
                if (read() != -1) {
                    throw invalidRequest();
                }
                long usedBytes = spans.get(spans.size() - 1).endOffset();
                storage.truncate(usedBytes);
                List&lt;ConfidentialSegment&gt; segments = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;(spans.size());
                for (Span span : spans) {
                    segments.add(storage.slice(span.offset(), span.length()));
                }
                return new NativeBatchDesanitizeRequest(
                        new NativePromptBatch(storage, segments), secureContext);
            } catch (RuntimeException | IOException | Error failure) {
                storage.close();
                throw failure;
            }
        }

        private NativePromptBatch readSegments(int storageCapacity) throws IOException {
            ConfidentialSegment storage = ConfidentialSegment.allocate(storageCapacity);
            List&lt;Span&gt; spans = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;();
            try {
                whitespace();
                expect(&apos;{&apos;);
                boolean segmentsSeen = false;
                int totalChars = 0;
                while (true) {
                    whitespace();
                    if (peek() == &apos;}&apos;) {
                        read();
                        break;
                    }
                    String field = readKey();
                    whitespace();
                    expect(&apos;:&apos;);
                    whitespace();
                    if (&quot;segments&quot;.equals(field) &amp;&amp; !segmentsSeen) {
                        totalChars = readSegmentArray(storage, spans, totalChars);
                        segmentsSeen = true;
                    } else if (&quot;tenantId&quot;.equals(field) &amp;&amp; peek() == &apos;&quot;&apos;) {
                        skipString();
                    } else {
                        throw invalidRequest();
                    }
                    whitespace();
                    int delimiter = read();
                    if (delimiter == &apos;}&apos;) {
                        break;
                    }
                    if (delimiter != &apos;,&apos;) {
                        throw invalidRequest();
                    }
                }
                if (!segmentsSeen || spans.isEmpty()) {
                    throw invalidRequest();
                }
                whitespace();
                if (read() != -1) {
                    throw invalidRequest();
                }
                long usedBytes = spans.get(spans.size() - 1).endOffset();
                storage.truncate(usedBytes);
                List&lt;ConfidentialSegment&gt; segments = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;(spans.size());
                for (Span span : spans) {
                    segments.add(storage.slice(span.offset(), span.length()));
                }
                return new NativePromptBatch(storage, segments);
            } catch (RuntimeException | IOException | Error failure) {
                storage.close();
                throw failure;
            }
        }

        private int readSegmentArray(
                ConfidentialSegment storage, List&lt;Span&gt; spans, int totalChars)
                throws IOException {
            expect(&apos;[&apos;);
            while (true) {
                whitespace();
                if (peek() == &apos;]&apos;) {
                    read();
                    return totalChars;
                }
                if (spans.size() == MAX_BATCH_SEGMENTS || peek() != &apos;&quot;&apos;) {
                    throw invalidRequest();
                }
                long start = spans.isEmpty()
                        ? 0
                        : spans.get(spans.size() - 1).endOffset();
                ParsedString parsed = readStringAt(
                        storage, start, false, false, MAX_PROMPT_CHARS);
                if (parsed.characters() &gt; MAX_PROMPT_CHARS
                        || totalChars &gt; MAX_BATCH_CHARS - parsed.characters()) {
                    throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                            &quot;Text segments exceed the supported size.&quot;);
                }
                totalChars += parsed.characters();
                spans.add(new Span(start, parsed.byteLength()));
                whitespace();
                int delimiter = read();
                if (delimiter == &apos;]&apos;) {
                    return totalChars;
                }
                if (delimiter != &apos;,&apos;) {
                    throw invalidRequest();
                }
                whitespace();
                if (peek() != &apos;&quot;&apos;) {
                    throw invalidRequest();
                }
            }
        }

        private int readString(ConfidentialSegment target) throws IOException {
            return readStringAt(
                    target, 0, true, target == null, target == null ? 64 : MAX_PROMPT_CHARS)
                    .characters();
        }

        private void skipString() throws IOException {
            readStringAt(null, 0, true, false, maxJsonBytes);
        }

        private ParsedString readStringAt(
                ConfidentialSegment target,
                long startOffset,
                boolean truncate,
                boolean capture,
                int maximumCharacters)
                throws IOException {
            expect(&apos;&quot;&apos;);
            StringBuilder key = capture ? new StringBuilder() : null;
            long writtenBytes = startOffset;
            long initialOffset = startOffset;
            int characters = 0;
            while (true) {
                int value = read();
                if (value &lt; 0) {
                    throw invalidRequest();
                }
                if (value == &apos;&quot;&apos;) {
                    if (capture) {
                        returnString = key.toString();
                        return new ParsedString(characters, writtenBytes - initialOffset);
                    }
                    if (truncate &amp;&amp; target != null) {
                        target.truncate(writtenBytes);
                    }
                    return new ParsedString(characters, writtenBytes - initialOffset);
                }

                int codePoint;
                if (value == &apos;\\&apos;) {
                    int escape = read();
                    codePoint = switch (escape) {
                        case &apos;&quot;&apos;, &apos;\\&apos;, &apos;/&apos; -&gt; escape;
                        case &apos;b&apos; -&gt; &apos;\b&apos;;
                        case &apos;f&apos; -&gt; &apos;\f&apos;;
                        case &apos;n&apos; -&gt; &apos;\n&apos;;
                        case &apos;r&apos; -&gt; &apos;\r&apos;;
                        case &apos;t&apos; -&gt; &apos;\t&apos;;
                        case &apos;u&apos; -&gt; readEscapedCodePoint();
                        default -&gt; throw invalidRequest();
                    };
                } else if (value &lt; 0x20) {
                    throw invalidRequest();
                } else if (value &lt; 0x80) {
                    codePoint = value;
                } else {
                    codePoint = readUtf8CodePoint(value);
                }

                int utf16Length = Character.charCount(codePoint);
                if (characters &gt; maximumCharacters - utf16Length) {
                    throw invalidRequest();
                }
                characters += utf16Length;
                if (capture) {
                    if (codePoint &gt; 0x7f) {
                        throw invalidRequest();
                    }
                    key.append((char) codePoint);
                } else if (target != null) {
                    try {
                        writtenBytes = writeUtf8(target, writtenBytes, codePoint);
                    } catch (IndexOutOfBoundsException capacityExceeded) {
                        throw invalidRequest();
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        private String readKey() throws IOException {
            String key = readCapturedString(64);
            return key;
        }

        private String readCapturedString(int maximumCharacters) throws IOException {
            readStringAt(null, 0, true, true, maximumCharacters);
            String key = returnString;
            returnString = null;
            return key;
        }

        private String returnString;

        private record ParsedString(int characters, long byteLength) {
        }

        private record Span(long offset, long length) {
            private long endOffset() {
                return offset + length;
            }
        }

        private int readEscapedCodePoint() throws IOException {
            int first = readHexCodeUnit();
            if (Character.isHighSurrogate((char) first)) {
                if (read() != &apos;\\&apos; || read() != &apos;u&apos;) {
                    throw invalidRequest();
                }
                int second = readHexCodeUnit();
                if (!Character.isLowSurrogate((char) second)) {
                    throw invalidRequest();
                }
                return Character.toCodePoint((char) first, (char) second);
            }
            if (Character.isLowSurrogate((char) first)) {
                throw invalidRequest();
            }
            return first;
        }

        private int readHexCodeUnit() throws IOException {
            int value = 0;
            for (int index = 0; index &lt; 4; index++) {
                int digit = Character.digit((char) read(), 16);
                if (digit &lt; 0) {
                    throw invalidRequest();
                }
                value = (value &lt;&lt; 4) | digit;
            }
            return value;
        }

        private int readUtf8CodePoint(int first) throws IOException {
            int count;
            int codePoint;
            if (first &gt;= 0xC2 &amp;&amp; first &lt;= 0xDF) {
                count = 1;
                codePoint = first &amp; 0x1F;
            } else if (first &gt;= 0xE0 &amp;&amp; first &lt;= 0xEF) {
                count = 2;
                codePoint = first &amp; 0x0F;
            } else if (first &gt;= 0xF0 &amp;&amp; first &lt;= 0xF4) {
                count = 3;
                codePoint = first &amp; 0x07;
            } else {
                throw invalidRequest();
            }
            for (int index = 0; index &lt; count; index++) {
                int next = read();
                if ((next &amp; 0xC0) != 0x80) {
                    throw invalidRequest();
                }
                codePoint = (codePoint &lt;&lt; 6) | (next &amp; 0x3F);
            }
            if ((count == 1 &amp;&amp; codePoint &lt; 0x80)
                    || (count == 2 &amp;&amp; codePoint &lt; 0x800)
                    || (count == 3 &amp;&amp; codePoint &lt; 0x10000)
                    || codePoint &gt; Character.MAX_CODE_POINT
                    || (codePoint &gt;= 0xD800 &amp;&amp; codePoint &lt;= 0xDFFF)) {
                throw invalidRequest();
            }
            return codePoint;
        }

        private static long writeUtf8(
                ConfidentialSegment target, long offset, int codePoint) {
            if (codePoint &lt;= 0x7F) {
                target.writeByte(offset, (byte) codePoint);
                return offset + 1;
            }
            if (codePoint &lt;= 0x7FF) {
                target.writeByte(offset++, (byte) (0xC0 | (codePoint &gt;&gt;&gt; 6)));
                target.writeByte(offset++, (byte) (0x80 | (codePoint &amp; 0x3F)));
                return offset;
            }
            if (codePoint &lt;= 0xFFFF) {
                target.writeByte(offset++, (byte) (0xE0 | (codePoint &gt;&gt;&gt; 12)));
                target.writeByte(offset++, (byte) (0x80 | ((codePoint &gt;&gt;&gt; 6) &amp; 0x3F)));
                target.writeByte(offset++, (byte) (0x80 | (codePoint &amp; 0x3F)));
                return offset;
            }
            target.writeByte(offset++, (byte) (0xF0 | (codePoint &gt;&gt;&gt; 18)));
            target.writeByte(offset++, (byte) (0x80 | ((codePoint &gt;&gt;&gt; 12) &amp; 0x3F)));
            target.writeByte(offset++, (byte) (0x80 | ((codePoint &gt;&gt;&gt; 6) &amp; 0x3F)));
            target.writeByte(offset++, (byte) (0x80 | (codePoint &amp; 0x3F)));
            return offset;
        }

        private int peek() throws IOException {
            if (pending == -2) {
                pending = readUnderlying();
            }
            return pending;
        }

        private int read() throws IOException {
            int value;
            if (pending != -2) {
                value = pending;
                pending = -2;
            } else {
                value = readUnderlying();
            }
            return value;
        }

        private int readUnderlying() throws IOException {
            int value = input.read();
            if (value &gt;= 0 &amp;&amp; ++bytesRead &gt; maxJsonBytes) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Request body exceeds the supported size.&quot;);
            }
            return value;
        }

        private void expect(int expected) throws IOException {
            if (read() != expected) {
                throw invalidRequest();
            }
        }

        private void whitespace() throws IOException {
            while (true) {
                int value = peek();
                if (value != &apos; &apos; &amp;&amp; value != &apos;\t&apos; &amp;&amp; value != &apos;\r&apos; &amp;&amp; value != &apos;\n&apos;) {
                    return;
                }
                read();
            }
        }

        private static IllegalArgumentException invalidRequest() {
            return new NativePromptFormatException();
        }
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/api/OidcJwtGatewayCredentialAuthenticator.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.api;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.LinkedHashSet;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.Set;

import org.springframework.security.oauth2.jwt.Jwt;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.jwt.JwtDecoder;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.jwt.JwtException;

public final class OidcJwtGatewayCredentialAuthenticator
        implements GatewayCredentialAuthenticator {
    private static final Set&lt;String&gt; GATEWAY_SCOPES =
            Set.of(&quot;gateway:sanitize&quot;, &quot;gateway:desanitize&quot;);

    private final JwtDecoder jwtDecoder;
    private final String issuer;
    private final String audience;
    private final String tenantClaim;
    private final String scopesClaim;
    private final Set&lt;String&gt; fixedScopes;
    private final Clock clock;

    public OidcJwtGatewayCredentialAuthenticator(
            JwtDecoder jwtDecoder,
            String issuer,
            String audience,
            String tenantClaim,
            String scopesClaim,
            Clock clock) {
        this(jwtDecoder, issuer, audience, tenantClaim, scopesClaim, Set.of(), clock);
    }

    public OidcJwtGatewayCredentialAuthenticator(
            JwtDecoder jwtDecoder,
            String issuer,
            String audience,
            String tenantClaim,
            String scopesClaim,
            Set&lt;String&gt; fixedScopes,
            Clock clock) {
        if (jwtDecoder == null || isBlank(issuer) || isBlank(audience)
                || isBlank(tenantClaim) || isBlank(scopesClaim)
                || fixedScopes == null || clock == null
                || fixedScopes.stream().anyMatch(scope -&gt; !GATEWAY_SCOPES.contains(scope))) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;OIDC gateway authentication settings are invalid.&quot;);
        }
        this.jwtDecoder = jwtDecoder;
        this.issuer = issuer;
        this.audience = audience;
        this.tenantClaim = tenantClaim;
        this.scopesClaim = scopesClaim;
        this.fixedScopes = Set.copyOf(fixedScopes);
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    @Override
    public Optional&lt;GatewayPrincipal&gt; authenticate(String bearerCredential) {
        if (bearerCredential == null || bearerCredential.isBlank()) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }

        final Jwt jwt;
        try {
            jwt = jwtDecoder.decode(bearerCredential);
        } catch (JwtException e) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }

        String subject = jwt.getSubject();
        Object tenantValue = jwt.getClaims().get(tenantClaim);
        String tenantId = tenantValue instanceof String value ? value : null;
        Instant now = clock.instant();
        Instant expiresAt = jwt.getExpiresAt();
        Instant notBefore = jwt.getNotBefore();
        if (subject == null || subject.isBlank()
                || tenantId == null || tenantId.isBlank()
                || jwt.getIssuer() == null || !issuer.equals(jwt.getIssuer().toString())
                || !jwt.getAudience().contains(audience)
                || expiresAt == null || !now.isBefore(expiresAt)
                || (notBefore != null &amp;&amp; now.isBefore(notBefore))) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }

        Optional&lt;Set&lt;String&gt;&gt; scopes = fixedScopes.isEmpty()
                ? readScopes(jwt.getClaims().get(scopesClaim))
                : Optional.of(fixedScopes);
        if (scopes.isEmpty()) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
        try {
            return Optional.of(new GatewayPrincipal(subject, tenantId, scopes.get()));
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
    }

    private static Optional&lt;Set&lt;String&gt;&gt; readScopes(Object value) {
        if (value instanceof String scopeString) {
            return Optional.of(scopeString.isBlank()
                    ? Set.of()
                    : recognizedScopes(List.of(scopeString.trim().split(&quot;\\s+&quot;))));
        }
        if (value instanceof Collection&lt;?&gt; scopeValues) {
            var scopeNames = new LinkedHashSet&lt;String&gt;();
            for (Object scope : scopeValues) {
                if (!(scope instanceof String scopeName) || scopeName.isBlank()) {
                    return Optional.empty();
                }
                scopeNames.add(scopeName);
            }
            return Optional.of(recognizedScopes(scopeNames));
        }
        return Optional.empty();
    }

    private static Set&lt;String&gt; recognizedScopes(Collection&lt;String&gt; values) {
        var recognized = new LinkedHashSet&lt;String&gt;();
        for (String value : values) {
            if (GATEWAY_SCOPES.contains(value)) {
                recognized.add(value);
            }
        }
        return Set.copyOf(recognized);
    }

    private static boolean isBlank(String value) {
        return value == null || value.isBlank();
    }

    private final AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseService keyReleaseService;
    private final String contextKeyId;

    public AwsNitroAttestedSecureContextKeyProvider(
            AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseService keyReleaseService,
            String contextKeyId) {
        if (keyReleaseService == null
                || contextKeyId == null
                || !contextKeyId.matches(&quot;[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}&quot;)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;AWS Nitro key release configuration is invalid.&quot;);
        }
        if (!keyReleaseService.contextKeyId().equals(contextKeyId)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Context key identifier does not match key release.&quot;);
        }

        this.contextKeyId = contextKeyId;
    }

    @Override
    public SecureContextKey currentKey() {
        return keyReleaseService.releaseContextKey();
    }

    @Override
    public Optional&lt;SecureContextKey&gt; findKey(String id) {
        if (!contextKeyId.equals(id)) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
        return Optional.of(keyReleaseService.releaseContextKey());
    }

import java.security.SecureRandom;

import java.util.HashMap;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.ConfidentialRuntimeVerifier;

import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Profile;

/**
 * Wires enclave-side AWS Nitro key release. Deployments must supply a verified
 * NSM implementation and a trusted parent KMS proxy; no ordinary HTTP Gateway
 * endpoint is exposed by this module.
 */
@Configuration(proxyBeanMethods = false)
@Profile(&quot;aws-nitro &amp; !azure-cvm &amp; !gcp-cvm &amp; !local-untrusted&quot;)
public class AwsNitroConfidentialGatewayConfiguration {
    @Bean
    @ConditionalOnMissingBean(ParentVsockKmsTransport.class)
    public ParentVsockKmsTransport parentVsockKmsTransport(
            @Value(&quot;${confidential.aws.nitro.vsock.parent-cid:3}&quot;) int parentCid,
            @Value(&quot;${confidential.aws.nitro.vsock.proxy-port:8000}&quot;) int proxyPort) {
        return new LinuxVsockParentKmsTransport(
                parentCid, proxyPort, new ObjectMapper());
    }

    @Bean
    public AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseService awsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseService(
            AwsNitroEnclaveAttestationSource attestationSource,
            ParentVsockKmsTransport kmsTransport,
            @Value(&quot;${confidential.aws.nitro.kms.key-id}&quot;) String kmsKeyId,
            @Value(&quot;${confidential.aws.nitro.kms.ciphertext-blob-base64}&quot;)
                    String encodedCiphertext,
            @Value(&quot;${confidential.aws.nitro.kms.context-key-id}&quot;) String contextKeyId) {
        byte[] ciphertext;
        try {
            ciphertext = Base64.getDecoder().decode(encodedCiphertext);
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                    &quot;confidential.aws.nitro.kms.ciphertext-blob-base64 must be Base64.&quot;, e);
        }
        try {
            return new AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseService(
                    attestationSource,
                    kmsTransport,
                    new SecureRandom(),
                    kmsKeyId,
                    ciphertext,
                    contextKeyId);
        } finally {
            java.util.Arrays.fill(ciphertext, (byte) 0);
        }
    }

    @Bean
    public SecureContextKeyProvider awsNitroSecureContextKeyProvider(
            AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseService keyReleaseService,
            @Value(&quot;${confidential.aws.nitro.kms.context-key-id}&quot;) String contextKeyId) {
        return new AwsNitroAttestedSecureContextKeyProvider(keyReleaseService, contextKeyId);
    }

    @Bean
    public ConfidentialRuntimeVerifier awsNitroConfidentialRuntimeVerifier(
            AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseService keyReleaseService) {
        return new AwsNitroConfidentialRuntimeVerifier(keyReleaseService);
    }

    @Bean
    public AwsNitroKmsAttestationPolicy awsNitroKmsAttestationPolicy(
            @Value(&quot;${confidential.aws.nitro.kms.image-sha384}&quot;) String imageSha384,
            @Value(&quot;${confidential.aws.nitro.kms.required-pcrs}&quot;) String rawPcrs) {
        return new AwsNitroKmsAttestationPolicy(imageSha384, parsePcrs(rawPcrs));
    }

    private static Map&lt;Integer, String&gt; parsePcrs(String rawPcrs) {
        if (rawPcrs == null || rawPcrs.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    &quot;At least one confidential.aws.nitro.kms.required-pcrs entry is required.&quot;);
        }
        var pcrs = new HashMap&lt;Integer, String&gt;();
        for (String item : rawPcrs.split(&quot;,&quot;)) {
            String[] pair = item.trim().split(&quot;=&quot;, -1);
            if (pair.length != 2) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                        &quot;AWS Nitro PCRs must use comma-separated index=sha384hex entries.&quot;);
            }
            try {
                int index = Integer.parseInt(pair[0]);
                if (pcrs.putIfAbsent(index, pair[1]) != null) {
                    throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;AWS Nitro PCR index is duplicated.&quot;);
                }
            } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;AWS Nitro PCR index must be an integer.&quot;, e);
            }
        }
        return Map.copyOf(pcrs);
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/aws/AwsNitroConfidentialRuntimeVerifier.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.aws;

import java.util.Arrays;

import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.ConfidentialRuntimeVerifier;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.SecureContextKey;

/**
 * Fails closed unless a fresh NSM Recipient decrypt succeeds and its response
 * can be unwrapped within this JVM. PCR/image checks depend on corresponding
 * key-policy conditions being provisioned externally; this verifier cannot
 * inspect or attest to that remote policy configuration.
 */
public final class AwsNitroConfidentialRuntimeVerifier implements ConfidentialRuntimeVerifier {
    private final AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseService keyReleaseService;

    public AwsNitroConfidentialRuntimeVerifier(
            AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseService keyReleaseService) {
        if (keyReleaseService == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;AWS Nitro key release service is required.&quot;);
        }
        this.keyReleaseService = keyReleaseService;
    }

    @Override
    public void verifyCurrentRuntime() {
        SecureContextKey released = keyReleaseService.releaseContextKey();
        byte[] encodedKey = released.key().getEncoded();
        if (encodedKey == null
                || !&quot;AES&quot;.equalsIgnoreCase(released.key().getAlgorithm())
                || encodedKey.length != 32) {
            if (encodedKey != null) {
                Arrays.fill(encodedKey, (byte) 0);
            }
            throw new AwsNitroEnclaveException(
                    &quot;AWS Nitro runtime verification received an invalid context key.&quot;);
        }
        Arrays.fill(encodedKey, (byte) 0);
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/aws/AwsNitroEnclaveAttestationSource.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.aws;

/**
 * Supplies a fresh NSM attestation document binding both inputs as the
 * document&apos;s nonce and public_key claims.
 *
 * &lt;p&gt;No JNI implementation is provided: this boundary must be connected to a
 * verified AWS NSM ABI implementation before it can be used in an enclave.&lt;/p&gt;
 */
@FunctionalInterface
public interface AwsNitroEnclaveAttestationSource {
    byte[] attest(byte[] nonce, byte[] ephemeralRsaPublicKey);

public final class AwsNitroEnclaveException extends RuntimeException {
    public AwsNitroEnclaveException(String message) {
        super(message);
    }

    public AwsNitroEnclaveException(String message, Throwable cause) {
        super(message, cause);
