}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/aws/AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseService.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.aws;

import java.security.GeneralSecurityException;
import java.security.KeyPair;
import java.security.KeyPairGenerator;
import java.security.SecureRandom;
import java.security.spec.MGF1ParameterSpec;
import java.util.Arrays;

import javax.crypto.Cipher;
import javax.crypto.spec.OAEPParameterSpec;
import javax.crypto.spec.PSource;
import javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec;

import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.SecureContextKey;

/**
 * Obtains a fresh NSM document and submits a KMS Recipient Decrypt request.
 * Configured KMS key-policy conditions are the sole PCR/image enforcement
 * point; this class does not parse PCRs or provision/verify the remote policy.
 */
public final class AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseService {
    private static final int NONCE_BYTES = 32;
    private static final int RSA_KEY_BITS = 2048;
    private static final OAEPParameterSpec KMS_OAEP_PARAMETERS = new OAEPParameterSpec(
            &quot;SHA-256&quot;, &quot;MGF1&quot;, MGF1ParameterSpec.SHA256, PSource.PSpecified.DEFAULT);

    private final AwsNitroEnclaveAttestationSource attestationSource;
    private final ParentVsockKmsTransport kmsTransport;
    private final SecureRandom secureRandom;
    private final String kmsKeyId;
    private final byte[] ciphertextBlob;
    private final String contextKeyId;

    public AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseService(
            AwsNitroEnclaveAttestationSource attestationSource,
            ParentVsockKmsTransport kmsTransport,
            SecureRandom secureRandom,
            String kmsKeyId,
            byte[] ciphertextBlob,
            String contextKeyId) {
        if (attestationSource == null || kmsTransport == null || secureRandom == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    &quot;NSM source, parent-vsock KMS transport, and secure random are required.&quot;);
        }
        if (kmsKeyId == null || kmsKeyId.isBlank() || kmsKeyId.length() &gt; 2048) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;A bounded KMS key ID is required.&quot;);
        }
        if (ciphertextBlob == null || ciphertextBlob.length &lt; 1
                || ciphertextBlob.length &gt; AwsNitroKmsDecryptRequest.MAX_CIPHERTEXT_BLOB_BYTES) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;KMS ciphertext exceeds its size bound.&quot;);
        }
        if (contextKeyId == null || !contextKeyId.matches(&quot;[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}&quot;)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Context key identifier is invalid.&quot;);
        }
        this.attestationSource = attestationSource;
        this.kmsTransport = kmsTransport;
        this.secureRandom = secureRandom;
        this.kmsKeyId = kmsKeyId;
        this.ciphertextBlob = ciphertextBlob.clone();
        this.contextKeyId = contextKeyId;
    }

    public SecureContextKey releaseContextKey() {
        byte[] nonce = new byte[NONCE_BYTES];
        byte[] attestationDocument = null;
        byte[] ciphertextForRecipient = null;
        byte[] unwrappedKey = null;
        try {
            secureRandom.nextBytes(nonce);
            KeyPairGenerator generator = KeyPairGenerator.getInstance(&quot;RSA&quot;);
            generator.initialize(RSA_KEY_BITS, secureRandom);
            KeyPair recipientKeyPair = generator.generateKeyPair();
            byte[] publicKey = recipientKeyPair.getPublic().getEncoded();

            byte[] sourceNonce = nonce.clone();
            byte[] sourcePublicKey = publicKey.clone();
            try {
                attestationDocument = attestationSource.attest(sourceNonce, sourcePublicKey);
            } finally {
                Arrays.fill(sourceNonce, (byte) 0);
                Arrays.fill(sourcePublicKey, (byte) 0);
                Arrays.fill(publicKey, (byte) 0);
            }
            if (attestationDocument == null || attestationDocument.length &lt; 1
                    || attestationDocument.length
                            &gt; AwsNitroKmsDecryptRequest.MAX_ATTESTATION_DOCUMENT_BYTES) {
                throw new AwsNitroEnclaveException(
                        &quot;NSM did not provide a bounded attestation document.&quot;);
            }

            AwsNitroKmsDecryptRequest request = new AwsNitroKmsDecryptRequest(
                    kmsKeyId,
                    ciphertextBlob,
                    new AwsNitroKmsRecipient(
                            attestationDocument,
                            AwsNitroKmsDecryptRequest.RSAES_OAEP_SHA_256));
            AwsNitroKmsDecryptResponse response = kmsTransport.decrypt(request);
            if (response == null) {
                throw new AwsNitroEnclaveException(&quot;KMS Recipient response is missing.&quot;);
            }
            ciphertextForRecipient = response.ciphertextForRecipient();
            Cipher unwrap = Cipher.getInstance(&quot;RSA/ECB/OAEPWithSHA-256AndMGF1Padding&quot;);
            unwrap.init(Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE, recipientKeyPair.getPrivate(), KMS_OAEP_PARAMETERS);
            unwrappedKey = unwrap.doFinal(ciphertextForRecipient);
            if (unwrappedKey.length != 32) {
                throw new AwsNitroEnclaveException(
                        &quot;KMS Recipient plaintext is not a 256-bit AES key.&quot;);
            }
            return new SecureContextKey(
                    contextKeyId, new SecretKeySpec(unwrappedKey, &quot;AES&quot;));
        } catch (AwsNitroEnclaveException e) {
            throw e;
        } catch (GeneralSecurityException | RuntimeException e) {
            throw new AwsNitroEnclaveException(
                    &quot;AWS Nitro attested key release failed closed.&quot;, e);
        } finally {
            Arrays.fill(nonce, (byte) 0);
            clear(attestationDocument);
            clear(ciphertextForRecipient);
            clear(unwrappedKey);
        }
    }

    public String contextKeyId() {
        return contextKeyId;
    }

    private static void clear(byte[] bytes) {
        if (bytes != null) {
            Arrays.fill(bytes, (byte) 0);
        }
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/aws/AwsNitroKmsAttestationPolicy.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.aws;

import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

/**
 * Values for KMS key-policy StringEquals conditions. PCR/image enforcement
 * occurs only if these conditions are installed in the applicable AWS KMS key
 * policy; this descriptor does not provision or verify that policy.
 */
public final class AwsNitroKmsAttestationPolicy {
    public static final String IMAGE_SHA384_CONDITION =
            &quot;kms:RecipientAttestation:ImageSha384&quot;;
    private static final Pattern SHA384_HEX = Pattern.compile(&quot;[0-9a-fA-F]{96}&quot;);

    private final Map&lt;String, String&gt; stringEqualsConditions;

    public AwsNitroKmsAttestationPolicy(String imageSha384, Map&lt;Integer, String&gt; pcrs) {
        requireSha384(imageSha384, &quot;ImageSha384&quot;);
        Objects.requireNonNull(pcrs, &quot;PCR requirements are required.&quot;);
        if (pcrs.isEmpty()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;At least one PCR policy requirement is required.&quot;);
        }
        for (Map.Entry&lt;Integer, String&gt; entry : pcrs.entrySet()) {
            Integer index = entry.getKey();
            if (index == null || index &lt; 0 || index &gt; 8) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Nitro PCR index must be between 0 and 8.&quot;);
            }
            requireSha384(entry.getValue(), &quot;PCR&quot; + index);
        }
        String pcr0 = pcrs.get(0);
        if (pcr0 != null &amp;&amp; !pcr0.equalsIgnoreCase(imageSha384)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    &quot;ImageSha384 and PCR0 must express the same AWS image measurement.&quot;);
        }

        Map&lt;String, String&gt; conditions = new LinkedHashMap&lt;&gt;();
        conditions.put(IMAGE_SHA384_CONDITION, imageSha384.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT));
        pcrs.entrySet().stream().sorted(Map.Entry.comparingByKey()).forEach(entry -&gt; {
            Integer index = entry.getKey();
            conditions.put(
                    &quot;kms:RecipientAttestation:PCR&quot; + index,
                    entry.getValue().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT));
        });
        this.stringEqualsConditions = Map.copyOf(conditions);
    }

    /**
     * Map to place under {@code Condition.StringEquals} in the KMS key policy
     * statement granting {@code kms:Decrypt}. Applying that policy is external
     * provisioning and is not performed or verified by this adapter.
     */
    public Map&lt;String, String&gt; stringEqualsConditions() {
        return stringEqualsConditions;
    }

    private static void requireSha384(String value, String field) {
        if (value == null || !SHA384_HEX.matcher(value).matches()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(field + &quot; must be a 96-character SHA-384 hex value.&quot;);
        }
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/aws/AwsNitroKmsDecryptRequest.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.aws;

import java.util.Objects;

/**
 * Bounded AWS KMS Decrypt request data, including its Recipient structure.
 */
public record AwsNitroKmsDecryptRequest(
        String keyId,
        byte[] ciphertextBlob,
        AwsNitroKmsRecipient recipient) {
    public static final int MAX_CIPHERTEXT_BLOB_BYTES = 6 * 1024;
    public static final int MAX_ATTESTATION_DOCUMENT_BYTES = 256 * 1024;
    public static final int MAX_REQUEST_BYTES = 272 * 1024;
    public static final String RSAES_OAEP_SHA_256 = &quot;RSAES_OAEP_SHA_256&quot;;

    public AwsNitroKmsDecryptRequest {
        if (keyId == null || keyId.isBlank() || keyId.length() &gt; 2048) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;A bounded KMS key ID is required.&quot;);
        }
        ciphertextBlob = copyAndRequireSize(
                ciphertextBlob, 1, MAX_CIPHERTEXT_BLOB_BYTES, &quot;CiphertextBlob&quot;);
        Objects.requireNonNull(recipient, &quot;KMS Recipient is required.&quot;);
        long requestBytes = (long) keyId.getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8).length
                + ciphertextBlob.length + recipient.attestationDocument().length
                + recipient.keyEncryptionAlgorithm().length();
        if (requestBytes &gt; MAX_REQUEST_BYTES) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;KMS Decrypt request exceeds its size bound.&quot;);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public byte[] ciphertextBlob() {
        return ciphertextBlob.clone();
    }

    private static byte[] copyAndRequireSize(byte[] value, int min, int max, String field) {
        Objects.requireNonNull(value, field + &quot; is required.&quot;);
        if (value.length &lt; min || value.length &gt; max) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(field + &quot; exceeds its size bound.&quot;);
        }
        return value.clone();
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/aws/AwsNitroKmsDecryptResponse.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.aws;

import java.util.Objects;

/**
 * KMS Recipient response. Plaintext is deliberately not represented here:
 * only CiphertextForRecipient may cross the transport boundary.
 */
public record AwsNitroKmsDecryptResponse(byte[] ciphertextForRecipient) {
    public static final int MAX_CIPHERTEXT_FOR_RECIPIENT_BYTES = 256;

    public AwsNitroKmsDecryptResponse {
        Objects.requireNonNull(ciphertextForRecipient, &quot;CiphertextForRecipient is required.&quot;);
        if (ciphertextForRecipient.length &lt; 1
                || ciphertextForRecipient.length &gt; MAX_CIPHERTEXT_FOR_RECIPIENT_BYTES) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;CiphertextForRecipient exceeds its size bound.&quot;);
        }
        ciphertextForRecipient = ciphertextForRecipient.clone();
    }

    @Override
    public byte[] ciphertextForRecipient() {
        return ciphertextForRecipient.clone();
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/aws/AwsNitroKmsRecipient.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.aws;

import java.util.Objects;

/**
 * AWS KMS Decrypt {@code Recipient} parameter for an attested Nitro enclave.
 */
public record AwsNitroKmsRecipient(
        byte[] attestationDocument,
        String keyEncryptionAlgorithm) {

    public AwsNitroKmsRecipient {
        Objects.requireNonNull(attestationDocument, &quot;AttestationDocument is required.&quot;);
        if (attestationDocument.length &lt; 1
                || attestationDocument.length
                        &gt; AwsNitroKmsDecryptRequest.MAX_ATTESTATION_DOCUMENT_BYTES) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;AttestationDocument exceeds its size bound.&quot;);
        }
        if (!AwsNitroKmsDecryptRequest.RSAES_OAEP_SHA_256.equals(keyEncryptionAlgorithm)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Unsupported KMS recipient encryption algorithm.&quot;);
        }
        attestationDocument = attestationDocument.clone();
    }

    @Override
    public byte[] attestationDocument() {
        return attestationDocument.clone();
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/aws/LinuxVsockParentKmsTransport.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.aws;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.lang.foreign.Arena;
import java.lang.foreign.FunctionDescriptor;
import java.lang.foreign.Linker;
import java.lang.foreign.MemorySegment;
import java.lang.foreign.SymbolLookup;
import java.lang.foreign.ValueLayout;
import java.lang.invoke.MethodHandle;
import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.nio.ByteOrder;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.Objects;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

/**
 * Sends a bounded, length-prefixed JSON KMS Decrypt request to a parent vsock
 * proxy. The proxy is responsible for AWS credentials and KMS HTTPS access.
 */
public final class LinuxVsockParentKmsTransport implements ParentVsockKmsTransport {
    private static final int AF_VSOCK = 40;
    private static final int SOCK_STREAM = 1;
    private static final int SOL_SOCKET = 1;
    private static final int SO_RCVTIMEO = 20;
    private static final int SO_SNDTIMEO = 21;
    private static final long IO_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 30;
    private static final int HEADER_SIZE = Integer.BYTES;
    private static final int MAX_FRAME_BYTES =
            AwsNitroKmsDecryptRequest.MAX_REQUEST_BYTES + 16 * 1024;
    private static final Linker LINKER = Linker.nativeLinker();
    private static final FunctionDescriptor SOCKET_DESCRIPTOR = FunctionDescriptor.of(
            ValueLayout.JAVA_INT, ValueLayout.JAVA_INT, ValueLayout.JAVA_INT, ValueLayout.JAVA_INT);
    private static final FunctionDescriptor CONNECT_DESCRIPTOR = FunctionDescriptor.of(
            ValueLayout.JAVA_INT,
            ValueLayout.JAVA_INT,
            ValueLayout.ADDRESS,
            ValueLayout.JAVA_INT);
    private static final FunctionDescriptor SOCKET_OPTION_DESCRIPTOR = FunctionDescriptor.of(
            ValueLayout.JAVA_INT,
            ValueLayout.JAVA_INT,
            ValueLayout.JAVA_INT,
            ValueLayout.JAVA_INT,
            ValueLayout.ADDRESS,
            ValueLayout.JAVA_INT);
    private static final FunctionDescriptor IO_DESCRIPTOR = FunctionDescriptor.of(
            ValueLayout.JAVA_LONG, ValueLayout.JAVA_INT, ValueLayout.ADDRESS, ValueLayout.JAVA_LONG);
    private static final FunctionDescriptor CLOSE_DESCRIPTOR = FunctionDescriptor.of(
            ValueLayout.JAVA_INT, ValueLayout.JAVA_INT);

    private final int parentCid;
    private final int proxyPort;
    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    private volatile NativeMethods nativeMethods;

    public LinuxVsockParentKmsTransport(
            int parentCid, int proxyPort, ObjectMapper objectMapper) {
        if (parentCid &lt; 0 || parentCid == 0xffff_ffff || proxyPort &lt; 1 || proxyPort &gt; 65535) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Parent vsock CID or proxy port is invalid.&quot;);
        }
        this.parentCid = parentCid;
        this.proxyPort = proxyPort;
        this.objectMapper = Objects.requireNonNull(objectMapper, &quot;objectMapper&quot;);
    }

    @Override
    public AwsNitroKmsDecryptResponse decrypt(AwsNitroKmsDecryptRequest request) {
        Objects.requireNonNull(request, &quot;request&quot;);
        if (!System.getProperty(&quot;os.name&quot;, &quot;&quot;).toLowerCase().contains(&quot;linux&quot;)) {
            throw new AwsNitroEnclaveException(
                    &quot;AWS Nitro parent-vsock transport is supported only on Linux.&quot;);
        }
        NativeMethods methods = nativeMethods();

        byte[] requestFrame = null;
        byte[] responseFrame = null;
        try {
            requestFrame = encodeRequest(request);
            try (Arena arena = Arena.ofConfined()) {
                int fd = openSocket(methods);
                try {
                    configureTimeouts(arena, methods, fd);
                    connectToParent(arena, methods, fd);
                    writeFrame(methods, fd, requestFrame);
                    responseFrame = readFrame(arena, methods, fd);
                } finally {
                    closeSocket(methods, fd);
                }
            }
            return decodeResponse(responseFrame);
        } catch (AwsNitroEnclaveException exception) {
            throw exception;
        } catch (IOException | RuntimeException exception) {
            throw new AwsNitroEnclaveException(
                    &quot;AWS KMS parent-vsock exchange failed.&quot;, exception);
        } finally {
            clear(requestFrame);
            clear(responseFrame);
        }
    }

    byte[] encodeRequest(AwsNitroKmsDecryptRequest request) throws IOException {
        byte[] ciphertext = request.ciphertextBlob();
        byte[] attestationDocument = request.recipient().attestationDocument();
        try {
            return objectMapper.writeValueAsBytes(new ProxyRequest(
                    1,
                    &quot;kms:Decrypt&quot;,
                    new KmsRequest(
                            request.keyId(),
                            Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(ciphertext),
                            new Recipient(
                                    Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(attestationDocument),
                                    request.recipient().keyEncryptionAlgorithm()))));
        } finally {
            clear(ciphertext);
            clear(attestationDocument);
        }
    }

    AwsNitroKmsDecryptResponse decodeResponse(byte[] response) throws IOException {
        JsonNode root = objectMapper.readTree(response);
        if (root.path(&quot;version&quot;).asInt(-1) != 1) {
            throw new AwsNitroEnclaveException(&quot;Parent-vsock proxy protocol version is invalid.&quot;);
        }
        JsonNode responseNode = root.path(&quot;response&quot;);
        String encoded = responseNode.path(&quot;CiphertextForRecipient&quot;).asText(null);
        if (encoded == null || encoded.isBlank()) {
            throw new AwsNitroEnclaveException(
                    &quot;Parent-vsock KMS response omitted CiphertextForRecipient.&quot;);
        }
        byte[] ciphertext;
        try {
            ciphertext = Base64.getDecoder().decode(encoded);
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException exception) {
            throw new AwsNitroEnclaveException(
                    &quot;Parent-vsock KMS response contains invalid base64.&quot;, exception);
        }
        try {
            return new AwsNitroKmsDecryptResponse(ciphertext);
        } finally {
            clear(ciphertext);
        }
    }

    private int openSocket(NativeMethods methods) {
        try {
            int fd = (int) methods.socket().invokeExact(AF_VSOCK, SOCK_STREAM, 0);
            if (fd &lt; 0) {
                throw new AwsNitroEnclaveException(&quot;Unable to open parent-vsock connection.&quot;);
            }
            return fd;
        } catch (AwsNitroEnclaveException exception) {
            throw exception;
        } catch (Throwable failure) {
            throw nativeFailure(&quot;Unable to open parent-vsock connection.&quot;, failure);
        }
    }

    private void configureTimeouts(Arena arena, NativeMethods methods, int fd) {
        MemorySegment timeout = arena.allocate(2L * Long.BYTES, Long.BYTES);
        timeout.set(ValueLayout.JAVA_LONG, 0, IO_TIMEOUT_SECONDS);
        timeout.set(ValueLayout.JAVA_LONG, Long.BYTES, 0L);
        setSocketOption(methods, fd, SO_RCVTIMEO, timeout);
        setSocketOption(methods, fd, SO_SNDTIMEO, timeout);
    }

    private void setSocketOption(NativeMethods methods, int fd, int option, MemorySegment value) {
        try {
            int result = (int) methods.setSocketOption().invokeExact(
                    fd, SOL_SOCKET, option, value, (int) (2L * Long.BYTES));
            if (result != 0) {
                throw new AwsNitroEnclaveException(&quot;Unable to configure vsock I/O timeout.&quot;);
            }
        } catch (AwsNitroEnclaveException exception) {
            throw exception;
        } catch (Throwable failure) {
            throw nativeFailure(&quot;Unable to configure vsock I/O timeout.&quot;, failure);
        }
    }

    private void connectToParent(Arena arena, NativeMethods methods, int fd) {
        MemorySegment address = arena.allocate(16, 8);
        address.fill((byte) 0);
        address.set(ValueLayout.JAVA_SHORT, 0, (short) AF_VSOCK);
        address.set(ValueLayout.JAVA_INT, 4, proxyPort);
        address.set(ValueLayout.JAVA_INT, 8, parentCid);
        try {
            int result = (int) methods.connect().invokeExact(fd, address, 16);
            if (result != 0) {
                throw new AwsNitroEnclaveException(&quot;Unable to connect to parent vsock proxy.&quot;);
            }
        } catch (AwsNitroEnclaveException exception) {
            throw exception;
        } catch (Throwable failure) {
            throw nativeFailure(&quot;Unable to connect to parent vsock proxy.&quot;, failure);
        }
    }

    private void writeFrame(NativeMethods methods, int fd, byte[] payload) {
        byte[] header = ByteBuffer.allocate(HEADER_SIZE)
                .order(ByteOrder.BIG_ENDIAN)
                .putInt(payload.length)
                .array();
        try {
            writeFully(methods, fd, MemorySegment.ofArray(header));
            writeFully(methods, fd, MemorySegment.ofArray(payload));
        } finally {
            clear(header);
        }
    }

    private byte[] readFrame(Arena arena, NativeMethods methods, int fd) {
        MemorySegment header = arena.allocate(HEADER_SIZE, 1);
        readFully(methods, fd, header);
        byte[] headerBytes = header.toArray(ValueLayout.JAVA_BYTE);
        int length;
        try {
            length = ByteBuffer.wrap(headerBytes).order(ByteOrder.BIG_ENDIAN).getInt();
        } finally {
            clear(headerBytes);
            header.fill((byte) 0);
        }
        if (length &lt; 1 || length &gt; MAX_FRAME_BYTES) {
            throw new AwsNitroEnclaveException(&quot;Parent-vsock response frame exceeds its size bound.&quot;);
        }
        MemorySegment body = arena.allocate(length, 1);
        readFully(methods, fd, body);
        return body.toArray(ValueLayout.JAVA_BYTE);
    }

    private void writeFully(NativeMethods methods, int fd, MemorySegment buffer) {
        long offset = 0;
        while (offset &lt; buffer.byteSize()) {
            try {
                long written = (long) methods.write().invokeExact(
                        fd, buffer.asSlice(offset), buffer.byteSize() - offset);
                if (written &lt;= 0) {
                    throw new AwsNitroEnclaveException(&quot;Parent-vsock write did not complete.&quot;);
                }
                offset += written;
            } catch (AwsNitroEnclaveException exception) {
                throw exception;
            } catch (Throwable failure) {
                throw nativeFailure(&quot;Parent-vsock write failed.&quot;, failure);
            }
        }
    }

    private void readFully(NativeMethods methods, int fd, MemorySegment buffer) {
        long offset = 0;
        while (offset &lt; buffer.byteSize()) {
            try {
                long received = (long) methods.read().invokeExact(
                        fd, buffer.asSlice(offset), buffer.byteSize() - offset);
                if (received &lt;= 0) {
                    throw new AwsNitroEnclaveException(
                            &quot;Parent-vsock closed before the full response was received.&quot;);
                }
                offset += received;
            } catch (AwsNitroEnclaveException exception) {
                throw exception;
            } catch (Throwable failure) {
                throw nativeFailure(&quot;Parent-vsock read failed.&quot;, failure);
            }
        }
    }

    private void closeSocket(NativeMethods methods, int fd) {
        try {
            int result = (int) methods.close().invokeExact(fd);
            if (result != 0) {
                throw new AwsNitroEnclaveException(&quot;Unable to close parent-vsock socket.&quot;);
            }
        } catch (AwsNitroEnclaveException exception) {
            throw exception;
        } catch (Throwable failure) {
            throw nativeFailure(&quot;Unable to close parent-vsock socket.&quot;, failure);
        }
    }

    private static MethodHandle downcall(
            SymbolLookup symbols, String name, FunctionDescriptor descriptor) {
        MemorySegment address = symbols.find(name)
                .orElseThrow(() -&gt; new UnsatisfiedLinkError(
                        &quot;Linux libc symbol is unavailable: &quot; + name));
        return LINKER.downcallHandle(address, descriptor);
    }

    private NativeMethods nativeMethods() {
        NativeMethods methods = nativeMethods;
        if (methods != null) {
            return methods;
        }
        synchronized (this) {
            methods = nativeMethods;
            if (methods == null) {
                SymbolLookup libc = LINKER.defaultLookup();
                methods = new NativeMethods(
                        downcall(libc, &quot;socket&quot;, SOCKET_DESCRIPTOR),
                        downcall(libc, &quot;connect&quot;, CONNECT_DESCRIPTOR),
                        downcall(libc, &quot;setsockopt&quot;, SOCKET_OPTION_DESCRIPTOR),
                        downcall(libc, &quot;read&quot;, IO_DESCRIPTOR),
                        downcall(libc, &quot;write&quot;, IO_DESCRIPTOR),
                        downcall(libc, &quot;close&quot;, CLOSE_DESCRIPTOR));
                nativeMethods = methods;
            }
        }
        return methods;
    }

    private static AwsNitroEnclaveException nativeFailure(String message, Throwable failure) {
        return new AwsNitroEnclaveException(message, failure);
    }

    private static void clear(byte[] bytes) {
        if (bytes != null) {
            Arrays.fill(bytes, (byte) 0);
        }
    }

    private record ProxyRequest(int version, String operation, KmsRequest request) {
    }

    private record KmsRequest(String KeyId, String CiphertextBlob, Recipient Recipient) {
    }

    private record Recipient(String AttestationDocument, String KeyEncryptionAlgorithm) {
    }

    private record NativeMethods(
            MethodHandle socket,
            MethodHandle connect,
            MethodHandle setSocketOption,
            MethodHandle read,
            MethodHandle write,
            MethodHandle close) {
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/aws/ParentVsockKmsTransport.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.aws;

/**
 * Explicit enclave-to-parent-vsock KMS transport boundary.
 *
 * &lt;p&gt;Nitro Enclaves have no external networking. A production implementation
 * must send the bounded request through the parent instance&apos;s vsock proxy to
 * AWS KMS and validate/parse the AWS response. No transport implementation or
 * provisioning integration is included in this package.&lt;/p&gt;
 */
@FunctionalInterface
public interface ParentVsockKmsTransport {
    AwsNitroKmsDecryptResponse decrypt(AwsNitroKmsDecryptRequest request);
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/azure/AzureAttestationException.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.azure;

public final class AzureAttestationException extends RuntimeException {
    public AzureAttestationException(String message) {
        super(message);
    }

    public AzureAttestationException(String message, Throwable cause) {
        super(message, cause);
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/azure/AzureAttestationJwksClient.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.azure;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.math.BigInteger;
import java.net.URI;
import java.net.http.HttpClient;
import java.net.http.HttpRequest;
import java.net.http.HttpResponse;
import java.security.PublicKey;
import java.security.spec.RSAPublicKeySpec;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.locks.ReentrantLock;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

public final class AzureAttestationJwksClient implements AzureAttestationKeyResolver {
    private static final int MAX_DOCUMENT_BYTES = 256 * 1024;
    private static final Duration CACHE_TTL = Duration.ofMinutes(5);
    private static final Duration UNKNOWN_KEY_REFRESH_INTERVAL = Duration.ofSeconds(30);

    private final URI openIdConfigurationUri;
    private final HttpClient httpClient;
    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    private final Clock clock;
    private final ReentrantLock refreshLock = new ReentrantLock();
    private volatile Map&lt;String, PublicKey&gt; cachedKeys = Map.of();
    private volatile Instant expiresAt = Instant.MIN;
    private volatile Instant lastRefreshAt = Instant.MIN;

    public AzureAttestationJwksClient(
            URI openIdConfigurationUri,
            HttpClient httpClient,
            ObjectMapper objectMapper,
            Clock clock) {
        if (!isAzureAttestationHttpsUri(openIdConfigurationUri)
                || !openIdConfigurationUri.getPath().endsWith(&quot;/.well-known/openid-configuration&quot;)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    &quot;Azure Attestation OpenID metadata must use HTTPS on an *.attest.azure.net host.&quot;);
        }
        this.openIdConfigurationUri = openIdConfigurationUri;
        this.httpClient = httpClient;
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    @Override
    public PublicKey resolveSigningKey(String keyId) {
        if (keyId == null || keyId.isBlank()) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Attestation JWT key identifier is missing.&quot;);
        }
        Map&lt;String, PublicKey&gt; keys = cachedKeys;
        if (clock.instant().isBefore(expiresAt) &amp;&amp; keys.containsKey(keyId)) {
            return keys.get(keyId);
        }

        refreshLock.lock();
        try {
            Instant now = clock.instant();
            boolean cacheExpired = !now.isBefore(expiresAt);
            boolean unknownKeyRefreshDue = !cachedKeys.containsKey(keyId)
                    &amp;&amp; !now.isBefore(lastRefreshAt.plus(UNKNOWN_KEY_REFRESH_INTERVAL));
            if (cacheExpired || unknownKeyRefreshDue) {
                refreshKeys();
            }
            PublicKey key = cachedKeys.get(keyId);
            if (key == null) {
                throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Attestation signing key is not trusted.&quot;);
            }
            return key;
        } finally {
            refreshLock.unlock();
        }
    }

    private void refreshKeys() {
        try {
            JsonNode metadata = getJson(openIdConfigurationUri);
            String jwksUriValue = metadata.path(&quot;jwks_uri&quot;).asText(&quot;&quot;);
            URI jwksUri = URI.create(jwksUriValue);
            if (!isAzureAttestationHttpsUri(jwksUri)
                    || !openIdConfigurationUri.getHost().equalsIgnoreCase(jwksUri.getHost())) {
                throw new AzureAttestationException(
                        &quot;Azure Attestation metadata supplied an untrusted JWKS location.&quot;);
            }

            JsonNode keysNode = getJson(jwksUri).path(&quot;keys&quot;);
            if (!keysNode.isArray()) {
                throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Azure Attestation JWKS is malformed.&quot;);
            }
            Map&lt;String, PublicKey&gt; keys = new HashMap&lt;&gt;();
            for (JsonNode key : keysNode) {
                if (!&quot;RSA&quot;.equals(key.path(&quot;kty&quot;).asText())
                        || !&quot;RS256&quot;.equals(key.path(&quot;alg&quot;).asText(&quot;RS256&quot;))
                        || !&quot;sig&quot;.equals(key.path(&quot;use&quot;).asText(&quot;sig&quot;))) {
                    continue;
                }
                String kid = key.path(&quot;kid&quot;).asText(&quot;&quot;);
                String modulus = key.path(&quot;n&quot;).asText(&quot;&quot;);
                String exponent = key.path(&quot;e&quot;).asText(&quot;&quot;);
                if (kid.isBlank() || modulus.isBlank() || exponent.isBlank()) {
                    continue;
                }
                PublicKey publicKey = rsaPublicKey(modulus, exponent);
                if (keys.putIfAbsent(kid, publicKey) != null) {
                    throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Azure Attestation JWKS has duplicate key IDs.&quot;);
                }
            }
            if (keys.isEmpty()) {
                throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Azure Attestation JWKS contains no RS256 keys.&quot;);
            }
            cachedKeys = Map.copyOf(keys);
            lastRefreshAt = clock.instant();
            expiresAt = lastRefreshAt.plus(CACHE_TTL);
        } catch (AzureAttestationException e) {
            throw e;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Unable to refresh Azure Attestation signing keys.&quot;, e);
        }
    }

    private JsonNode getJson(URI uri) throws IOException, InterruptedException {
        HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder(uri)
                .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(5))
                .header(&quot;Accept&quot;, &quot;application/json&quot;)
                .GET()
                .build();
        HttpResponse&lt;java.io.InputStream&gt; response = httpClient.send(
                request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofInputStream());
        try (var body = response.body()) {
            if (response.statusCode() != 200) {
                throw new AzureAttestationException(
                        &quot;Azure Attestation signing metadata request failed.&quot;);
            }
            byte[] bytes = body.readNBytes(MAX_DOCUMENT_BYTES + 1);
            if (bytes.length &gt; MAX_DOCUMENT_BYTES) {
                throw new AzureAttestationException(
                        &quot;Azure Attestation signing metadata exceeded the size limit.&quot;);
            }
            return objectMapper.readTree(bytes);
        }
    }

    private static PublicKey rsaPublicKey(String modulus, String exponent) throws Exception {
        var decoder = Base64.getUrlDecoder();
        BigInteger n = new BigInteger(1, decoder.decode(modulus));
        BigInteger e = new BigInteger(1, decoder.decode(exponent));
        return java.security.KeyFactory.getInstance(&quot;RSA&quot;)
                .generatePublic(new RSAPublicKeySpec(n, e));
    }

    private static boolean isAzureAttestationHttpsUri(URI uri) {
        if (uri == null || !&quot;https&quot;.equalsIgnoreCase(uri.getScheme())
                || uri.getHost() == null || uri.getUserInfo() != null
                || uri.getFragment() != null || uri.getQuery() != null) {
            return false;
        }
        return uri.getHost().toLowerCase().endsWith(&quot;.attest.azure.net&quot;);
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/azure/AzureAttestationJwtVerifier.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.azure;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.security.GeneralSecurityException;
import java.security.PublicKey;
import java.security.Signature;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.Iterator;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonParser;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

public final class AzureAttestationJwtVerifier {
    private static final int MAX_TOKEN_LENGTH = 64 * 1024;
    private static final Duration CLOCK_SKEW = Duration.ofSeconds(30);
    private static final Duration MAX_TOKEN_LIFETIME = Duration.ofHours(24);

    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    private final AzureAttestationKeyResolver keyResolver;
    private final AzureAttestationPolicy policy;
    private final Clock clock;

    public AzureAttestationJwtVerifier(
            ObjectMapper objectMapper,
            AzureAttestationKeyResolver keyResolver,
            AzureAttestationPolicy policy,
            Clock clock) {
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
        this.keyResolver = keyResolver;
        this.policy = policy;
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    public VerifiedAzureAttestation verify(String compactJwt, String expectedNonce) {
        if (compactJwt == null || compactJwt.length() &gt; MAX_TOKEN_LENGTH
                || expectedNonce == null || expectedNonce.isBlank()) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Attestation evidence is missing or oversized.&quot;);
        }

        String[] parts = compactJwt.split(&quot;\\.&quot;, -1);
        if (parts.length != 3 || parts[0].isBlank() || parts[1].isBlank() || parts[2].isBlank()) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Attestation evidence is not a compact JWT.&quot;);
        }

        try {
            JsonNode header = parseSegment(parts[0]);
            if (!&quot;RS256&quot;.equals(text(header, &quot;alg&quot;))) {
                throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Unsupported attestation JWT algorithm.&quot;);
            }
            String keyId = text(header, &quot;kid&quot;);
            PublicKey signingKey = keyResolver.resolveSigningKey(keyId);
            verifySignature(parts, signingKey);

            JsonNode claims = parseSegment(parts[1]);
            validateClaims(claims, expectedNonce);
            JsonNode sevsnpClaims = sevsnpClaims(claims);
            return new VerifiedAzureAttestation(
                        compactJwt,
                        text(claims, &quot;iss&quot;),
                        text(claims, &quot;x-ms-policy-hash&quot;),
                        text(sevsnpClaims, &quot;x-ms-sevsnpvm-launchmeasurement&quot;),
                        Instant.ofEpochSecond(requiredLong(claims, &quot;exp&quot;)));
        } catch (AzureAttestationException e) {
            throw e;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Attestation evidence could not be verified.&quot;, e);
        }
    }

    private JsonNode parseSegment(String segment) throws Exception {
        byte[] decoded = Base64.getUrlDecoder().decode(segment);
        try (JsonParser parser = objectMapper.getFactory().createParser(decoded)) {
            parser.enable(JsonParser.Feature.STRICT_DUPLICATE_DETECTION);
            JsonNode result = objectMapper.readTree(parser);
            if (result == null || !result.isObject()) {
                throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Attestation JWT segment must be a JSON object.&quot;);
            }
            if (parser.nextToken() != null) {
                throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Attestation JWT segment has trailing JSON data.&quot;);
            }
            return result;
        }
    }

    private static void verifySignature(String[] parts, PublicKey publicKey)
            throws GeneralSecurityException {
        Signature verifier = Signature.getInstance(&quot;SHA256withRSA&quot;);
        verifier.initVerify(publicKey);
        verifier.update((parts[0] + &quot;.&quot; + parts[1]).getBytes(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII));
        byte[] signature = Base64.getUrlDecoder().decode(parts[2]);
        if (!verifier.verify(signature)) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Attestation JWT signature is invalid.&quot;);
        }
    }

    private void validateClaims(JsonNode claims, String expectedNonce) {
        if (!policy.issuer().equals(text(claims, &quot;iss&quot;))) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Attestation issuer does not match policy.&quot;);
        }
        JsonNode audienceClaim = claims.get(&quot;aud&quot;);
        if (policy.audience().isBlank()) {
            if (audienceClaim != null) {
                throw new AzureAttestationException(
                        &quot;Attestation audience is present but no audience is configured.&quot;);
            }
        } else if (!hasAudience(audienceClaim, policy.audience())) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Attestation audience does not match policy.&quot;);
        }

        Instant now = clock.instant();
        long expiresAt = requiredLong(claims, &quot;exp&quot;);
        if (!now.minus(CLOCK_SKEW).isBefore(Instant.ofEpochSecond(expiresAt))) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Attestation evidence has expired.&quot;);
        }
        long issuedAt = requiredLong(claims, &quot;iat&quot;);
        if (now.plus(CLOCK_SKEW).isBefore(Instant.ofEpochSecond(issuedAt))
                || issuedAt &gt;= expiresAt
                || expiresAt - issuedAt &gt; MAX_TOKEN_LIFETIME.toSeconds()) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Attestation evidence has invalid issue times.&quot;);
        }
        JsonNode notBefore = claims.get(&quot;nbf&quot;);
        if (notBefore != null) {
            if (!notBefore.canConvertToLong()) {
                throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Attestation not-before claim is invalid.&quot;);
            }
            if (now.plus(CLOCK_SKEW).isBefore(Instant.ofEpochSecond(notBefore.longValue()))) {
                throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Attestation evidence is not yet valid.&quot;);
            }
        }

        if (!isExpectedNonce(claims, expectedNonce)) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Attestation nonce does not match the challenge.&quot;);
        }
        if (!&quot;azurevm&quot;.equals(text(claims, &quot;x-ms-attestation-type&quot;))) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Evidence is not Azure CVM guest attestation.&quot;);
        }
        JsonNode teeClaims = claims.path(&quot;x-ms-isolation-tee&quot;);
        if (!&quot;sevsnpvm&quot;.equals(text(teeClaims, &quot;x-ms-attestation-type&quot;))
                || !&quot;azure-compliant-cvm&quot;.equals(text(teeClaims, &quot;x-ms-compliance-status&quot;))) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Evidence is not Azure AMD SEV-SNP attestation.&quot;);
        }
        if (!Boolean.TRUE.equals(booleanValue(claims, &quot;secureboot&quot;))) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Secure Boot is not enabled in attested claims.&quot;);
        }
        JsonNode vmConfiguration = teeClaims.path(&quot;x-ms-runtime&quot;).path(&quot;vm-configuration&quot;);
        if (!Boolean.TRUE.equals(booleanValue(vmConfiguration, &quot;secure-boot&quot;))
                || !Boolean.TRUE.equals(booleanValue(vmConfiguration, &quot;tpm-enabled&quot;))) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(
                    &quot;Attested VM configuration does not enable Secure Boot and vTPM.&quot;);
        }
        if (!containsTpmEphemeralEncryptionKey(claims.path(&quot;x-ms-runtime&quot;).path(&quot;keys&quot;))) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(
                    &quot;Attestation evidence does not contain the TPM ephemeral encryption key.&quot;);
        }
        JsonNode sevsnpClaims = sevsnpClaims(claims);
        if (!Boolean.FALSE.equals(booleanValue(sevsnpClaims, &quot;x-ms-sevsnpvm-is-debuggable&quot;))) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;SEV-SNP debugging is enabled or unreported.&quot;);
        }
        if (requiredLong(sevsnpClaims, &quot;x-ms-sevsnpvm-vmpl&quot;) != 0) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;SEV-SNP evidence was not generated at VMPL 0.&quot;);
        }

        String measurement = text(sevsnpClaims, &quot;x-ms-sevsnpvm-launchmeasurement&quot;);
        if (!policy.approvedLaunchMeasurements().contains(measurement)) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Guest launch measurement is not approved.&quot;);
        }
        if (!policy.expectedPolicyHash().equals(text(claims, &quot;x-ms-policy-hash&quot;))) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Azure Attestation policy hash does not match.&quot;);
        }
        policy.minimumSecurityVersions().forEach((claimName, minimum) -&gt; {
            long actual = requiredLong(sevsnpClaims, claimName);
            if (actual &lt; minimum) {
                throw new AzureAttestationException(
                        &quot;Attested security version is below the configured minimum.&quot;);
            }
        });
    }

    private static JsonNode sevsnpClaims(JsonNode claims) {
        JsonNode teeClaims = claims.path(&quot;x-ms-isolation-tee&quot;);
        if (!teeClaims.isObject()) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Azure SEV-SNP evidence claims are missing.&quot;);
        }
        return teeClaims;
    }

    private static boolean isExpectedNonce(JsonNode claims, String expectedNonce) {
        JsonNode nestedNonce = claims.path(&quot;x-ms-runtime&quot;).path(&quot;client-payload&quot;).path(&quot;nonce&quot;);
        if (nestedNonce.isTextual()) {
            String expectedEncoded = Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(
                    expectedNonce.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            return expectedEncoded.equals(nestedNonce.textValue());
        }
        JsonNode directNonce = claims.get(&quot;nonce&quot;);
        return directNonce != null &amp;&amp; directNonce.isTextual()
                &amp;&amp; expectedNonce.equals(directNonce.textValue());
    }

    private static boolean containsTpmEphemeralEncryptionKey(JsonNode keys) {
        if (!keys.isArray()) {
            return false;
        }
        for (JsonNode key : keys) {
            JsonNode keyOperations = key.path(&quot;key_ops&quot;);
            boolean canEncrypt = false;
            if (keyOperations.isArray()) {
                for (JsonNode operation : keyOperations) {
                    canEncrypt |= operation.isTextual() &amp;&amp; &quot;encrypt&quot;.equals(operation.textValue());
                }
            }
            if (&quot;RSA&quot;.equals(key.path(&quot;kty&quot;).asText())
                    &amp;&amp; &quot;TpmEphemeralEncryptionKey&quot;.equals(key.path(&quot;kid&quot;).asText())
                    &amp;&amp; canEncrypt
                    &amp;&amp; key.path(&quot;n&quot;).isTextual()
                    &amp;&amp; key.path(&quot;e&quot;).isTextual()) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private static boolean hasAudience(JsonNode audience, String expected) {
        if (audience == null) {
            return false;
        }
        if (audience.isTextual()) {
            return expected.equals(audience.textValue());
        }
        if (audience.isArray()) {
            Iterator&lt;JsonNode&gt; values = audience.elements();
            while (values.hasNext()) {
                JsonNode value = values.next();
                if (value.isTextual() &amp;&amp; expected.equals(value.textValue())) {
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private static String text(JsonNode node, String claim) {
        JsonNode value = node == null ? null : node.get(claim);
        if (value == null || !value.isTextual() || value.textValue().isBlank()) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Required attestation claim is missing: &quot; + claim);
        }
        return value.textValue();
    }

    private static Boolean booleanValue(JsonNode node, String claim) {
        JsonNode value = node == null ? null : node.get(claim);
        return value != null &amp;&amp; value.isBoolean() ? value.booleanValue() : null;
    }

    private static long requiredLong(JsonNode node, String claim) {
        JsonNode value = node == null ? null : node.get(claim);
        if (value == null || !value.canConvertToLong()) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Required numeric attestation claim is missing: &quot; + claim);
        }
        return value.longValue();
    }

    public record VerifiedAzureAttestation(
            String token,
            String issuer,
            String policyHash,
            String launchMeasurement,
            Instant expiresAt) {
        public VerifiedAzureAttestation {
            if (token == null || issuer == null || policyHash == null
                    || launchMeasurement == null || expiresAt == null) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Verified attestation fields must be present.&quot;);
            }
        }
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/azure/AzureAttestationKeyResolver.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.azure;

import java.security.PublicKey;

@FunctionalInterface
public interface AzureAttestationKeyResolver {
    PublicKey resolveSigningKey(String keyId);
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/azure/AzureAttestationPolicy.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.azure;

import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;

public record AzureAttestationPolicy(
        String issuer,
        String audience,
        Set&lt;String&gt; approvedLaunchMeasurements,
        String expectedPolicyHash,
        Map&lt;String, Integer&gt; minimumSecurityVersions) {

    public AzureAttestationPolicy {
        if (issuer == null || issuer.isBlank()
                || audience == null
                || approvedLaunchMeasurements == null
                || approvedLaunchMeasurements.isEmpty()
                || expectedPolicyHash == null
                || !expectedPolicyHash.matches(&quot;[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}&quot;)
                || minimumSecurityVersions == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    &quot;Azure attestation requires an issuer, audience, approved measurement, &quot;
                            + &quot;policy hash, and security-version policy.&quot;);
        }
        approvedLaunchMeasurements = Set.copyOf(approvedLaunchMeasurements);
        minimumSecurityVersions = Map.copyOf(minimumSecurityVersions);
        if (approvedLaunchMeasurements.stream()
                        .anyMatch(value -&gt; !value.matches(&quot;(?i)[0-9a-f]{96}&quot;))
                || minimumSecurityVersions.values().stream().anyMatch(value -&gt; value &lt; 0)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Azure attestation policy contains invalid values.&quot;);
        }
        if (minimumSecurityVersions.keySet().stream().anyMatch(name -&gt;
                !Set.of(
                        &quot;x-ms-sevsnpvm-bootloader-svn&quot;,
                        &quot;x-ms-sevsnpvm-snpfw-svn&quot;,
                        &quot;x-ms-sevsnpvm-microcode-svn&quot;,
                        &quot;x-ms-sevsnpvm-tee-svn&quot;,
                        &quot;x-ms-sevsnpvm-guestsvn&quot;).contains(name))) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Unsupported SEV-SNP security version claim.&quot;);
        }
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/azure/AzureAttestedKeyReleaseService.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.azure;

import java.security.SecureRandom;
import java.util.Base64;

import javax.crypto.SecretKey;

import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.SecureContextKey;

public final class AzureAttestedKeyReleaseService {
    private final AzureGuestAttestationTokenProvider tokenProvider;
    private final AzureAttestationJwtVerifier jwtVerifier;
    private final AzureKeyVaultSecureKeyReleaseClient keyReleaseClient;
    private final AzureGuestKeyUnwrapper keyUnwrapper;
    private final SecureRandom secureRandom;
    private final String contextKeyId;

    public AzureAttestedKeyReleaseService(
            AzureGuestAttestationTokenProvider tokenProvider,
            AzureAttestationJwtVerifier jwtVerifier,
            AzureKeyVaultSecureKeyReleaseClient keyReleaseClient,
            AzureGuestKeyUnwrapper keyUnwrapper,
            SecureRandom secureRandom,
            String contextKeyId) {
        if (contextKeyId == null || !contextKeyId.matches(&quot;[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}&quot;)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Context key identifier is invalid.&quot;);
        }
        this.tokenProvider = tokenProvider;
        this.jwtVerifier = jwtVerifier;
        this.keyReleaseClient = keyReleaseClient;
        this.keyUnwrapper = keyUnwrapper;
        this.secureRandom = secureRandom;
        this.contextKeyId = contextKeyId;
    }

    public SecureContextKey releaseContextKey() {
        byte[] randomNonce = new byte[32];
        secureRandom.nextBytes(randomNonce);
        String nonce = Base64.getUrlEncoder().withoutPadding().encodeToString(randomNonce);
        java.util.Arrays.fill(randomNonce, (byte) 0);

        String token = tokenProvider.attest(nonce);
        var verified = jwtVerifier.verify(token, nonce);
        String releaseValue = keyReleaseClient.release(verified.token(), nonce);
        SecretKey key = keyUnwrapper.unwrap(releaseValue);
        byte[] encoded = key == null ? null : key.getEncoded();
        if (encoded == null || encoded.length != 32
                || !&quot;AES&quot;.equalsIgnoreCase(key.getAlgorithm())) {
            if (encoded != null) {
                java.util.Arrays.fill(encoded, (byte) 0);
            }
            throw new AzureAttestationException(
                    &quot;Released context key is not a 256-bit AES key.&quot;);
        }
        try {
            return new SecureContextKey(contextKeyId, key);
        } finally {
            java.util.Arrays.fill(encoded, (byte) 0);
        }
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/azure/AzureAttestedSecureContextKeyProvider.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.azure;

import java.util.Optional;

import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.SecureContextKey;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.SecureContextKeyProvider;

public final class AzureAttestedSecureContextKeyProvider implements SecureContextKeyProvider {
    private final AzureAttestedKeyReleaseService keyReleaseService;
    private final String contextKeyId;

    public AzureAttestedSecureContextKeyProvider(
            AzureAttestedKeyReleaseService keyReleaseService,
            String contextKeyId) {
        this.keyReleaseService = keyReleaseService;
        this.contextKeyId = contextKeyId;
    }

    @Override
    public SecureContextKey currentKey() {
        return keyReleaseService.releaseContextKey();
    }

    @Override
    public Optional&lt;SecureContextKey&gt; findKey(String id) {
        if (!contextKeyId.equals(id)) {
            return Optional.empty();
        }
        return Optional.of(keyReleaseService.releaseContextKey());
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/azure/AzureBuildProvenanceVerifier.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.azure;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.LinkOption;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.security.GeneralSecurityException;
import java.security.PublicKey;
import java.security.Signature;
import java.security.KeyFactory;
import java.security.spec.X509EncodedKeySpec;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.HexFormat;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonParser;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

public final class AzureBuildProvenanceVerifier {
    private static final int MAX_MANIFEST_BYTES = 64 * 1024;
    private static final int MAX_SIGNATURE_BYTES = 4 * 1024;
    private static final long MAX_ARTIFACT_BYTES = 2L * 1024 * 1024 * 1024;

    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;

    public AzureBuildProvenanceVerifier(ObjectMapper objectMapper) {
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
    }

    public static PublicKey loadEd25519PublicKey(Path pemPath) {
        try {
            byte[] bytes = readBoundedRegularFile(pemPath, MAX_SIGNATURE_BYTES);
            String pem = new String(bytes, java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.US_ASCII).trim();
            String header = &quot;-----BEGIN PUBLIC KEY-----&quot;;
            String footer = &quot;-----END PUBLIC KEY-----&quot;;
            if (!pem.startsWith(header) || !pem.endsWith(footer)) {
                throw new AzureAttestationException(
                        &quot;Build provenance public key must be an X.509 PEM public key.&quot;);
            }
            String encoded = pem.substring(header.length(), pem.length() - footer.length())
                    .replaceAll(&quot;\\s&quot;, &quot;&quot;);
            byte[] der = Base64.getDecoder().decode(encoded);
            PublicKey key = KeyFactory.getInstance(&quot;Ed25519&quot;)
                    .generatePublic(new X509EncodedKeySpec(der));
            if (!&quot;EdDSA&quot;.equalsIgnoreCase(key.getAlgorithm())
                    &amp;&amp; !&quot;Ed25519&quot;.equalsIgnoreCase(key.getAlgorithm())) {
                throw new AzureAttestationException(
                        &quot;Build provenance public key must use Ed25519.&quot;);
            }
            return key;
        } catch (AzureAttestationException e) {
            throw e;
        } catch (IOException | GeneralSecurityException | IllegalArgumentException e) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(
                    &quot;Unable to load the build provenance public key.&quot;, e);
        }
    }

    public VerifiedBuild verify(
            Path artifactPath,
            Path manifestPath,
            Path signaturePath,
            PublicKey trustedBuildKey) {
        if (artifactPath == null || manifestPath == null || signaturePath == null
                || trustedBuildKey == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Build provenance inputs must be configured.&quot;);
        }
        try {
            byte[] manifestBytes = readBoundedRegularFile(manifestPath, MAX_MANIFEST_BYTES);
            byte[] signatureBytes = decodeSignature(signaturePath);
            verifySignature(manifestBytes, signatureBytes, trustedBuildKey);
            JsonNode manifest = parseManifest(manifestBytes);

            String artifactName = requiredText(manifest, &quot;artifact&quot;);
            if (!artifactPath.getFileName().toString().equals(artifactName)) {
                throw new AzureAttestationException(
                        &quot;Build provenance does not identify the configured artifact.&quot;);
            }
            String expectedArtifactDigest = requiredDigest(manifest, &quot;sha256&quot;);
            String expectedSbomDigest = requiredDigest(manifest, &quot;sbomSha256&quot;);
            String sourceRevision = requiredText(manifest, &quot;sourceRevision&quot;);
            if (!sourceRevision.matches(&quot;(?i)[0-9a-f]{7,64}&quot;)) {
                throw new AzureAttestationException(
                        &quot;Build provenance source revision is invalid.&quot;);
            }
            JsonNode schemaVersion = manifest.get(&quot;schemaVersion&quot;);
            if (schemaVersion == null || !schemaVersion.isIntegralNumber()
                    || schemaVersion.longValue() != 1) {
                throw new AzureAttestationException(
                        &quot;Build provenance schema version is unsupported.&quot;);
            }

            Path sbomPath = artifactPath.resolveSibling(artifactName + &quot;.sbom.json&quot;);
            String actualArtifactDigest = sha256(artifactPath);
            String actualSbomDigest = sha256(sbomPath);
            if (!expectedArtifactDigest.equals(actualArtifactDigest)
                    || !expectedSbomDigest.equals(actualSbomDigest)) {
                throw new AzureAttestationException(
                        &quot;Build artifact or SBOM digest does not match signed provenance.&quot;);
            }
            return new VerifiedBuild(
                    artifactPath.toAbsolutePath().normalize(),
                    artifactName,
                    actualArtifactDigest,
                    actualSbomDigest,
                    sourceRevision);
        } catch (AzureAttestationException e) {
            throw e;
        } catch (IOException | GeneralSecurityException e) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(
                    &quot;Unable to verify signed build provenance.&quot;, e);
        }
    }

    private JsonNode parseManifest(byte[] bytes) throws IOException {
        try (JsonParser parser = objectMapper.getFactory().createParser(bytes)) {
            parser.enable(JsonParser.Feature.STRICT_DUPLICATE_DETECTION);
            JsonNode manifest = objectMapper.readTree(parser);
            if (manifest == null || !manifest.isObject() || parser.nextToken() != null) {
                throw new AzureAttestationException(
                        &quot;Build provenance manifest is not a single JSON object.&quot;);
            }
            return manifest;
        }
    }

    private static byte[] decodeSignature(Path signaturePath) throws IOException {
        byte[] bytes = readBoundedRegularFile(signaturePath, MAX_SIGNATURE_BYTES);
        try {
            String encoded = new String(bytes, java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.US_ASCII).trim();
            return Base64.getDecoder().decode(encoded);
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(
                    &quot;Build provenance signature is not valid base64.&quot;, e);
        }
    }

    private static void verifySignature(
            byte[] manifest,
            byte[] signatureBytes,
            PublicKey trustedBuildKey) throws GeneralSecurityException {
        if (!&quot;EdDSA&quot;.equalsIgnoreCase(trustedBuildKey.getAlgorithm())
                &amp;&amp; !&quot;Ed25519&quot;.equalsIgnoreCase(trustedBuildKey.getAlgorithm())) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(
                    &quot;Build provenance trust key must use Ed25519.&quot;);
        }
        Signature signature = Signature.getInstance(&quot;Ed25519&quot;);
        signature.initVerify(trustedBuildKey);
        signature.update(manifest);
        if (!signature.verify(signatureBytes)) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(
                    &quot;Build provenance signature is not trusted.&quot;);
        }
    }

    private static byte[] readBoundedRegularFile(Path path, int maximumBytes)
            throws IOException {
        if (Files.isSymbolicLink(path)
                || !Files.isRegularFile(path, LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)
                || Files.size(path) &gt; maximumBytes) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(
                    &quot;Build provenance input is missing, unsafe, or oversized.&quot;);
        }
        try (InputStream input = Files.newInputStream(path)) {
            byte[] bytes = input.readNBytes(maximumBytes + 1);
            if (bytes.length &gt; maximumBytes) {
                throw new AzureAttestationException(
                        &quot;Build provenance input exceeded the size limit.&quot;);
            }
            return bytes;
        }
    }

    private static String sha256(Path path) throws IOException {
        if (Files.isSymbolicLink(path)
                || !Files.isRegularFile(path, LinkOption.NOFOLLOW_LINKS)
                || Files.size(path) &gt; MAX_ARTIFACT_BYTES) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(
                    &quot;Build artifact or SBOM is missing, unsafe, or oversized.&quot;);
        }
        try (InputStream input = Files.newInputStream(path)) {
            var digest = java.security.MessageDigest.getInstance(&quot;SHA-256&quot;);
            byte[] buffer = new byte[16 * 1024];
            int read;
            while ((read = input.read(buffer)) != -1) {
                digest.update(buffer, 0, read);
            }
            return HexFormat.of().formatHex(digest.digest());
        } catch (java.security.NoSuchAlgorithmException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;SHA-256 is not available.&quot;, e);
        }
    }

    private static String requiredText(JsonNode object, String field) {
        JsonNode value = object.get(field);
        if (value == null || !value.isTextual() || value.textValue().isBlank()) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(
                    &quot;Build provenance field is missing: &quot; + field);
        }
        return value.textValue();
    }

    private static String requiredDigest(JsonNode object, String field) {
        String digest = requiredText(object, field);
        if (!digest.matches(&quot;(?i)[0-9a-f]{64}&quot;)) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(
                    &quot;Build provenance digest is invalid: &quot; + field);
        }
        return digest.toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT);
    }

    public record VerifiedBuild(
            Path artifactPath,
            String artifact,
            String artifactSha256,
            String sbomSha256,
            String sourceRevision) {
        public VerifiedBuild {
            if (artifactPath == null || artifact == null || artifactSha256 == null
                    || sbomSha256 == null || sourceRevision == null) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                        &quot;Verified build provenance fields must be present.&quot;);
            }
        }
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/azure/AzureConfidentialGatewayConfiguration.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.azure;

import java.net.URI;
import java.net.http.HttpClient;
import java.security.SecureRandom;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.stream.Collectors;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.ConfidentialRuntimeVerifier;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.SecureContextKeyProvider;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Bean;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Configuration;
import org.springframework.context.annotation.Profile;

@Configuration(proxyBeanMethods = false)
@Profile(&quot;azure-cvm&quot;)
public class AzureConfidentialGatewayConfiguration {
    @Bean
    public AzureAttestationPolicy azureAttestationPolicy(
            @Value(&quot;${confidential.azure.attestation.issuer}&quot;) String issuer,
            @Value(&quot;${confidential.azure.attestation.audience:}&quot;) String audience,
            @Value(&quot;${confidential.azure.attestation.approved-launch-measurements}&quot;)
                    String measurements,
            @Value(&quot;${confidential.azure.attestation.policy-hash}&quot;) String policyHash,
            @Value(&quot;${confidential.azure.attestation.minimum-security-versions:}&quot;)
                    String minimumVersions) {
        Set&lt;String&gt; allowedMeasurements = Arrays.stream(measurements.split(&quot;,&quot;))
                .map(String::trim)
                .filter(value -&gt; !value.isEmpty())
                .collect(Collectors.toUnmodifiableSet());
        return new AzureAttestationPolicy(
                issuer,
                audience,
                allowedMeasurements,
                policyHash,
                parseMinimumVersions(minimumVersions));
    }

    @Bean
    public AzureAttestationKeyResolver azureAttestationKeyResolver(
            @Value(&quot;${confidential.azure.attestation.openid-configuration-uri}&quot;)
                    URI openIdConfigurationUri,
            ObjectMapper objectMapper) {
        return new AzureAttestationJwksClient(
                openIdConfigurationUri,
                HttpClient.newBuilder().followRedirects(HttpClient.Redirect.NEVER).build(),
                objectMapper,
                Clock.systemUTC());
    }

    @Bean
    public AzureGuestAttestationNativeClient azureGuestAttestationNativeClient(
