            AzureGuestAttestationTokenProvider tokenProvider,
            AzureAttestationJwtVerifier jwtVerifier) {
        return new AzureConfidentialRuntimeVerifier(
                tokenProvider, jwtVerifier, new SecureRandom());
    }

    private static java.util.Map&lt;String, Integer&gt; parseMinimumVersions(String raw) {
        if (raw == null || raw.isBlank()) {
            return java.util.Map.of();
        }
        var result = new HashMap&lt;String, Integer&gt;();
        for (String item : raw.split(&quot;,&quot;)) {
            String[] pair = item.trim().split(&quot;=&quot;, -1);
            if (pair.length != 2 || pair[0].isBlank()) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                        &quot;Minimum security versions must use claim=value pairs.&quot;);
            }
            try {
                int value = Integer.parseInt(pair[1]);
                if (value &lt; 0 || result.putIfAbsent(pair[0], value) != null) {
                    throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                            &quot;Minimum security version is negative or duplicated.&quot;);
                }
            } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                        &quot;Minimum security version must be an integer.&quot;, e);
            }
        }
        return java.util.Map.copyOf(result);
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/azure/AzureConfidentialRuntimeVerifier.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.azure;

import java.security.SecureRandom;
import java.util.Base64;

import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.ConfidentialRuntimeVerifier;

public final class AzureConfidentialRuntimeVerifier implements ConfidentialRuntimeVerifier {
    private final AzureGuestAttestationTokenProvider tokenProvider;
    private final AzureAttestationJwtVerifier jwtVerifier;
    private final SecureRandom secureRandom;

    public AzureConfidentialRuntimeVerifier(
            AzureGuestAttestationTokenProvider tokenProvider,
            AzureAttestationJwtVerifier jwtVerifier,
            SecureRandom secureRandom) {
        this.tokenProvider = tokenProvider;
        this.jwtVerifier = jwtVerifier;
        this.secureRandom = secureRandom;
    }

    @Override
    public void verifyCurrentRuntime() {
        byte[] challenge = new byte[32];
        secureRandom.nextBytes(challenge);
        String nonce = Base64.getUrlEncoder().withoutPadding().encodeToString(challenge);
        java.util.Arrays.fill(challenge, (byte) 0);

        String token = tokenProvider.attest(nonce);
        jwtVerifier.verify(token, nonce);
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/azure/AzureGuestAttestationNativeClient.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.azure;

import java.lang.foreign.Arena;
import java.lang.foreign.FunctionDescriptor;
import java.lang.foreign.Linker;
import java.lang.foreign.MemorySegment;
import java.lang.foreign.SymbolLookup;
import java.lang.foreign.ValueLayout;
import java.lang.invoke.MethodHandle;
import java.net.URI;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.Objects;

import javax.crypto.SecretKey;
import javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec;

/**
 * Project Panama adapter for the C ABI bridge to Microsoft&apos;s Linux
 * azguestattestation client library.
 */
public final class AzureGuestAttestationNativeClient
        implements AzureGuestAttestationTokenProvider, AzureGuestKeyUnwrapper {
    private static final int MAX_RELEASE_TOKEN_LENGTH = 1_000_000;
    private static final String NATIVE_LIBRARY = &quot;opentron_azure_attestation&quot;;
    private static final long MAX_ATTESTATION_TOKEN_BYTES = 64 * 1024;
    private static final long AES_KEY_BYTES = 32;
    private static final Linker LINKER = Linker.nativeLinker();
    private static final FunctionDescriptor ATTEST_DESCRIPTOR = FunctionDescriptor.of(
            ValueLayout.JAVA_INT,
            ValueLayout.ADDRESS,
            ValueLayout.ADDRESS,
            ValueLayout.ADDRESS,
            ValueLayout.JAVA_LONG,
            ValueLayout.ADDRESS);
    private static final FunctionDescriptor UNWRAP_DESCRIPTOR = FunctionDescriptor.of(
            ValueLayout.JAVA_INT,
            ValueLayout.ADDRESS,
            ValueLayout.ADDRESS,
            ValueLayout.JAVA_LONG,
            ValueLayout.ADDRESS);

    private final String attestationEndpoint;
    private final NativeOperations nativeOperations;

    public AzureGuestAttestationNativeClient(URI attestationEndpoint) {
        this(attestationEndpoint, null);
    }

    AzureGuestAttestationNativeClient(URI attestationEndpoint, NativeOperations nativeOperations) {
        if (attestationEndpoint == null || !&quot;https&quot;.equalsIgnoreCase(attestationEndpoint.getScheme())
                || attestationEndpoint.getHost() == null
                || !attestationEndpoint.getHost().toLowerCase().endsWith(&quot;.attest.azure.net&quot;)
                || attestationEndpoint.getUserInfo() != null
                || attestationEndpoint.getFragment() != null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    &quot;Azure guest attestation endpoint must be an Azure Attestation HTTPS URI.&quot;);
        }
        this.attestationEndpoint = attestationEndpoint.toString();
        this.nativeOperations = nativeOperations == null
                ? new PanamaNativeOperations()
                : nativeOperations;
    }

    @Override
    public String attest(String nonce) {
        if (nonce == null || !nonce.matches(&quot;[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}&quot;)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;A 256-bit base64url nonce is required.&quot;);
        }
        String token = nativeOperations.attest(attestationEndpoint, nonce);
        if (token == null || token.isBlank() || token.length() &gt; 64 * 1024) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(
                    &quot;Microsoft guest attestation returned invalid evidence.&quot;);
        }
        return token;
    }

    @Override
    public SecretKey unwrap(String keyReleaseValue) {
        if (keyReleaseValue == null || keyReleaseValue.isBlank()
                || keyReleaseValue.length() &gt; MAX_RELEASE_TOKEN_LENGTH
                || !isCompactSignedToken(keyReleaseValue)) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Azure Key Vault release value is invalid.&quot;);
        }
        byte[] keyBytes = nativeOperations.unwrap(keyReleaseValue);
        if (keyBytes == null || keyBytes.length != 32) {
            if (keyBytes != null) {
                Arrays.fill(keyBytes, (byte) 0);
            }
            throw new AzureAttestationException(
                    &quot;Microsoft guest attestation did not return a 256-bit AES key.&quot;);
        }
        try {
            return new SecretKeySpec(keyBytes, &quot;AES&quot;);
        } finally {
            Arrays.fill(keyBytes, (byte) 0);
        }
    }

    private static boolean isCompactSignedToken(String value) {
        int firstDot = value.indexOf(&apos;.&apos;);
        int secondDot = firstDot &lt; 0 ? -1 : value.indexOf(&apos;.&apos;, firstDot + 1);
        return firstDot &gt; 0
                &amp;&amp; secondDot &gt; firstDot + 1
                &amp;&amp; secondDot &lt; value.length() - 1
                &amp;&amp; value.indexOf(&apos;.&apos;, secondDot + 1) &lt; 0;
    }

    interface NativeOperations {
        String attest(String endpoint, String nonce);

        byte[] unwrap(String keyReleaseValue);
    }

    private static final class PanamaNativeOperations implements NativeOperations {
        private final MethodHandle attest;
        private final MethodHandle unwrap;

        private PanamaNativeOperations() {
            System.loadLibrary(NATIVE_LIBRARY);
            SymbolLookup symbols = SymbolLookup.loaderLookup();
            attest = LINKER.downcallHandle(
                    symbols.find(&quot;opentron_azure_attest&quot;)
                            .orElseThrow(() -&gt; new UnsatisfiedLinkError(
                                    &quot;Azure native attestation symbol is missing.&quot;)),
                    ATTEST_DESCRIPTOR);
            unwrap = LINKER.downcallHandle(
                    symbols.find(&quot;opentron_azure_unwrap_key&quot;)
                            .orElseThrow(() -&gt; new UnsatisfiedLinkError(
                                    &quot;Azure native key unwrap symbol is missing.&quot;)),
                    UNWRAP_DESCRIPTOR);
        }

        @Override
        public String attest(String endpoint, String nonce) {
            try (Arena arena = Arena.ofConfined()) {
                MemorySegment endpointSegment = allocateCString(arena, endpoint);
                MemorySegment nonceSegment = allocateCString(arena, nonce);
                MemorySegment output = arena.allocate(MAX_ATTESTATION_TOKEN_BYTES, 1);
                MemorySegment written = arena.allocate(ValueLayout.JAVA_LONG);
                try {
                    int status = invokeAttest(
                            endpointSegment, nonceSegment, output, written);
                    long length = written.get(ValueLayout.JAVA_LONG, 0);
                    if (status != 0 || length &lt;= 0 || length &gt; MAX_ATTESTATION_TOKEN_BYTES) {
                        throw new AzureAttestationException(
                                &quot;Microsoft guest attestation native operation failed.&quot;);
                    }
                    byte[] token = output.asSlice(0, length).toArray(ValueLayout.JAVA_BYTE);
                    try {
                        return new String(token, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
                    } finally {
                        Arrays.fill(token, (byte) 0);
                    }
                } finally {
                    endpointSegment.fill((byte) 0);
                    nonceSegment.fill((byte) 0);
                    output.fill((byte) 0);
                    written.fill((byte) 0);
                }
            }
        }

        @Override
        public byte[] unwrap(String keyReleaseValue) {
            try (Arena arena = Arena.ofConfined()) {
                MemorySegment releaseValue = allocateCString(arena, keyReleaseValue);
                MemorySegment output = arena.allocate(AES_KEY_BYTES, 1);
                MemorySegment written = arena.allocate(ValueLayout.JAVA_LONG);
                try {
                    int status = invokeUnwrap(releaseValue, output, written);
                    long length = written.get(ValueLayout.JAVA_LONG, 0);
                    if (status != 0 || length != AES_KEY_BYTES) {
                        throw new AzureAttestationException(
                                &quot;TPM-backed Azure key release unwrap failed.&quot;);
                    }
                    return output.asSlice(0, length).toArray(ValueLayout.JAVA_BYTE);
                } finally {
                    releaseValue.fill((byte) 0);
                    output.fill((byte) 0);
                    written.fill((byte) 0);
                }
            }
        }

        private int invokeAttest(
                MemorySegment endpoint,
                MemorySegment nonce,
                MemorySegment output,
                MemorySegment written) {
            try {
                return (int) attest.invokeExact(
                        endpoint, nonce, output, MAX_ATTESTATION_TOKEN_BYTES, written);
            } catch (RuntimeException | Error failure) {
                throw failure;
            } catch (Throwable failure) {
                throw new AzureAttestationException(
                        &quot;Microsoft guest attestation native operation failed.&quot;, failure);
            }
        }

        private int invokeUnwrap(
                MemorySegment releaseValue, MemorySegment output, MemorySegment written) {
            try {
                return (int) unwrap.invokeExact(
                        releaseValue, output, AES_KEY_BYTES, written);
            } catch (RuntimeException | Error failure) {
                throw failure;
            } catch (Throwable failure) {
                throw new AzureAttestationException(
                        &quot;TPM-backed Azure key release unwrap failed.&quot;, failure);
            }
        }

        private static MemorySegment allocateCString(Arena arena, String value) {
            Objects.requireNonNull(value, &quot;value&quot;);
            byte[] encoded = value.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
            try {
                MemorySegment segment = arena.allocate(encoded.length + 1L, 1);
                segment.asSlice(0, encoded.length)
                        .copyFrom(MemorySegment.ofArray(encoded));
                segment.set(ValueLayout.JAVA_BYTE, encoded.length, (byte) 0);
                return segment;
            } finally {
                Arrays.fill(encoded, (byte) 0);
            }
        }
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/azure/AzureGuestAttestationTokenProvider.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.azure;

 * the library to bind the supplied nonce into the hardware report data.
 */
@FunctionalInterface
public interface AzureGuestAttestationTokenProvider {
    String attest(String nonce);

/**

 * The implementation must reject malformed or unauthenticated release output.

@FunctionalInterface

}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/azure/AzureKeyVaultSecureKeyReleaseClient.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.azure;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.net.URI;
import java.net.http.HttpClient;
import java.net.http.HttpRequest;
import java.net.http.HttpResponse;
import java.time.Duration;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

public final class AzureKeyVaultSecureKeyReleaseClient {
    private static final int MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES = 256 * 1024;
    private static final String API_VERSION = &quot;2025-07-01&quot;;
    private static final String ENCRYPTION_ALGORITHM = &quot;RSA_AES_KEY_WRAP_256&quot;;

    private final URI vaultUri;
    private final String keyName;
    private final String keyVersion;
    private final AzureKeyVaultAccessTokenProvider tokenProvider;
    private final HttpClient httpClient;
    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;

    public AzureKeyVaultSecureKeyReleaseClient(
            URI vaultUri,
            String keyName,
            String keyVersion,
            AzureKeyVaultAccessTokenProvider tokenProvider,
            HttpClient httpClient,
            ObjectMapper objectMapper) {
        validateVaultUri(vaultUri);
        if (!validKeyPart(keyName) || !validKeyPart(keyVersion)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Key Vault key name or version is invalid.&quot;);
        }
        this.vaultUri = stripTrailingSlash(vaultUri);
        this.keyName = keyName;
        this.keyVersion = keyVersion;
        this.tokenProvider = tokenProvider;
        this.httpClient = httpClient;
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
    }

    public String release(String attestationToken, String nonce) {
        if (attestationToken == null || attestationToken.isBlank()
                || nonce == null || nonce.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Verified attestation token and nonce are required.&quot;);
        }
        try {
            byte[] requestBody = objectMapper.writeValueAsBytes(
                    new ReleaseRequest(attestationToken, nonce, ENCRYPTION_ALGORITHM));
            URI releaseUri = URI.create(vaultUri + &quot;/keys/&quot; + keyName + &quot;/&quot; + keyVersion
                    + &quot;/release?api-version=&quot; + API_VERSION);
            HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder(releaseUri)
                    .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(10))
                    .header(&quot;Authorization&quot;, &quot;Bearer &quot; + tokenProvider.getKeyVaultAccessToken())
                    .header(&quot;Content-Type&quot;, &quot;application/json&quot;)
                    .header(&quot;Accept&quot;, &quot;application/json&quot;)
                    .POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofByteArray(requestBody))
                    .build();
            HttpResponse&lt;java.io.InputStream&gt; response = httpClient.send(
                    request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofInputStream());
            try (var body = response.body()) {
                if (response.statusCode() != 200) {
                    throw new AzureAttestationException(
                            &quot;Azure Key Vault denied or failed secure key release (HTTP &quot;
                                    + response.statusCode() + &quot;).&quot;);
                }
                byte[] responseBytes = body.readNBytes(MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES + 1);
                if (responseBytes.length &gt; MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES) {
                    throw new AzureAttestationException(
                            &quot;Azure Key Vault release response is oversized.&quot;);
                }
                JsonNode json = objectMapper.readTree(responseBytes);
                String value = json.path(&quot;value&quot;).asText(&quot;&quot;);
                if (value.isBlank()) {
                    throw new AzureAttestationException(
                            &quot;Azure Key Vault response did not contain a key release value.&quot;);
                }
                return value;
            }
        } catch (AzureAttestationException e) {
            throw e;
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            throw new AzureAttestationException(
                    &quot;Azure Key Vault key release request was interrupted.&quot;, e);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Azure Key Vault key release request failed.&quot;, e);
        }
    }

    private static void validateVaultUri(URI uri) {
        if (uri == null || !&quot;https&quot;.equalsIgnoreCase(uri.getScheme())
                || uri.getHost() == null || uri.getUserInfo() != null
                || uri.getQuery() != null || uri.getFragment() != null
                || uri.getPort() != -1
                || (uri.getPath() != null &amp;&amp; !uri.getPath().isEmpty()
                        &amp;&amp; !&quot;/&quot;.equals(uri.getPath()))) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Key Vault must be configured with an HTTPS URI.&quot;);
        }
        String host = uri.getHost().toLowerCase();
        if (!host.endsWith(&quot;.vault.azure.net&quot;) &amp;&amp; !host.endsWith(&quot;.managedhsm.azure.net&quot;)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Key Vault URI must use an Azure public cloud host.&quot;);
        }
    }

    private static boolean validKeyPart(String value) {
        return value != null &amp;&amp; value.matches(&quot;[A-Za-z0-9-]{1,127}&quot;);
    }

    private static URI stripTrailingSlash(URI uri) {
        String value = uri.toString();
        return URI.create(value.endsWith(&quot;/&quot;) ? value.substring(0, value.length() - 1) : value);
    }

    private record ReleaseRequest(String target, String nonce, String enc) {
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/azure/AzureManagedIdentityTokenProvider.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.azure;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.net.URI;
import java.net.URLEncoder;
import java.net.http.HttpClient;
import java.net.http.HttpRequest;
import java.net.http.HttpResponse;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.concurrent.locks.ReentrantLock;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

public final class AzureManagedIdentityTokenProvider implements AzureKeyVaultAccessTokenProvider {
    private static final URI IMDS_TOKEN_ENDPOINT =
            URI.create(&quot;http://169.254.169.254/metadata/identity/oauth2/token&quot;);

    private final HttpClient httpClient;
    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper;
    private final String managedIdentityClientId;
    private final Clock clock;
    private final ReentrantLock refreshLock = new ReentrantLock();
    private volatile String cachedAccessToken;
    private volatile Instant cachedTokenExpiresAt = Instant.MIN;

    public AzureManagedIdentityTokenProvider(
            HttpClient httpClient,
            ObjectMapper objectMapper,
            String managedIdentityClientId,
            Clock clock) {
        this.httpClient = httpClient;
        this.objectMapper = objectMapper;
        this.managedIdentityClientId = managedIdentityClientId == null
                ? &quot;&quot;
                : managedIdentityClientId.trim();
        this.clock = clock;
    }

    @Override
    public String getKeyVaultAccessToken() {
        if (isCachedTokenValid()) {
            return cachedAccessToken;
        }
        refreshLock.lock();
        try {
            if (isCachedTokenValid()) {
                return cachedAccessToken;
            }
            return requestAccessToken();
        } finally {
            refreshLock.unlock();
        }
    }

    private String requestAccessToken() {
        StringBuilder query = new StringBuilder(
                &quot;?api-version=2018-02-01&amp;resource=&quot; + encode(&quot;https://vault.azure.net/&quot;));
        if (!managedIdentityClientId.isBlank()) {
            query.append(&quot;&amp;client_id=&quot;).append(encode(managedIdentityClientId));
        }
        HttpRequest request = HttpRequest.newBuilder(
                        URI.create(IMDS_TOKEN_ENDPOINT + query.toString()))
                .timeout(Duration.ofSeconds(3))
                .header(&quot;Metadata&quot;, &quot;true&quot;)
                .header(&quot;Accept&quot;, &quot;application/json&quot;)
                .GET()
                .build();
        try {
            HttpResponse&lt;java.io.InputStream&gt; response = httpClient.send(
                    request, HttpResponse.BodyHandlers.ofInputStream());
            try (var body = response.body()) {
                if (response.statusCode() != 200) {
                    throw new AzureAttestationException(
                            &quot;Azure managed identity did not issue a Key Vault token.&quot;);
                }
                byte[] responseBytes = body.readNBytes(64 * 1024 + 1);
                if (responseBytes.length &gt; 64 * 1024) {
                    throw new AzureAttestationException(
                            &quot;Azure managed identity response exceeded the size limit.&quot;);
                }
                JsonNode json = objectMapper.readTree(responseBytes);
                String accessToken = json.path(&quot;access_token&quot;).asText(&quot;&quot;);
                long expiresOn = parseExpiration(json, clock.instant());
                if (accessToken.isBlank() || expiresOn &lt;= clock.instant().getEpochSecond() + 60) {
                    throw new AzureAttestationException(
                            &quot;Azure managed identity response did not contain a valid token.&quot;);
                }
                cachedAccessToken = accessToken;
                cachedTokenExpiresAt = Instant.ofEpochSecond(expiresOn - 60);
                return accessToken;
            }
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            throw new AzureAttestationException(
                    &quot;Azure managed identity token request was interrupted.&quot;, e);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            throw new AzureAttestationException(
                    &quot;Azure managed identity token request failed.&quot;, e);
        }
    }

    private boolean isCachedTokenValid() {
        return cachedAccessToken != null &amp;&amp; clock.instant().isBefore(cachedTokenExpiresAt);
    }

    private static long parseExpiration(JsonNode response, Instant now) {
        JsonNode expiresOn = response.get(&quot;expires_on&quot;);
        if (expiresOn != null &amp;&amp; expiresOn.asText().matches(&quot;\\d+&quot;)) {
            try {
                return Long.parseLong(expiresOn.asText());
            } catch (NumberFormatException e) {
                throw new AzureAttestationException(
                        &quot;Azure managed identity response has an invalid expiration.&quot;, e);
            }
        }
        JsonNode expiresIn = response.get(&quot;expires_in&quot;);
        if (expiresIn != null &amp;&amp; expiresIn.asText().matches(&quot;\\d+&quot;)) {
            try {
                return Math.addExact(now.getEpochSecond(), Long.parseLong(expiresIn.asText()));
            } catch (ArithmeticException | NumberFormatException e) {
                throw new AzureAttestationException(
                        &quot;Azure managed identity response has an invalid token lifetime.&quot;, e);
            }
        }
        return 0;
    }

    private static String encode(String value) {
        return URLEncoder.encode(value, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/gcp/GcpAttestationException.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.gcp;

public final class GcpAttestationException extends RuntimeException {
    public GcpAttestationException(String message) {
        super(message);
    }

    public GcpAttestationException(String message, Throwable cause) {
        super(message, cause);
    }

}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/gcp/GcpAttestationPolicy.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.gcp;

import java.util.Set;

/**
 * Policy for Google Cloud Attestation EAT claims. Instance entries may match the
 * official GCA {@code instance_name} or {@code instance_id} value.
 */
public record GcpAttestationPolicy(
        String audience,
        HardwareModel hardwareModel,
        Set&lt;String&gt; allowedProjects,
        Set&lt;String&gt; allowedZones,
        Set&lt;String&gt; allowedInstances) {

    public enum HardwareModel {
        AMD_SEV(&quot;GCP_AMD_SEV&quot;),
        INTEL_TDX(&quot;GCP_INTEL_TDX&quot;);

        private final String claimValue;

        HardwareModel(String claimValue) {
            this.claimValue = claimValue;
        }

        String claimValue() {
            return claimValue;
        }
    }

    public GcpAttestationPolicy {
        if (audience == null || audience.isBlank() || audience.length() &gt; 512) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;A GCA audience of at most 512 characters is required.&quot;);
        }
        if (hardwareModel == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;An explicit GCA hardware model is required.&quot;);
        }
        allowedProjects = immutableNonEmptySet(allowedProjects, &quot;project&quot;);
        allowedZones = immutableNonEmptySet(allowedZones, &quot;zone&quot;);
        allowedInstances = immutableNonEmptySet(allowedInstances, &quot;instance&quot;);
    }

    private static Set&lt;String&gt; immutableNonEmptySet(Set&lt;String&gt; values, String label) {
        if (values == null || values.isEmpty()
                || values.stream().anyMatch(value -&gt; value == null || value.isBlank())) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;At least one allowed &quot; + label + &quot; is required.&quot;);
        }
        return Set.copyOf(values);
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/gcp/GcpAttestationTokenSource.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.gcp;

/**
 * Deployment-supplied source of a GCA-issued compact JWT bound to the requested
 * audience and nonce. Google documents the token claims and the launcher flow,
 * but this adapter intentionally does not assume an undocumented metadata URL.
 * The deployment must supply an implementation using its approved acquisition
 * mechanism; it must not return caller-supplied or unverified evidence.
 */
@FunctionalInterface
public interface GcpAttestationTokenSource {
    String fetch(String audience, String nonce);

        return Arrays.stream(value.split(&quot;,&quot;))
                .map(String::trim)

import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.ConfidentialRuntimeVerifier;

public final class GcpConfidentialRuntimeVerifier implements ConfidentialRuntimeVerifier {
    private final GcpGcaAttestationVerifier attestationVerifier;

    public GcpConfidentialRuntimeVerifier(GcpGcaAttestationVerifier attestationVerifier) {
        this.attestationVerifier = attestationVerifier;

}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/gcp/GcpGcaAttestationVerifier.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.gcp;

import java.security.SecureRandom;
import java.math.BigInteger;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.jwt.Jwt;

/** Produces and validates fresh Google Cloud Attestation evidence against local policy. */
public final class GcpGcaAttestationVerifier {
    static final String GOOGLE_ISSUER = &quot;https://confidentialcomputing.googleapis.com&quot;;
    static final String EAT_PROFILE =
            &quot;https://cloud.google.com/confidential-computing/confidential-vm/docs/token-claims&quot;;
    private static final Duration CLOCK_SKEW = Duration.ofSeconds(30);
    private static final Duration MAX_TOKEN_LIFETIME = Duration.ofHours(2);
    private static final Duration MAX_TOKEN_AGE = Duration.ofMinutes(10);
    private static final int NONCE_BYTES = 32;

    private final GcpAttestationTokenSource tokenSource;
    private final GcpAttestationJwtDecoder jwtDecoder;
    private final GcpAttestationPolicy policy;
    private final Clock clock;
    private final SecureRandom secureRandom;

    public GcpGcaAttestationVerifier(
            GcpAttestationTokenSource tokenSource,
            GcpAttestationJwtDecoder jwtDecoder,
            GcpAttestationPolicy policy,
            Clock clock,
            SecureRandom secureRandom) {
        this.tokenSource = tokenSource;
        this.jwtDecoder = jwtDecoder;
        this.policy = policy;
        this.clock = clock;
        this.secureRandom = secureRandom;
    }

    public Jwt attestCurrentRuntime() {
        byte[] random = new byte[NONCE_BYTES];
        secureRandom.nextBytes(random);
        String nonce = Base64.getUrlEncoder().withoutPadding().encodeToString(random);
        java.util.Arrays.fill(random, (byte) 0);
        String token = tokenSource.fetch(policy.audience(), nonce);
        Jwt jwt = jwtDecoder.decode(token);
        if (jwt == null) {
            reject(&quot;GCA JWT decoder returned no verified claims.&quot;);
        }
        validateClaims(jwt, nonce);
        return jwt;
    }

    private void validateClaims(Jwt jwt, String expectedNonce) {
        if (!GOOGLE_ISSUER.equals(jwt.getClaimAsString(&quot;iss&quot;))) {
            reject(&quot;GCA issuer does not match the pinned Google issuer.&quot;);
        }
        if (!hasAudience(jwt.getClaims().get(&quot;aud&quot;), policy.audience())) {
            reject(&quot;GCA audience does not match policy.&quot;);
        }
        if (!EAT_PROFILE.equals(jwt.getClaimAsString(&quot;eat_profile&quot;))) {
            reject(&quot;Evidence does not identify the official Google CVM EAT profile.&quot;);
        }
        validateTimes(jwt);
        if (!hasExpectedNonce(jwt.getClaims().get(&quot;eat_nonce&quot;), expectedNonce)) {
            reject(&quot;GCA nonce does not match the fresh request challenge.&quot;);
        }
        if (!policy.hardwareModel().claimValue().equals(jwt.getClaimAsString(&quot;hwmodel&quot;))) {
            reject(&quot;GCA hardware model does not match the selected policy.&quot;);
        }
        if (!Boolean.TRUE.equals(jwt.getClaim(&quot;secboot&quot;))) {
            reject(&quot;GCA evidence does not assert that Secure Boot is enabled.&quot;);
        }
        validateGceIdentity(jwt);
    }

    private void validateTimes(Jwt jwt) {
        Instant now = clock.instant();
        Instant issuedAt = jwt.getIssuedAt();
        Instant expiresAt = jwt.getExpiresAt();
        Instant notBefore = instantClaim(jwt.getClaims().get(&quot;nbf&quot;));
        if (issuedAt == null || expiresAt == null || notBefore == null
                || issuedAt.isAfter(now.plus(CLOCK_SKEW))
                || issuedAt.isBefore(now.minus(MAX_TOKEN_AGE))
                || !expiresAt.isAfter(now.minus(CLOCK_SKEW))
                || !notBefore.isBefore(now.plus(CLOCK_SKEW))
                || !expiresAt.isAfter(issuedAt)
                || Duration.between(issuedAt, expiresAt).compareTo(MAX_TOKEN_LIFETIME) &gt; 0) {
            reject(&quot;GCA issue, not-before, or expiration times are invalid or stale.&quot;);
        }
    }

    private void validateGceIdentity(Jwt jwt) {
        Map&lt;String, Object&gt; submods = jwt.getClaim(&quot;submods&quot;);
        if (submods == null || !(submods.get(&quot;gce&quot;) instanceof Map&lt;?, ?&gt;)) {
            reject(&quot;GCA evidence does not contain the required submods.gce identity.&quot;);
        }
        Map&lt;?, ?&gt; gce = (Map&lt;?, ?&gt;) submods.get(&quot;gce&quot;);
        String zone = string(gce.get(&quot;zone&quot;));
        String project;
        String instance;
        String expectedSubject;
        if (policy.hardwareModel() == GcpAttestationPolicy.HardwareModel.AMD_SEV) {
            project = string(gce.get(&quot;project_id&quot;));
            instance = string(gce.get(&quot;instance_name&quot;));
            if (project == null || instance == null) {
                reject(&quot;AMD SEV GCA identity is missing project_id or instance_name.&quot;);
            }
            expectedSubject = &quot;https://www.googleapis.com/compute/v1/projects/&quot;
                    + project + &quot;/zones/&quot; + zone + &quot;/instances/&quot; + instance;
        } else {
            project = string(gce.get(&quot;project_number&quot;));
            instance = string(gce.get(&quot;instance_id&quot;));
            if (project == null || instance == null) {
                reject(&quot;Intel TDX GCA identity is missing project_number or instance_id.&quot;);
            }
            expectedSubject = &quot;//compute.googleapis.com/projects/&quot;
                    + project + &quot;/zones/&quot; + zone + &quot;/instances/&quot; + instance;
        }
        if (zone == null || !policy.allowedZones().contains(zone)
                || !policy.allowedProjects().contains(project)
                || !policy.allowedInstances().contains(instance)
                || !expectedSubject.equals(jwt.getSubject())) {
            reject(&quot;GCA project, zone, instance, or subject is outside the allowlist.&quot;);
        }
    }

    private static boolean hasAudience(Object claim, String expected) {
        if (claim instanceof String value) {
            return expected.equals(value);
        }
        return claim instanceof List&lt;?&gt; values &amp;&amp; values.stream().anyMatch(expected::equals);
    }

    private static boolean hasExpectedNonce(Object claim, String expected) {
        if (claim instanceof String value) {
            return validNonce(value) &amp;&amp; expected.equals(value);
        }
        if (!(claim instanceof List&lt;?&gt; values) || values.isEmpty() || values.size() &gt; 6) {
            return false;
        }
        boolean matched = false;
        java.util.HashSet&lt;String&gt; seen = new java.util.HashSet&lt;&gt;();
        for (Object item : values) {
            if (!(item instanceof String value) || !validNonce(value) || !seen.add(value)) {
                return false;
            }
            matched |= expected.equals(value);
        }
        return matched;
    }

    private static boolean validNonce(String nonce) {
        int byteLength = nonce.getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.UTF_8).length;
        return byteLength &gt;= 10 &amp;&amp; byteLength &lt;= 74;
    }

    private static String string(Object value) {
        return value instanceof String text &amp;&amp; !text.isBlank() ? text : null;
    }

    private static Instant instantClaim(Object value) {
        if (value instanceof Instant instant) {
            return instant;
        }
        if (!(value instanceof Byte || value instanceof Short
                || value instanceof Integer || value instanceof Long
                || value instanceof BigInteger)) {
            return null;
        }
        try {
            long epochSeconds = value instanceof BigInteger integer
                    ? integer.longValueExact() : ((Number) value).longValue();
            return Instant.ofEpochSecond(epochSeconds);
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    private static void reject(String message) {
        throw new GcpAttestationException(message);
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/gcp/GcpHttpTransport.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.gcp;

import java.net.URI;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.Map;

/** Injectable bounded HTTP boundary used for metadata, JWKS, STS, and KMS calls. */
@FunctionalInterface
public interface GcpHttpTransport {
    HttpResponse postOrGet(
            String method,
            URI uri,
            Map&lt;String, String&gt; headers,
            byte[] body,
            Duration timeout,
            int maximumResponseBytes);

    record HttpResponse(int statusCode, byte[] body) {
        public HttpResponse {
            body = body == null ? new byte[0] : body.clone();
        }

        @Override
        public byte[] body() {
            return body.clone();
        }

        public void clear() {
            java.util.Arrays.fill(body, (byte) 0);
        }
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/gcp/GcpWifKmsSecureContextKeyProvider.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.gcp;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonParser;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import java.net.URI;
import java.net.URLEncoder;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;
import javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.SecureContextKey;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.SecureContextKeyProvider;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.jwt.Jwt;

/**
 * Releases a configured encrypted context DEK only after fresh local GCA
 * validation, then exchanges that exact token through WIF before Cloud KMS decrypt.
 */
public final class GcpWifKmsSecureContextKeyProvider implements SecureContextKeyProvider {
    private static final URI STS_URI = URI.create(&quot;https://sts.googleapis.com/v1/token&quot;);
    private static final String CLOUD_PLATFORM_SCOPE = &quot;https://www.googleapis.com/auth/cloud-platform&quot;;
    private static final String GRANT_TYPE = &quot;urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:token-exchange&quot;;
    private static final String SUBJECT_TOKEN_TYPE = &quot;urn:ietf:params:oauth:token-type:jwt&quot;;
    private static final String REQUESTED_TOKEN_TYPE =
            &quot;urn:ietf:params:oauth:token-type:access_token&quot;;
    private static final int MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES = 64 * 1024;
    private static final Duration REQUEST_TIMEOUT = Duration.ofSeconds(5);
    private static final Pattern KMS_RESOURCE = Pattern.compile(
            &quot;projects/[A-Za-z0-9-]+/locations/[A-Za-z0-9-]+/keyRings/[A-Za-z0-9_-]+/cryptoKeys/[A-Za-z0-9_-]+&quot;);
    private static final Pattern WIF_AUDIENCE = Pattern.compile(
            &quot;//iam\\.googleapis\\.com/projects/[0-9]+/locations/global/&quot;
                    + &quot;workloadIdentityPools/[A-Za-z0-9_-]+/providers/[A-Za-z0-9_-]+&quot;);

    private final GcpGcaAttestationVerifier attestationVerifier;
    private final GcpHttpTransport transport;
    private final ObjectMapper mapper;
    private final String wifAudience;
    private final String kmsKeyResource;
    private final String contextKeyId;
    private final String ciphertext;

    public GcpWifKmsSecureContextKeyProvider(
            GcpGcaAttestationVerifier attestationVerifier,
            GcpHttpTransport transport,
            ObjectMapper mapper,
            String wifAudience,
            String kmsKeyResource,
            String contextKeyId,
            String ciphertext) {
        if (wifAudience == null || !WIF_AUDIENCE.matcher(wifAudience).matches()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;A canonical Workload Identity Provider audience is required.&quot;);
        }
        if (kmsKeyResource == null || !KMS_RESOURCE.matcher(kmsKeyResource).matches()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;A canonical Cloud KMS CryptoKey resource is required.&quot;);
        }
        if (contextKeyId == null || !contextKeyId.matches(&quot;[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,64}&quot;)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Context key identifier is invalid.&quot;);
        }
        if (ciphertext == null || ciphertext.isBlank() || ciphertext.length() &gt; MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;A bounded pre-encrypted context DEK is required.&quot;);
        }
        try {
            if (Base64.getDecoder().decode(ciphertext).length == 0) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Encrypted context DEK is empty.&quot;);
            }
        } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Encrypted context DEK must be Base64 encoded.&quot;, e);
        }
        this.attestationVerifier = attestationVerifier;
        this.transport = transport;
        this.mapper = mapper;
        this.wifAudience = wifAudience;
        this.kmsKeyResource = kmsKeyResource;
        this.contextKeyId = contextKeyId;
        this.ciphertext = ciphertext;
    }

    @Override
    public SecureContextKey currentKey() {
        Jwt attestation = attestationVerifier.attestCurrentRuntime();
        String accessToken = exchangeForAccessToken(attestation.getTokenValue());
        byte[] dek = decrypt(accessToken);
        try {
            if (dek.length != 32) {
                throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;Cloud KMS did not return a 256-bit context DEK.&quot;);
            }
            return new SecureContextKey(contextKeyId, new SecretKeySpec(dek, &quot;AES&quot;));
        } finally {
            java.util.Arrays.fill(dek, (byte) 0);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public Optional&lt;SecureContextKey&gt; findKey(String id) {
        return contextKeyId.equals(id) ? Optional.of(currentKey()) : Optional.empty();
    }

    private String exchangeForAccessToken(String attestationJwt) {
        String form = &quot;grant_type=&quot; + encode(GRANT_TYPE)
                + &quot;&amp;audience=&quot; + encode(wifAudience)
                + &quot;&amp;requested_token_type=&quot; + encode(REQUESTED_TOKEN_TYPE)
                + &quot;&amp;subject_token_type=&quot; + encode(SUBJECT_TOKEN_TYPE)
                + &quot;&amp;subject_token=&quot; + encode(attestationJwt)
                + &quot;&amp;scope=&quot; + encode(CLOUD_PLATFORM_SCOPE);
        byte[] requestBody = form.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        try {
            GcpHttpTransport.HttpResponse response = transport.postOrGet(
                    &quot;POST&quot;,
                    STS_URI,
                    Map.of(
                            &quot;Content-Type&quot;, &quot;application/x-www-form-urlencoded&quot;,
                            &quot;Accept&quot;, &quot;application/json&quot;),
                    requestBody,
                    REQUEST_TIMEOUT,
                    MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES);
            JsonNode json = parseSuccess(response, &quot;WIF STS token exchange&quot;);
            String token = text(json, &quot;access_token&quot;);
            String tokenType = text(json, &quot;token_type&quot;);
            JsonNode expiresIn = json.get(&quot;expires_in&quot;);
            if (token == null || token.length() &gt; 16_384 || !&quot;Bearer&quot;.equalsIgnoreCase(tokenType)
                    || expiresIn == null || !expiresIn.canConvertToInt()
                    || expiresIn.intValue() &lt; 1 || expiresIn.intValue() &gt; 3600) {
                throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;WIF STS returned an invalid access token.&quot;);
            }
            return token;
        } finally {
            java.util.Arrays.fill(requestBody, (byte) 0);
        }
    }

    private byte[] decrypt(String accessToken) {
        URI uri = URI.create(&quot;https://cloudkms.googleapis.com/v1/&quot;
                + kmsKeyResource + &quot;:decrypt&quot;);
        byte[] requestBody = (&quot;{\&quot;ciphertext\&quot;:\&quot;&quot; + ciphertext + &quot;\&quot;}&quot;)
                .getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        try {
            GcpHttpTransport.HttpResponse response = transport.postOrGet(
                    &quot;POST&quot;,
                    uri,
                    Map.of(
                            &quot;Authorization&quot;, &quot;Bearer &quot; + accessToken,
                            &quot;Content-Type&quot;, &quot;application/json&quot;,
                            &quot;Accept&quot;, &quot;application/json&quot;),
                    requestBody,
                    REQUEST_TIMEOUT,
                    MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES);
            JsonNode json = parseSuccess(response, &quot;Cloud KMS decrypt&quot;);
            String encodedPlaintext = text(json, &quot;plaintext&quot;);
            if (encodedPlaintext == null || encodedPlaintext.length() &gt; MAX_RESPONSE_BYTES) {
                throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;Cloud KMS returned no bounded plaintext.&quot;);
            }
            try {
                return Base64.getDecoder().decode(encodedPlaintext);
            } catch (IllegalArgumentException e) {
                throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;Cloud KMS plaintext is not valid Base64.&quot;, e);
            }
        } finally {
            java.util.Arrays.fill(requestBody, (byte) 0);
        }
    }

    private JsonNode parseSuccess(GcpHttpTransport.HttpResponse response, String operation) {
        byte[] body = response.body();
        try {
            if (response.statusCode() != 200) {
                throw new GcpAttestationException(operation + &quot; was rejected.&quot;);
            }
            try (JsonParser parser = mapper.getFactory().createParser(body)) {
                parser.enable(JsonParser.Feature.STRICT_DUPLICATE_DETECTION);
                JsonNode node = mapper.readTree(parser);
                if (node == null || !node.isObject() || parser.nextToken() != null) {
                    throw new GcpAttestationException(operation + &quot; returned invalid JSON.&quot;);
                }
                return node;
            } catch (GcpAttestationException e) {
                throw e;
            } catch (Exception e) {
                throw new GcpAttestationException(operation + &quot; returned invalid JSON.&quot;, e);
            }
        } finally {
            java.util.Arrays.fill(body, (byte) 0);
            response.clear();
        }
    }

    private static String text(JsonNode node, String name) {
        JsonNode value = node.get(name);
        return value != null &amp;&amp; value.isTextual() ? value.textValue() : null;
    }

    private static String encode(String value) {
        return URLEncoder.encode(value, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/gcp/GoogleGcaJwtDecoder.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.gcp;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.JsonParser;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.core.type.TypeReference;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import java.math.BigInteger;
import java.net.URI;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.security.KeyFactory;
import java.security.Signature;
import java.security.interfaces.RSAPublicKey;
import java.security.spec.RSAPublicKeySpec;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.jwt.Jwt;

/** Verifies RS256 signatures against Google&apos;s pinned GCA issuer JWKS URI. */
public final class GoogleGcaJwtDecoder implements GcpAttestationJwtDecoder {
    static final URI GOOGLE_GCA_JWKS_URI = URI.create(
            &quot;https://www.googleapis.com/service_accounts/v1/metadata/jwk/&quot;
                    + &quot;signer@confidentialspace-sign.iam.gserviceaccount.com&quot;);
    private static final int MAX_JWT_BYTES = 64 * 1024;
    private static final int MAX_JWKS_BYTES = 256 * 1024;
    private static final Duration REQUEST_TIMEOUT = Duration.ofSeconds(4);
    private static final Pattern KEY_ID = Pattern.compile(&quot;[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,128}&quot;);

    private final GcpHttpTransport transport;
    private final ObjectMapper mapper;

    public GoogleGcaJwtDecoder(GcpHttpTransport transport, ObjectMapper mapper) {
        this.transport = transport;
        this.mapper = mapper;
    }

    @Override
    public Jwt decode(String compactJwt) {
        if (compactJwt == null || compactJwt.length() &gt; MAX_JWT_BYTES) {
            throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;GCA JWT is missing or oversized.&quot;);
        }
        String[] parts = compactJwt.split(&quot;\\.&quot;, -1);
        if (parts.length != 3 || parts[0].isEmpty() || parts[1].isEmpty() || parts[2].isEmpty()) {
            throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;GCA evidence is not a compact JWT.&quot;);
        }
        try {
            JsonNode header = parseSegment(parts[0]);
            if (!&quot;RS256&quot;.equals(text(header, &quot;alg&quot;))) {
                throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;GCA JWT algorithm is not the pinned RS256 algorithm.&quot;);
            }
            if (header.has(&quot;crit&quot;) || header.has(&quot;jku&quot;) || header.has(&quot;jwk&quot;)
                    || header.has(&quot;x5u&quot;) || header.has(&quot;x5c&quot;)
                    || hasUnsupportedHeader(header)) {
                throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;GCA JWT contains unsupported JOSE header parameters.&quot;);
            }
            String kid = text(header, &quot;kid&quot;);
            if (kid == null || !KEY_ID.matcher(kid).matches()) {
                throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;GCA JWT key identifier is invalid.&quot;);
            }
            verifySignature(parts, findKey(kid));

            JsonNode payload = parseSegment(parts[1]);
            Map&lt;String, Object&gt; headers = mapper.convertValue(
                    header, new TypeReference&lt;Map&lt;String, Object&gt;&gt;() {});
            Map&lt;String, Object&gt; claims = mapper.convertValue(
                    payload, new TypeReference&lt;Map&lt;String, Object&gt;&gt;() {});
            Instant issuedAt = epoch(claims.get(&quot;iat&quot;));
            Instant expiresAt = epoch(claims.get(&quot;exp&quot;));
            if (issuedAt == null || expiresAt == null) {
                throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;GCA JWT is missing valid issue or expiration times.&quot;);
            }
            return new Jwt(compactJwt, issuedAt, expiresAt, headers, claims);
        } catch (GcpAttestationException e) {
            throw e;
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;GCA JWT signature or encoding is invalid.&quot;, e);
        }
    }

    private RSAPublicKey findKey(String keyId) throws Exception {
        GcpHttpTransport.HttpResponse response = transport.postOrGet(
                &quot;GET&quot;,
                GOOGLE_GCA_JWKS_URI,
                Map.of(&quot;Accept&quot;, &quot;application/json&quot;),
                null,
                REQUEST_TIMEOUT,
                MAX_JWKS_BYTES);
        if (response.statusCode() != 200) {
            response.clear();
            throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;Google GCA signing keys could not be retrieved.&quot;);
        }
        byte[] jwksBytes = response.body();
        JsonNode keys;
        try {
            keys = parseJson(jwksBytes);
        } finally {
            java.util.Arrays.fill(jwksBytes, (byte) 0);
            response.clear();
        }
        JsonNode key = null;
        for (JsonNode candidate : keys.path(&quot;keys&quot;)) {
            if (keyId.equals(text(candidate, &quot;kid&quot;))) {
                if (key != null) {
                    throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;Google GCA JWKS contains a duplicate key identifier.&quot;);
                }
                key = candidate;
            }
        }
        if (key == null || !&quot;RSA&quot;.equals(text(key, &quot;kty&quot;))
                || !&quot;RS256&quot;.equals(text(key, &quot;alg&quot;))
                || (text(key, &quot;use&quot;) != null &amp;&amp; !&quot;sig&quot;.equals(text(key, &quot;use&quot;)))) {
            throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;No compatible Google GCA signing key was found.&quot;);
        }
        BigInteger modulus = unsignedBase64Url(text(key, &quot;n&quot;));
        BigInteger exponent = unsignedBase64Url(text(key, &quot;e&quot;));
        if (modulus.bitLength() &lt; 2048 || modulus.bitLength() &gt; 8192
                || exponent.compareTo(BigInteger.valueOf(3)) &lt; 0 || !exponent.testBit(0)) {
            throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;Google GCA RSA signing key parameters are invalid.&quot;);
        }
        return (RSAPublicKey) KeyFactory.getInstance(&quot;RSA&quot;)
                .generatePublic(new RSAPublicKeySpec(modulus, exponent));
    }

    private JsonNode parseSegment(String segment) throws Exception {
        return parseJson(Base64.getUrlDecoder().decode(segment));
    }

    private JsonNode parseJson(byte[] json) throws Exception {
        try (JsonParser parser = mapper.getFactory().createParser(json)) {
            parser.enable(JsonParser.Feature.STRICT_DUPLICATE_DETECTION);
            JsonNode node = mapper.readTree(parser);
            if (node == null || !node.isObject() || parser.nextToken() != null) {
                throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;GCA JWT or JWKS must contain one JSON object.&quot;);
            }
            return node;
        }
    }

    private static void verifySignature(String[] parts, RSAPublicKey key) throws Exception {
        Signature verifier = Signature.getInstance(&quot;SHA256withRSA&quot;);
        verifier.initVerify(key);
        verifier.update((parts[0] + &quot;.&quot; + parts[1]).getBytes(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII));
        if (!verifier.verify(Base64.getUrlDecoder().decode(parts[2]))) {
            throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;GCA JWT signature is invalid.&quot;);
        }
    }

    private static BigInteger unsignedBase64Url(String value) {
        if (value == null || value.isBlank()) {
            throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;Google GCA JWKS RSA key is malformed.&quot;);
        }
        return new BigInteger(1, Base64.getUrlDecoder().decode(value));
    }

    private static Instant epoch(Object value) {
        if (!(value instanceof Byte || value instanceof Short
                || value instanceof Integer || value instanceof Long
                || value instanceof BigInteger)) {
            return null;
        }
        try {
            long epochSeconds = value instanceof BigInteger integer
                    ? integer.longValueExact() : ((Number) value).longValue();
            return Instant.ofEpochSecond(epochSeconds);
        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
            return null;
        }
    }

    private static boolean hasUnsupportedHeader(JsonNode header) {
        var fields = header.fieldNames();
        while (fields.hasNext()) {
            String name = fields.next();
            if (!&quot;alg&quot;.equals(name) &amp;&amp; !&quot;kid&quot;.equals(name) &amp;&amp; !&quot;typ&quot;.equals(name)) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private static String text(JsonNode node, String field) {
        JsonNode value = node.get(field);
        return value != null &amp;&amp; value.isTextual() ? value.textValue() : null;
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/gcp/GoTpmGcpAttestationTokenSource.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.gcp;

import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.concurrent.ExecutionException;
import java.util.concurrent.Executors;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeoutException;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

/** Acquires nonce-bound GCA evidence using Google&apos;s vTPM attestation tool. */
public final class GoTpmGcpAttestationTokenSource implements GcpAttestationTokenSource {
    private static final int MAX_TOKEN_BYTES = 16 * 1024;
    private static final int MAX_DIAGNOSTIC_BYTES = 32 * 1024;
    private static final Pattern AUDIENCE = Pattern.compile(&quot;[^\\p{Cntrl}\\s]{1,2048}&quot;);
    private static final Pattern NONCE = Pattern.compile(&quot;[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,64}&quot;);
    private static final Pattern COMPACT_JWT = Pattern.compile(
            &quot;[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]+\\.[A-Za-z0-9_-]+&quot;);

    private final Path executable;
    private final String teeTechnology;
    private final Path eventLog;
    private final Duration timeout;
    private final CommandExecutor commandExecutor;

    public GoTpmGcpAttestationTokenSource(
            Path executable,
            String teeTechnology,
            Path eventLog,
            Duration timeout) {
        this(executable, teeTechnology, eventLog, timeout, GoTpmGcpAttestationTokenSource::execute);
    }

    GoTpmGcpAttestationTokenSource(
            Path executable,
            String teeTechnology,
            Path eventLog,
            Duration timeout,
            CommandExecutor commandExecutor) {
        this.executable = Objects.requireNonNull(executable, &quot;executable&quot;);
        if (teeTechnology == null
                || (!teeTechnology.isEmpty() &amp;&amp; !&quot;sev-snp&quot;.equals(teeTechnology)
                        &amp;&amp; !&quot;tdx&quot;.equals(teeTechnology))) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;GCA TEE technology must be empty, sev-snp, or tdx.&quot;);
        }
        this.eventLog = Objects.requireNonNull(eventLog, &quot;eventLog&quot;);
        if (!eventLog.isAbsolute()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;GCA event log path must be absolute.&quot;);
        }
        this.teeTechnology = teeTechnology;
        this.timeout = Objects.requireNonNull(timeout, &quot;timeout&quot;);
        if (timeout.isZero() || timeout.isNegative() || timeout.compareTo(Duration.ofMinutes(2)) &gt; 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;GCA token acquisition timeout must be in (0, 2 minutes].&quot;);
        }
        this.commandExecutor = Objects.requireNonNull(commandExecutor, &quot;commandExecutor&quot;);
    }

    @Override
    public String fetch(String audience, String nonce) {
        if (audience == null || !AUDIENCE.matcher(audience).matches()) {
            throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;GCA audience is missing or invalid.&quot;);
        }
        if (nonce == null || !NONCE.matcher(nonce).matches()) {
            throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;GCA nonce is missing or invalid.&quot;);
        }
        List&lt;String&gt; command = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;(List.of(
                executable.toString(),
                &quot;token&quot;,
                &quot;--audience&quot;,
                audience,
                &quot;--custom-nonce&quot;,
                nonce,
                &quot;--event-log&quot;,
                eventLog.toString()));
        if (!teeTechnology.isEmpty()) {
            command.add(&quot;--tee-technology&quot;);
            command.add(teeTechnology);
        }
        String token = commandExecutor.execute(command, timeout);
        if (token == null || !COMPACT_JWT.matcher(token).matches()) {
            throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;Google gotpm did not return a compact attestation JWT.&quot;);
        }
        return token;
    }

    private static String execute(List&lt;String&gt; command, Duration timeout) {
        Process process;
        try {
            process = new ProcessBuilder(command).start();
        } catch (IOException exception) {
            throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;Unable to start Google&apos;s GCA token acquisition tool.&quot;, exception);
        }

        byte[] stdout = null;
        byte[] stderr = null;
        try (var readers = Executors.newVirtualThreadPerTaskExecutor()) {
            var stdoutFuture = readers.submit(() -&gt; readBounded(process.getInputStream(), MAX_TOKEN_BYTES));
            var stderrFuture = readers.submit(() -&gt; readBounded(process.getErrorStream(), MAX_DIAGNOSTIC_BYTES));
            if (!process.waitFor(timeout.toMillis(), TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS)) {
                process.destroyForcibly();
                throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;Google GCA token acquisition timed out.&quot;);
            }
            stdout = stdoutFuture.get();
            stderr = stderrFuture.get();
            if (process.exitValue() != 0) {
                throw new GcpAttestationException(
                        &quot;Google GCA token acquisition failed with exit code &quot; + process.exitValue() + &quot;.&quot;);
            }
            return new String(stdout, StandardCharsets.US_ASCII).trim();
        } catch (InterruptedException exception) {
            process.destroyForcibly();
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;Google GCA token acquisition was interrupted.&quot;, exception);
        } catch (ExecutionException exception) {
            process.destroyForcibly();
            throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;Unable to read Google GCA token acquisition output.&quot;, exception);
        } finally {
            if (process.isAlive()) {
                process.destroyForcibly();
            }
            if (stdout != null) {
                Arrays.fill(stdout, (byte) 0);
            }
            if (stderr != null) {
                Arrays.fill(stderr, (byte) 0);
            }
        }
    }

    private static byte[] readBounded(InputStream input, int maximumBytes) throws IOException {
        try (input; ByteArrayOutputStream output = new ByteArrayOutputStream()) {
            byte[] buffer = new byte[4096];
            try {
                int count;
                while ((count = input.read(buffer)) != -1) {
                    if (output.size() + count &gt; maximumBytes) {
                        throw new IOException(&quot;GCA command output exceeded its configured limit.&quot;);
                    }
                    output.write(buffer, 0, count);
                }
                return output.toByteArray();
            } finally {
                Arrays.fill(buffer, (byte) 0);
            }
        }
    }

    @FunctionalInterface
    interface CommandExecutor {
        String execute(List&lt;String&gt; command, Duration timeout);
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/gcp/JavaGcpHttpTransport.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.gcp;

import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.net.URI;
import java.net.http.HttpClient;
import java.net.http.HttpRequest;
import java.net.http.HttpResponse.BodyHandler;
import java.net.http.HttpResponse.BodySubscriber;
import java.net.http.HttpResponse.ResponseInfo;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.CompletableFuture;
import java.util.concurrent.Flow;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeoutException;

/** Java HTTP transport with redirect denial, endpoint restrictions, and body bounds. */
public final class JavaGcpHttpTransport implements GcpHttpTransport {
    private final HttpClient client;

    public JavaGcpHttpTransport(Duration connectTimeout) {
        client = HttpClient.newBuilder()
                .connectTimeout(connectTimeout)
                .followRedirects(HttpClient.Redirect.NEVER)
                .build();
    }

    @Override
    public GcpHttpTransport.HttpResponse postOrGet(
            String method,
            URI uri,
            Map&lt;String, String&gt; headers,
            byte[] body,
            Duration timeout,
            int maximumResponseBytes) {
        if (uri == null || !&quot;https&quot;.equalsIgnoreCase(uri.getScheme())) {
            throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;External GCP endpoints must use HTTPS.&quot;);
        }
        if (maximumResponseBytes &lt; 1 || timeout == null || timeout.isNegative() || timeout.isZero()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;HTTP response bound and timeout must be positive.&quot;);
        }
        CompletableFuture&lt;?&gt; pending = null;
        try {
            HttpRequest.Builder builder = HttpRequest.newBuilder(uri).timeout(timeout);
            headers.forEach(builder::header);
            if (&quot;GET&quot;.equals(method)) {
                builder.GET();
            } else if (&quot;POST&quot;.equals(method)) {
                builder.POST(HttpRequest.BodyPublishers.ofByteArray(body == null ? new byte[0] : body));
            } else {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Unsupported HTTP method.&quot;);
            }
            var responseFuture = client.sendAsync(
                    builder.build(), boundedBodyHandler(maximumResponseBytes));
            pending = responseFuture;
            var response = responseFuture.get(timeout.toMillis(), TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS);
            byte[] responseBody = response.body();
            try {
                return new GcpHttpTransport.HttpResponse(response.statusCode(), responseBody);
            } finally {
                java.util.Arrays.fill(responseBody, (byte) 0);
            }
        } catch (TimeoutException e) {
            if (pending != null) {
                pending.cancel(true);
            }
            throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;GCP security endpoint request timed out.&quot;, e);
        } catch (InterruptedException e) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;GCP security endpoint request was interrupted.&quot;, e);
        } catch (java.util.concurrent.ExecutionException e) {
            throw new GcpAttestationException(&quot;GCP security endpoint request failed.&quot;, e.getCause());
        }
    }

    private static BodyHandler&lt;byte[]&gt; boundedBodyHandler(int maximumBytes) {
        return (ResponseInfo ignored) -&gt; new BodySubscriber&lt;&gt;() {
            private final CompletableFuture&lt;byte[]&gt; body = new CompletableFuture&lt;&gt;();
            private final WipingByteArrayOutputStream output = new WipingByteArrayOutputStream(
                    Math.min(maximumBytes, 8192));
            private Flow.Subscription subscription;

            @Override
            public java.util.concurrent.CompletionStage&lt;byte[]&gt; getBody() {
                return body;
            }

            @Override
            public void onSubscribe(Flow.Subscription value) {
                subscription = value;
                subscription.request(1);
            }

            @Override
            public void onNext(List&lt;ByteBuffer&gt; buffers) {
                long incoming = buffers.stream().mapToLong(ByteBuffer::remaining).sum();
                if ((long) output.size() + incoming &gt; maximumBytes) {
                    subscription.cancel();
                    output.wipe();
                    body.completeExceptionally(new GcpAttestationException(
                            &quot;GCP security endpoint response exceeded its size limit.&quot;));
                    return;
                }
                for (ByteBuffer buffer : buffers) {
                    byte[] chunk = new byte[buffer.remaining()];
                    buffer.get(chunk);
                    output.write(chunk, 0, chunk.length);
                    java.util.Arrays.fill(chunk, (byte) 0);
                }
                subscription.request(1);
            }

            @Override
            public void onError(Throwable error) {
                output.wipe();
                body.completeExceptionally(error);
            }

            @Override
            public void onComplete() {
                body.complete(output.drain());
            }
        };
    }

    private static final class WipingByteArrayOutputStream extends ByteArrayOutputStream {
        private WipingByteArrayOutputStream(int size) {
            super(size);
        }

        private byte[] drain() {
            byte[] result = toByteArray();
            wipe();
            return result;
        }

        private void wipe() {
            java.util.Arrays.fill(buf, (byte) 0);
            reset();
        }
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/onnx/BertWordPieceTokenizer.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.onnx;

import java.io.BufferedReader;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.text.Normalizer;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.HashMap;
