import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

final class BertWordPieceTokenizer {
    private static final int MAX_WORD_CODE_POINTS = 100;

    private final Map&lt;String, Integer&gt; vocabulary;
    private final TrieNode wordPieceRoot;
    private final int unknownId;
    private final int classificationId;
    private final int separatorId;
    private final int paddingId;
    private final boolean lowercase;

    BertWordPieceTokenizer(
            Path vocabularyPath,
            Path specialTokensPath,
            boolean lowercase,
            ObjectMapper objectMapper) {
        Objects.requireNonNull(vocabularyPath, &quot;vocabularyPath&quot;);
        Objects.requireNonNull(specialTokensPath, &quot;specialTokensPath&quot;);
        Objects.requireNonNull(objectMapper, &quot;objectMapper&quot;);
        Vocabulary loadedVocabulary = loadVocabulary(vocabularyPath);
        this.vocabulary = loadedVocabulary.ids();
        this.wordPieceRoot = loadedVocabulary.root();
        Map&lt;String, String&gt; specialTokens = loadSpecialTokens(specialTokensPath, objectMapper);
        this.unknownId = requireTokenId(specialTokens.get(&quot;unk_token&quot;));
        this.classificationId = requireTokenId(specialTokens.get(&quot;cls_token&quot;));
        this.separatorId = requireTokenId(specialTokens.get(&quot;sep_token&quot;));
        this.paddingId = requireTokenId(specialTokens.get(&quot;pad_token&quot;));
        this.lowercase = lowercase;
    }

    int classificationId() {
        return classificationId;
    }

    int separatorId() {
        return separatorId;
    }

    int paddingId() {
        return paddingId;
    }

    List&lt;Token&gt; tokenize(CharSequence text) {
        Objects.requireNonNull(text, &quot;text&quot;);
        List&lt;Token&gt; tokens = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;();
        int wordStart = -1;
        for (int offset = 0; offset &lt; text.length();) {
            int codePoint = Character.codePointAt(text, offset);
            int nextOffset = offset + Character.charCount(codePoint);
            if (isControl(codePoint) || isWhitespace(codePoint)) {
                if (wordStart &gt;= 0) {
                    tokenizeWord(text, wordStart, offset, tokens);
                    wordStart = -1;
                }
            } else if (isPunctuation(codePoint) || isCjk(codePoint)) {
                if (wordStart &gt;= 0) {
                    tokenizeWord(text, wordStart, offset, tokens);
                    wordStart = -1;
                }
                tokenizeWord(text, offset, nextOffset, tokens);
            } else if (wordStart &lt; 0) {
                wordStart = offset;
            }
            offset = nextOffset;
        }
        if (wordStart &gt;= 0) {
            tokenizeWord(text, wordStart, text.length(), tokens);
        }
        return List.copyOf(tokens);
    }

    private int requireTokenId(String token) {
        Integer id = vocabulary.get(token);
        if (token == null || id == null) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                    &quot;BERT vocabulary does not contain a required special token.&quot;);
        }
        return id;
    }

    private void tokenizeWord(CharSequence text, int start, int end, List&lt;Token&gt; tokens) {
        NormalizedWord word = normalizeWord(text, start, end, lowercase);
        int[] normalized = word.codePoints();

        if (normalized.length == 0) {
            return;
        }
        if (normalized.length &gt; MAX_WORD_CODE_POINTS) {
            tokens.add(new Token(unknownId, start, end));
            return;
        }

        int cursor = 0;
        List&lt;Token&gt; wordPieces = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;();
        while (cursor &lt; normalized.length) {
            TrieNode node = wordPieceRoot;
            if (cursor &gt; 0) {
                node = node.children().get((int) &apos;#&apos;);
                if (node != null) {
                    node = node.children().get((int) &apos;#&apos;);
                }
            }
            int matchEnd = cursor;
            Integer matchId = null;
            for (int offset = cursor; node != null &amp;&amp; offset &lt; normalized.length; offset++) {
                node = node.children().get(normalized[offset]);
                if (node != null &amp;&amp; node.tokenId() != null) {
                    matchId = node.tokenId();
                    matchEnd = offset + 1;
                }
            }
            if (matchId == null || matchEnd == cursor) {
                tokens.add(new Token(unknownId, start, end));
                return;
            }
            wordPieces.add(new Token(
                    matchId,
                    word.starts()[cursor],
                    word.ends()[matchEnd - 1],
                    start,
                    end));
            cursor = matchEnd;
        }
        tokens.addAll(wordPieces);
    }

    private static NormalizedWord normalizeWord(
            CharSequence text, int start, int end, boolean lowercase) {
        int capacity = Math.max(1, end - start);
        int[] codePoints = new int[capacity];
        int[] starts = new int[capacity];
        int[] ends = new int[capacity];
        int size = 0;
        for (int offset = start; offset &lt; end;) {
            int codePoint = Character.codePointAt(text, offset);
            int sourceEnd = offset + Character.charCount(codePoint);
            if (codePoint &lt;= 0x7f) {
                int normalizedCodePoint = lowercase
                        ? Character.toLowerCase(codePoint)
                        : codePoint;
                if (size == codePoints.length) {
                    int newCapacity = Math.max(4, codePoints.length * 2);
                    codePoints = java.util.Arrays.copyOf(codePoints, newCapacity);
                    starts = java.util.Arrays.copyOf(starts, newCapacity);
                    ends = java.util.Arrays.copyOf(ends, newCapacity);
                }
                codePoints[size] = normalizedCodePoint;
                starts[size] = offset;
                ends[size] = sourceEnd;
                size++;
            } else {
                String normalizedCharacter = normalize(codePoint, lowercase);
                for (int normalizedOffset = 0;
                        normalizedOffset &lt; normalizedCharacter.length();) {
                    int normalizedCodePoint = normalizedCharacter.codePointAt(normalizedOffset);
                    normalizedOffset += Character.charCount(normalizedCodePoint);
                    if (size == codePoints.length) {
                        int newCapacity = Math.max(4, codePoints.length * 2);
                        codePoints = java.util.Arrays.copyOf(codePoints, newCapacity);
                        starts = java.util.Arrays.copyOf(starts, newCapacity);
                        ends = java.util.Arrays.copyOf(ends, newCapacity);
                    }
                    codePoints[size] = normalizedCodePoint;
                    starts[size] = offset;
                    ends[size] = sourceEnd;
                    size++;
                }
            }
            offset = sourceEnd;
        }
        return new NormalizedWord(
                java.util.Arrays.copyOf(codePoints, size),
                java.util.Arrays.copyOf(starts, size),
                java.util.Arrays.copyOf(ends, size));
    }

    private static String normalize(int codePoint, boolean lowercase) {
        String value = new String(Character.toChars(codePoint));
        if (lowercase) {
            value = value.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT);
        }
        String decomposed = Normalizer.normalize(value, Normalizer.Form.NFD);
        StringBuilder result = new StringBuilder(decomposed.length());
        decomposed.codePoints()
                .filter(character -&gt; Character.getType(character) != Character.NON_SPACING_MARK)
                .forEach(result::appendCodePoint);
        return result.toString();
    }

    private static Vocabulary loadVocabulary(Path path) {
        if (!Files.isRegularFile(path)) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;Configured BERT vocabulary is missing.&quot;);
        }
        Map&lt;String, Integer&gt; vocabulary = new HashMap&lt;&gt;();
        TrieNode root = new TrieNode();
        try (BufferedReader reader = Files.newBufferedReader(path)) {
            String token;
            int id = 0;
            while ((token = reader.readLine()) != null) {
                if (token.isEmpty() || vocabulary.putIfAbsent(token, id) != null) {
                    throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;BERT vocabulary contains an empty or duplicate token.&quot;);
                }
                TrieNode node = root;
                for (int codePoint : token.codePoints().toArray()) {
                    node = node.children().computeIfAbsent(
                            codePoint, ignored -&gt; new TrieNode());
                }
                node.setTokenId(id);
                id++;
            }
        } catch (IOException exception) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;Unable to read configured BERT vocabulary.&quot;, exception);
        }
        if (vocabulary.isEmpty()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;Configured BERT vocabulary is empty.&quot;);
        }
        return new Vocabulary(Map.copyOf(vocabulary), root);
    }

    private static Map&lt;String, String&gt; loadSpecialTokens(Path path, ObjectMapper objectMapper) {
        if (!Files.isRegularFile(path)) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;Configured BERT special-token map is missing.&quot;);
        }
        try {
            JsonNode root = objectMapper.readTree(path.toFile());
            Map&lt;String, String&gt; tokens = new HashMap&lt;&gt;();
            for (String name : List.of(&quot;unk_token&quot;, &quot;cls_token&quot;, &quot;sep_token&quot;, &quot;pad_token&quot;)) {
                JsonNode token = root.get(name);
                if (token == null || !token.isTextual()) {
                    throw new IllegalStateException(
                            &quot;BERT special-token map is missing a required token.&quot;);
                }
                tokens.put(name, token.textValue());
            }
            return Map.copyOf(tokens);
        } catch (IOException exception) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;Unable to read configured BERT special-token map.&quot;, exception);
        }
    }

    private static boolean isWhitespace(int codePoint) {
        return Character.isWhitespace(codePoint) || Character.isSpaceChar(codePoint);
    }

    private static boolean isControl(int codePoint) {
        int type = Character.getType(codePoint);
        return (type == Character.CONTROL &amp;&amp; codePoint != &apos;\t&apos; &amp;&amp; codePoint != &apos;\n&apos; &amp;&amp; codePoint != &apos;\r&apos;)
                || type == Character.FORMAT;
    }

    private static boolean isPunctuation(int codePoint) {
        int type = Character.getType(codePoint);
        return type == Character.CONNECTOR_PUNCTUATION
                || type == Character.DASH_PUNCTUATION
                || type == Character.START_PUNCTUATION
                || type == Character.END_PUNCTUATION
                || type == Character.INITIAL_QUOTE_PUNCTUATION
                || type == Character.FINAL_QUOTE_PUNCTUATION
                || type == Character.OTHER_PUNCTUATION;
    }

    private static boolean isCjk(int codePoint) {
        return (codePoint &gt;= 0x4E00 &amp;&amp; codePoint &lt;= 0x9FFF)
                || (codePoint &gt;= 0x3400 &amp;&amp; codePoint &lt;= 0x4DBF)
                || (codePoint &gt;= 0x20000 &amp;&amp; codePoint &lt;= 0x2A6DF)
                || (codePoint &gt;= 0x2A700 &amp;&amp; codePoint &lt;= 0x2B73F)
                || (codePoint &gt;= 0x2B740 &amp;&amp; codePoint &lt;= 0x2B81F)
                || (codePoint &gt;= 0x2B820 &amp;&amp; codePoint &lt;= 0x2CEAF)
                || (codePoint &gt;= 0xF900 &amp;&amp; codePoint &lt;= 0xFAFF)
                || (codePoint &gt;= 0x2F800 &amp;&amp; codePoint &lt;= 0x2FA1F);
    }

    record Token(long id, int start, int end, int wordStart, int wordEnd) {
        Token(long id, int start, int end) {
            this(id, start, end, start, end);
        }
    }

    private record Vocabulary(Map&lt;String, Integer&gt; ids, TrieNode root) {
    }

    private record NormalizedWord(int[] codePoints, int[] starts, int[] ends) {
    }

    private static final class TrieNode {
        private final Map&lt;Integer, TrieNode&gt; children = new HashMap&lt;&gt;();
        private Integer tokenId;

        private Map&lt;Integer, TrieNode&gt; children() {
            return children;
        }

        private Integer tokenId() {
            return tokenId;
        }

        private void setTokenId(int tokenId) {
            this.tokenId = tokenId;
        }
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/onnx/OnnxPiiDetector.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.onnx;

import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.nio.ByteOrder;
import java.nio.LongBuffer;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.TreeMap;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean;

import ai.onnxruntime.NodeInfo;
import ai.onnxruntime.OnnxTensor;
import ai.onnxruntime.OnnxValue;
import ai.onnxruntime.OrtException;
import ai.onnxruntime.OrtSession;
import ai.onnxruntime.TensorInfo;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.DetectedEntity;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.PiiDetector;

public final class OnnxPiiDetector implements PiiDetector, AutoCloseable {
    private final OrtSession session;
    private final BertWordPieceTokenizer tokenizer;
    private final String inputIdsName;
    private final String attentionMaskName;
    private final String tokenTypeIdsName;
    private final String logitsName;
    private final Map&lt;Long, EntityLabel&gt; labels;
    private final int maximumSequenceLength;
    private final int tensorSequenceLength;
    private final int labelCount;
    private final AtomicBoolean closed = new AtomicBoolean();

    OnnxPiiDetector(
            OrtSession session,
            BertWordPieceTokenizer tokenizer,
            String inputIdsName,
            String attentionMaskName,
            String tokenTypeIdsName,
            String logitsName,
            Map&lt;Long, EntityLabel&gt; labels,
            int maximumSequenceLength,
            int tensorSequenceLength,
            int labelCount) {
        this.session = Objects.requireNonNull(session, &quot;session&quot;);
        this.tokenizer = Objects.requireNonNull(tokenizer, &quot;tokenizer&quot;);
        this.inputIdsName = requireName(inputIdsName, &quot;input IDs&quot;);
        this.attentionMaskName = requireName(attentionMaskName, &quot;attention mask&quot;);
        this.tokenTypeIdsName = tokenTypeIdsName;
        this.logitsName = requireName(logitsName, &quot;logits&quot;);
        if (this.inputIdsName.equals(this.attentionMaskName)
                || this.inputIdsName.equals(this.logitsName)
                || this.attentionMaskName.equals(this.logitsName)
                || this.inputIdsName.equals(this.tokenTypeIdsName)
                || this.attentionMaskName.equals(this.tokenTypeIdsName)
                || this.logitsName.equals(this.tokenTypeIdsName)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;ONNX input and output tensor names must be distinct.&quot;);
        }
        this.labels = Map.copyOf(labels);
        if (maximumSequenceLength &lt; 3
                || (tensorSequenceLength &gt; 0 &amp;&amp; tensorSequenceLength &lt; maximumSequenceLength)
                || labelCount &lt; 1
                || this.labels.isEmpty()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;ONNX sequence and label configuration is invalid.&quot;);
        }
        this.maximumSequenceLength = maximumSequenceLength;
        this.tensorSequenceLength = tensorSequenceLength;
        this.labelCount = labelCount;
    }

    public static OnnxPiiDetector open(
            OnnxPiiModelArtifactVerifier.VerifiedModel model,
            java.nio.file.Path vocabularyPath,
            java.nio.file.Path specialTokensPath,
            boolean lowercase,
            String inputIdsName,
            String attentionMaskName,
            String logitsName,
            String labelJson,
            int maximumSequenceLength,
            ObjectMapper objectMapper) {
        Objects.requireNonNull(model, &quot;model&quot;);
        Map&lt;Long, EntityLabel&gt; labels = parseLabels(labelJson, objectMapper);
        try {
            ai.onnxruntime.OrtEnvironment environment =
                    ai.onnxruntime.OrtEnvironment.getEnvironment();
            OrtSession.SessionOptions options = new OrtSession.SessionOptions();
            OrtSession session;
            try {
                session = environment.createSession(model.path().toString(), options);
            } finally {
                options.close();
            }

            try {
                ModelContract contract = validateModelContract(
                        session,
                        inputIdsName,
                        attentionMaskName,
                        logitsName,
                        maximumSequenceLength,
                        labels.size());
                BertWordPieceTokenizer tokenizer = new BertWordPieceTokenizer(
                        vocabularyPath, specialTokensPath, lowercase, objectMapper);
                return new OnnxPiiDetector(
                        session,
                        tokenizer,
                        inputIdsName,
                        attentionMaskName,
                        contract.tokenTypeIdsName(),
                        logitsName,
                        labels,
                        maximumSequenceLength,
                        contract.tensorSequenceLength(),
                        labels.size());
            } catch (RuntimeException exception) {
                session.close();
                throw exception;
            }
        } catch (OrtException exception) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;Unable to initialize the configured ONNX PII model.&quot;, exception);
        }
    }

    @Override
    public String version() {
        return &quot;onnx-token-classification-1&quot;;
    }

    @Override
    public List&lt;DetectedEntity&gt; detect(CharSequence text) {
        if (text == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Text must not be null.&quot;);
        }
        if (closed.get()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;ONNX PII detector is closed.&quot;);
        }
        List&lt;BertWordPieceTokenizer.Token&gt; tokens = tokenizer.tokenize(text);
        if (tokens.isEmpty()) {
            return List.of();
        }

        List&lt;DetectedEntity&gt; detected = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;();
        int contentLimit = maximumSequenceLength - 2;
        int overlap = Math.min(64, contentLimit / 4);
        int step = Math.max(1, contentLimit - overlap);
        for (int windowStart = 0; windowStart &lt; tokens.size(); windowStart += step) {
            int windowEnd = Math.min(tokens.size(), windowStart + contentLimit);
            inferWindow(tokens, windowStart, windowEnd, detected);
        }
        return mergeOverlappingEntities(detected);
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        if (closed.compareAndSet(false, true)) {
            try {
                session.close();
            } catch (OrtException exception) {
                throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;Unable to close ONNX PII model session.&quot;, exception);
            }
        }
    }

    private void inferWindow(
            List&lt;BertWordPieceTokenizer.Token&gt; tokens,
            int start,
            int end,
            List&lt;DetectedEntity&gt; detected) {
        int sequenceLength = end - start + 2;
        int tensorLength = tensorSequenceLength &gt; 0
                ? tensorSequenceLength
                : sequenceLength;
        int[] predictedLabels;
        try (DirectLongInput ids = new DirectLongInput(tensorLength);
                DirectLongInput attentionMask = new DirectLongInput(tensorLength);
                DirectLongInput tokenTypeIds = tokenTypeIdsName == null
                        ? null
                        : new DirectLongInput(tensorLength)) {
            ids.fill(tokenizer.paddingId());
            if (tokenTypeIds != null) {
                tokenTypeIds.fill(0);
            }
            ids.put(0, tokenizer.classificationId());
            for (int index = 0; index &lt; sequenceLength; index++) {
                attentionMask.put(index, 1);
            }
            for (int index = start; index &lt; end; index++) {
                ids.put(index - start + 1, tokens.get(index).id());
            }
            ids.put(sequenceLength - 1, tokenizer.separatorId());
            Map&lt;String, OnnxTensor&gt; inputs = new HashMap&lt;&gt;();
            inputs.put(inputIdsName, ids.tensor());
            inputs.put(attentionMaskName, attentionMask.tensor());
            if (tokenTypeIds != null) {
                inputs.put(tokenTypeIdsName, tokenTypeIds.tensor());
            }
            try (OrtSession.Result result = session.run(inputs)) {
                OnnxValue value = result.get(logitsName)
                        .orElseThrow(() -&gt; new IllegalStateException(
                                &quot;Configured ONNX session did not return the logits output.&quot;));
                if (!(value instanceof OnnxTensor logits)) {
                    throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;ONNX logits output is not a tensor.&quot;);
                }
                predictedLabels = readPredictedLabels(logits, tensorLength);
            } catch (OrtException exception) {
                throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;ONNX PII inference failed.&quot;, exception);
            }
        }
        try {
            collectWindowEntities(tokens, start, end, predictedLabels, detected);
        } finally {
            java.util.Arrays.fill(predictedLabels, -1);
        }
    }

    private int[] readPredictedLabels(OnnxTensor logits, int sequenceLength) {
        TensorInfo info = logits.getInfo();
        long[] shape = info.getShape();
        if (info.type != ai.onnxruntime.OnnxJavaType.FLOAT
                || shape.length != 3
                || shape[0] != 1
                || shape[1] != sequenceLength
                || shape[2] != labelCount) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;ONNX logits tensor does not match the configured output contract.&quot;);
        }
        java.nio.FloatBuffer values = logits.getFloatBuffer();
        int[] predictions = new int[sequenceLength];
        for (int token = 0; token &lt; sequenceLength; token++) {
            int bestLabel = 0;
            float bestScore = Float.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
            int rowOffset = token * labelCount;
            for (int label = 0; label &lt; labelCount; label++) {
                float score = values.get(rowOffset + label);
                if (!Float.isFinite(score)) {
                    throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;ONNX logits contain a non-finite value.&quot;);
                }
                if (score &gt; bestScore) {
                    bestScore = score;
                    bestLabel = label;
                }
            }
            predictions[token] = bestLabel;
        }
        return predictions;
    }

    private void collectWindowEntities(
            List&lt;BertWordPieceTokenizer.Token&gt; tokens,
            int start,
            int end,
            int[] predictions,
            List&lt;DetectedEntity&gt; output) {
        String activeType = null;
        int entityStart = -1;
        int entityEnd = -1;
        for (int index = 0; index &lt; end - start; index++) {
            EntityLabel label = labels.get((long) predictions[index + 1]);
            if (label == null || label.type() == null) {
                if (activeType != null) {
                    output.add(new DetectedEntity(activeType, entityStart, entityEnd));
                    activeType = null;
                }
                continue;
            }

            BertWordPieceTokenizer.Token token = tokens.get(start + index);
            int tokenStart = token.wordStart();
            int tokenEnd = token.wordEnd();
            boolean continuation = label.continuation()
                    &amp;&amp; label.type().equals(activeType);
            if (continuation) {
                entityEnd = Math.max(entityEnd, tokenEnd);
                continue;
            }
            if (activeType != null) {
                output.add(new DetectedEntity(activeType, entityStart, entityEnd));
            }
            activeType = label.type();
            entityStart = tokenStart;
            entityEnd = tokenEnd;
        }
        if (activeType != null) {
            output.add(new DetectedEntity(activeType, entityStart, entityEnd));
        }
    }

    private static List&lt;DetectedEntity&gt; mergeOverlappingEntities(List&lt;DetectedEntity&gt; entities) {
        entities.sort(Comparator.comparingInt(DetectedEntity::start)
                .thenComparingInt(DetectedEntity::end));
        List&lt;DetectedEntity&gt; merged = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;();
        for (DetectedEntity entity : entities) {
            if (!merged.isEmpty()) {
                DetectedEntity previous = merged.get(merged.size() - 1);
                if (entity.start() &lt; previous.end()) {
                    merged.set(merged.size() - 1, new DetectedEntity(
                            previous.type(), previous.start(), Math.max(previous.end(), entity.end())));
                    continue;
                }
            }
            merged.add(entity);
        }
        return List.copyOf(merged);
    }

    private static Map&lt;Long, EntityLabel&gt; parseLabels(String json, ObjectMapper objectMapper) {
        Objects.requireNonNull(json, &quot;labelJson&quot;);
        Objects.requireNonNull(objectMapper, &quot;objectMapper&quot;);
        try {
            JsonNode root = objectMapper.readTree(json);
            if (root == null || !root.isObject() || root.isEmpty()) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;ONNX label mapping must be a non-empty JSON object.&quot;);
            }
            Map&lt;Long, EntityLabel&gt; labels = new TreeMap&lt;&gt;();
            for (var entry = root.fields(); entry.hasNext();) {
                Map.Entry&lt;String, JsonNode&gt; field = entry.next();
                long id;
                try {
                    id = Long.parseLong(field.getKey());
                } catch (NumberFormatException exception) {
                    throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;ONNX label IDs must be non-negative integers.&quot;);
                }
                if (id &lt; 0 || !field.getValue().isTextual()) {
                    throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;ONNX label mapping has an invalid entry.&quot;);
                }
                String label = field.getValue().textValue();
                EntityLabel parsed;
                if (&quot;O&quot;.equals(label)) {
                    parsed = new EntityLabel(null, false);
                } else if (label.matches(&quot;[BI]-[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]{0,63}&quot;)) {
                    parsed = new EntityLabel(label.substring(2), label.charAt(0) == &apos;I&apos;);
                } else {
                    throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;ONNX labels must use O or BIO entity labels.&quot;);
                }
                if (labels.putIfAbsent(id, parsed) != null) {
                    throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;ONNX label mapping contains duplicate IDs.&quot;);
                }
            }
            long expected = 0;
            for (long id : labels.keySet()) {
                if (id != expected++) {
                    throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;ONNX label IDs must be contiguous from zero.&quot;);
                }
            }
            return Map.copyOf(labels);
        } catch (java.io.IOException exception) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;ONNX label mapping is not valid JSON.&quot;, exception);
        }
    }

    private static ModelContract validateModelContract(
            OrtSession session,
            String idsName,
            String maskName,
            String logitsName,
            int maximumSequenceLength,
            int labelCount) {
        try {
            Map&lt;String, NodeInfo&gt; inputs = session.getInputInfo();
            String tokenTypeIdsName = inputs.containsKey(&quot;token_type_ids&quot;)
                    ? &quot;token_type_ids&quot;
                    : null;
            if (inputs.size() != (tokenTypeIdsName == null ? 2 : 3)
                    || !inputs.containsKey(idsName)
                    || !inputs.containsKey(maskName)
                    || (tokenTypeIdsName != null
                            &amp;&amp; (tokenTypeIdsName.equals(idsName) || tokenTypeIdsName.equals(maskName)))) {
                throw new IllegalStateException(
                        &quot;ONNX model inputs must be input_ids and attention_mask, with optional token_type_ids.&quot;);
            }
            TensorInfo ids = requireTensorInfo(inputs, idsName);
            TensorInfo mask = requireTensorInfo(inputs, maskName);
            TensorInfo tokenTypeIds = tokenTypeIdsName == null
                    ? null
                    : requireTensorInfo(inputs, tokenTypeIdsName);
            if (ids.type != ai.onnxruntime.OnnxJavaType.INT64
                    || mask.type != ai.onnxruntime.OnnxJavaType.INT64
                    || ids.getShape().length != 2
                    || mask.getShape().length != 2
                    || !supportsSingleBatch(ids.getShape()[0])
                    || !supportsSingleBatch(mask.getShape()[0])
                    || (tokenTypeIds != null
                            &amp;&amp; (tokenTypeIds.type != ai.onnxruntime.OnnxJavaType.INT64
                                    || tokenTypeIds.getShape().length != 2
                                    || !supportsSingleBatch(tokenTypeIds.getShape()[0])))) {
                throw new IllegalStateException(
                        &quot;ONNX model must accept rank-two INT64 input_ids, attention_mask, and optional token_type_ids tensors.&quot;);
            }
            TensorInfo logits = requireTensorInfo(session.getOutputInfo(), logitsName);
            long[] outputShape = logits.getShape();
            if (logits.type != ai.onnxruntime.OnnxJavaType.FLOAT
                    || outputShape.length != 3
                    || !supportsSingleBatch(outputShape[0])
                    || (outputShape[2] &gt; 0 &amp;&amp; outputShape[2] != labelCount)) {
                throw new IllegalStateException(
                        &quot;ONNX model output must be FLOAT32 [batch, sequence, configured labels].&quot;);
            }
            validateSequenceDimension(ids.getShape()[1], maximumSequenceLength);
            validateSequenceDimension(mask.getShape()[1], maximumSequenceLength);
            if (tokenTypeIds != null) {
                validateSequenceDimension(tokenTypeIds.getShape()[1], maximumSequenceLength);
            }
            validateSequenceDimension(outputShape[1], maximumSequenceLength);
            long idsLength = ids.getShape()[1];
            long maskLength = mask.getShape()[1];
            long tokenTypeIdsLength = tokenTypeIds == null
                    ? 0
                    : tokenTypeIds.getShape()[1];
            long outputLength = outputShape[1];
            if ((idsLength &gt; 0 &amp;&amp; maskLength &gt; 0 &amp;&amp; idsLength != maskLength)
                    || (idsLength &gt; 0 &amp;&amp; tokenTypeIdsLength &gt; 0 &amp;&amp; idsLength != tokenTypeIdsLength)
                    || (maskLength &gt; 0 &amp;&amp; tokenTypeIdsLength &gt; 0 &amp;&amp; maskLength != tokenTypeIdsLength)
                    || (idsLength &gt; 0 &amp;&amp; outputLength &gt; 0 &amp;&amp; idsLength != outputLength)
                    || (maskLength &gt; 0 &amp;&amp; outputLength &gt; 0 &amp;&amp; maskLength != outputLength)
                    || (tokenTypeIdsLength &gt; 0
                            &amp;&amp; outputLength &gt; 0
                            &amp;&amp; tokenTypeIdsLength != outputLength)) {
                throw new IllegalStateException(
                        &quot;ONNX input and output sequence dimensions do not match.&quot;);
            }
            long fixedSequenceLength = idsLength &gt; 0
                    ? idsLength
                    : maskLength &gt; 0
                            ? maskLength
                            : tokenTypeIdsLength &gt; 0 ? tokenTypeIdsLength : outputLength;
            return new ModelContract(
                    fixedSequenceLength &gt; 0 ? Math.toIntExact(fixedSequenceLength) : 0,
                    tokenTypeIdsName);
        } catch (OrtException exception) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;Unable to inspect ONNX model tensor metadata.&quot;, exception);
        }
    }

    private static TensorInfo requireTensorInfo(Map&lt;String, NodeInfo&gt; nodes, String name) {
        NodeInfo node = nodes.get(name);
        if (node == null || !(node.getInfo() instanceof TensorInfo tensorInfo)) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;ONNX model is missing configured tensor: &quot; + name);
        }
        return tensorInfo;
    }

    private static void validateSequenceDimension(long dimension, int maximumSequenceLength) {
        if (dimension &gt; 0 &amp;&amp; dimension &lt; maximumSequenceLength) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                    &quot;Configured ONNX maximum sequence length exceeds the model tensor dimension.&quot;);
        }
    }

    private static boolean supportsSingleBatch(long dimension) {
        return dimension &lt;= 0 || dimension == 1;
    }

    private static String requireName(String name, String label) {
        if (name == null || name.isBlank()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;ONNX &quot; + label + &quot; tensor name must not be blank.&quot;);
        }
        return name;
    }

    record EntityLabel(String type, boolean continuation) {
    }

    private record ModelContract(int tensorSequenceLength, String tokenTypeIdsName) {
    }

    private static final class DirectLongInput implements AutoCloseable {
        private final ByteBuffer bytes;
        private final LongBuffer values;
        private final OnnxTensor tensor;

        private DirectLongInput(int length) {
            bytes = ByteBuffer.allocateDirect(Math.multiplyExact(length, Long.BYTES))
                    .order(ByteOrder.nativeOrder());
            values = bytes.asLongBuffer();
            try {
                tensor = OnnxTensor.createTensor(
                        ai.onnxruntime.OrtEnvironment.getEnvironment(),
                        values,
                        new long[] {1, length});
            } catch (OrtException exception) {
                for (int index = 0; index &lt; values.capacity(); index++) {
                    values.put(index, 0L);
                }
                throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;Unable to allocate ONNX input tensor.&quot;, exception);
            }
        }

        private void put(int index, long value) {
            values.put(index, value);
        }

        private void fill(long value) {
            for (int index = 0; index &lt; values.capacity(); index++) {
                values.put(index, value);
            }
        }

        private OnnxTensor tensor() {
            return tensor;
        }

        @Override
        public void close() {
            try {
                tensor.close();
            } finally {
                for (int index = 0; index &lt; values.capacity(); index++) {
                    values.put(index, 0L);
                }
                bytes.clear();
                while (bytes.hasRemaining()) {
                    bytes.put((byte) 0);
                }
            }
        }
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/onnx/OnnxPiiModelArtifactVerifier.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.onnx;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.channels.FileChannel;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.nio.file.StandardOpenOption;
import java.security.MessageDigest;
import java.security.NoSuchAlgorithmException;
import java.util.HexFormat;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Objects;

import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.ConfidentialSegment;

public final class OnnxPiiModelArtifactVerifier {
    private static final int SCAN_CHUNK_BYTES = 1024 * 1024;

    private OnnxPiiModelArtifactVerifier() {
    }

    public static VerifiedModel verify(Path modelPath, String expectedSha256) {
        Objects.requireNonNull(modelPath, &quot;modelPath&quot;);
        if (expectedSha256 == null || !expectedSha256.matches(&quot;[0-9a-fA-F]{64}&quot;)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Expected ONNX model SHA-256 must be 64 hex characters.&quot;);
        }

        Path normalizedPath = modelPath.toAbsolutePath().normalize();
        if (!Files.isRegularFile(normalizedPath)) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;Configured ONNX model is missing or not a regular file.&quot;);
        }

        try {
            long fileSize = Files.size(normalizedPath);
            if (fileSize == 0) {
                throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;Configured ONNX model is empty.&quot;);
            }
            MessageDigest digest = MessageDigest.getInstance(&quot;SHA-256&quot;);
            try (FileChannel file = FileChannel.open(normalizedPath, StandardOpenOption.READ);
                    ConfidentialSegment chunk =
                            ConfidentialSegment.allocate(SCAN_CHUNK_BYTES)) {
                long fileOffset = 0;
                while (fileOffset &lt; fileSize) {
                    int remaining = (int) Math.min(SCAN_CHUNK_BYTES, fileSize - fileOffset);
                    int chunkOffset = 0;
                    while (chunkOffset &lt; remaining) {
                        int bytesRead = chunk.readFrom(
                                file,
                                fileOffset + chunkOffset,
                                chunkOffset,
                                remaining - chunkOffset);
                        if (bytesRead &lt;= 0) {
                            throw new IOException(&quot;Model file ended while computing its digest.&quot;);
                        }
                        chunkOffset += bytesRead;
                    }
                    chunk.updateDigest(digest, 0, remaining);
                    chunk.zeroize();
                    fileOffset += remaining;
                }
            }

            byte[] actualDigest = digest.digest();
            byte[] expectedDigest = HexFormat.of().parseHex(expectedSha256);
            try {
                if (!MessageDigest.isEqual(actualDigest, expectedDigest)) {
                    throw new IllegalStateException(
                            &quot;Configured ONNX model SHA-256 does not match the pinned digest.&quot;);
                }
            } finally {
                java.util.Arrays.fill(actualDigest, (byte) 0);
                java.util.Arrays.fill(expectedDigest, (byte) 0);
            }
            return new VerifiedModel(
                    normalizedPath, expectedSha256.toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT), fileSize);
        } catch (NoSuchAlgorithmException exception) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;SHA-256 is unavailable in this Java runtime.&quot;, exception);
        } catch (IOException exception) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;Unable to read configured ONNX model.&quot;, exception);
        }
    }

    public record VerifiedModel(Path path, String sha256, long sizeBytes) {
        public VerifiedModel {
            Objects.requireNonNull(path, &quot;path&quot;);
            Objects.requireNonNull(sha256, &quot;sha256&quot;);
            if (sizeBytes &lt; 1) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Verified ONNX model must not be empty.&quot;);
            }
        }
    }

}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/AesGcmSecureContextCodec.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway;

import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.security.GeneralSecurityException;
import java.security.SecureRandom;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.Optional;

import javax.crypto.Cipher;
import javax.crypto.spec.GCMParameterSpec;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.ConfidentialSegment;

public final class AesGcmSecureContextCodec {
    private static final String VERSION = &quot;v2&quot;;
    private static final int NONCE_BYTES = 12;
    private static final int TAG_BITS = 128;
    private static final int MAX_CONTEXT_LENGTH = 2_000_000;
    private static final int MAX_MAPPING_COUNT = 10_000;
    private static final int MAX_TOKEN_BYTES = 512;
    private static final long HEADER_BYTES = Long.BYTES + Integer.BYTES;
    private static final long MAPPING_HEADER_BYTES = 2L * Integer.BYTES;
    private static final Base64.Encoder ENCODER = Base64.getUrlEncoder().withoutPadding();
    private static final Base64.Decoder DECODER = Base64.getUrlDecoder();

    private final SecureContextKeyProvider keyProvider;
    private final SecureRandom secureRandom;
    private final Clock clock;

    public AesGcmSecureContextCodec(
            SecureContextKeyProvider keyProvider,
            ObjectMapper objectMapper,
            SecureRandom secureRandom,
            Clock clock) {
        this.keyProvider = Objects.requireNonNull(keyProvider, &quot;keyProvider&quot;);
        Objects.requireNonNull(objectMapper, &quot;objectMapper&quot;);
        this.secureRandom = Objects.requireNonNull(secureRandom, &quot;secureRandom&quot;);
        this.clock = Objects.requireNonNull(clock, &quot;clock&quot;);
    }

    public String sealNative(
            String tenantId,
            Map&lt;String, ConfidentialSegment&gt; tokenMappings,
            Instant expiresAt) {
        validateTenantId(tenantId);
        if (tokenMappings == null || expiresAt == null || !expiresAt.isAfter(clock.instant())) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Secure context parameters are invalid.&quot;);
        }
        if (tokenMappings.size() &gt; MAX_MAPPING_COUNT) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Secure context has too many mappings.&quot;);
        }

        long plaintextSize = HEADER_BYTES;
        for (Map.Entry&lt;String, ConfidentialSegment&gt; entry : tokenMappings.entrySet()) {
            String token = Objects.requireNonNull(entry.getKey(), &quot;token&quot;);
            ConfidentialSegment value = Objects.requireNonNull(entry.getValue(), &quot;value&quot;);
            long tokenLength = ConfidentialSegment.utf8Length(token);
            if (tokenLength == 0 || tokenLength &gt; MAX_TOKEN_BYTES) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Secure context token is invalid.&quot;);
            }
            long entrySize = MAPPING_HEADER_BYTES + tokenLength + value.byteSize();
            if (entrySize &gt; MAX_CONTEXT_LENGTH - plaintextSize) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Secure context exceeds the supported size.&quot;);
            }
            plaintextSize += entrySize;
        }

        byte[] nonce = new byte[NONCE_BYTES];
        try (ConfidentialSegment plaintext = ConfidentialSegment.allocate(plaintextSize);
                ConfidentialSegment ciphertext =
                        ConfidentialSegment.allocate(plaintextSize + TAG_BITS / Byte.SIZE)) {
            SecureContextKey key = keyProvider.currentKey();
            secureRandom.nextBytes(nonce);
            plaintext.writeLong(0, expiresAt.toEpochMilli());
            plaintext.writeInt(Long.BYTES, tokenMappings.size());
            long offset = HEADER_BYTES;
            for (Map.Entry&lt;String, ConfidentialSegment&gt; entry : tokenMappings.entrySet()) {
                byte[] tokenBytes = entry.getKey().getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
                ConfidentialSegment value = entry.getValue();
                try {
                    plaintext.writeInt(offset, tokenBytes.length);
                    plaintext.writeInt(
                            offset + Integer.BYTES, Math.toIntExact(value.byteSize()));
                    offset += MAPPING_HEADER_BYTES;
                    writeBytes(plaintext, offset, tokenBytes);
                    offset += tokenBytes.length;
                    value.copyTo(plaintext, 0, offset, value.byteSize());
                    offset += value.byteSize();
                } finally {
                    Arrays.fill(tokenBytes, (byte) 0);
                }
            }

            Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(&quot;AES/GCM/NoPadding&quot;);
            cipher.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, key.key(), new GCMParameterSpec(TAG_BITS, nonce));
            cipher.updateAAD(aad(tenantId, key.id()));
            ByteBuffer output = ciphertext.memory().asByteBuffer();
            int encryptedLength = cipher.doFinal(
                    plaintext.memory().asByteBuffer(), output);
            ciphertext.truncate(encryptedLength);
            byte[] encryptedBytes = ciphertext.memory().toArray(
                    java.lang.foreign.ValueLayout.JAVA_BYTE);
            try {
                String secureContext = String.join(
                        &quot;.&quot;,
                        VERSION,
                        key.id(),
                        ENCODER.encodeToString(nonce),
                        ENCODER.encodeToString(encryptedBytes));
                if (secureContext.length() &gt; MAX_CONTEXT_LENGTH) {
                    throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                            &quot;Secure context exceeds the supported size.&quot;);
                }
                return secureContext;
            } finally {
                Arrays.fill(encryptedBytes, (byte) 0);
            }
        } catch (GeneralSecurityException | RuntimeException e) {
            if (e instanceof IllegalArgumentException argumentException) {
                throw argumentException;
            }
            throw new SecureContextException();
        } finally {
            Arrays.fill(nonce, (byte) 0);
        }
    }

    public SecureContextMappings openNative(String tenantId, String secureContext) {
        validateTenantId(tenantId);
        ConfidentialSegment encryptedSegment = null;
        ConfidentialSegment plaintext = null;
        try {
            if (secureContext == null || secureContext.length() &gt; MAX_CONTEXT_LENGTH) {
                throw new SecureContextException();
            }
            String[] parts = secureContext.split(&quot;\\.&quot;, -1);
            if (parts.length != 4 || !VERSION.equals(parts[0])) {
                throw new SecureContextException();
            }

            Optional&lt;SecureContextKey&gt; key = keyProvider.findKey(parts[1]);
            if (key.isEmpty()) {
                throw new SecureContextException();
            }

            byte[] nonce = DECODER.decode(parts[2]);
            byte[] ciphertext = DECODER.decode(parts[3]);
            try {
                if (nonce.length != NONCE_BYTES || ciphertext.length &lt;= TAG_BITS / 8) {
                    throw new SecureContextException();
                }

                encryptedSegment = ConfidentialSegment.allocate(ciphertext.length);
                writeBytes(encryptedSegment, 0, ciphertext);
                plaintext = ConfidentialSegment.allocate(
                        encryptedSegment.byteSize() - TAG_BITS / Byte.SIZE);
                Cipher cipher = Cipher.getInstance(&quot;AES/GCM/NoPadding&quot;);
                cipher.init(
                        Cipher.DECRYPT_MODE,
                        key.get().key(),
                        new GCMParameterSpec(TAG_BITS, nonce));
                cipher.updateAAD(aad(tenantId, parts[1]));
                int plaintextLength = cipher.doFinal(
                        encryptedSegment.memory().asByteBuffer(),
                        plaintext.memory().asByteBuffer());
                plaintext.truncate(plaintextLength);
                SecureContextMappings mappings = parseMappings(plaintext);
                plaintext = null;
                return mappings;
            } finally {
                Arrays.fill(nonce, (byte) 0);
                Arrays.fill(ciphertext, (byte) 0);
            }
        } catch (GeneralSecurityException | RuntimeException e) {
            throw new SecureContextException();
        } finally {
            if (encryptedSegment != null) {
                encryptedSegment.close();
            }
            if (plaintext != null) {
                plaintext.close();
            }
        }
    }

    private SecureContextMappings parseMappings(ConfidentialSegment plaintext) {
        if (plaintext.byteSize() &lt; HEADER_BYTES) {
            throw new SecureContextException();
        }
        long expiresAtEpochMillis = plaintext.readLong(0);
        int count = plaintext.readInt(Long.BYTES);
        if (count &lt; 0 || count &gt; MAX_MAPPING_COUNT) {
            throw new SecureContextException();
        }
        Map&lt;String, ConfidentialSegment&gt; mappings = new LinkedHashMap&lt;&gt;();
        long offset = HEADER_BYTES;
        try {
            for (int index = 0; index &lt; count; index++) {
                requireRange(offset, MAPPING_HEADER_BYTES, plaintext.byteSize());
                int tokenLength = plaintext.readInt(offset);
                int valueLength = plaintext.readInt(offset + Integer.BYTES);
                offset += MAPPING_HEADER_BYTES;
                if (tokenLength &lt; 1 || tokenLength &gt; MAX_TOKEN_BYTES || valueLength &lt; 0) {
                    throw new SecureContextException();
                }
                requireRange(offset, (long) tokenLength + valueLength, plaintext.byteSize());
                String token = readToken(plaintext, offset, tokenLength);
                offset += tokenLength;
                ConfidentialSegment value = plaintext.slice(offset, valueLength);
                if (mappings.putIfAbsent(token, value) != null) {
                    value.close();
                    throw new SecureContextException();
                }
                offset += valueLength;
            }
            if (offset != plaintext.byteSize()
                    || !Instant.ofEpochMilli(expiresAtEpochMillis).isAfter(clock.instant())) {
                throw new SecureContextException();
            }
            return new SecureContextMappings(plaintext, mappings);
        } catch (RuntimeException failure) {
            closeMappings(mappings);
            throw failure;
        }
    }

    private static String readToken(
            ConfidentialSegment segment, long offset, int length) {
        byte[] bytes = new byte[length];
        try {
            for (int index = 0; index &lt; length; index++) {
                bytes[index] = segment.readByte(offset + index);
            }
            return StandardCharsets.UTF_8.newDecoder().decode(ByteBuffer.wrap(bytes)).toString();
        } catch (java.nio.charset.CharacterCodingException failure) {
            throw new SecureContextException();
        } finally {
            Arrays.fill(bytes, (byte) 0);
        }
    }

    private static void writeBytes(
            ConfidentialSegment destination, long offset, byte[] bytes) {
        for (int index = 0; index &lt; bytes.length; index++) {
            destination.writeByte(offset + index, bytes[index]);
        }
    }

    private static void requireRange(long offset, long length, long capacity) {
        if (offset &lt; 0 || length &lt; 0 || offset &gt; capacity - length) {
            throw new SecureContextException();
        }
    }

    private static void closeMappings(Map&lt;String, ConfidentialSegment&gt; mappings) {
        RuntimeException failure = null;
        for (ConfidentialSegment segment : mappings.values()) {
            try {
                segment.close();
            } catch (RuntimeException closeFailure) {
                if (failure == null) {
                    failure = closeFailure;
                } else {
                    failure.addSuppressed(closeFailure);
                }
            }
        }
        if (failure != null) {
            throw failure;
        }
    }

    private static byte[] aad(String tenantId, String keyId) {
        return (VERSION + &quot;\u0000&quot; + tenantId + &quot;\u0000&quot; + keyId)
                .getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    }

    private static void validateTenantId(String tenantId) {
        if (tenantId == null || tenantId.isBlank() || tenantId.length() &gt; 256) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Tenant identifier is invalid.&quot;);
        }
    }

    public static final class SecureContextMappings implements AutoCloseable {
        private final ConfidentialSegment plaintext;
        private final Map&lt;String, ConfidentialSegment&gt; values;
        private boolean closed;

        private SecureContextMappings(
                ConfidentialSegment plaintext,
                Map&lt;String, ConfidentialSegment&gt; values) {
            this.plaintext = plaintext;
            this.values = Map.copyOf(values);
        }

        public Map&lt;String, ConfidentialSegment&gt; values() {
            if (closed) {
                throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;Secure context mappings are closed.&quot;);
            }
            return values;
        }

        @Override
        public void close() {
            if (closed) {
                return;
            }
            closed = true;
            try {
                closeMappings(values);
            } finally {
                plaintext.close();
            }
        }
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/ConfidentialGatewayService.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.OutputStream;
import java.security.SecureRandom;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Objects;

import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.ConfidentialSegment;

public final class ConfidentialGatewayService {
    private static final String TOKEN_PREFIX = &quot;&lt;OJE_&quot;;
    private static final String TOKEN_SUFFIX = &quot;&gt;&quot;;
    private static final int TOKEN_RANDOM_BYTES = 16;
    private static final int MAX_TEXT_LENGTH = 1_000_000;
    private static final int MAX_BATCH_SEGMENTS = 100;
    private static final int MAX_BATCH_TEXT_LENGTH = 5_000_000;
    private static final int MAX_ENTITIES = 10_000;

    private final PiiDetector piiDetector;
    private final AesGcmSecureContextCodec contextCodec;
    private final Duration contextLifetime;
    private final SecureRandom secureRandom;
    private final Clock clock;
    private final ConfidentialOperationGovernor operationGovernor;

    public ConfidentialGatewayService(
            PiiDetector piiDetector,
            AesGcmSecureContextCodec contextCodec,
            Duration contextLifetime,
            SecureRandom secureRandom,
            Clock clock) {
        this(piiDetector, contextCodec, contextLifetime, secureRandom, clock,
                new ConfidentialOperationGovernor(64));
    }

    public ConfidentialGatewayService(
            PiiDetector piiDetector,
            AesGcmSecureContextCodec contextCodec,
            Duration contextLifetime,
            SecureRandom secureRandom,
            Clock clock,
            ConfidentialOperationGovernor operationGovernor) {
        if (contextLifetime == null || contextLifetime.isZero() || contextLifetime.isNegative()) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Secure context lifetime must be positive.&quot;);
        }
        this.piiDetector = piiDetector;
        this.contextCodec = contextCodec;
        this.contextLifetime = contextLifetime;
        this.secureRandom = secureRandom;
        this.clock = clock;
        this.operationGovernor = Objects.requireNonNull(operationGovernor, &quot;operationGovernor&quot;);
    }

    public SanitizeResult sanitize(String tenantId, String text) {
        if (text == null || text.length() &gt; MAX_TEXT_LENGTH) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Text is missing or exceeds the supported size.&quot;);
        }
        return operationGovernor.execute(() -&gt; {
            try (ConfidentialSegment source = ConfidentialSegment.fromUtf8(text)) {
                return sanitizeNativeSegmentWithinPermit(tenantId, source);
            }
        });
    }

    public SanitizeResult sanitize(String tenantId, ConfidentialSegment source) {
        Objects.requireNonNull(source, &quot;source&quot;);
        return operationGovernor.execute(
                () -&gt; sanitizeNativeSegmentWithinPermit(tenantId, source));
    }

    public ConfidentialOperationGovernor.Permit acquireOperationPermit() {
        return operationGovernor.acquire();
    }

    public SanitizeResult sanitize(
            String tenantId,
            ConfidentialSegment source,
            ConfidentialOperationGovernor.Permit permit) {
        Objects.requireNonNull(source, &quot;source&quot;);
        return operationGovernor.execute(permit,
                () -&gt; sanitizeNativeSegmentWithinPermit(tenantId, source));
    }

    private SanitizeResult sanitizeNativeSegmentWithinPermit(
            String tenantId, ConfidentialSegment source) {
        try (NativeSecretMappings tokenMappings = new NativeSecretMappings()) {
            try (ConfidentialSegment sanitized = sanitizeToNative(source, tokenMappings)) {
                Instant expiresAt = clock.instant().plus(contextLifetime);
                String secureContext = contextCodec.sealNative(
                        tenantId, tokenMappings.values(), expiresAt);
                return new SanitizeResult(
                        sanitized.toUtf8String(), secureContext, piiDetector.version(), expiresAt);
            }
        }
    }

    public void writeSanitizeJson(
            String tenantId,
            ConfidentialSegment source,
            ConfidentialOperationGovernor.Permit permit,
            OutputStream output) throws IOException {
        Objects.requireNonNull(source, &quot;source&quot;);
        Objects.requireNonNull(output, &quot;output&quot;);
        try {
            operationGovernor.execute(permit, () -&gt; {
                try (NativeSecretMappings tokenMappings = new NativeSecretMappings();
                        ConfidentialSegment sanitized = sanitizeToNative(source, tokenMappings);
                        NativeJsonStringWriter writer = new NativeJsonStringWriter(output)) {
                    Instant expiresAt = clock.instant().plus(contextLifetime);
                    String secureContext = contextCodec.sealNative(
                            tenantId, tokenMappings.values(), expiresAt);
                    writeSanitizeResponseJson(
                            writer, sanitized, secureContext, piiDetector.version(), expiresAt);
                    return null;
                } catch (IOException exception) {
                    throw new GatewayResponseWriteException(exception);
                }
            });
        } catch (GatewayResponseWriteException exception) {
            throw exception.ioException();
        }
    }

    public void writeSanitizeBatchJson(
            String tenantId,
            List&lt;ConfidentialSegment&gt; sources,
            ConfidentialOperationGovernor.Permit permit,
            OutputStream output) throws IOException {
        if (sources == null || sources.isEmpty() || sources.size() &gt; MAX_BATCH_SEGMENTS) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    &quot;Request must contain between 1 and 100 text segments.&quot;);
        }
        Objects.requireNonNull(output, &quot;output&quot;);
        try {
            operationGovernor.execute(permit, () -&gt; {
                try (NativeSecretMappings tokenMappings = new NativeSecretMappings();
                        NativeJsonStringWriter writer = new NativeJsonStringWriter(output)) {
                    List&lt;ConfidentialSegment&gt; sanitized = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;(sources.size());
                    long totalCharacters = 0;
                    try {
                        for (ConfidentialSegment source : sources) {
                            NativeUtf8CharSequence text = new NativeUtf8CharSequence(
                                    Objects.requireNonNull(source, &quot;source&quot;));
                            totalCharacters += text.length();
                            if (text.length() &gt; MAX_TEXT_LENGTH
                                    || totalCharacters &gt; MAX_BATCH_TEXT_LENGTH) {
                                throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                                        &quot;Text segments exceed the supported size.&quot;);
                            }
                            sanitized.add(sanitizeToNative(source, tokenMappings));
                        }
                        Instant expiresAt = clock.instant().plus(contextLifetime);
                        String secureContext = contextCodec.sealNative(
                                tenantId, tokenMappings.values(), expiresAt);
                        writer.writeAscii(&quot;{\&quot;sanitizedSegments\&quot;:[&quot;);
                        for (int index = 0; index &lt; sanitized.size(); index++) {
                            if (index &gt; 0) {
                                writer.writeAscii(&quot;,&quot;);
                            }
                            writer.writeJsonString(new NativeUtf8CharSequence(
                                    sanitized.get(index)));
                        }
                        writer.writeAscii(&quot;],\&quot;secureContext\&quot;:&quot;);
                        writer.writeJsonString(secureContext);
                        writer.writeAscii(&quot;,\&quot;detectorVersion\&quot;:&quot;);
                        writer.writeJsonString(piiDetector.version());
                        writer.writeAscii(&quot;,\&quot;expiresAt\&quot;:&quot;);
                        writer.writeJsonString(expiresAt.toString());
                        writer.writeAscii(&quot;}&quot;);
                        return null;
                    } finally {
                        closeSegments(sanitized);
                    }
                } catch (IOException exception) {
                    throw new GatewayResponseWriteException(exception);
                }
            });
        } catch (GatewayResponseWriteException exception) {
            throw exception.ioException();
        }
    }

    public BatchSanitizeResult sanitizeSegments(String tenantId, List&lt;String&gt; segments) {
        validateSegments(segments);
        return operationGovernor.execute(() -&gt; sanitizeSegmentsWithinPermit(tenantId, segments));
    }

    public BatchSanitizeResult sanitizeNativeSegments(
            String tenantId, List&lt;ConfidentialSegment&gt; segments) {
        if (segments == null || segments.isEmpty() || segments.size() &gt; MAX_BATCH_SEGMENTS) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    &quot;Request must contain between 1 and 100 text segments.&quot;);
        }
        return operationGovernor.execute(
                () -&gt; sanitizeNativeSegmentsWithinPermit(tenantId, segments));
    }

    public BatchSanitizeResult sanitizeNativeSegments(
            String tenantId,
            List&lt;ConfidentialSegment&gt; segments,
            ConfidentialOperationGovernor.Permit permit) {
        if (segments == null || segments.isEmpty() || segments.size() &gt; MAX_BATCH_SEGMENTS) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    &quot;Request must contain between 1 and 100 text segments.&quot;);
        }
        return operationGovernor.execute(permit,
                () -&gt; sanitizeNativeSegmentsWithinPermit(tenantId, segments));
    }

    private BatchSanitizeResult sanitizeNativeSegmentsWithinPermit(
            String tenantId, List&lt;ConfidentialSegment&gt; segments) {
        try (NativeSecretMappings tokenMappings = new NativeSecretMappings()) {
            List&lt;String&gt; sanitizedSegments = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;(segments.size());
            long totalCharacters = 0;
            for (ConfidentialSegment segment : segments) {
                NativeUtf8CharSequence text = new NativeUtf8CharSequence(
                        Objects.requireNonNull(segment, &quot;segment&quot;));
                totalCharacters += text.length();
                if (text.length() &gt; MAX_TEXT_LENGTH
                        || totalCharacters &gt; MAX_BATCH_TEXT_LENGTH) {
                    throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                            &quot;Text segments exceed the supported size.&quot;);
                }
                sanitizedSegments.add(sanitizeText(segment, tokenMappings));
            }
            Instant expiresAt = clock.instant().plus(contextLifetime);
            String secureContext = contextCodec.sealNative(
                    tenantId, tokenMappings.values(), expiresAt);
            return new BatchSanitizeResult(
                    List.copyOf(sanitizedSegments),
                    secureContext,
                    piiDetector.version(),
                    expiresAt);
        }
    }

    private BatchSanitizeResult sanitizeSegmentsWithinPermit(
            String tenantId, List&lt;String&gt; segments) {
        List&lt;ConfidentialSegment&gt; sources = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;(segments.size());
        try {
            try (NativeSecretMappings tokenMappings = new NativeSecretMappings()) {
                List&lt;String&gt; sanitizedSegments = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;(segments.size());
                for (String segment : segments) {
                    ConfidentialSegment source = ConfidentialSegment.fromUtf8(segment);
                    sources.add(source);
                    sanitizedSegments.add(sanitizeText(source, tokenMappings));
                }
                Instant expiresAt = clock.instant().plus(contextLifetime);
                String secureContext = contextCodec.sealNative(
                        tenantId, tokenMappings.values(), expiresAt);
                return new BatchSanitizeResult(
                        List.copyOf(sanitizedSegments),
                        secureContext,
                        piiDetector.version(),
                        expiresAt);
            }
        } finally {
            for (ConfidentialSegment source : sources) {
                source.close();
            }
        }
    }

    public String desanitize(String tenantId, String modelResponse, String secureContext) {
        if (modelResponse == null || modelResponse.length() &gt; MAX_TEXT_LENGTH) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Model response is missing or exceeds the supported size.&quot;);
        }
        return operationGovernor.execute(() -&gt; {
            try (ConfidentialSegment response =
                    ConfidentialSegment.fromUtf8(modelResponse)) {
                return desanitizeNativeWithinPermit(tenantId, response, secureContext);
            }
        });
    }

    public String desanitize(
            String tenantId,
            ConfidentialSegment modelResponse,
            String secureContext,
            ConfidentialOperationGovernor.Permit permit) {
        Objects.requireNonNull(modelResponse, &quot;modelResponse&quot;);
        return operationGovernor.execute(permit,
                () -&gt; desanitizeNativeWithinPermit(tenantId, modelResponse, secureContext));
    }

    private String desanitizeNativeWithinPermit(
            String tenantId, ConfidentialSegment modelResponse, String secureContext) {
        NativeUtf8CharSequence response = new NativeUtf8CharSequence(modelResponse);
        if (response.length() &gt; MAX_TEXT_LENGTH) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    &quot;Model response exceeds the supported size.&quot;);
        }
        try (AesGcmSecureContextCodec.SecureContextMappings tokenMappings =
                contextCodec.openNative(tenantId, secureContext)) {
            return restoreText(modelResponse, tokenMappings.values());
        }
    }

    public void writeDesanitizeJson(
            String tenantId,
            ConfidentialSegment modelResponse,
            String secureContext,
            ConfidentialOperationGovernor.Permit permit,

        Objects.requireNonNull(modelResponse, &quot;modelResponse&quot;);
        Objects.requireNonNull(output, &quot;output&quot;);
        try {
            operationGovernor.execute(permit, () -&gt; {
                NativeUtf8CharSequence response = new NativeUtf8CharSequence(modelResponse);
                if (response.length() &gt; MAX_TEXT_LENGTH) {

                            &quot;Model response exceeds the supported size.&quot;);
                }
