                                contextCodec.openNative(tenantId, secureContext);
                        NativeJsonStringWriter writer = new NativeJsonStringWriter(output)) {
                    writer.writeAscii(&quot;{\&quot;desanitizedText\&quot;:&quot;);
                    writeRestoredJsonString(writer, response, tokenMappings.values());
                    writer.writeAscii(&quot;}&quot;);
                    return null;
                } catch (IOException exception) {
                    throw new GatewayResponseWriteException(exception);
                }
            });
        } catch (GatewayResponseWriteException exception) {
            throw exception.ioException();
        }
    }

    public List&lt;String&gt; desanitizeSegments(
            String tenantId, List&lt;String&gt; modelResponses, String secureContext) {
        validateSegments(modelResponses);
        return operationGovernor.execute(() -&gt; {
            try (AesGcmSecureContextCodec.SecureContextMappings tokenMappings =
                    contextCodec.openNative(tenantId, secureContext)) {
                List&lt;String&gt; restored = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;(modelResponses.size());
                for (String response : modelResponses) {
                    try (ConfidentialSegment nativeResponse =
                            ConfidentialSegment.fromUtf8(response)) {
                        restored.add(restoreText(nativeResponse, tokenMappings.values()));
                    }
                }
                return List.copyOf(restored);
            }
        });
    }

    public List&lt;String&gt; desanitizeNativeSegments(
            String tenantId,
            List&lt;ConfidentialSegment&gt; modelResponses,
            String secureContext,
            ConfidentialOperationGovernor.Permit permit) {
        if (modelResponses == null || modelResponses.isEmpty()
                || modelResponses.size() &gt; MAX_BATCH_SEGMENTS) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    &quot;Request must contain between 1 and 100 text segments.&quot;);
        }
        return operationGovernor.execute(permit, () -&gt; {
            long totalCharacters = 0;
            for (ConfidentialSegment response : modelResponses) {
                int length = new NativeUtf8CharSequence(
                        Objects.requireNonNull(response, &quot;modelResponse&quot;)).length();
                totalCharacters += length;
                if (length &gt; MAX_TEXT_LENGTH || totalCharacters &gt; MAX_BATCH_TEXT_LENGTH) {
                    throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                            &quot;Model responses exceed the supported size.&quot;);
                }
            }
            try (AesGcmSecureContextCodec.SecureContextMappings tokenMappings =
                    contextCodec.openNative(tenantId, secureContext)) {
                List&lt;String&gt; restored = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;(modelResponses.size());
                for (ConfidentialSegment response : modelResponses) {
                    restored.add(restoreText(response, tokenMappings.values()));
                }
                return List.copyOf(restored);
            }
        });
    }

    public void writeDesanitizeBatchJson(
            String tenantId,
            List&lt;ConfidentialSegment&gt; modelResponses,
            String secureContext,
            ConfidentialOperationGovernor.Permit permit,
            OutputStream output) throws IOException {
        if (modelResponses == null || modelResponses.isEmpty()
                || modelResponses.size() &gt; MAX_BATCH_SEGMENTS) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    &quot;Request must contain between 1 and 100 text segments.&quot;);
        }
        Objects.requireNonNull(output, &quot;output&quot;);
        try {
            operationGovernor.execute(permit, () -&gt; {
                long totalCharacters = 0;
                List&lt;NativeUtf8CharSequence&gt; responses =
                        new ArrayList&lt;&gt;(modelResponses.size());
                for (ConfidentialSegment response : modelResponses) {
                    NativeUtf8CharSequence text = new NativeUtf8CharSequence(
                            Objects.requireNonNull(response, &quot;modelResponse&quot;));
                    totalCharacters += text.length();
                    if (text.length() &gt; MAX_TEXT_LENGTH
                            || totalCharacters &gt; MAX_BATCH_TEXT_LENGTH) {
                        throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                                &quot;Model responses exceed the supported size.&quot;);
                    }
                    responses.add(text);
                }
                try (AesGcmSecureContextCodec.SecureContextMappings tokenMappings =
                                contextCodec.openNative(tenantId, secureContext);
                        NativeJsonStringWriter writer = new NativeJsonStringWriter(output)) {
                    writer.writeAscii(&quot;{\&quot;desanitizedResponses\&quot;:[&quot;);
                    for (int index = 0; index &lt; responses.size(); index++) {
                        if (index &gt; 0) {
                            writer.writeAscii(&quot;,&quot;);
                        }
                        writeRestoredJsonString(
                                writer, responses.get(index), tokenMappings.values());
                    }
                    writer.writeAscii(&quot;]}&quot;);
                    return null;
                } catch (IOException exception) {
                    throw new GatewayResponseWriteException(exception);
                }
            });
        } catch (GatewayResponseWriteException exception) {
            throw exception.ioException();
        }
    }

    private String sanitizeText(
            ConfidentialSegment source, NativeSecretMappings tokenMappings) {
        try (ConfidentialSegment sanitized = sanitizeToNative(source, tokenMappings)) {
            return sanitized.toUtf8String();
        }
    }

    private ConfidentialSegment sanitizeToNative(
            ConfidentialSegment source, NativeSecretMappings tokenMappings) {
        NativeUtf8CharSequence text = new NativeUtf8CharSequence(source);
        if (text.length() &gt; MAX_TEXT_LENGTH) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Text is missing or exceeds the supported size.&quot;);
        }
        List&lt;DetectedEntity&gt; entities = piiDetector.detect(text);
        validateEntities(text, entities);
        if (tokenMappings.size() + entities.size() &gt; MAX_ENTITIES) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Request contains too many supported entities.&quot;);
        }

        int[] utf8Offsets = utf8Offsets(text);
        List&lt;TokenizedEntity&gt; replacements = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;(entities.size());
        long sanitizedSize = source.byteSize();
        for (DetectedEntity entity : entities) {
            if (splitsSurrogatePair(text, entity.start())
                    || splitsSurrogatePair(text, entity.end())) {
                throw new IllegalStateException(
                        &quot;PII detector returned an entity boundary inside a surrogate pair.&quot;);
            }
            String token;
            do {
                token = newToken(entity.type());
            } while (contains(text, token) || tokenMappings.containsToken(token));
            tokenMappings.put(token, source.slice(
                    utf8Offsets[entity.start()],
                    utf8Offsets[entity.end()] - utf8Offsets[entity.start()]));
            long tokenByteLength = ConfidentialSegment.utf8Length(token);
            replacements.add(new TokenizedEntity(
                    entity.start(), entity.end(), token, utf8Offsets[entity.start()],
                    utf8Offsets[entity.end()], tokenByteLength));
            sanitizedSize += tokenByteLength
                    - (long) (utf8Offsets[entity.end()] - utf8Offsets[entity.start()]);
        }

        ConfidentialSegment sanitized = ConfidentialSegment.allocate(sanitizedSize);
        try {
            long sourceOffset = 0;
            long targetOffset = 0;
            for (TokenizedEntity replacement : replacements) {
                long unchangedLength = replacement.utf8Start() - sourceOffset;
                source.copyTo(sanitized, sourceOffset, targetOffset, unchangedLength);
                targetOffset += unchangedLength;
                sanitized.writeUtf8(replacement.token(), targetOffset);
                targetOffset += replacement.tokenByteLength();
                sourceOffset = replacement.utf8End();
            }
            source.copyTo(
                    sanitized, sourceOffset, targetOffset, source.byteSize() - sourceOffset);
            return sanitized;
        } catch (RuntimeException | Error failure) {
            sanitized.close();
            throw failure;
        }
    }

    private static void writeSanitizeResponseJson(
            NativeJsonStringWriter writer,
            ConfidentialSegment sanitized,
            String secureContext,
            String detectorVersion,
            Instant expiresAt) throws IOException {
        writer.writeAscii(&quot;{\&quot;sanitizedText\&quot;:&quot;);
        writer.writeJsonString(new NativeUtf8CharSequence(sanitized));
        writer.writeAscii(&quot;,\&quot;secureContext\&quot;:&quot;);
        writer.writeJsonString(secureContext);
        writer.writeAscii(&quot;,\&quot;detectorVersion\&quot;:&quot;);
        writer.writeJsonString(detectorVersion);
        writer.writeAscii(&quot;,\&quot;expiresAt\&quot;:&quot;);
        writer.writeJsonString(expiresAt.toString());
        writer.writeAscii(&quot;}&quot;);
    }

    private static void closeSegments(List&lt;ConfidentialSegment&gt; segments) {
        RuntimeException failure = null;
        for (ConfidentialSegment segment : segments) {
            try {
                segment.close();
            } catch (RuntimeException closeFailure) {
                if (failure == null) {
                    failure = closeFailure;
                } else {
                    failure.addSuppressed(closeFailure);
                }
            }
        }
        if (failure != null) {
            throw failure;
        }
    }

    private static int[] utf8Offsets(CharSequence text) {
        int[] offsets = new int[text.length() + 1];
        int charOffset = 0;
        int byteOffset = 0;
        while (charOffset &lt; text.length()) {
            char current = text.charAt(charOffset);
            if (Character.isHighSurrogate(current)
                    &amp;&amp; charOffset + 1 &lt; text.length()
                    &amp;&amp; Character.isLowSurrogate(text.charAt(charOffset + 1))) {
                offsets[charOffset + 1] = byteOffset;
                byteOffset += 4;
                charOffset += 2;
                offsets[charOffset] = byteOffset;
            } else {
                byteOffset += Character.isSurrogate(current)
                        ? 1
                        : current &lt;= 0x7f ? 1 : current &lt;= 0x7ff ? 2 : 3;
                offsets[++charOffset] = byteOffset;
            }
        }
        return offsets;
    }

    private static boolean splitsSurrogatePair(CharSequence text, int charOffset) {
        return charOffset &gt; 0
                &amp;&amp; charOffset &lt; text.length()
                &amp;&amp; Character.isHighSurrogate(text.charAt(charOffset - 1))
                &amp;&amp; Character.isLowSurrogate(text.charAt(charOffset));
    }

    private static String restoreText(
            ConfidentialSegment response, Map&lt;String, ConfidentialSegment&gt; tokenMappings) {
        CharSequence text = new NativeUtf8CharSequence(response);
        StringBuilder restored = new StringBuilder(text.length());
        int cursor = 0;
        while (cursor &lt; text.length()) {
            int tokenStart = indexOf(text, TOKEN_PREFIX, cursor);
            if (tokenStart &lt; 0) {
                append(restored, text, cursor, text.length());
                break;
            }
            int tokenEnd = indexOf(text, TOKEN_SUFFIX, tokenStart + TOKEN_PREFIX.length());
            if (tokenEnd &lt; 0) {
                append(restored, text, cursor, text.length());
                break;
            }

            append(restored, text, cursor, tokenStart);
            String token = text.subSequence(tokenStart, tokenEnd + TOKEN_SUFFIX.length()).toString();
            ConfidentialSegment value = tokenMappings.get(token);
            restored.append(value == null ? token : new NativeUtf8CharSequence(value));
            cursor = tokenEnd + TOKEN_SUFFIX.length();
        }
        return restored.toString();
    }

    private static void writeRestoredJsonString(
            NativeJsonStringWriter writer,
            CharSequence text,
            Map&lt;String, ConfidentialSegment&gt; tokenMappings) throws IOException {
        writer.writeAscii(&quot;\&quot;&quot;);
        int cursor = 0;
        while (cursor &lt; text.length()) {
            int tokenStart = indexOf(text, TOKEN_PREFIX, cursor);
            if (tokenStart &lt; 0) {
                writer.writeJsonStringContent(text.subSequence(cursor, text.length()));
                break;
            }
            int tokenEnd = indexOf(text, TOKEN_SUFFIX, tokenStart + TOKEN_PREFIX.length());
            if (tokenEnd &lt; 0) {
                writer.writeJsonStringContent(text.subSequence(cursor, text.length()));
                break;
            }
            writer.writeJsonStringContent(text.subSequence(cursor, tokenStart));
            String token = text.subSequence(
                    tokenStart, tokenEnd + TOKEN_SUFFIX.length()).toString();
            ConfidentialSegment value = tokenMappings.get(token);
            if (value == null) {
                writer.writeJsonStringContent(text.subSequence(
                        tokenStart, tokenEnd + TOKEN_SUFFIX.length()));
            } else {
                writer.writeJsonStringContent(new NativeUtf8CharSequence(value));
            }
            cursor = tokenEnd + TOKEN_SUFFIX.length();
        }
        writer.writeAscii(&quot;\&quot;&quot;);
    }

    private static int indexOf(CharSequence text, String sought, int start) {
        int lastStart = text.length() - sought.length();
        for (int offset = start; offset &lt;= lastStart; offset++) {
            int index = 0;
            while (index &lt; sought.length()
                    &amp;&amp; text.charAt(offset + index) == sought.charAt(index)) {
                index++;
            }
            if (index == sought.length()) {
                return offset;
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }

    private static void append(StringBuilder target, CharSequence source, int start, int end) {
        for (int index = start; index &lt; end; index++) {
            target.append(source.charAt(index));
        }
    }

    private static void validateSegments(List&lt;String&gt; segments) {
        if (segments == null || segments.isEmpty() || segments.size() &gt; MAX_BATCH_SEGMENTS) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    &quot;Request must contain between 1 and 100 text segments.&quot;);
        }
        long totalLength = 0;
        for (String segment : segments) {
            if (segment == null) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Text segments must not be null.&quot;);
            }
            totalLength += segment.length();
            if (segment.length() &gt; MAX_TEXT_LENGTH || totalLength &gt; MAX_BATCH_TEXT_LENGTH) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Text segments exceed the supported size.&quot;);
            }
        }
    }

    private String newToken(String type) {
        byte[] bytes = new byte[TOKEN_RANDOM_BYTES];
        secureRandom.nextBytes(bytes);
        return TOKEN_PREFIX + type + &quot;_&quot; + java.util.HexFormat.of().formatHex(bytes) + TOKEN_SUFFIX;
    }

    private static void validateEntities(CharSequence text, List&lt;DetectedEntity&gt; entities) {
        if (entities == null) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;PII detector returned no result.&quot;);
        }

        int previousEnd = 0;
        for (DetectedEntity entity : entities) {
            if (entity == null || entity.end() &gt; text.length()
                    || entity.start() &lt; previousEnd || entity.end() &lt;= entity.start()) {
                throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;PII detector returned invalid entity offsets.&quot;);
            }
            previousEnd = entity.end();
        }
    }

    private static boolean contains(CharSequence text, String value) {
        int lastStart = text.length() - value.length();
        for (int start = 0; start &lt;= lastStart; start++) {
            int index = 0;
            while (index &lt; value.length() &amp;&amp; text.charAt(start + index) == value.charAt(index)) {
                index++;
            }
            if (index == value.length()) {
                return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    public record SanitizeResult(
            String sanitizedText,
            String secureContext,
            String detectorVersion,
            Instant expiresAt) {
    }

    public record BatchSanitizeResult(
            List&lt;String&gt; sanitizedSegments,
            String secureContext,
            String detectorVersion,
            Instant expiresAt) {
    }

    private record TokenizedEntity(
            int start, int end, String token, int utf8Start, int utf8End, long tokenByteLength) {
    }

    private static final class GatewayResponseWriteException extends RuntimeException {
        private final IOException ioException;

        private GatewayResponseWriteException(IOException ioException) {
            super(ioException);
            this.ioException = ioException;
        }

        private IOException ioException() {
            return ioException;
        }
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/ConfidentialOperationGovernor.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway;

import java.util.Objects;
import java.util.concurrent.Semaphore;
import java.util.function.Supplier;

public final class ConfidentialOperationGovernor {
    private final Semaphore permits;
    private final int maximumConcurrentOperations;

    public ConfidentialOperationGovernor(int maximumConcurrentOperations) {
        if (maximumConcurrentOperations &lt; 1) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(
                    &quot;Maximum concurrent confidential operations must be positive.&quot;);
        }
        this.maximumConcurrentOperations = maximumConcurrentOperations;
        permits = new Semaphore(maximumConcurrentOperations, true);
    }

    public &lt;T&gt; T execute(Supplier&lt;T&gt; operation) {
        Objects.requireNonNull(operation, &quot;operation&quot;);
        try (Permit permit = acquire()) {
            return execute(permit, operation);
        }
    }

    public Permit acquire() {
        try {
            permits.acquire();
        } catch (InterruptedException exception) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            throw new IllegalStateException(
                    &quot;Interrupted while waiting for a confidential operation permit.&quot;, exception);
        }
        return new Permit(this);
    }

    &lt;T&gt; T execute(Permit permit, Supplier&lt;T&gt; operation) {
        Objects.requireNonNull(operation, &quot;operation&quot;);
        if (permit == null || permit.governor != this) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;A live permit from this governor is required.&quot;);
        }
        return permit.execute(operation);
    }

    public static final class Permit implements AutoCloseable {
        private final ConfidentialOperationGovernor governor;
        private boolean closed;

        private Permit(ConfidentialOperationGovernor governor) {
            this.governor = governor;
        }

        @Override
        public synchronized void close() {
            if (!closed) {
                closed = true;
                governor.permits.release();
            }
        }

        private synchronized &lt;T&gt; T execute(Supplier&lt;T&gt; operation) {
            if (closed) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;A live permit from this governor is required.&quot;);
            }
            return operation.get();
        }
    }

    public int maximumConcurrentOperations() {
        return maximumConcurrentOperations;
    }

import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.OutputStream;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.Objects;

final class NativeJsonStringWriter implements AutoCloseable {
    private static final byte[] HEX = &quot;0123456789abcdef&quot;.getBytes(java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets.US_ASCII);

    private final OutputStream output;
    private final byte[] buffer = new byte[4096];
    private int position;

    NativeJsonStringWriter(OutputStream output) {
        this.output = Objects.requireNonNull(output, &quot;output&quot;);
    }

    void writeAscii(String value) throws IOException {
        for (int index = 0; index &lt; value.length(); index++) {
            writeByte((byte) value.charAt(index));
        }
    }

    void writeJsonString(CharSequence value) throws IOException {
        writeByte((byte) &apos;&quot;&apos;);
        writeJsonStringContent(value);
        writeByte((byte) &apos;&quot;&apos;);
    }

    void writeJsonStringContent(CharSequence value) throws IOException {
        for (int index = 0; index &lt; value.length();) {
            char current = value.charAt(index++);
            int codePoint;
            if (Character.isHighSurrogate(current)
                    &amp;&amp; index &lt; value.length()
                    &amp;&amp; Character.isLowSurrogate(value.charAt(index))) {
                codePoint = Character.toCodePoint(current, value.charAt(index++));
            } else {
                codePoint = current;
            }
            if (codePoint == &apos;&quot;&apos; || codePoint == &apos;\\&apos;) {
                writeByte((byte) &apos;\\&apos;);
                writeByte((byte) codePoint);
            } else if (codePoint &lt; 0x20) {
                writeByte((byte) &apos;\\&apos;);
                writeByte((byte) &apos;u&apos;);
                writeByte((byte) &apos;0&apos;);
                writeByte((byte) &apos;0&apos;);
                writeByte(HEX[(codePoint &gt;&gt;&gt; 4) &amp; 0x0f]);
                writeByte(HEX[codePoint &amp; 0x0f]);
            } else {
                writeUtf8(codePoint);
            }
        }
    }

    @Override
    public void close() throws IOException {
        try {
            flush();
        } finally {
            Arrays.fill(buffer, (byte) 0);
        }
    }

    private void writeUtf8(int codePoint) throws IOException {
        if (codePoint &lt;= 0x7f) {
            writeByte((byte) codePoint);
        } else if (codePoint &lt;= 0x7ff) {
            writeByte((byte) (0xc0 | (codePoint &gt;&gt;&gt; 6)));
            writeByte((byte) (0x80 | (codePoint &amp; 0x3f)));
        } else if (codePoint &lt;= 0xffff) {
            writeByte((byte) (0xe0 | (codePoint &gt;&gt;&gt; 12)));
            writeByte((byte) (0x80 | ((codePoint &gt;&gt;&gt; 6) &amp; 0x3f)));
            writeByte((byte) (0x80 | (codePoint &amp; 0x3f)));
        } else {
            writeByte((byte) (0xf0 | (codePoint &gt;&gt;&gt; 18)));
            writeByte((byte) (0x80 | ((codePoint &gt;&gt;&gt; 12) &amp; 0x3f)));
            writeByte((byte) (0x80 | ((codePoint &gt;&gt;&gt; 6) &amp; 0x3f)));
            writeByte((byte) (0x80 | (codePoint &amp; 0x3f)));
        }
    }

    private void writeByte(byte value) throws IOException {
        buffer[position++] = value;
        if (position == buffer.length) {
            flush();
        }
    }

    private void flush() throws IOException {
        if (position &gt; 0) {
            int written = position;
            try {
                output.write(buffer, 0, written);
            } finally {
                Arrays.fill(buffer, 0, written, (byte) 0);
                position = 0;
            }
        }
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/NativeSecretMappings.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway;

import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Objects;

import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.ConfidentialSegment;

final class NativeSecretMappings implements AutoCloseable {
    private final Map&lt;String, ConfidentialSegment&gt; values = new LinkedHashMap&lt;&gt;();
    private boolean closed;

    void put(String token, ConfidentialSegment value) {
        if (closed) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;Secret mappings are closed.&quot;);
        }
        Objects.requireNonNull(token, &quot;token&quot;);
        Objects.requireNonNull(value, &quot;value&quot;);
        if (values.putIfAbsent(token, value) != null) {
            value.close();
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Token mapping already exists.&quot;);
        }
    }

    int size() {
        if (closed) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;Secret mappings are closed.&quot;);
        }
        return values.size();
    }

    boolean containsToken(String token) {
        if (closed) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;Secret mappings are closed.&quot;);
        }
        return values.containsKey(token);
    }

    Map&lt;String, ConfidentialSegment&gt; values() {
        if (closed) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;Secret mappings are closed.&quot;);
        }
        return Map.copyOf(values);
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        if (closed) {
            return;
        }
        closed = true;
        RuntimeException failure = null;
        for (ConfidentialSegment value : values.values()) {
            try {
                value.close();
            } catch (RuntimeException closeFailure) {
                if (failure == null) {
                    failure = closeFailure;
                } else {
                    failure.addSuppressed(closeFailure);
                }
            }
        }
        values.clear();
        if (failure != null) {
            throw failure;
        }
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/NativeUtf8CharSequence.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway;

import java.util.Objects;

import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.ConfidentialSegment;

public final class NativeUtf8CharSequence implements CharSequence {
    private final ConfidentialSegment segment;
    private final int[] byteOffsets;
    private final int start;
    private final int end;

    public NativeUtf8CharSequence(ConfidentialSegment segment) {
        this(segment, 0, buildOffsets(Objects.requireNonNull(segment, &quot;segment&quot;)), -1);
    }

    private NativeUtf8CharSequence(
            ConfidentialSegment segment, int start, int[] byteOffsets, int end) {
        this.segment = segment;
        this.start = start;
        this.end = end &lt; 0 ? byteOffsets.length - 1 : end;
        this.byteOffsets = byteOffsets;
    }

    @Override
    public int length() {
        return end - start;
    }

    @Override
    public char charAt(int index) {
        if (index &lt; 0 || index &gt;= length()) {
            throw new IndexOutOfBoundsException(index);
        }
        int absolute = start + index;
        int codePoint = codePointAt(absolute);
        if (codePoint &lt;= Character.MAX_VALUE) {
            return (char) codePoint;
        }
        return byteOffsets[absolute + 1] == byteOffsets[absolute]
                ? Character.highSurrogate(codePoint)
                : Character.lowSurrogate(codePoint);
    }

    @Override
    public CharSequence subSequence(int beginIndex, int endIndex) {
        if (beginIndex &lt; 0 || endIndex &lt; beginIndex || endIndex &gt; length()) {
            throw new IndexOutOfBoundsException();
        }
        return new NativeUtf8CharSequence(
                segment, start + beginIndex, byteOffsets, start + endIndex);
    }

    @Override
    public String toString() {
        char[] characters = new char[length()];
        for (int index = 0; index &lt; characters.length; index++) {
            characters[index] = charAt(index);
        }
        return new String(characters);
    }

    private int codePointAt(int charOffset) {
        long byteOffset = byteOffsets[charOffset];
        int first = Byte.toUnsignedInt(segment.readByte(byteOffset));
        if (first &lt; 0x80) {
            return first;
        }
        int continuationCount = first &lt; 0xE0 ? 1 : first &lt; 0xF0 ? 2 : 3;
        int codePoint = first &amp; (continuationCount == 1 ? 0x1F
                : continuationCount == 2 ? 0x0F : 0x07);
        for (int index = 1; index &lt;= continuationCount; index++) {
            codePoint = (codePoint &lt;&lt; 6)
                    | (Byte.toUnsignedInt(segment.readByte(byteOffset + index)) &amp; 0x3F);
        }
        return codePoint;
    }

    private static int[] buildOffsets(ConfidentialSegment segment) {
        if (segment.byteSize() &gt; Integer.MAX_VALUE - 1) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Native text exceeds supported CharSequence size.&quot;);
        }
        int characterCount = 0;
        for (long offset = 0; offset &lt; segment.byteSize();) {
            int first = Byte.toUnsignedInt(segment.readByte(offset));
            int byteCount = utf8Length(first);
            int codePoint = readCodePoint(segment, offset, first, byteCount);
            characterCount += Character.charCount(codePoint);
            offset += byteCount;
        }

        int[] offsets = new int[characterCount + 1];
        int charOffset = 0;
        long byteOffset = 0;
        while (byteOffset &lt; segment.byteSize()) {
            offsets[charOffset++] = Math.toIntExact(byteOffset);
            int first = Byte.toUnsignedInt(segment.readByte(byteOffset));
            int byteCount = utf8Length(first);
            int codePoint = readCodePoint(segment, byteOffset, first, byteCount);
            if (codePoint &gt; Character.MAX_VALUE) {
                offsets[charOffset++] = Math.toIntExact(byteOffset);
            }
            byteOffset += byteCount;
        }
        offsets[charOffset] = Math.toIntExact(byteOffset);
        return java.util.Arrays.copyOf(offsets, charOffset + 1);
    }

    private static int readCodePoint(
            ConfidentialSegment segment, long byteOffset, int first, int byteCount) {
        int codePoint = first &amp; (byteCount == 1 ? 0x7F
                : byteCount == 2 ? 0x1F : byteCount == 3 ? 0x0F : 0x07);
        for (int continuation = 1; continuation &lt; byteCount; continuation++) {
            if (byteOffset + continuation &gt;= segment.byteSize()) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Native text contains incomplete UTF-8.&quot;);
            }
            int next = Byte.toUnsignedInt(segment.readByte(byteOffset + continuation));
            if ((next &amp; 0xC0) != 0x80) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Native text contains invalid UTF-8.&quot;);
            }
            codePoint = (codePoint &lt;&lt; 6) | (next &amp; 0x3F);
        }
        if (!validCodePoint(codePoint, byteCount)) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Native text contains invalid UTF-8.&quot;);
        }
        return codePoint;
    }

    private static int utf8Length(int firstByte) {
        if (firstByte &lt; 0x80) {
            return 1;
        }
        if (firstByte &gt;= 0xC2 &amp;&amp; firstByte &lt;= 0xDF) {
            return 2;
        }
        if (firstByte &gt;= 0xE0 &amp;&amp; firstByte &lt;= 0xEF) {
            return 3;
        }
        if (firstByte &gt;= 0xF0 &amp;&amp; firstByte &lt;= 0xF4) {
            return 4;
        }
        throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Native text contains invalid UTF-8.&quot;);
    }

    private static boolean validCodePoint(int codePoint, int byteCount) {
        return codePoint &lt;= Character.MAX_CODE_POINT
                &amp;&amp; (codePoint &lt; 0xD800 || codePoint &gt; 0xDFFF)
                &amp;&amp; !(byteCount == 2 &amp;&amp; codePoint &lt; 0x80)
                &amp;&amp; !(byteCount == 3 &amp;&amp; codePoint &lt; 0x800)
                &amp;&amp; !(byteCount == 4 &amp;&amp; codePoint &lt; 0x10000);
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/PiiDetector.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.regex.Matcher;
import java.util.regex.Pattern;

/**
 * A limited development detector for common email and North American phone
 * number formats. It is not suitable as a complete PII detector.
 */
public final class PrototypeRuleBasedPiiDetector implements PiiDetector {
    private static final Pattern EMAIL = Pattern.compile(
            &quot;(?&lt;![\\w.+-])[\\w.!#$%&amp;&apos;*+/=?^`{|}~-]+@[A-Za-z0-9](?:[A-Za-z0-9-]{0,61}[A-Za-z0-9])?(?:\\.[A-Za-z0-9](?:[A-Za-z0-9-]{0,61}[A-Za-z0-9])?)+(?![\\w.-])&quot;);
    private static final Pattern NORTH_AMERICAN_PHONE = Pattern.compile(
            &quot;(?&lt;!\\w)(?:\\+?1[\\s.-]?)?(?:\\(\\d{3}\\)|\\d{3})[\\s.-]?\\d{3}[\\s.-]?\\d{4}(?!\\w)&quot;);

    @Override
    public String version() {
        return &quot;prototype-rules-1&quot;;
    }

    @Override
    public List&lt;DetectedEntity&gt; detect(CharSequence text) {
        if (text == null) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Text must not be null.&quot;);
        }

        List&lt;DetectedEntity&gt; entities = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;();
        collect(EMAIL.matcher(text), &quot;EMAIL&quot;, entities);
        collect(NORTH_AMERICAN_PHONE.matcher(text), &quot;PHONE_NUMBER&quot;, entities);
        entities.sort(Comparator.comparingInt(DetectedEntity::start)
                .thenComparingInt(DetectedEntity::end));

        int previousEnd = -1;
        for (DetectedEntity entity : entities) {
            if (entity.start() &lt; previousEnd) {
                throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;PII detector returned overlapping entities.&quot;);
            }
            previousEnd = entity.end();
        }
        return List.copyOf(entities);
    }

    private static void collect(
            Matcher matcher, String type, List&lt;DetectedEntity&gt; entities) {
        while (matcher.find()) {
            entities.add(new DetectedEntity(type, matcher.start(), matcher.end()));
        }
    }

import java.util.List;

import org.aspectj.lang.ProceedingJoinPoint;

import org.springframework.core.annotation.Order;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;

@Aspect
@Component
@Order(Ordered.HIGHEST_PRECEDENCE)
public class ConfidentialAIAspect {
    private final List&lt;ConfidentialRuntimeVerifier&gt; verifiers;

    public ConfidentialAIAspect(List&lt;ConfidentialRuntimeVerifier&gt; verifiers) {
        this.verifiers = List.copyOf(verifiers);
    }

    @Around(
            value = &quot;@annotation(confidentialAI) || @within(confidentialAI)&quot;,
            argNames = &quot;joinPoint,confidentialAI&quot;)
    public Object requireConfidentialRuntime(
            ProceedingJoinPoint joinPoint, ConfidentialAI confidentialAI) throws Throwable {
        return requireConfidentialRuntime(joinPoint);
    }

    public Object requireConfidentialRuntime(ProceedingJoinPoint joinPoint) throws Throwable {
        if (verifiers.size() != 1) {
            throw new ConfidentialExecutionDeniedException(
                    &quot;Confidential execution requires exactly one configured runtime verifier.&quot;);
        }

        verifiers.get(0).verifyCurrentRuntime();
        return joinPoint.proceed();

import java.lang.foreign.Arena;
import java.lang.foreign.MemorySegment;
import java.lang.foreign.ValueLayout;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.ByteBuffer;
import java.nio.CharBuffer;
import java.nio.channels.FileChannel;
import java.nio.charset.CharacterCodingException;
import java.nio.charset.CodingErrorAction;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.security.MessageDigest;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.Objects;

/**
 * Owns UTF-8 bytes in native memory and clears that memory before releasing it.
 */
public final class ConfidentialSegment implements AutoCloseable {
    private final Arena arena;
    private final boolean ownsArena;
    private MemorySegment segment;
    private long byteSize;
    private boolean closed;

    private ConfidentialSegment(Arena arena, MemorySegment segment, long byteSize) {
        this(arena, segment, byteSize, true);
    }

    private ConfidentialSegment(
            Arena arena, MemorySegment segment, long byteSize, boolean ownsArena) {
        this.arena = arena;
        this.segment = segment;
        this.byteSize = byteSize;
        this.ownsArena = ownsArena;
    }

    public static ConfidentialSegment fromUtf8(String value) {
        Objects.requireNonNull(value, &quot;value&quot;);
        long byteSize = utf8Length(value);
        Arena arena = Arena.ofConfined();
        MemorySegment segment = null;
        try {
            segment = arena.allocate(byteSize, 1);
            ByteBuffer output = segment.asByteBuffer();
            var encoder = StandardCharsets.UTF_8.newEncoder()
                    .onMalformedInput(CodingErrorAction.REPLACE)
                    .onUnmappableCharacter(CodingErrorAction.REPLACE)
                    .replaceWith(new byte[] {&apos;?&apos;});
            try {
                var encoded = encoder.encode(CharBuffer.wrap(value), output, true);
                if (encoded.isError()) {
                    encoded.throwException();
                }
                var flushed = encoder.flush(output);
                if (flushed.isError()) {
                    flushed.throwException();
                }
            } catch (CharacterCodingException exception) {
                throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;Unable to encode UTF-8 into native memory.&quot;, exception);
            }
            if (output.position() != byteSize) {
                throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;UTF-8 byte length changed during native encoding.&quot;);
            }
            return new ConfidentialSegment(arena, segment, byteSize);
        } catch (RuntimeException | Error failure) {
            if (segment != null) {
                segment.fill((byte) 0);
            }
            try {
                arena.close();
            } catch (RuntimeException | Error closeFailure) {
                failure.addSuppressed(closeFailure);
            }
            throw failure;
        }
    }

    public static ConfidentialSegment allocate(long byteSize) {
        if (byteSize &lt; 0) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Segment size must not be negative.&quot;);
        }
        Arena arena = Arena.ofConfined();
        try {
            return new ConfidentialSegment(arena, arena.allocate(byteSize, 1), byteSize);
        } catch (RuntimeException | Error failure) {
            try {
                arena.close();
            } catch (RuntimeException | Error closeFailure) {
                failure.addSuppressed(closeFailure);
            }
            throw failure;
        }
    }

    public MemorySegment memory() {
        ensureOpen();
        return segment;
    }

    public long byteSize() {
        ensureOpen();
        return byteSize;
    }

    public ConfidentialSegment slice(long offset, long length) {
        ensureOpen();
        checkRange(offset, length, byteSize);
        return new ConfidentialSegment(arena, segment.asSlice(offset, length), length, false);
    }

    public void truncate(long newByteSize) {
        ensureOpen();
        if (newByteSize &lt; 0 || newByteSize &gt; byteSize) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Truncated segment size is invalid.&quot;);
        }
        segment.asSlice(newByteSize, byteSize - newByteSize).fill((byte) 0);
        segment = segment.asSlice(0, newByteSize);
        byteSize = newByteSize;
    }

    public void writeByte(long offset, byte value) {
        ensureOpen();
        checkRange(offset, 1, byteSize);
        segment.set(ValueLayout.JAVA_BYTE, offset, value);
    }

    public byte readByte(long offset) {
        ensureOpen();
        checkRange(offset, 1, byteSize);
        return segment.get(ValueLayout.JAVA_BYTE, offset);
    }

    public void writeInt(long offset, int value) {
        ensureOpen();
        checkRange(offset, Integer.BYTES, byteSize);
        for (int index = 0; index &lt; Integer.BYTES; index++) {
            segment.set(ValueLayout.JAVA_BYTE, offset + index,
                    (byte) (value &gt;&gt;&gt; (index * Byte.SIZE)));
        }
    }

    public int readInt(long offset) {
        ensureOpen();
        checkRange(offset, Integer.BYTES, byteSize);
        int value = 0;
        for (int index = 0; index &lt; Integer.BYTES; index++) {
            value |= Byte.toUnsignedInt(segment.get(ValueLayout.JAVA_BYTE, offset + index))
                    &lt;&lt; (index * Byte.SIZE);
        }
        return value;
    }

    public void writeLong(long offset, long value) {
        ensureOpen();
        checkRange(offset, Long.BYTES, byteSize);
        for (int index = 0; index &lt; Long.BYTES; index++) {
            segment.set(ValueLayout.JAVA_BYTE, offset + index,
                    (byte) (value &gt;&gt;&gt; (index * Byte.SIZE)));
        }
    }

    public long readLong(long offset) {
        ensureOpen();
        checkRange(offset, Long.BYTES, byteSize);
        long value = 0;
        for (int index = 0; index &lt; Long.BYTES; index++) {
            value |= (long) Byte.toUnsignedInt(
                    segment.get(ValueLayout.JAVA_BYTE, offset + index))
                    &lt;&lt; (index * Byte.SIZE);
        }
        return value;
    }

    public void copyTo(
            ConfidentialSegment target, long sourceOffset, long targetOffset, long length) {
        ensureOpen();
        Objects.requireNonNull(target, &quot;target&quot;).ensureOpen();
        checkRange(sourceOffset, length, byteSize);
        checkRange(targetOffset, length, target.byteSize);
        target.segment.asSlice(targetOffset, length)
                .copyFrom(segment.asSlice(sourceOffset, length));
    }

    public int readFrom(
            FileChannel source, long sourceOffset, long targetOffset, int length)
            throws IOException {
        ensureOpen();
        Objects.requireNonNull(source, &quot;source&quot;);
        checkRange(targetOffset, length, byteSize);
        if (sourceOffset &lt; 0 || length &lt; 0) {
            throw new IndexOutOfBoundsException(&quot;Requested file range is invalid.&quot;);
        }
        if (length == 0) {
            return 0;
        }
        MemorySegment destination = segment.asSlice(targetOffset, length);
        destination.fill((byte) 0);
        return source.read(destination.asByteBuffer(), sourceOffset);
    }

    public void updateDigest(MessageDigest digest, long offset, long length) {
        ensureOpen();
        Objects.requireNonNull(digest, &quot;digest&quot;);
        checkRange(offset, length, byteSize);
        if (length &gt; Integer.MAX_VALUE) {
            throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Digest slice exceeds ByteBuffer capacity.&quot;);
        }
        digest.update(segment.asSlice(offset, length).asByteBuffer());
    }

    public void writeUtf8(String value, long targetOffset) {
        ensureOpen();
        Objects.requireNonNull(value, &quot;value&quot;);
        long requiredBytes = utf8Length(value);
        checkRange(targetOffset, requiredBytes, byteSize);
        long offset = targetOffset;
        for (int index = 0; index &lt; value.length();) {
            char current = value.charAt(index++);
            int codePoint;
            if (Character.isHighSurrogate(current)
                    &amp;&amp; index &lt; value.length()
                    &amp;&amp; Character.isLowSurrogate(value.charAt(index))) {
                codePoint = Character.toCodePoint(current, value.charAt(index++));
            } else if (Character.isSurrogate(current)) {
                writeByte(offset++, (byte) &apos;?&apos;);
                continue;
            } else {
                codePoint = current;
            }
            if (codePoint &lt;= 0x7f) {
                writeByte(offset++, (byte) codePoint);
            } else if (codePoint &lt;= 0x7ff) {
                writeByte(offset++, (byte) (0xc0 | (codePoint &gt;&gt;&gt; 6)));
                writeByte(offset++, (byte) (0x80 | (codePoint &amp; 0x3f)));
            } else if (codePoint &lt;= 0xffff) {
                writeByte(offset++, (byte) (0xe0 | (codePoint &gt;&gt;&gt; 12)));
                writeByte(offset++, (byte) (0x80 | ((codePoint &gt;&gt;&gt; 6) &amp; 0x3f)));
                writeByte(offset++, (byte) (0x80 | (codePoint &amp; 0x3f)));
            } else {
                writeByte(offset++, (byte) (0xf0 | (codePoint &gt;&gt;&gt; 18)));
                writeByte(offset++, (byte) (0x80 | ((codePoint &gt;&gt;&gt; 12) &amp; 0x3f)));
                writeByte(offset++, (byte) (0x80 | ((codePoint &gt;&gt;&gt; 6) &amp; 0x3f)));
                writeByte(offset++, (byte) (0x80 | (codePoint &amp; 0x3f)));
            }
        }
    }

    public String toUtf8String() {
        ensureOpen();
        byte[] encoded = segment.toArray(ValueLayout.JAVA_BYTE);
        try {
            return new String(encoded, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        } finally {
            Arrays.fill(encoded, (byte) 0);
        }
    }

    public static long utf8Length(String value) {
        Objects.requireNonNull(value, &quot;value&quot;);
        long length = 0;
        for (int offset = 0; offset &lt; value.length();) {
            char current = value.charAt(offset++);
            if (Character.isHighSurrogate(current)
                    &amp;&amp; offset &lt; value.length()
                    &amp;&amp; Character.isLowSurrogate(value.charAt(offset))) {
                offset++;
                length += 4;
            } else if (Character.isSurrogate(current) || current &lt;= 0x7f) {
                length++;
            } else if (current &lt;= 0x7ff) {
                length += 2;
            } else {
                length += 3;
            }
        }
        return length;
    }

    public void zeroize() {
        ensureOpen();
        segment.fill((byte) 0);
    }

    @Override
    public void close() {
        if (closed) {
            return;
        }
        segment.fill((byte) 0);
        if (ownsArena) {
            arena.close();
        }
        closed = true;
    }

    private static void checkRange(long offset, long length, long capacity) {
        if (offset &lt; 0 || length &lt; 0 || offset &gt; capacity - length) {
            throw new IndexOutOfBoundsException(&quot;Requested range exceeds the segment bounds.&quot;);
        }
    }

    private void ensureOpen() {
        if (closed) {
            throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;Confidential segment is closed.&quot;);
        }
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/main/native/azure-attestation/azure_guest_attestation_ffi.cpp">#include &quot;azure_guest_attestation_ffi.h&quot;

#include &lt;AttestationClient.h&gt;
#include &lt;nlohmann/json.hpp&gt;
#include &lt;openssl/crypto.h&gt;
#include &lt;openssl/evp.h&gt;

#include &lt;algorithm&gt;
#include &lt;cstdint&gt;
#include &lt;cstring&gt;
#include &lt;exception&gt;
#include &lt;memory&gt;
#include &lt;mutex&gt;
#include &lt;stdexcept&gt;
#include &lt;string&gt;
#include &lt;vector&gt;

namespace {
using json = nlohmann::json;

constexpr std::size_t kMaximumJwtBytes = 64 * 1024;
constexpr std::size_t kMaximumReleaseValueBytes = 1&apos;000&apos;000;
constexpr std::size_t kRsa2048Bytes = 256;
constexpr std::size_t kAes256Bytes = 32;
std::mutex g_attestationMutex;

class QuietLogger final : public attest::AttestationLogger {
public:
    void Log(const char*, LogLevel, const char*, const int, const char*, ...) override {
    }
};

class ClientSession final {
public:
    ClientSession() {
        auto* logger = new QuietLogger();
        if (!Initialize(logger, &amp;client_)) {
            Uninitialize();
            throw std::runtime_error(&quot;Microsoft guest-attestation client initialization failed.&quot;);
        }
    }

    ~ClientSession() {
        Uninitialize();
    }

    ClientSession(const ClientSession&amp;) = delete;
    ClientSession&amp; operator=(const ClientSession&amp;) = delete;

    AttestationClient* get() const {
        return client_;
    }

private:
    AttestationClient* client_ = nullptr;
};

struct LibraryBufferDeleter {
    AttestationClient* client;
    std::size_t sensitiveBytes = 0;

    void operator()(unsigned char* value) const {
        if (value != nullptr) {
            if (sensitiveBytes &gt; 0) {
                OPENSSL_cleanse(value, sensitiveBytes);
            }
            client-&gt;Free(value);
        }
    }
};

std::vector&lt;unsigned char&gt; decodeBase64Url(const std::string&amp; input) {
    if (input.empty() || input.size() &gt; kMaximumReleaseValueBytes) {
        throw std::runtime_error(&quot;Azure Key Vault release value contains invalid base64url data.&quot;);
    }
    std::string normalized = input;
    std::replace(normalized.begin(), normalized.end(), &apos;-&apos;, &apos;+&apos;);
    std::replace(normalized.begin(), normalized.end(), &apos;_&apos;, &apos;/&apos;);
    if (normalized.find_first_not_of(
                &quot;ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/=&quot;)
            != std::string::npos) {
        throw std::runtime_error(&quot;Azure Key Vault release value contains invalid base64url data.&quot;);
    }
    const auto existingPadding = normalized.find(&apos;=&apos;);
    if (existingPadding != std::string::npos
            &amp;&amp; normalized.find_first_not_of(&apos;=&apos;, existingPadding) != std::string::npos) {
        throw std::runtime_error(&quot;Azure Key Vault release value contains invalid base64url data.&quot;);
    }
    const std::size_t padding = existingPadding == std::string::npos
            ? 0
            : normalized.size() - existingPadding;
    if (padding &gt; 2 || normalized.size() % 4 == 1
            || (padding &gt; 0 &amp;&amp; normalized.size() % 4 != 0)) {
        throw std::runtime_error(&quot;Azure Key Vault release value contains invalid base64url data.&quot;);
    }
    if (padding == 0) {
        normalized.append((4 - normalized.size() % 4) % 4, &apos;=&apos;);
    }
    std::vector&lt;unsigned char&gt; decoded((normalized.size() / 4) * 3);
    const int decodedSize = EVP_DecodeBlock(
            decoded.data(),
            reinterpret_cast&lt;const unsigned char*&gt;(normalized.data()),
            static_cast&lt;int&gt;(normalized.size()));
    if (decodedSize &lt; 0 || static_cast&lt;std::size_t&gt;(decodedSize) &lt; padding) {
        throw std::runtime_error(&quot;Azure Key Vault release value contains invalid base64url data.&quot;);
    }
    decoded.resize(static_cast&lt;std::size_t&gt;(decodedSize) - padding);
    return decoded;
}

std::string bytesToString(const std::vector&lt;unsigned char&gt;&amp; bytes) {
    return std::string(bytes.begin(), bytes.end());
}

std::vector&lt;unsigned char&gt; extractCiphertext(const std::string&amp; releaseValue) {
    if (releaseValue.size() &gt; kMaximumReleaseValueBytes) {
        throw std::runtime_error(&quot;Azure Key Vault release value exceeds the supported size.&quot;);
    }
    const auto firstDot = releaseValue.find(&apos;.&apos;);
    const auto secondDot = firstDot == std::string::npos
            ? std::string::npos
            : releaseValue.find(&apos;.&apos;, firstDot + 1);
    if (firstDot == std::string::npos || secondDot == std::string::npos
            || releaseValue.find(&apos;.&apos;, secondDot + 1) != std::string::npos) {
        throw std::runtime_error(&quot;Azure Key Vault release value is not a compact signed token.&quot;);
    }

    const std::string payloadPart =
            releaseValue.substr(firstDot + 1, secondDot - firstDot - 1);
    const std::string payload = bytesToString(decodeBase64Url(payloadPart));
    const json releasePayload = json::parse(payload);
    const auto keyHsm = releasePayload.at(&quot;response&quot;)
            .at(&quot;key&quot;).at(&quot;key&quot;).at(&quot;key_hsm&quot;).get&lt;std::string&gt;();
    const std::string keyHsmJson = bytesToString(decodeBase64Url(keyHsm));
    const json encryptedKey = json::parse(keyHsmJson);
    const auto ciphertext = encryptedKey.at(&quot;ciphertext&quot;).get&lt;std::string&gt;();
    auto decodedCiphertext = decodeBase64Url(ciphertext);
    if (decodedCiphertext.size() &lt;= kRsa2048Bytes) {
        OPENSSL_cleanse(decodedCiphertext.data(), decodedCiphertext.size());
        throw std::runtime_error(&quot;Azure Key Vault wrapped key has an invalid size.&quot;);
    }
    const std::size_t wrappedKeySize = decodedCiphertext.size() - kRsa2048Bytes;
    if (wrappedKeySize &lt; 16 || wrappedKeySize % 8 != 0) {
        OPENSSL_cleanse(decodedCiphertext.data(), decodedCiphertext.size());
        throw std::runtime_error(&quot;Azure Key Vault wrapped key has an invalid size.&quot;);
    }
    return decodedCiphertext;
}

std::vector&lt;unsigned char&gt; unwrapAes256Key(
        AttestationClient* client,
        const std::vector&lt;unsigned char&gt;&amp; ciphertext) {
    unsigned char* transferKey = nullptr;
    uint32_t transferKeySize = 0;
    const auto result = client-&gt;Decrypt(
            attest::EncryptionType::NONE,
            ciphertext.data(),
            static_cast&lt;uint32_t&gt;(kRsa2048Bytes),
            nullptr,
            0,
            &amp;transferKey,
            &amp;transferKeySize,
            attest::RsaScheme::RsaOaep,
            attest::RsaHashAlg::RsaSha256);

    std::unique_ptr&lt;unsigned char, LibraryBufferDeleter&gt; transferKeyOwner(
            transferKey, LibraryBufferDeleter{client, transferKeySize});
    if (result.code_ != attest::AttestationResult::ErrorCode::SUCCESS
            || transferKey == nullptr || transferKeySize != kAes256Bytes) {
        throw std::runtime_error(&quot;TPM-backed RSA-OAEP key unwrap failed.&quot;);
    }

    const std::size_t wrappedKeySize = ciphertext.size() - kRsa2048Bytes;
    std::vector&lt;unsigned char&gt; plaintext(wrappedKeySize);
    EVP_CIPHER_CTX* rawContext = EVP_CIPHER_CTX_new();
    if (rawContext == nullptr) {
        throw std::runtime_error(&quot;Unable to initialize AES key unwrap.&quot;);
    }
    std::unique_ptr&lt;EVP_CIPHER_CTX, decltype(&amp;EVP_CIPHER_CTX_free)&gt; context(
            rawContext, EVP_CIPHER_CTX_free);
    EVP_CIPHER_CTX_set_flags(context.get(), EVP_CIPHER_CTX_FLAG_WRAP_ALLOW);

    int written = 0;
    int finalWritten = 0;
    const unsigned char* wrappedKey = ciphertext.data() + kRsa2048Bytes;
    if (EVP_DecryptInit_ex(context.get(), EVP_aes_256_wrap_pad(), nullptr, nullptr, nullptr) != 1
            || EVP_DecryptInit_ex(
                    context.get(), nullptr, nullptr, transferKey, nullptr) != 1
            || EVP_DecryptUpdate(
                    context.get(),
                    plaintext.data(),
                    &amp;written,
                    wrappedKey,
                    static_cast&lt;int&gt;(wrappedKeySize)) != 1
            || EVP_DecryptFinal_ex(
                    context.get(), plaintext.data() + written, &amp;finalWritten) != 1) {
        OPENSSL_cleanse(plaintext.data(), plaintext.size());
        throw std::runtime_error(&quot;AES-256 key unwrap failed.&quot;);
    }
    plaintext.resize(static_cast&lt;std::size_t&gt;(written + finalWritten));
    if (plaintext.size() != kAes256Bytes) {
        OPENSSL_cleanse(plaintext.data(), plaintext.size());
        throw std::runtime_error(&quot;Released context key is not 256 bits.&quot;);
    }
    return plaintext;
}

}  // namespace

int opentron_azure_attest(
        const char* endpointValue,
        const char* nonceValue,
        unsigned char* output,
        uint64_t outputCapacity,
        uint64_t* outputLength) noexcept {
    if (outputLength != nullptr) {
        *outputLength = 0;
    }
    if (endpointValue == nullptr || nonceValue == nullptr || output == nullptr
            || outputLength == nullptr || outputCapacity &gt; SIZE_MAX) {
        return 1;
    }
    try {
        const std::string endpoint(endpointValue);
        const std::string nonce(nonceValue);
        if (endpoint.empty() || nonce.size() != 43
                || nonce.find_first_not_of(
                        &quot;ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789_-&quot;)
                        != std::string::npos) {
            throw std::invalid_argument(&quot;Azure attestation endpoint or nonce is invalid.&quot;);
        }

        std::lock_guard&lt;std::mutex&gt; lock(g_attestationMutex);
        ClientSession session;
        attest::ClientParameters parameters{};
        parameters.version = CLIENT_PARAMS_VERSION;
        parameters.attestation_endpoint_url =
                reinterpret_cast&lt;const unsigned char*&gt;(endpoint.c_str());
        const json clientPayload{{&quot;nonce&quot;, nonce}};
        const std::string clientPayloadString = clientPayload.dump();
        parameters.client_payload =
                reinterpret_cast&lt;const unsigned char*&gt;(clientPayloadString.c_str());

        unsigned char* rawToken = nullptr;
        const auto result = session.get()-&gt;Attest(parameters, &amp;rawToken);
        std::unique_ptr&lt;unsigned char, LibraryBufferDeleter&gt; tokenOwner(
                rawToken, LibraryBufferDeleter{session.get(), 0});
        if (result.code_ != attest::AttestationResult::ErrorCode::SUCCESS
                || rawToken == nullptr) {
            throw std::runtime_error(&quot;Microsoft guest attestation failed.&quot;);
        }
        const std::string token(reinterpret_cast&lt;const char*&gt;(rawToken));
        if (token.empty() || token.size() &gt; kMaximumJwtBytes) {
            throw std::runtime_error(&quot;Microsoft guest attestation returned invalid evidence.&quot;);
        }
        if (token.size() &gt; outputCapacity) {
            return 2;
        }
        std::memcpy(output, token.data(), token.size());
        *outputLength = token.size();
        return 0;
    } catch (...) {
        OPENSSL_cleanse(output, static_cast&lt;std::size_t&gt;(
                std::min(outputCapacity, static_cast&lt;uint64_t&gt;(kMaximumJwtBytes))));
        return 1;
    }
}

int opentron_azure_unwrap_key(
        const char* releaseValue,
        unsigned char* output,
        uint64_t outputCapacity,
        uint64_t* outputLength) noexcept {
    if (outputLength != nullptr) {
        *outputLength = 0;
    }
    if (releaseValue == nullptr || output == nullptr || outputLength == nullptr
            || outputCapacity &gt; SIZE_MAX) {
        return 1;
    }
    try {
        const std::string releaseJwt(releaseValue);
        std::lock_guard&lt;std::mutex&gt; lock(g_attestationMutex);
        ClientSession session;
        auto ciphertext = extractCiphertext(releaseJwt);
        auto key = unwrapAes256Key(session.get(), ciphertext);
        OPENSSL_cleanse(ciphertext.data(), ciphertext.size());

        if (key.size() &gt; outputCapacity) {
            OPENSSL_cleanse(key.data(), key.size());
            return 2;
        }
        std::memcpy(output, key.data(), key.size());
        *outputLength = key.size();
        OPENSSL_cleanse(key.data(), key.size());
        return 0;
    } catch (...) {
        OPENSSL_cleanse(output, static_cast&lt;std::size_t&gt;(
                std::min(outputCapacity, static_cast&lt;uint64_t&gt;(kAes256Bytes))));
        return 1;
    }

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

import javax.crypto.spec.SecretKeySpec;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.ConfidentialGatewayService;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.SecureContextKey;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.SecureContextKeyProvider;
import org.springframework.boot.test.context.runner.ApplicationContextRunner;

class AzureOidcGatewayApiConfigurationTest {
    private final ApplicationContextRunner contextRunner = new ApplicationContextRunner()
            .withUserConfiguration(
                    AzureOidcGatewayApiConfiguration.class,
                    ConfidentialGatewayController.class,
                    ConfidentialGatewayApiExceptionHandler.class)
            .withBean(ObjectMapper.class, ObjectMapper::new)
            .withBean(
                    SecureContextKeyProvider.class,
                    () -&gt; new SecureContextKeyProvider() {
                        private final SecureContextKey key = new SecureContextKey(
                                &quot;test-key&quot;,
                                new SecretKeySpec(new byte[32], &quot;AES&quot;));

                        @Override
                        public SecureContextKey currentKey() {
                            return key;
                        }

                        @Override
                        public java.util.Optional&lt;SecureContextKey&gt; findKey(String id) {
                            return key.id().equals(id)
                                    ? java.util.Optional.of(key)
                                    : java.util.Optional.empty();
                        }
                    })
            .withBean(
                    GatewayCredentialAuthenticator.class,
                    () -&gt; credential -&gt; java.util.Optional.empty());

    @Test
    void createsAzureBackedGatewayOnlyWhenOidcAndExplicitEnablementArePresent() {
        contextRunner
                .withPropertyValues(
                        &quot;spring.profiles.active=azure-cvm,gateway-oidc&quot;,
                        &quot;confidential.gateway.enabled=true&quot;)
                .run(context -&gt; {
                    assertThat(context).hasNotFailed();
                    assertThat(context).hasSingleBean(ConfidentialGatewayService.class);
                    assertThat(context).hasSingleBean(ConfidentialGatewayController.class);
                    assertThat(context).hasSingleBean(GatewayQuotaService.class);
                });
    }

    @Test
    void doesNotCreateAzureGatewayWithoutBothProfilesAndExplicitEnablement() {
        contextRunner
                .withPropertyValues(
                        &quot;spring.profiles.active=azure-cvm&quot;,
                        &quot;confidential.gateway.enabled=true&quot;)
                .run(context -&gt; {
                    assertThat(context).hasNotFailed();
                    assertThat(context).doesNotHaveBean(ConfidentialGatewayService.class);
                    assertThat(context).doesNotHaveBean(ConfidentialGatewayController.class);
                });

        contextRunner
                .withPropertyValues(&quot;spring.profiles.active=azure-cvm,gateway-oidc&quot;)
                .run(context -&gt; {
                    assertThat(context).hasNotFailed();
                    assertThat(context).doesNotHaveBean(ConfidentialGatewayService.class);
                    assertThat(context).doesNotHaveBean(ConfidentialGatewayController.class);
                });
    }

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

import java.util.Set;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.mock.web.MockHttpServletRequest;
import org.springframework.mock.web.MockHttpServletResponse;

class ConfidentialGatewayApiKeyInterceptorTest {
    private static final String TOKEN = &quot;0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef&quot;;

    @Test
    void acceptsConfiguredBearerToken() throws Exception {
        ConfidentialGatewayApiKeyInterceptor interceptor =
                new ConfidentialGatewayApiKeyInterceptor(authenticator(TOKEN));
        MockHttpServletRequest request = request();
        request.addHeader(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, &quot;Bearer &quot; + TOKEN);
        MockHttpServletResponse response = new MockHttpServletResponse();

        assertTrue(interceptor.preHandle(request, response, new Object()));
        assertEquals(200, response.getStatus());
        assertEquals(&quot;tenant-test&quot;,
                GatewayRequestContext.requirePrincipal(request).tenantId());
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsMissingBearerToken() throws Exception {
        ConfidentialGatewayApiKeyInterceptor interceptor =
                new ConfidentialGatewayApiKeyInterceptor(authenticator(TOKEN));
        MockHttpServletRequest request = request();
        MockHttpServletResponse response = new MockHttpServletResponse();

        assertFalse(interceptor.preHandle(request, response, new Object()));
        assertEquals(401, response.getStatus());
        assertEquals(&quot;Bearer&quot;, response.getHeader(HttpHeaders.WWW_AUTHENTICATE));
        assertEquals(&quot;no-store&quot;, response.getHeader(HttpHeaders.CACHE_CONTROL));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsIncorrectBearerToken() throws Exception {
        ConfidentialGatewayApiKeyInterceptor interceptor =
                new ConfidentialGatewayApiKeyInterceptor(authenticator(TOKEN));
        MockHttpServletRequest request = request();
        request.addHeader(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, &quot;Bearer &quot; + &quot;f&quot;.repeat(32));
        MockHttpServletResponse response = new MockHttpServletResponse();

        assertFalse(interceptor.preHandle(request, response, new Object()));
        assertEquals(401, response.getStatus());
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsShortConfiguredToken() {
        org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows(
                IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -&gt; new LocalStaticBearerCredentialAuthenticator(
                        &quot;too-short&quot;, &quot;tenant-test&quot;));
    }

    @Test
    void requiresScopeForOperation() throws Exception {
        GatewayCredentialAuthenticator authenticator = token -&gt; java.util.Optional.of(
                new GatewayPrincipal(&quot;subject-1&quot;, &quot;tenant-test&quot;, Set.of(&quot;gateway:sanitize&quot;)));
        ConfidentialGatewayApiKeyInterceptor interceptor =
                new ConfidentialGatewayApiKeyInterceptor(authenticator);
        MockHttpServletRequest request = request();
        request.setRequestURI(&quot;/v1/confidential/desanitize&quot;);
        request.addHeader(HttpHeaders.AUTHORIZATION, &quot;Bearer token&quot;);
        MockHttpServletResponse response = new MockHttpServletResponse();

        assertFalse(interceptor.preHandle(request, response, new Object()));
        assertEquals(403, response.getStatus());
    }

    @Test
    void hidesUnknownConfidentialRoutes() throws Exception {
        ConfidentialGatewayApiKeyInterceptor interceptor =
                new ConfidentialGatewayApiKeyInterceptor(authenticator(TOKEN));
        MockHttpServletRequest request = request();
        request.setRequestURI(&quot;/v1/confidential/admin&quot;);
        MockHttpServletResponse response = new MockHttpServletResponse();

        assertFalse(interceptor.preHandle(request, response, new Object()));
        assertEquals(404, response.getStatus());
    }

    private static GatewayCredentialAuthenticator authenticator(String token) {
        return new LocalStaticBearerCredentialAuthenticator(token, &quot;tenant-test&quot;);
    }

    private static MockHttpServletRequest request() {
        MockHttpServletRequest request = new MockHttpServletRequest(&quot;POST&quot;, &quot;/v1/confidential/sanitize&quot;);
        request.setRequestURI(&quot;/v1/confidential/sanitize&quot;);
        return request;
    }

import static org.hamcrest.Matchers.containsString;
import static org.hamcrest.Matchers.not;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.header;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;

import java.security.SecureRandom;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.ZoneOffset;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

import javax.crypto.KeyGenerator;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.AesGcmSecureContextCodec;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.ConfidentialGatewayService;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.PrototypeRuleBasedPiiDetector;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.SecureContextKey;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.SecureContextKeyProvider;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.MockMvc;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.setup.MockMvcBuilders;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

class ConfidentialGatewayControllerTest {
    private static final String TOKEN = &quot;0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef&quot;;
    private MockMvc mockMvc;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() throws Exception {
        Clock clock = Clock.fixed(Instant.parse(&quot;2026-01-01T00:00:00Z&quot;), ZoneOffset.UTC);
        var service = buildGatewayService(clock);
        var controller = new ConfidentialGatewayController(
                service,
                new FixedWindowGatewayQuotaService(
                        100, 10_000_000, 100, Duration.ofMinutes(1), clock));
        var authenticator =
                new LocalStaticBearerCredentialAuthenticator(TOKEN, &quot;tenant-test&quot;);
        mockMvc = MockMvcBuilders.standaloneSetup(controller)
                .setControllerAdvice(new ConfidentialGatewayApiExceptionHandler())
                .addInterceptors(new ConfidentialGatewayApiKeyInterceptor(authenticator))
                .build();
    }

    private static ConfidentialGatewayService buildGatewayService() throws Exception {
        return buildGatewayService(
                Clock.fixed(Instant.parse(&quot;2026-01-01T00:00:00Z&quot;), ZoneOffset.UTC));
    }

    private static ConfidentialGatewayService buildGatewayService(Clock clock) throws Exception {
        var generator = KeyGenerator.getInstance(&quot;AES&quot;);
        generator.init(256);
        SecureContextKey key = new SecureContextKey(&quot;test-key&quot;, generator.generateKey());
        SecureContextKeyProvider keyProvider = new SecureContextKeyProvider() {
            @Override
            public SecureContextKey currentKey() {
                return key;
            }

            @Override
            public Optional&lt;SecureContextKey&gt; findKey(String id) {
                return key.id().equals(id) ? Optional.of(key) : Optional.empty();
            }
        };
        var codec = new AesGcmSecureContextCodec(
                keyProvider, new ObjectMapper(), new SecureRandom(), clock);
        return new ConfidentialGatewayService(
                new PrototypeRuleBasedPiiDetector(),
                codec,
                Duration.ofMinutes(10),
                new SecureRandom(),
                clock);
    }

    @Test
    void sanitizeAndDesanitizeRequireBearerAuthAndDoNotCacheResponses() throws Exception {
        var sanitizeResponse = mockMvc.perform(post(&quot;/v1/confidential/sanitize&quot;)
                        .header(&quot;Authorization&quot;, &quot;Bearer &quot; + TOKEN)
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content(&quot;{\&quot;text\&quot;:\&quot;Contact ada@example.com\&quot;}&quot;))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(header().string(&quot;Cache-Control&quot;, containsString(&quot;no-store&quot;)))
                .andExpect(jsonPath(&quot;$.sanitizedText&quot;, not(containsString(&quot;ada@example.com&quot;))))
                .andReturn()
                .getResponse()
                .getContentAsString();

        var json = new ObjectMapper().readTree(sanitizeResponse);
        String sanitizedText = json.get(&quot;sanitizedText&quot;).asText();
        String secureContext = json.get(&quot;secureContext&quot;).asText();

        mockMvc.perform(post(&quot;/v1/confidential/desanitize&quot;)
                        .header(&quot;Authorization&quot;, &quot;Bearer &quot; + TOKEN)
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content(new ObjectMapper().writeValueAsString(
                                new DesanitizeRequest(sanitizedText, secureContext))))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(header().string(&quot;Cache-Control&quot;, containsString(&quot;no-store&quot;)))
                .andExpect(jsonPath(&quot;$.desanitizedText&quot;).value(&quot;Contact ada@example.com&quot;));
    }

    @Test
    void streamsRestoredJsonWithoutChangingEscapingOrResponseShape() throws Exception {
        String original = &quot;Contact \&quot;ada@example.com\&quot;\n😀&quot;;
        String sanitizeBody = mockMvc.perform(post(&quot;/v1/confidential/sanitize&quot;)
                        .header(&quot;Authorization&quot;, &quot;Bearer &quot; + TOKEN)
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content(new ObjectMapper().writeValueAsString(
                                java.util.Map.of(&quot;text&quot;, original))))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andReturn()
                .getResponse()
                .getContentAsString();
        var sanitized = new ObjectMapper().readTree(sanitizeBody);
        String request = new ObjectMapper().writeValueAsString(
                java.util.Map.of(
                        &quot;modelResponse&quot;, sanitized.path(&quot;sanitizedText&quot;).asText(),
                        &quot;secureContext&quot;, sanitized.path(&quot;secureContext&quot;).asText()));

        String response = mockMvc.perform(post(&quot;/v1/confidential/desanitize&quot;)
                        .header(&quot;Authorization&quot;, &quot;Bearer &quot; + TOKEN)
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content(request))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(header().string(&quot;Cache-Control&quot;, containsString(&quot;no-store&quot;)))
                .andReturn()
                .getResponse()
                .getContentAsString();

        assertEquals(original,
                new ObjectMapper().readTree(response).path(&quot;desanitizedText&quot;).asText());
    }

    @Test
    void streamsSanitizedJsonWithoutChangingResponseShape() throws Exception {
        String original = &quot;Contact \&quot;ada@example.com\&quot;\n😀&quot;;
        String response = mockMvc.perform(post(&quot;/v1/confidential/sanitize&quot;)
                        .header(&quot;Authorization&quot;, &quot;Bearer &quot; + TOKEN)
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content(new ObjectMapper().writeValueAsString(
                                java.util.Map.of(&quot;text&quot;, original))))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(header().string(&quot;Cache-Control&quot;, containsString(&quot;no-store&quot;)))
                .andReturn()
                .getResponse()
                .getContentAsString();

        var json = new ObjectMapper().readTree(response);
        assertFalse(json.path(&quot;sanitizedText&quot;).asText().contains(&quot;ada@example.com&quot;));
        assertTrue(json.path(&quot;secureContext&quot;).asText().startsWith(&quot;v2.&quot;));
        assertEquals(&quot;prototype-rules-1&quot;, json.path(&quot;detectorVersion&quot;).asText());
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsRequestsWithoutBearerCredentials() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(post(&quot;/v1/confidential/sanitize&quot;)
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content(&quot;{\&quot;text\&quot;:\&quot;Contact ada@example.com\&quot;}&quot;))
                .andExpect(status().isUnauthorized())
                .andExpect(header().string(&quot;WWW-Authenticate&quot;, &quot;Bearer&quot;))

    }

    @Test
    void rejectsInvalidRequestBodiesWithoutEchoingInput() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(post(&quot;/v1/confidential/sanitize&quot;)
                        .header(&quot;Authorization&quot;, &quot;Bearer &quot; + TOKEN)
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content(&quot;{\&quot;text\&quot;:null}&quot;))
                .andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
                .andExpect(jsonPath(&quot;$.code&quot;).value(&quot;INVALID_REQUEST&quot;))
                .andExpect(jsonPath(&quot;$.message&quot;, not(containsString(&quot;ada@example.com&quot;))));

        mockMvc.perform(post(&quot;/v1/confidential/sanitize&quot;)
                        .header(&quot;Authorization&quot;, &quot;Bearer &quot; + TOKEN)
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content(&quot;{\&quot;text\&quot;:\&quot;valid after malformed input\&quot;}&quot;))
                .andExpect(status().isOk());
    }

    @Test
    void mapsMalformedJsonToSafeClientError() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(post(&quot;/v1/confidential/sanitize&quot;)
                        .header(&quot;Authorization&quot;, &quot;Bearer &quot; + TOKEN)
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)

                .andExpect(jsonPath(&quot;$.message&quot;).value(&quot;Request body is invalid.&quot;));
    }

    @Test
    void returns429WhenTenantQuotaIsExceeded() throws Exception {
        var quota = new FixedWindowGatewayQuotaService(
                1,
                100,
                10,
                Duration.ofMinutes(1),
                Clock.fixed(Instant.parse(&quot;2026-01-01T00:00:00Z&quot;), ZoneOffset.UTC));
        var controller = new ConfidentialGatewayController(
