                new LocalStaticBearerCredentialAuthenticator(TOKEN, &quot;tenant-test&quot;);
        var quotaMvc = MockMvcBuilders.standaloneSetup(controller)
                .setControllerAdvice(new ConfidentialGatewayApiExceptionHandler())
                .addInterceptors(new ConfidentialGatewayApiKeyInterceptor(authenticator))
                .build();

        quotaMvc.perform(post(&quot;/v1/confidential/sanitize&quot;)
                        .header(&quot;Authorization&quot;, &quot;Bearer &quot; + TOKEN)
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content(&quot;{\&quot;text\&quot;:\&quot;first\&quot;}&quot;))
                .andExpect(status().isOk());
        quotaMvc.perform(post(&quot;/v1/confidential/sanitize&quot;)
                        .header(&quot;Authorization&quot;, &quot;Bearer &quot; + TOKEN)
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content(&quot;{\&quot;text\&quot;:\&quot;second\&quot;}&quot;))
                .andExpect(status().isTooManyRequests())
                .andExpect(jsonPath(&quot;$.code&quot;).value(&quot;QUOTA_EXCEEDED&quot;));
    }

    @Test
    void tenantIdentityComesFromAuthenticatedCredentialAndContextsCannotCrossTenants()
            throws Exception {
        GatewayCredentialAuthenticator authenticator = credential -&gt; switch (credential) {
            case &quot;tenant-one-token-0123456789abcdef&quot; -&gt; Optional.of(new GatewayPrincipal(
                    &quot;subject-one&quot;,
                    &quot;tenant-one&quot;,
                    java.util.Set.of(&quot;gateway:sanitize&quot;, &quot;gateway:desanitize&quot;)));
            case &quot;tenant-two-token-0123456789abcdef&quot; -&gt; Optional.of(new GatewayPrincipal(
                    &quot;subject-two&quot;,
                    &quot;tenant-two&quot;,
                    java.util.Set.of(&quot;gateway:sanitize&quot;, &quot;gateway:desanitize&quot;)));
            default -&gt; Optional.empty();
        };
        var controller = new ConfidentialGatewayController(
                buildGatewayService(),
                new FixedWindowGatewayQuotaService(
                        100,
                        10_000_000,
                        100,
                        Duration.ofMinutes(1),
                        Clock.fixed(Instant.parse(&quot;2026-01-01T00:00:00Z&quot;), ZoneOffset.UTC)));
        var multiTenantMvc = MockMvcBuilders.standaloneSetup(controller)
                .setControllerAdvice(new ConfidentialGatewayApiExceptionHandler())
                .addInterceptors(new ConfidentialGatewayApiKeyInterceptor(authenticator))
                .build();
        String response = multiTenantMvc.perform(post(&quot;/v1/confidential/sanitize&quot;)
                        .header(&quot;Authorization&quot;, &quot;Bearer tenant-one-token-0123456789abcdef&quot;)
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content(&quot;{\&quot;text\&quot;:\&quot;Contact ada@example.com\&quot;,\&quot;tenantId\&quot;:\&quot;tenant-two\&quot;}&quot;))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andReturn()
                .getResponse()
                .getContentAsString();
        var json = new ObjectMapper().readTree(response);

        multiTenantMvc.perform(post(&quot;/v1/confidential/desanitize&quot;)
                        .header(&quot;Authorization&quot;, &quot;Bearer tenant-two-token-0123456789abcdef&quot;)
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content(new ObjectMapper().writeValueAsString(
                                new DesanitizeRequest(
                                        json.path(&quot;sanitizedText&quot;).asText(),
                                        json.path(&quot;secureContext&quot;).asText()))))
                .andExpect(status().isBadRequest())
                .andExpect(jsonPath(&quot;$.code&quot;).value(&quot;INVALID_CONTEXT&quot;));
    }

    @Test
    void batchEndpointsSanitizeAndRestoreSegments() throws Exception {
        var sanitizeResponse = mockMvc.perform(post(&quot;/v1/confidential/sanitize/batch&quot;)
                        .header(&quot;Authorization&quot;, &quot;Bearer &quot; + TOKEN)
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content(&quot;{\&quot;segments\&quot;:[\&quot;Email ada@example.com\&quot;,\&quot;Phone 212-555-0199\&quot;]}&quot;))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(header().string(&quot;Cache-Control&quot;, containsString(&quot;no-store&quot;)))
                .andExpect(jsonPath(&quot;$.sanitizedSegments.length()&quot;).value(2))
                .andExpect(jsonPath(&quot;$.sanitizedSegments[0]&quot;, not(containsString(&quot;ada@example.com&quot;))))
                .andReturn()
                .getResponse()
                .getContentAsString();

        var json = new ObjectMapper().readTree(sanitizeResponse);
        var request = new BatchDesanitizeRequest(
                List.of(
                        json.get(&quot;sanitizedSegments&quot;).get(0).asText(),
                        json.get(&quot;sanitizedSegments&quot;).get(1).asText()),
                json.get(&quot;secureContext&quot;).asText());

        mockMvc.perform(post(&quot;/v1/confidential/desanitize/batch&quot;)
                        .header(&quot;Authorization&quot;, &quot;Bearer &quot; + TOKEN)
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content(new ObjectMapper().writeValueAsString(request)))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath(&quot;$.desanitizedResponses[0]&quot;).value(&quot;Email ada@example.com&quot;))
                .andExpect(jsonPath(&quot;$.desanitizedResponses[1]&quot;).value(&quot;Phone 212-555-0199&quot;));
    }

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.time.ZoneOffset;
import java.util.Set;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class FixedWindowGatewayQuotaServiceTest {
    private static final GatewayPrincipal TENANT_ONE =
            new GatewayPrincipal(&quot;user-one&quot;, &quot;tenant-one&quot;, Set.of(&quot;gateway:sanitize&quot;));
    private static final GatewayPrincipal TENANT_TWO =
            new GatewayPrincipal(&quot;user-two&quot;, &quot;tenant-two&quot;, Set.of(&quot;gateway:sanitize&quot;));
    private static final Instant START = Instant.parse(&quot;2026-10-17T12:00:00Z&quot;);

    @Test
    void enforcesRequestAndTextBudgetsIndependentlyPerTenant() {
        var service = new FixedWindowGatewayQuotaService(
                2, 10, 10, Duration.ofMinutes(1), Clock.fixed(START, ZoneOffset.UTC));

        service.checkAndConsume(TENANT_ONE, 6);
        assertThrows(
                GatewayQuotaExceededException.class,
                () -&gt; service.checkAndConsume(TENANT_ONE, 5));
        service.checkAndConsume(TENANT_ONE, 4);
        assertThrows(
                GatewayQuotaExceededException.class,
                () -&gt; service.checkAndConsume(TENANT_ONE, 0));

        service.checkAndConsume(TENANT_TWO, 10);
    }

    @Test
    void resetsUsageAtWindowBoundaryAndRejectsOldWindowCalls() {
        var clock = new AdjustableClock(START);
        var service = new FixedWindowGatewayQuotaService(
                1, 10, 10, Duration.ofMinutes(1), clock);
        service.checkAndConsume(TENANT_ONE, 10);

        clock.set(START.plusSeconds(60));
        service.checkAndConsume(TENANT_ONE, 10);

        clock.set(START.plusSeconds(30));
        assertThrows(
                GatewayQuotaExceededException.class,
                () -&gt; service.checkAndConsume(TENANT_ONE, 0));
    }

    @Test
    void boundsTenantStateAndRejectsInvalidSettings() {
        var service = new FixedWindowGatewayQuotaService(
                10, 100, 1, Duration.ofMinutes(1), Clock.fixed(START, ZoneOffset.UTC));
        service.checkAndConsume(TENANT_ONE, 0);
        assertThrows(
                GatewayQuotaExceededException.class,
                () -&gt; service.checkAndConsume(TENANT_TWO, 0));
        assertThrows(
                IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -&gt; new FixedWindowGatewayQuotaService(
                        0, 100, 1, Duration.ofMinutes(1), Clock.fixed(START, ZoneOffset.UTC)));
    }

    private static final class AdjustableClock extends Clock {
        private final AtomicReference&lt;Instant&gt; instant;

        private AdjustableClock(Instant instant) {
            this.instant = new AtomicReference&lt;&gt;(instant);
        }

        private void set(Instant value) {
            instant.set(value);
        }

        @Override
        public ZoneId getZone() {
            return ZoneOffset.UTC;
        }

        @Override
        public Clock withZone(ZoneId zone) {
            if (!ZoneOffset.UTC.equals(zone)) {
                throw new UnsupportedOperationException(&quot;Test clock only supports UTC.&quot;);
            }
            return this;
        }

        @Override
        public Instant instant() {
            return instant.get();
        }
    }

}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/test/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/api/NativePromptJsonReaderTest.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.api;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;

import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.List;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.ConfidentialSegment;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.NativeUtf8CharSequence;

class NativePromptJsonReaderTest {
    @Test
    void decodesJsonEscapesAndUtf8DirectlyIntoNativePromptMemory() throws Exception {
        byte[] body = &quot; {\&quot;text\&quot;:\&quot;Contact ada\\u0040example.com 😀\&quot;}&quot;
                .getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

        try (ConfidentialSegment prompt = NativePromptJsonReader.readText(
                new ByteArrayInputStream(body), body.length)) {
            assertEquals(&quot;Contact ada@example.com 😀&quot;,
                    new NativeUtf8CharSequence(prompt).toString());
        }
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsMalformedJsonAndUnpairedSurrogateEscapes() {
        byte[] malformed = &quot;{\&quot;text\&quot;:\&quot;broken\&quot; trailing}&quot;.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        byte[] surrogate = &quot;{\&quot;text\&quot;:\&quot;\\uD800\&quot;}&quot;.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

        assertThrows(NativePromptFormatException.class,
                () -&gt; NativePromptJsonReader.readText(
                        new ByteArrayInputStream(malformed), malformed.length));
        assertThrows(NativePromptFormatException.class,
                () -&gt; NativePromptJsonReader.readText(
                        new ByteArrayInputStream(surrogate), surrogate.length));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsNonTextFieldsAndOversizedBodies() {
        byte[] body = &quot;{\&quot;text\&quot;:\&quot;x\&quot;,\&quot;other\&quot;:\&quot;y\&quot;}&quot;.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

        assertThrows(NativePromptFormatException.class,
                () -&gt; NativePromptJsonReader.readText(
                        new ByteArrayInputStream(body), body.length));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -&gt; NativePromptJsonReader.readText(
                        new ByteArrayInputStream(new byte[0]), Long.MAX_VALUE));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsBodiesWhoseDeclaredLengthCannotHoldDecodedText() {
        byte[] body = &quot;{\&quot;text\&quot;:\&quot;long enough\&quot;}&quot;.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

        assertThrows(NativePromptFormatException.class,
                () -&gt; NativePromptJsonReader.readText(
                        new ByteArrayInputStream(body), 1));
    }

    @Test
    void readsBatchPromptsIntoSlicesOfOneNativeAllocation() throws Exception {
        byte[] body = (&quot;{\&quot;tenantId\&quot;:\&quot;tenant-one\&quot;,&quot;
                + &quot;\&quot;segments\&quot;:[\&quot;ada@example.com\&quot;,\&quot;😀 212-555-0199\&quot;]}&quot;)
                .getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

        try (NativePromptJsonReader.NativePromptBatch batch =
                NativePromptJsonReader.readSegments(
                        new ByteArrayInputStream(body), body.length)) {
            assertEquals(List.of(&quot;ada@example.com&quot;, &quot;😀 212-555-0199&quot;),
                    batch.segments().stream()
                            .map(segment -&gt; new NativeUtf8CharSequence(segment).toString())
                            .toList());
        }
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsEmptyAndTrailingCommaBatches() {
        byte[] empty = &quot;{\&quot;segments\&quot;:[]}&quot;.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        byte[] trailingComma = &quot;{\&quot;segments\&quot;:[\&quot;one\&quot;,]}&quot;.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

        assertThrows(NativePromptFormatException.class,
                () -&gt; NativePromptJsonReader.readSegments(
                        new ByteArrayInputStream(empty), empty.length));
        assertThrows(NativePromptFormatException.class,
                () -&gt; NativePromptJsonReader.readSegments(
                        new ByteArrayInputStream(trailingComma), trailingComma.length));
    }

    @Test
    void readsDesanitizeModelResponseIntoNativeMemory() throws Exception {
        byte[] body = &quot;{\&quot;secureContext\&quot;:\&quot;v2.k.n.ciphertext\&quot;,&quot;
                .concat(&quot;\&quot;modelResponse\&quot;:\&quot;The answer is 😀\&quot;}&quot;)
                .getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

        try (NativePromptJsonReader.NativeDesanitizeRequest request =
                NativePromptJsonReader.readDesanitize(
                        new ByteArrayInputStream(body), body.length)) {
            assertEquals(&quot;The answer is 😀&quot;,
                    new NativeUtf8CharSequence(request.response()).toString());
            assertEquals(&quot;v2.k.n.ciphertext&quot;, request.secureContext());
        }
    }

    @Test
    void readsBatchDesanitizeResponsesIntoNativeMemory() throws Exception {
        byte[] body = &quot;{\&quot;modelResponses\&quot;:[\&quot;one\&quot;,\&quot;two 😀\&quot;],&quot;
                .concat(&quot;\&quot;secureContext\&quot;:\&quot;v2.k.n.ciphertext\&quot;}&quot;)
                .getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

        try (NativePromptJsonReader.NativeBatchDesanitizeRequest request =
                NativePromptJsonReader.readBatchDesanitize(
                        new ByteArrayInputStream(body), body.length)) {
            assertEquals(List.of(&quot;one&quot;, &quot;two 😀&quot;),
                    request.responses().segments().stream()
                            .map(segment -&gt; new NativeUtf8CharSequence(segment).toString())
                            .toList());
        }
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/test/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/api/OidcJwtGatewayCredentialAuthenticatorTest.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.api;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.ZoneOffset;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.jwt.Jwt;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.jwt.JwtDecoder;

class OidcJwtGatewayCredentialAuthenticatorTest {
    private static final String ISSUER = &quot;https://identity.example.test/&quot;;
    private static final String AUDIENCE = &quot;confidential-gateway&quot;;
    private static final Instant NOW = Instant.parse(&quot;2026-01-01T00:00:00Z&quot;);

    @Test
    void createsPrincipalOnlyFromValidIssuerAudienceAndClaims() {
        var authenticator = authenticator(jwt(
                Map.of(
                        &quot;tenant_id&quot;, &quot;tenant-a&quot;,
                        &quot;scope&quot;, &quot;openid gateway:sanitize gateway:desanitize profile&quot;),
                NOW.plusSeconds(60),
                null,
                ISSUER,
                List.of(AUDIENCE)));

        var principal = authenticator.authenticate(&quot;signed-token&quot;);

        assertTrue(principal.isPresent());
        assertEquals(&quot;subject-a&quot;, principal.get().subject());
        assertEquals(&quot;tenant-a&quot;, principal.get().tenantId());
        assertEquals(
                java.util.Set.of(&quot;gateway:sanitize&quot;, &quot;gateway:desanitize&quot;),
                principal.get().scopes());
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsWrongIssuerAudienceExpiredOrNotYetValidTokens() {
        assertRejected(jwt(Map.of(&quot;tenant_id&quot;, &quot;tenant-a&quot;, &quot;scope&quot;, &quot;gateway:sanitize&quot;),
                NOW.plusSeconds(60), null, &quot;https://untrusted.example/&quot;, List.of(AUDIENCE)));
        assertRejected(jwt(Map.of(&quot;tenant_id&quot;, &quot;tenant-a&quot;, &quot;scope&quot;, &quot;gateway:sanitize&quot;),
                NOW.plusSeconds(60), null, ISSUER, List.of(&quot;other-service&quot;)));
        assertRejected(jwt(Map.of(&quot;tenant_id&quot;, &quot;tenant-a&quot;, &quot;scope&quot;, &quot;gateway:sanitize&quot;),
                NOW, null, ISSUER, List.of(AUDIENCE)));
        assertRejected(jwt(Map.of(&quot;tenant_id&quot;, &quot;tenant-a&quot;, &quot;scope&quot;, &quot;gateway:sanitize&quot;),
                NOW.plusSeconds(60), NOW.plusSeconds(1), ISSUER, List.of(AUDIENCE)));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsMissingTenantAndMalformedScopeClaims() {
        assertRejected(jwt(Map.of(&quot;scope&quot;, &quot;gateway:sanitize&quot;),
                NOW.plusSeconds(60), null, ISSUER, List.of(AUDIENCE)));
        assertRejected(jwt(Map.of(&quot;tenant_id&quot;, &quot;tenant-a&quot;, &quot;scope&quot;, 123),
                NOW.plusSeconds(60), null, ISSUER, List.of(AUDIENCE)));
    }

    @Test
    void grantsConfiguredScopesToValidatedTokensWithoutScopeClaims() {
        var authenticator = new OidcJwtGatewayCredentialAuthenticator(
                token -&gt; jwt(Map.of(), NOW.plusSeconds(60), null, ISSUER, List.of(AUDIENCE)),
                ISSUER,
                AUDIENCE,
                &quot;sub&quot;,
                &quot;scope&quot;,
                Set.of(&quot;gateway:sanitize&quot;, &quot;gateway:desanitize&quot;),
                Clock.fixed(NOW, ZoneOffset.UTC));

        var principal = authenticator.authenticate(&quot;signed-token&quot;);

        assertTrue(principal.isPresent());
        assertEquals(&quot;subject-a&quot;, principal.get().tenantId());
        assertEquals(Set.of(&quot;gateway:sanitize&quot;, &quot;gateway:desanitize&quot;), principal.get().scopes());
    }

    @Test
    void fixedScopesDoNotBypassIssuerOrAudienceValidation() {
        var authenticator = new OidcJwtGatewayCredentialAuthenticator(
                token -&gt; jwt(Map.of(), NOW.plusSeconds(60), null, ISSUER, List.of(&quot;other-service&quot;)),
                ISSUER,
                AUDIENCE,
                &quot;sub&quot;,
                &quot;scope&quot;,
                Set.of(&quot;gateway:sanitize&quot;),
                Clock.fixed(NOW, ZoneOffset.UTC));

        assertFalse(authenticator.authenticate(&quot;signed-token&quot;).isPresent());
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsUnknownFixedScopes() {
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -&gt; new OidcJwtGatewayCredentialAuthenticator(
                token -&gt; jwt(Map.of(), NOW.plusSeconds(60), null, ISSUER, List.of(AUDIENCE)),
                ISSUER,
                AUDIENCE,
                &quot;sub&quot;,
                &quot;scope&quot;,
                Set.of(&quot;admin&quot;),
                Clock.fixed(NOW, ZoneOffset.UTC)));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsTokensThatCannotBeDecoded() {
        JwtDecoder decoder = token -&gt; {
            throw new org.springframework.security.oauth2.jwt.JwtException(&quot;invalid signature&quot;);
        };
        assertFalse(authenticator(decoder).authenticate(&quot;bad-token&quot;).isPresent());
        assertFalse(authenticator(decoder).authenticate(&quot; &quot;).isPresent());
    }

    private static void assertRejected(Jwt jwt) {
        assertFalse(authenticator(jwt).authenticate(&quot;signed-token&quot;).isPresent());
    }

    private static OidcJwtGatewayCredentialAuthenticator authenticator(Jwt jwt) {
        return authenticator(token -&gt; jwt);
    }

    private static OidcJwtGatewayCredentialAuthenticator authenticator(JwtDecoder decoder) {
        return new OidcJwtGatewayCredentialAuthenticator(
                decoder,
                ISSUER,
                AUDIENCE,
                &quot;tenant_id&quot;,
                &quot;scope&quot;,
                Clock.fixed(NOW, ZoneOffset.UTC));
    }

    private static Jwt jwt(
            Map&lt;String, Object&gt; claims,
            Instant expiresAt,
            Instant notBefore,
            String issuer,
            List&lt;String&gt; audience) {
        var builder = Jwt.withTokenValue(&quot;signed-token&quot;)
                .header(&quot;alg&quot;, &quot;RS256&quot;)
                .issuer(issuer)
                .subject(&quot;subject-a&quot;)
                .audience(audience)
                .issuedAt(NOW.minusSeconds(60))
                .expiresAt(expiresAt)
                .claims(values -&gt; values.putAll(claims));
        if (notBefore != null) {
            builder.claim(&quot;nbf&quot;, notBefore);
        }
        return builder.build();
    }

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertDoesNotThrow;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;

import java.security.SecureRandom;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class AwsNitroConfidentialRuntimeVerifierTest {
    @Test
    void failsClosedWhenKmsPolicyGatedRecipientReleaseFails() {
        AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseService service = new AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseService(
                (nonce, publicKey) -&gt; new byte[] { 1 },
                request -&gt; {
                    throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;No KMS release&quot;);
                },
                new SecureRandom(),
                &quot;key&quot;,
                new byte[] { 1 },
                &quot;context-key&quot;);
        AwsNitroConfidentialRuntimeVerifier verifier =
                new AwsNitroConfidentialRuntimeVerifier(service);

        assertThrows(AwsNitroEnclaveException.class, verifier::verifyCurrentRuntime);
    }

    @Test
    void permitsOnlySuccessfulKeyRelease() {
        byte[] secret = new byte[32];
        byte[][] recipientPublicKey = new byte[1][];
        AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseService service = new AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseService(
                (nonce, publicKey) -&gt; {
                    recipientPublicKey[0] = publicKey.clone();
                    return new byte[] { 1 };
                },
                request -&gt; {
                    try {
                        java.security.PublicKey recipient = java.security.KeyFactory
                                .getInstance(&quot;RSA&quot;)
                                .generatePublic(new java.security.spec.X509EncodedKeySpec(
                                        recipientPublicKey[0]));
                        javax.crypto.Cipher cipher = javax.crypto.Cipher.getInstance(
                                &quot;RSA/ECB/OAEPWithSHA-256AndMGF1Padding&quot;);
                        cipher.init(javax.crypto.Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, recipient,
                                new javax.crypto.spec.OAEPParameterSpec(
                                        &quot;SHA-256&quot;, &quot;MGF1&quot;,
                                        java.security.spec.MGF1ParameterSpec.SHA256,
                                        javax.crypto.spec.PSource.PSpecified.DEFAULT));
                        return new AwsNitroKmsDecryptResponse(cipher.doFinal(secret));
                    } catch (Exception e) {
                        throw new AssertionError(&quot;Test KMS transport failed.&quot;, e);
                    }
                },
                new SecureRandom(),
                &quot;key&quot;,
                new byte[] { 1 },
                &quot;context-key&quot;);
        AwsNitroConfidentialRuntimeVerifier verifier =
                new AwsNitroConfidentialRuntimeVerifier(service);

        assertDoesNotThrow(verifier::verifyCurrentRuntime);
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/test/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/aws/AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseServiceTest.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.aws;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertArrayEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.security.KeyFactory;
import java.security.PublicKey;
import java.security.SecureRandom;
import java.security.spec.X509EncodedKeySpec;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.List;

import javax.crypto.Cipher;
import javax.crypto.spec.OAEPParameterSpec;
import javax.crypto.spec.PSource;

import java.security.spec.MGF1ParameterSpec;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.SecureContextKey;

class AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseServiceTest {
    private static final byte[] AES_KEY = new byte[32];
    private static final byte[] CIPHERTEXT_BLOB = &quot;kms-wrapped-context-key&quot;.getBytes(
            StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    private static final OAEPParameterSpec OAEP = new OAEPParameterSpec(
            &quot;SHA-256&quot;, &quot;MGF1&quot;, MGF1ParameterSpec.SHA256, PSource.PSpecified.DEFAULT);

    @Test
    void createsFreshNonceAndRecipientKeyAndUsesKmsRecipientResponseForKey() throws Exception {
        Arrays.fill(AES_KEY, (byte) 0x5a);
        List&lt;byte[]&gt; seenNonces = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;();
        List&lt;byte[]&gt; seenPublicKeys = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;();
        List&lt;byte[]&gt; seenDocuments = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;();
        List&lt;AwsNitroKmsDecryptRequest&gt; requests = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;();

        AwsNitroEnclaveAttestationSource nsmTestBoundary = (nonce, publicKey) -&gt; {
            assertEquals(32, nonce.length);
            seenNonces.add(nonce.clone());
            seenPublicKeys.add(publicKey.clone());
            return fixtureDocument(nonce, publicKey);
        };
        ParentVsockKmsTransport kmsTestBoundary = request -&gt; {
            requests.add(request);
            seenDocuments.add(request.recipient().attestationDocument());
            return recipientResponse(seenPublicKeys.get(requests.size() - 1), AES_KEY);
        };
        AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseService release = service(nsmTestBoundary, kmsTestBoundary);
        AwsNitroAttestedSecureContextKeyProvider provider =
                new AwsNitroAttestedSecureContextKeyProvider(release, &quot;context-key&quot;);

        SecureContextKey key = provider.currentKey();
        assertEquals(&quot;context-key&quot;, key.id());
        assertArrayEquals(AES_KEY, key.key().getEncoded());
        assertEquals(1, requests.size());
        AwsNitroKmsDecryptRequest request = requests.get(0);
        assertEquals(&quot;arn:aws:kms:us-east-1:123456789012:key/test&quot;, request.keyId());
        assertArrayEquals(CIPHERTEXT_BLOB, request.ciphertextBlob());
        assertEquals(
                AwsNitroKmsDecryptRequest.RSAES_OAEP_SHA_256,
                request.recipient().keyEncryptionAlgorithm());
        assertArrayEquals(fixtureDocument(seenNonces.get(0), seenPublicKeys.get(0)),
                request.recipient().attestationDocument());
        assertArrayEquals(seenDocuments.get(0), request.recipient().attestationDocument());

        PublicKey recipientPublicKey = KeyFactory.getInstance(&quot;RSA&quot;).generatePublic(
                new X509EncodedKeySpec(seenPublicKeys.get(0)));
        assertEquals(&quot;RSA&quot;, recipientPublicKey.getAlgorithm());
        assertEquals(2048, ((java.security.interfaces.RSAPublicKey) recipientPublicKey)
                .getModulus().bitLength());
        assertTrue(provider.findKey(&quot;context-key&quot;).isPresent());
        assertTrue(provider.findKey(&quot;other-key&quot;).isEmpty());
        assertEquals(2, seenNonces.size());
        assertNotEquals(Arrays.toString(seenNonces.get(0)), Arrays.toString(seenNonces.get(1)));
        assertFalse(Arrays.equals(seenPublicKeys.get(0), seenPublicKeys.get(1)));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsMissingOrMalformedRecipientCiphertext() {
        assertThrows(NullPointerException.class, () -&gt; new AwsNitroKmsDecryptResponse(null));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -&gt; new AwsNitroKmsDecryptResponse(new byte[0]));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -&gt; new AwsNitroKmsDecryptResponse(
                        new byte[AwsNitroKmsDecryptResponse.MAX_CIPHERTEXT_FOR_RECIPIENT_BYTES + 1]));

        AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseService missingResponse = service(
                (nonce, publicKey) -&gt; new byte[] { 1 },
                request -&gt; null);
        assertThrows(AwsNitroEnclaveException.class, missingResponse::releaseContextKey);
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsRecipientCiphertextThatCannotBeUnwrappedByThisEnclaveKey() {
        AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseService service = service(
                (nonce, publicKey) -&gt; new byte[] { 7 },
                request -&gt; new AwsNitroKmsDecryptResponse(new byte[256]));
        assertThrows(AwsNitroEnclaveException.class, service::releaseContextKey);
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsNonAesPlaintextAfterRecipientUnwrap() {
        AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseService service = service(
                (nonce, publicKey) -&gt; new byte[] { 7 },
                request -&gt; recipientResponse(latestPublicKey.get(), new byte[15]));
        assertThrows(AwsNitroEnclaveException.class, service::releaseContextKey);
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsAesKeysOtherThan256Bits() {
        for (int length : List.of(16, 24)) {
            AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseService service = service(
                    (nonce, publicKey) -&gt; new byte[] { 7 },
                    request -&gt; recipientResponse(latestPublicKey.get(), new byte[length]));
            assertThrows(AwsNitroEnclaveException.class, service::releaseContextKey);
        }
    }

    @Test
    void failsClosedWhenNsmOrParentVsockKmsTransportFails() {
        AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseService nsmFailure = service(
                (nonce, publicKey) -&gt; {
                    throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;NSM unavailable&quot;);
                },
                request -&gt; null);
        assertThrows(AwsNitroEnclaveException.class, nsmFailure::releaseContextKey);

        AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseService transportFailure = service(
                (nonce, publicKey) -&gt; new byte[] { 1 },
                request -&gt; {
                    throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;vsock unavailable&quot;);
                });
        assertThrows(AwsNitroEnclaveException.class, transportFailure::releaseContextKey);
    }

    @Test
    void boundsRequestAndDefensivelyCopiesRecipientData() {
        byte[] ciphertext = { 1, 2 };
        byte[] document = { 3, 4 };
        AwsNitroKmsDecryptRequest request = new AwsNitroKmsDecryptRequest(
                &quot;key&quot;,
                ciphertext,
                new AwsNitroKmsRecipient(
                        document, AwsNitroKmsDecryptRequest.RSAES_OAEP_SHA_256));
        ciphertext[0] = 9;
        document[0] = 9;
        assertArrayEquals(new byte[] { 1, 2 }, request.ciphertextBlob());
        assertArrayEquals(new byte[] { 3, 4 }, request.recipient().attestationDocument());
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -&gt; new AwsNitroKmsDecryptRequest(
                &quot;key&quot;,
                new byte[AwsNitroKmsDecryptRequest.MAX_CIPHERTEXT_BLOB_BYTES + 1],
                new AwsNitroKmsRecipient(
                        new byte[] { 1 }, AwsNitroKmsDecryptRequest.RSAES_OAEP_SHA_256)));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -&gt; new AwsNitroKmsRecipient(new byte[] { 1 }, &quot;PLAINTEXT&quot;));
    }

    private static final ThreadLocal&lt;byte[]&gt; latestPublicKey = new ThreadLocal&lt;&gt;();

    private static AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseService service(
            AwsNitroEnclaveAttestationSource attestationSource,
            ParentVsockKmsTransport kmsTransport) {
        AwsNitroEnclaveAttestationSource capturingSource = (nonce, publicKey) -&gt; {
            latestPublicKey.set(publicKey.clone());
            return attestationSource.attest(nonce, publicKey);
        };
        return new AwsNitroEnclaveKeyReleaseService(
                capturingSource,
                kmsTransport,
                new SecureRandom(),
                &quot;arn:aws:kms:us-east-1:123456789012:key/test&quot;,
                CIPHERTEXT_BLOB,
                &quot;context-key&quot;);
    }

    private static byte[] fixtureDocument(byte[] nonce, byte[] publicKey) {
        ByteArrayOutputStream out = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        out.writeBytes(nonce);
        out.writeBytes(publicKey);
        return out.toByteArray();
    }

    private static AwsNitroKmsDecryptResponse recipientResponse(
            byte[] publicKey,
            byte[] plaintext) {
        try {
            PublicKey recipient = KeyFactory.getInstance(&quot;RSA&quot;).generatePublic(
                    new X509EncodedKeySpec(publicKey));
            Cipher encrypt = Cipher.getInstance(&quot;RSA/ECB/OAEPWithSHA-256AndMGF1Padding&quot;);
            encrypt.init(Cipher.ENCRYPT_MODE, recipient, OAEP);
            return new AwsNitroKmsDecryptResponse(encrypt.doFinal(plaintext));
        } catch (Exception e) {
            throw new AssertionError(&quot;Test KMS transport could not form recipient ciphertext.&quot;, e);
        }
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/test/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/aws/AwsNitroKmsAttestationPolicyTest.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.aws;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;

import java.util.Map;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class AwsNitroKmsAttestationPolicyTest {
    private static final String IMAGE = &quot;a&quot;.repeat(96);
    private static final String PCR0 = IMAGE;
    private static final String PCR8 = &quot;c&quot;.repeat(96);

    @Test
    void exposesImageSha384AndExplicitPcrConditionsForKmsKeyPolicy() {
        AwsNitroKmsAttestationPolicy policy = new AwsNitroKmsAttestationPolicy(
                IMAGE, Map.of(0, PCR0, 8, PCR8));

        assertEquals(
                Map.of(
                        &quot;kms:RecipientAttestation:ImageSha384&quot;, IMAGE,
                        &quot;kms:RecipientAttestation:PCR0&quot;, PCR0,
                        &quot;kms:RecipientAttestation:PCR8&quot;, PCR8),
                policy.stringEqualsConditions());
        assertThrows(UnsupportedOperationException.class,
                () -&gt; policy.stringEqualsConditions().put(&quot;kms:Decrypt&quot;, &quot;anything&quot;));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsMissingOrInvalidImageAndPcrConditions() {
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -&gt; new AwsNitroKmsAttestationPolicy(null, Map.of(0, PCR0)));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -&gt; new AwsNitroKmsAttestationPolicy(IMAGE, Map.of()));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -&gt; new AwsNitroKmsAttestationPolicy(IMAGE, Map.of(9, PCR0)));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -&gt; new AwsNitroKmsAttestationPolicy(IMAGE, Map.of(0, &quot;b&quot;.repeat(96))));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -&gt; new AwsNitroKmsAttestationPolicy(IMAGE, Map.of(0, &quot;not-a-digest&quot;)));
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/test/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/aws/LinuxVsockParentKmsTransportTest.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.aws;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertArrayEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;

import java.util.Base64;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.JsonNode;
import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

class LinuxVsockParentKmsTransportTest {
    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper = new ObjectMapper();

    @Test
    void encodesKmsRecipientRequestForParentProxy() throws Exception {
        var transport = new LinuxVsockParentKmsTransport(3, 8000, objectMapper);
        var request = new AwsNitroKmsDecryptRequest(
                &quot;arn:aws:kms:us-east-1:123456789012:key/example&quot;,
                new byte[] { 1, 2, 3 },
                new AwsNitroKmsRecipient(
                        new byte[] { 4, 5 },
                        AwsNitroKmsDecryptRequest.RSAES_OAEP_SHA_256));

        byte[] frame = transport.encodeRequest(request);
        try {
            JsonNode json = objectMapper.readTree(frame);
            assertEquals(1, json.path(&quot;version&quot;).asInt());
            assertEquals(&quot;kms:Decrypt&quot;, json.path(&quot;operation&quot;).asText());
            JsonNode kmsRequest = json.path(&quot;request&quot;);
            assertEquals(request.keyId(), kmsRequest.path(&quot;KeyId&quot;).asText());
            assertEquals(
                    Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(new byte[] { 1, 2, 3 }),
                    kmsRequest.path(&quot;CiphertextBlob&quot;).asText());
            assertEquals(
                    Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(new byte[] { 4, 5 }),
                    kmsRequest.path(&quot;Recipient&quot;).path(&quot;AttestationDocument&quot;).asText());
        } finally {
            java.util.Arrays.fill(frame, (byte) 0);
        }
    }

    @Test
    void parsesOnlyCiphertextForRecipientFromVersionedResponse() throws Exception {
        var transport = new LinuxVsockParentKmsTransport(3, 8000, objectMapper);
        byte[] response = objectMapper.writeValueAsBytes(
                java.util.Map.of(
                        &quot;version&quot;, 1,
                        &quot;response&quot;,
                        java.util.Map.of(
                                &quot;CiphertextForRecipient&quot;,
                                Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(new byte[] { 9, 8, 7 }))));

        assertArrayEquals(
                new byte[] { 9, 8, 7 },
                transport.decodeResponse(response).ciphertextForRecipient());
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsInvalidProxyResponsesAndEndpointConfiguration() throws Exception {
        var transport = new LinuxVsockParentKmsTransport(3, 8000, objectMapper);

        assertThrows(
                AwsNitroEnclaveException.class,
                () -&gt; transport.decodeResponse(&quot;{\&quot;version\&quot;:2}&quot;.getBytes()));
        assertThrows(
                AwsNitroEnclaveException.class,
                () -&gt; transport.decodeResponse(
                        &quot;{\&quot;version\&quot;:1,\&quot;response\&quot;:{\&quot;CiphertextForRecipient\&quot;:\&quot;%%%\&quot;}}&quot;
                                .getBytes()));
        assertThrows(
                IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -&gt; new LinuxVsockParentKmsTransport(-1, 8000, objectMapper));
        assertThrows(
                IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -&gt; new LinuxVsockParentKmsTransport(3, 65536, objectMapper));
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/test/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/azure/AzureAttestationJwtVerifierTest.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.azure;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.security.KeyPair;
import java.security.KeyPairGenerator;
import java.security.Signature;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.ZoneOffset;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

class AzureAttestationJwtVerifierTest {
    private static final Instant NOW = Instant.parse(&quot;2026-10-17T08:30:00Z&quot;);
    private static final String ISSUER = &quot;https://test.wus.attest.azure.net&quot;;
    private static final String NONCE = &quot;fresh-challenge&quot;;
    private static final String MEASUREMENT = &quot;a&quot;.repeat(96);
    private static final String POLICY_HASH = &quot;p&quot;.repeat(43);

    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper = new ObjectMapper();
    private KeyPair signingKey;
    private AzureAttestationJwtVerifier verifier;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() throws Exception {
        KeyPairGenerator generator = KeyPairGenerator.getInstance(&quot;RSA&quot;);
        generator.initialize(2048);
        signingKey = generator.generateKeyPair();
        var policy = new AzureAttestationPolicy(
                ISSUER,
                &quot;opentron-gateway&quot;,
                Set.of(MEASUREMENT),
                POLICY_HASH,
                Map.of(&quot;x-ms-sevsnpvm-snpfw-svn&quot;, 5));
        verifier = new AzureAttestationJwtVerifier(
                objectMapper,
                keyId -&gt; {
                    if (!&quot;key-1&quot;.equals(keyId)) {
                        throw new AzureAttestationException(&quot;Untrusted signing key.&quot;);
                    }
                    return signingKey.getPublic();
                },
                policy,
                Clock.fixed(NOW, ZoneOffset.UTC));
    }

    @Test
    void acceptsFreshSignedAzureSevSnpEvidenceMatchingPolicy() throws Exception {
        var verified = verifier.verify(token(claims(NONCE, MEASUREMENT, false, NOW.plusSeconds(60))), NONCE);

        assertEquals(ISSUER, verified.issuer());
        assertEquals(POLICY_HASH, verified.policyHash());
        assertEquals(MEASUREMENT, verified.launchMeasurement());
        assertEquals(NOW.plusSeconds(60), verified.expiresAt());
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsNonceReplayAndWrongLaunchMeasurement() throws Exception {
        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; verifier.verify(token(claims(&quot;another-challenge&quot;, MEASUREMENT, false,
                        NOW.plusSeconds(60))), NONCE));
        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; verifier.verify(token(claims(NONCE, &quot;b&quot;.repeat(96), false,
                        NOW.plusSeconds(60))), NONCE));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsWrongIssuerAudienceAndPolicyHash() throws Exception {
        Map&lt;String, Object&gt; wrongIssuer = claims(NONCE, MEASUREMENT, false, NOW.plusSeconds(60));
        wrongIssuer.put(&quot;iss&quot;, &quot;https://untrusted.attest.azure.net&quot;);
        assertThrows(AzureAttestationException.class, () -&gt; verifier.verify(token(wrongIssuer), NONCE));

        Map&lt;String, Object&gt; wrongAudience = claims(NONCE, MEASUREMENT, false, NOW.plusSeconds(60));
        wrongAudience.put(&quot;aud&quot;, &quot;another-service&quot;);
        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; verifier.verify(token(wrongAudience), NONCE));

        Map&lt;String, Object&gt; wrongPolicyHash = claims(NONCE, MEASUREMENT, false, NOW.plusSeconds(60));
        wrongPolicyHash.put(&quot;x-ms-policy-hash&quot;, &quot;q&quot;.repeat(43));
        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; verifier.verify(token(wrongPolicyHash), NONCE));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsWrongAttestationTypeAndDisabledSecureBoot() throws Exception {
        Map&lt;String, Object&gt; wrongType = claims(NONCE, MEASUREMENT, false, NOW.plusSeconds(60));
        @SuppressWarnings(&quot;unchecked&quot;)
        Map&lt;String, Object&gt; wrongTypeTee = (Map&lt;String, Object&gt;) wrongType.get(&quot;x-ms-isolation-tee&quot;);
        wrongTypeTee.put(&quot;x-ms-attestation-type&quot;, &quot;sgx&quot;);
        assertThrows(AzureAttestationException.class, () -&gt; verifier.verify(token(wrongType), NONCE));

        Map&lt;String, Object&gt; secureBootDisabled = claims(NONCE, MEASUREMENT, false, NOW.plusSeconds(60));
        secureBootDisabled.put(&quot;secureboot&quot;, false);
        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; verifier.verify(token(secureBootDisabled), NONCE));
    }

    @Test
    void acceptsDocumentedCvmClaimsWhenMaaTokenOmitsAudience() throws Exception {
        AzureAttestationPolicy noAudiencePolicy = new AzureAttestationPolicy(
                ISSUER,
                &quot;&quot;,
                Set.of(MEASUREMENT),
                POLICY_HASH,
                Map.of(&quot;x-ms-sevsnpvm-snpfw-svn&quot;, 5));
        var noAudienceVerifier = new AzureAttestationJwtVerifier(
                objectMapper,
                keyId -&gt; signingKey.getPublic(),
                noAudiencePolicy,
                Clock.fixed(NOW, ZoneOffset.UTC));
        Map&lt;String, Object&gt; tokenClaims = claims(
                NONCE, MEASUREMENT, false, NOW.plusSeconds(60));
        tokenClaims.remove(&quot;aud&quot;);

        assertEquals(ISSUER, noAudienceVerifier.verify(token(tokenClaims), NONCE).issuer());

        Map&lt;String, Object&gt; unexpectedAudience = claims(
                NONCE, MEASUREMENT, false, NOW.plusSeconds(60));
        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; noAudienceVerifier.verify(token(unexpectedAudience), NONCE));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsDebuggableExpiredAndBelowMinimumEvidence() throws Exception {
        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; verifier.verify(token(claims(NONCE, MEASUREMENT, true,
                        NOW.plusSeconds(60))), NONCE));
        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; verifier.verify(token(claims(NONCE, MEASUREMENT, false,
                        NOW.minusSeconds(60))), NONCE));

        Map&lt;String, Object&gt; lowSvn = claims(NONCE, MEASUREMENT, false, NOW.plusSeconds(60));
        @SuppressWarnings(&quot;unchecked&quot;)
        Map&lt;String, Object&gt; lowSvnTee = (Map&lt;String, Object&gt;) lowSvn.get(&quot;x-ms-isolation-tee&quot;);
        lowSvnTee.put(&quot;x-ms-sevsnpvm-snpfw-svn&quot;, 4);
        assertThrows(AzureAttestationException.class, () -&gt; verifier.verify(token(lowSvn), NONCE));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsTamperedSignatureAndUntrustedKeyId() throws Exception {
        String valid = token(claims(NONCE, MEASUREMENT, false, NOW.plusSeconds(60)));
        String[] parts = valid.split(&quot;\\.&quot;);
        String tampered = parts[0] + &quot;.&quot; + parts[1] + &quot;.&quot;
                + Base64.getUrlEncoder().withoutPadding().encodeToString(new byte[256]);
        assertThrows(AzureAttestationException.class, () -&gt; verifier.verify(tampered, NONCE));

        String wrongKeyId = sign(
                Map.of(&quot;alg&quot;, &quot;RS256&quot;, &quot;kid&quot;, &quot;unknown&quot;),
                claims(NONCE, MEASUREMENT, false, NOW.plusSeconds(60)));
        assertThrows(AzureAttestationException.class, () -&gt; verifier.verify(wrongKeyId, NONCE));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsMalformedOversizedAndUnsupportedTokens() throws Exception {
        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; verifier.verify(&quot;not-a-jwt&quot;, NONCE));
        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; verifier.verify(&quot;x&quot;.repeat(64 * 1024 + 1), NONCE));

        String unsupportedAlgorithm = sign(
                Map.of(&quot;alg&quot;, &quot;none&quot;, &quot;kid&quot;, &quot;key-1&quot;),
                claims(NONCE, MEASUREMENT, false, NOW.plusSeconds(60)));
        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; verifier.verify(unsupportedAlgorithm, NONCE));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsInvalidTimesMissingRequiredClaimsAndTpmKey() throws Exception {
        Map&lt;String, Object&gt; longLived =
                claims(NONCE, MEASUREMENT, false, NOW.plus(Duration.ofHours(25)));
        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; verifier.verify(token(longLived), NONCE));

        Map&lt;String, Object&gt; missingIssuer =
                claims(NONCE, MEASUREMENT, false, NOW.plusSeconds(60));
        missingIssuer.remove(&quot;iss&quot;);
        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; verifier.verify(token(missingIssuer), NONCE));

        Map&lt;String, Object&gt; missingTpmKey =
                claims(NONCE, MEASUREMENT, false, NOW.plusSeconds(60));
        missingTpmKey.put(&quot;x-ms-runtime&quot;, Map.of(&quot;keys&quot;, List.of()));
        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; verifier.verify(token(missingTpmKey), NONCE));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsInvalidPolicyMeasurementAndHashAtConstruction() {
        assertThrows(
                IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -&gt; new AzureAttestationPolicy(
                        ISSUER, &quot;&quot;, Set.of(&quot;not-a-measurement&quot;), POLICY_HASH, Map.of()));
        assertThrows(
                IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -&gt; new AzureAttestationPolicy(
                        ISSUER, &quot;&quot;, Set.of(MEASUREMENT), &quot;not-a-policy-hash&quot;, Map.of()));
    }

    private Map&lt;String, Object&gt; claims(
            String nonce,
            String measurement,
            boolean debuggable,
            Instant expiresAt) {
        return new java.util.HashMap&lt;&gt;(Map.ofEntries(
                Map.entry(&quot;iss&quot;, ISSUER),
                Map.entry(&quot;aud&quot;, List.of(&quot;other&quot;, &quot;opentron-gateway&quot;)),
                Map.entry(&quot;iat&quot;, NOW.getEpochSecond()),
                Map.entry(&quot;exp&quot;, expiresAt.getEpochSecond()),
                Map.entry(&quot;x-ms-attestation-type&quot;, &quot;azurevm&quot;),
                Map.entry(&quot;secureboot&quot;, true),
                Map.entry(&quot;x-ms-policy-hash&quot;, POLICY_HASH),
                Map.entry(&quot;x-ms-runtime&quot;, Map.of(
                        &quot;client-payload&quot;, Map.of(
                                &quot;nonce&quot;, Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(
                                        nonce.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8))),
                        &quot;keys&quot;, List.of(Map.of(
                                &quot;kid&quot;, &quot;TpmEphemeralEncryptionKey&quot;,
                                &quot;kty&quot;, &quot;RSA&quot;,
                                &quot;n&quot;, &quot;modulus&quot;,
                                &quot;e&quot;, &quot;AQAB&quot;,
                                &quot;key_ops&quot;, List.of(&quot;encrypt&quot;))))),
                Map.entry(&quot;x-ms-isolation-tee&quot;, new java.util.HashMap&lt;&gt;(Map.of(
                        &quot;x-ms-attestation-type&quot;, &quot;sevsnpvm&quot;,
                        &quot;x-ms-compliance-status&quot;, &quot;azure-compliant-cvm&quot;,
                        &quot;x-ms-runtime&quot;, Map.of(&quot;vm-configuration&quot;, Map.of(
                                &quot;secure-boot&quot;, true,
                                &quot;tpm-enabled&quot;, true)),
                        &quot;x-ms-sevsnpvm-is-debuggable&quot;, debuggable,
                        &quot;x-ms-sevsnpvm-vmpl&quot;, 0,
                        &quot;x-ms-sevsnpvm-launchmeasurement&quot;, measurement,
                        &quot;x-ms-sevsnpvm-snpfw-svn&quot;, 5)))));
    }

    private String token(Map&lt;String, Object&gt; claims) throws Exception {
        return sign(Map.of(&quot;alg&quot;, &quot;RS256&quot;, &quot;kid&quot;, &quot;key-1&quot;), claims);
    }

    private String sign(Map&lt;String, Object&gt; header, Map&lt;String, Object&gt; claims) throws Exception {
        Base64.Encoder encoder = Base64.getUrlEncoder().withoutPadding();
        String encodedHeader = encoder.encodeToString(objectMapper.writeValueAsBytes(header));
        String encodedClaims = encoder.encodeToString(objectMapper.writeValueAsBytes(claims));
        String signingInput = encodedHeader + &quot;.&quot; + encodedClaims;
        Signature signature = Signature.getInstance(&quot;SHA256withRSA&quot;);
        signature.initSign(signingKey.getPrivate());
        signature.update(signingInput.getBytes(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII));
        return signingInput + &quot;.&quot; + encoder.encodeToString(signature.sign());
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/test/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/azure/AzureBuildProvenanceVerifierTest.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.azure;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.security.KeyPair;
import java.security.KeyPairGenerator;
import java.security.Signature;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.HexFormat;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

class AzureBuildProvenanceVerifierTest {
    private static final String REVISION = &quot;0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef01234567&quot;;

    @TempDir
    Path tempDirectory;

    private KeyPair signingKey;
    private AzureBuildProvenanceVerifier verifier;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() throws Exception {
        var keyPairGenerator = KeyPairGenerator.getInstance(&quot;Ed25519&quot;);
        signingKey = keyPairGenerator.generateKeyPair();
        verifier = new AzureBuildProvenanceVerifier(new ObjectMapper());
    }

    @Test
    void verifiesSignatureArtifactAndSbomDigests() throws Exception {
        Path artifact = write(&quot;opentron-backend.jar&quot;, &quot;jar bytes&quot;);
        Path sbom = write(&quot;opentron-backend.jar.sbom.json&quot;, &quot;{\&quot;components\&quot;:[]}&quot;);
        Path manifest = writeManifest(artifact, sbom);
        Path signature = sign(manifest);

        var verified = verifier.verify(artifact, manifest, signature, signingKey.getPublic());

        assertEquals(artifact.toAbsolutePath().normalize(), verified.artifactPath());
        assertEquals(&quot;opentron-backend.jar&quot;, verified.artifact());
        assertEquals(digest(artifact), verified.artifactSha256());
        assertEquals(digest(sbom), verified.sbomSha256());
        assertEquals(REVISION, verified.sourceRevision());
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsTamperedManifestArtifactAndSbom() throws Exception {
        Path artifact = write(&quot;opentron-backend.jar&quot;, &quot;jar bytes&quot;);
        Path sbom = write(&quot;opentron-backend.jar.sbom.json&quot;, &quot;{\&quot;components\&quot;:[]}&quot;);
        Path manifest = writeManifest(artifact, sbom);
        Path signature = sign(manifest);

        Files.writeString(manifest, Files.readString(manifest) + &quot; &quot;, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
        Path tamperedManifest = manifest;
        Path originalSignature = signature;
        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; verifier.verify(
                        artifact, tamperedManifest, originalSignature, signingKey.getPublic()));

        manifest = writeManifest(artifact, sbom);
        signature = sign(manifest);
        Files.writeString(artifact, &quot;changed jar bytes&quot;);
        Path finalManifest = manifest;
        Path finalSignature = signature;
        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; verifier.verify(
                        artifact, finalManifest, finalSignature, signingKey.getPublic()));

        Files.writeString(artifact, &quot;jar bytes&quot;);
        manifest = writeManifest(artifact, sbom);
        signature = sign(manifest);
        Files.writeString(sbom, &quot;{\&quot;components\&quot;:[\&quot;changed\&quot;]}&quot;);
        Path sbomChangedManifest = manifest;
        Path sbomChangedSignature = signature;
        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; verifier.verify(
                        artifact,
                        sbomChangedManifest,
                        sbomChangedSignature,
                        signingKey.getPublic()));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsUntrustedKeyMalformedManifestAndWrongArtifactName() throws Exception {
        Path artifact = write(&quot;opentron-backend.jar&quot;, &quot;jar bytes&quot;);
        Path sbom = write(&quot;opentron-backend.jar.sbom.json&quot;, &quot;{\&quot;components\&quot;:[]}&quot;);
        Path manifest = writeManifest(artifact, sbom);
        Path signature = sign(manifest);
        KeyPairGenerator generator = KeyPairGenerator.getInstance(&quot;Ed25519&quot;);
        KeyPair untrustedKey = generator.generateKeyPair();

        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; verifier.verify(artifact, manifest, signature, untrustedKey.getPublic()));

        Path malformed = write(&quot;malformed.json&quot;, &quot;{\&quot;schemaVersion\&quot;:1,\&quot;schemaVersion\&quot;:1}&quot;);
        Path malformedSignature = sign(malformed);
        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; verifier.verify(
                        artifact, malformed, malformedSignature, signingKey.getPublic()));

        byte[] wrongName = manifest(artifact, sbom, &quot;another.jar&quot;);
        Path wrongNameManifest = write(&quot;wrong-name.json&quot;, new String(wrongName, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        Path wrongNameSignature = sign(wrongNameManifest);
        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; verifier.verify(
                        artifact, wrongNameManifest, wrongNameSignature, signingKey.getPublic()));
    }

    @Test
    void loadsPinnedEd25519PublicKeyFromPem() throws Exception {
        String publicKeyPem = &quot;-----BEGIN PUBLIC KEY-----\n&quot;
                + Base64.getMimeEncoder(64, new byte[] {&apos;\n&apos;})
                        .encodeToString(signingKey.getPublic().getEncoded())
                + &quot;\n-----END PUBLIC KEY-----\n&quot;;
        Path publicKeyPath = write(&quot;build-signing-public.pem&quot;, publicKeyPem);

        assertEquals(
                signingKey.getPublic(),
                AzureBuildProvenanceVerifier.loadEd25519PublicKey(publicKeyPath));
    }

    private Path writeManifest(Path artifact, Path sbom) throws Exception {
        return write(
                &quot;provenance.json&quot;,
                new String(manifest(artifact, sbom, artifact.getFileName().toString()),
                        StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
    }

    private byte[] manifest(Path artifact, Path sbom, String artifactName) throws Exception {
        return new ObjectMapper().writeValueAsBytes(java.util.Map.of(
                &quot;schemaVersion&quot;, 1,
                &quot;artifact&quot;, artifactName,
                &quot;sha256&quot;, digest(artifact),
                &quot;sbomSha256&quot;, digest(sbom),
                &quot;sourceRevision&quot;, REVISION));
    }

    private Path sign(Path manifest) throws Exception {
        byte[] contents = Files.readAllBytes(manifest);
        Signature signer = Signature.getInstance(&quot;Ed25519&quot;);
        signer.initSign(signingKey.getPrivate());
        signer.update(contents);
        return write(
                &quot;provenance.sig&quot;,
                Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(signer.sign()));
    }

    private Path write(String fileName, String contents) throws Exception {
        Path path = tempDirectory.resolve(fileName);
        return Files.writeString(path, contents, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
    }

    private static String digest(Path path) throws Exception {
        return HexFormat.of().formatHex(
                java.security.MessageDigest.getInstance(&quot;SHA-256&quot;).digest(Files.readAllBytes(path)));
    }

class AzureGuestAttestationNativeClientTest {

    @Test
    void passesEndpointAndNonceToNativeAttestationAndValidatesResponse() {
        var client = new AzureGuestAttestationNativeClient(ENDPOINT, new StubNativeOperations() {
            @Override
            public String attest(String endpoint, String nonce) {
                assertEquals(ENDPOINT.toString(), endpoint);
                assertEquals(&quot;A&quot;.repeat(43), nonce);
                return &quot;signed.jwt.token&quot;;
            }
        });

        assertEquals(&quot;signed.jwt.token&quot;, client.attest(&quot;A&quot;.repeat(43)));
    }

    @Test

        var client = new AzureGuestAttestationNativeClient(
                ENDPOINT,
                new StubNativeOperations() {
                    @Override
                    public String attest(String endpoint, String nonce) {
                        throw new AssertionError(&quot;Native operation must not run.&quot;);
                    }
                });

        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -&gt; client.attest(&quot;bad&quot;));
    }

    @Test
    void zeroesNativeKeyBufferAfterCreatingSecretKey() {
        byte[] nativeKey = new byte[32];
        Arrays.fill(nativeKey, (byte) 7);
        var client = new AzureGuestAttestationNativeClient(
                ENDPOINT,
                new StubNativeOperations() {
                    @Override
                    public byte[] unwrap(String releaseValue) {
                        assertEquals(RELEASE_JWT, releaseValue);
                        return nativeKey;
                    }
                });

        var key = client.unwrap(RELEASE_JWT);

        assertEquals(&quot;AES&quot;, key.getAlgorithm());
        assertArrayEquals(new byte[32], nativeKey);
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsInvalidNativeResponsesAndZeroesUnexpectedKeyMaterial() {
        var emptyEvidenceClient = new AzureGuestAttestationNativeClient(
                ENDPOINT, new StubNativeOperations() {
                    @Override
                    public String attest(String endpoint, String nonce) {
                        return &quot; &quot;;
                    }
                });
        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; emptyEvidenceClient.attest(&quot;A&quot;.repeat(43)));

        byte[] wrongLengthKey = new byte[16];
        Arrays.fill(wrongLengthKey, (byte) 9);
        var wrongKeyClient = new AzureGuestAttestationNativeClient(
                ENDPOINT, new StubNativeOperations() {
                    @Override
                    public byte[] unwrap(String releaseValue) {
                        return wrongLengthKey;
                    }
                });
        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; wrongKeyClient.unwrap(RELEASE_JWT));
        assertArrayEquals(new byte[16], wrongLengthKey);

        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; wrongKeyClient.unwrap(&quot;x&quot;.repeat(1_000_001)));
        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; wrongKeyClient.unwrap(&quot;not-a-compact-token&quot;));
        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; wrongKeyClient.unwrap(&quot;a.b.c.d&quot;));
    }

    private abstract static class StubNativeOperations
            implements AzureGuestAttestationNativeClient.NativeOperations {
        @Override
        public String attest(String endpoint, String nonce) {
            return &quot;not-used&quot;;
        }

        @Override
        public byte[] unwrap(String keyReleaseValue) {
            return new byte[32];
        }
    }

class AzureKeyVaultSecureKeyReleaseClientTest {
    private final ObjectMapper objectMapper = new ObjectMapper();

    @Test
    void postsAttestationAndNonceToVersionedSecureReleaseEndpoint() throws Exception {
        HttpClient httpClient = mock(HttpClient.class);
        @SuppressWarnings(&quot;unchecked&quot;)
        HttpResponse&lt;java.io.InputStream&gt; response = mock(HttpResponse.class);
        when(response.statusCode()).thenReturn(200);
        when(response.body()).thenReturn(new ByteArrayInputStream(
                &quot;{\&quot;value\&quot;:\&quot;signed-encrypted-release\&quot;}&quot;.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)));
        when(httpClient.&lt;java.io.InputStream&gt;send(any(HttpRequest.class), any()))
                .thenReturn(response);
        AzureKeyVaultAccessTokenProvider accessTokenProvider = () -&gt; &quot;vault-token&quot;;
        var client = new AzureKeyVaultSecureKeyReleaseClient(
                URI.create(&quot;https://example.vault.azure.net/&quot;),
                &quot;gateway-context-key&quot;,
                &quot;key-version-1&quot;,
                accessTokenProvider,
                httpClient,
                objectMapper);

        String releaseValue = client.release(&quot;verified-maa-jwt&quot;, &quot;fresh-nonce&quot;);

        assertEquals(&quot;signed-encrypted-release&quot;, releaseValue);
        var requestCaptor = org.mockito.ArgumentCaptor.forClass(HttpRequest.class);
        verify(httpClient).&lt;java.io.InputStream&gt;send(requestCaptor.capture(), any());
        HttpRequest request = requestCaptor.getValue();
        assertEquals(
                URI.create(&quot;https://example.vault.azure.net/keys/gateway-context-key/&quot;
                        + &quot;key-version-1/release?api-version=2025-07-01&quot;),
                request.uri());
        assertEquals(&quot;POST&quot;, request.method());
        assertEquals(&quot;Bearer vault-token&quot;, request.headers().firstValue(&quot;Authorization&quot;).orElseThrow());
        JsonNode requestJson = objectMapper.readTree(publisherBytes(request));
        assertEquals(&quot;verified-maa-jwt&quot;, requestJson.path(&quot;target&quot;).asText());
        assertEquals(&quot;fresh-nonce&quot;, requestJson.path(&quot;nonce&quot;).asText());
        assertEquals(&quot;RSA_AES_KEY_WRAP_256&quot;, requestJson.path(&quot;enc&quot;).asText());
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsNonAzureOrNonHttpsVaultUris() {
        assertThrows(
                IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -&gt; new AzureKeyVaultSecureKeyReleaseClient(
                        URI.create(&quot;http://example.vault.azure.net&quot;),
                        &quot;key&quot;,

                        mock(HttpClient.class),
                        objectMapper));
    }

    @Test
    void failsClosedWhenKeyVaultDeniesRelease() throws Exception {
        HttpClient httpClient = mock(HttpClient.class);
        @SuppressWarnings(&quot;unchecked&quot;)
        HttpResponse&lt;java.io.InputStream&gt; response = mock(HttpResponse.class);
        when(response.statusCode()).thenReturn(403);
        when(response.body()).thenReturn(new ByteArrayInputStream(new byte[0]));
        when(httpClient.&lt;java.io.InputStream&gt;send(any(HttpRequest.class), any()))
                .thenReturn(response);
        var client = new AzureKeyVaultSecureKeyReleaseClient(
                URI.create(&quot;https://example.vault.azure.net/&quot;),

                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; client.release(&quot;verified-maa-jwt&quot;, &quot;fresh-nonce&quot;));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsSuccessfulResponseWithoutReleaseValue() throws Exception {
        HttpClient httpClient = mock(HttpClient.class);
        @SuppressWarnings(&quot;unchecked&quot;)
        HttpResponse&lt;java.io.InputStream&gt; response = mock(HttpResponse.class);
        when(response.statusCode()).thenReturn(200);
        when(response.body()).thenReturn(new ByteArrayInputStream(
                &quot;{\&quot;status\&quot;:\&quot;success\&quot;}&quot;.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8)));
        when(httpClient.&lt;java.io.InputStream&gt;send(any(HttpRequest.class), any()))
                .thenReturn(response);
        var client = new AzureKeyVaultSecureKeyReleaseClient(
                URI.create(&quot;https://example.vault.azure.net/&quot;),
                &quot;gateway-context-key&quot;,
                &quot;key-version-1&quot;,
                () -&gt; &quot;vault-token&quot;,
                httpClient,
                objectMapper);

        assertThrows(
                AzureAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; client.release(&quot;verified-maa-jwt&quot;, &quot;fresh-nonce&quot;));
    }

    private static byte[] publisherBytes(HttpRequest request) throws Exception {
        var output = new java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream();
        request.bodyPublisher().orElseThrow().subscribe(new Flow.Subscriber&lt;&gt;() {
            @Override
            public void onSubscribe(Flow.Subscription subscription) {
                subscription.request(Long.MAX_VALUE);
            }

            @Override
            public void onNext(ByteBuffer item) {
                byte[] bytes = new byte[item.remaining()];
                item.get(bytes);
                output.writeBytes(bytes);
            }

            @Override
            public void onError(Throwable throwable) {
                throw new AssertionError(throwable);
            }

            @Override
            public void onComplete() {
            }
        });
        return output.toByteArray();
    }

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import java.net.URI;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.security.KeyPair;
import java.security.KeyPairGenerator;
import java.security.Signature;
import java.security.interfaces.RSAPublicKey;
