import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.ZoneOffset;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Set;
import java.net.URLDecoder;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.security.oauth2.jwt.Jwt;

class GcpConfidentialGatewayTest {
    private static final Instant NOW = Instant.parse(&quot;2026-10-17T08:30:00Z&quot;);
    private static final String GCA_AUDIENCE =
            &quot;//iam.googleapis.com/projects/123456789/locations/global/&quot;
                    + &quot;workloadIdentityPools/pool/providers/gca&quot;;
    private static final String WIF_AUDIENCE = GCA_AUDIENCE;
    private static final String KMS_RESOURCE =
            &quot;projects/trusted-project/locations/global/keyRings/runtime/cryptoKeys/context&quot;;
    private static final String JWT = &quot;header.payload.signature&quot;;
    private final ObjectMapper mapper = new ObjectMapper();

    @Test
    void rejectsInvalidEvidenceBeforeStsOrKms() {
        RecordingTransport transport = new RecordingTransport(mapper);
        GcpGcaAttestationVerifier verifier = verifier(
                policy(GcpAttestationPolicy.HardwareModel.AMD_SEV),
                false,
                false);
        GcpWifKmsSecureContextKeyProvider keys = keyProvider(verifier, transport);

        assertThrows(GcpAttestationException.class, keys::currentKey);
        assertTrue(transport.requests.isEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    void acceptsAllowlistedAmdClaimsThenExchangesAttestationAndDecryptsKey() {
        RecordingTransport transport = new RecordingTransport(mapper);
        GcpGcaAttestationVerifier verifier = verifier(
                policy(GcpAttestationPolicy.HardwareModel.AMD_SEV),
                true,
                false);
        GcpWifKmsSecureContextKeyProvider keys = keyProvider(verifier, transport);

        var released = keys.currentKey();

        assertEquals(&quot;context-dek&quot;, released.id());
        assertEquals(&quot;AES&quot;, released.key().getAlgorithm());
        assertEquals(32, released.key().getEncoded().length);
        assertEquals(List.of(
                        URI.create(&quot;https://sts.googleapis.com/v1/token&quot;),
                        URI.create(&quot;https://cloudkms.googleapis.com/v1/&quot; + KMS_RESOURCE + &quot;:decrypt&quot;)),
                transport.requests.stream().map(Request::uri).toList());
        assertTrue(transport.decodedForm.get().contains(&quot;subject_token=&quot; + JWT));
        assertTrue(transport.authorization.get().startsWith(&quot;Bearer federated-access-token&quot;));
    }

    @Test
    void acceptsTdxSpecificGceIdentityAndDoesNotRequireUnpublishedClaims() {
        RecordingTransport transport = new RecordingTransport(mapper);
        GcpAttestationPolicy policy = policy(GcpAttestationPolicy.HardwareModel.INTEL_TDX);
        GcpGcaAttestationVerifier verifier = verifier(policy, true, true);

        Jwt result = verifier.attestCurrentRuntime();

        assertEquals(&quot;GCP_INTEL_TDX&quot;, result.getClaimAsString(&quot;hwmodel&quot;));
        assertFalse(result.hasClaim(&quot;dbgstat&quot;));
        assertTrue(transport.requests.isEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    void googleDecoderAcceptsPinnedRsaSignatureAndRejectsTampering() throws Exception {
        KeyPairGenerator generator = KeyPairGenerator.getInstance(&quot;RSA&quot;);
        generator.initialize(2048);
        KeyPair keyPair = generator.generateKeyPair();
        String jwks = mapper.writeValueAsString(Map.of(
                &quot;keys&quot;,
                List.of(Map.of(
                        &quot;kty&quot;, &quot;RSA&quot;,
                        &quot;kid&quot;, &quot;gca-key&quot;,
                        &quot;alg&quot;, &quot;RS256&quot;,
                        &quot;use&quot;, &quot;sig&quot;,
                        &quot;n&quot;, base64Unsigned(((RSAPublicKey) keyPair.getPublic()).getModulus()),
                        &quot;e&quot;, base64Unsigned(((RSAPublicKey) keyPair.getPublic()).getPublicExponent())))));
        GcpHttpTransport transport = (method, uri, headers, body, timeout, maxBytes) -&gt; {
            assertEquals(GoogleGcaJwtDecoder.GOOGLE_GCA_JWKS_URI, uri);
            return new GcpHttpTransport.HttpResponse(200, jwks.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
        };
        GoogleGcaJwtDecoder decoder = new GoogleGcaJwtDecoder(transport, mapper);
        String signed = signedJwt(keyPair);

        assertEquals(&quot;https://confidentialcomputing.googleapis.com&quot;,
                decoder.decode(signed).getClaimAsString(&quot;iss&quot;));
        int payloadStart = signed.indexOf(&apos;.&apos;) + 1;
        String tampered = signed.substring(0, payloadStart)
                + (signed.charAt(payloadStart) == &apos;a&apos; ? &apos;b&apos; : &apos;a&apos;)
                + signed.substring(payloadStart + 1);
        assertThrows(GcpAttestationException.class, () -&gt; decoder.decode(tampered));
    }

    @Test
    void productionTransportRejectsNonHttpsExternalEndpoint() {
        JavaGcpHttpTransport transport = new JavaGcpHttpTransport(Duration.ofSeconds(1));
        assertThrows(
                GcpAttestationException.class,
                () -&gt; transport.postOrGet(
                        &quot;GET&quot;,
                        URI.create(&quot;http://example.org/metadata&quot;),
                        Map.of(),
                        null,
                        Duration.ofSeconds(1),
                        1024));
    }

    private GcpWifKmsSecureContextKeyProvider keyProvider(
            GcpGcaAttestationVerifier verifier,
            RecordingTransport transport) {
        return new GcpWifKmsSecureContextKeyProvider(
                verifier,
                transport,
                mapper,
                WIF_AUDIENCE,
                KMS_RESOURCE,
                &quot;context-dek&quot;,
                &quot;AQIDBA==&quot;);
    }

    private GcpGcaAttestationVerifier verifier(
            GcpAttestationPolicy policy,
            boolean secureBoot,
            boolean tdx) {
        AtomicReference&lt;String&gt; challenge = new AtomicReference&lt;&gt;();
        GcpAttestationTokenSource source = (audience, nonce) -&gt; {
            assertEquals(policy.audience(), audience);
            challenge.set(nonce);
            return JWT;
        };
        GcpAttestationJwtDecoder decoder = token -&gt; {
            assertEquals(JWT, token);
            return testJwt(challenge.get(), secureBoot, tdx);
        };
        return new GcpGcaAttestationVerifier(
                source,
                decoder,
                policy,
                Clock.fixed(NOW, ZoneOffset.UTC),
                new java.security.SecureRandom());
    }

    private Jwt testJwt(String nonce, boolean secureBoot, boolean tdx) {
        Map&lt;String, Object&gt; gce = new LinkedHashMap&lt;&gt;();
        gce.put(&quot;zone&quot;, &quot;us-central1-a&quot;);
        if (tdx) {
            gce.put(&quot;project_number&quot;, &quot;123456789&quot;);
            gce.put(&quot;instance_id&quot;, &quot;987654321&quot;);
        } else {
            gce.put(&quot;project_id&quot;, &quot;trusted-project&quot;);
            gce.put(&quot;instance_name&quot;, &quot;trusted-instance&quot;);
        }
        String subject = tdx
                ? &quot;//compute.googleapis.com/projects/123456789/zones/us-central1-a/instances/987654321&quot;
                : &quot;https://www.googleapis.com/compute/v1/projects/trusted-project/&quot;
                        + &quot;zones/us-central1-a/instances/trusted-instance&quot;;
        Map&lt;String, Object&gt; claims = new LinkedHashMap&lt;&gt;();
        claims.put(&quot;iss&quot;, GcpGcaAttestationVerifier.GOOGLE_ISSUER);
        claims.put(&quot;sub&quot;, subject);
        claims.put(&quot;aud&quot;, GCA_AUDIENCE);
        claims.put(&quot;iat&quot;, NOW.getEpochSecond());
        claims.put(&quot;exp&quot;, NOW.plusSeconds(300).getEpochSecond());
        claims.put(&quot;nbf&quot;, NOW.minusSeconds(1).getEpochSecond());
        claims.put(&quot;eat_nonce&quot;, tdx ? nonce : List.of(&quot;provider-nonce&quot;, nonce));
        claims.put(&quot;eat_profile&quot;, GcpGcaAttestationVerifier.EAT_PROFILE);
        claims.put(&quot;hwmodel&quot;, tdx ? &quot;GCP_INTEL_TDX&quot; : &quot;GCP_AMD_SEV&quot;);
        claims.put(&quot;secboot&quot;, secureBoot);
        claims.put(&quot;submods&quot;, Map.of(&quot;gce&quot;, gce));
        return new Jwt(JWT, NOW, NOW.plusSeconds(300), Map.of(&quot;alg&quot;, &quot;RS256&quot;), claims);
    }

    private static GcpAttestationPolicy policy(GcpAttestationPolicy.HardwareModel model) {
        return new GcpAttestationPolicy(
                GCA_AUDIENCE,
                model,
                Set.of(model == GcpAttestationPolicy.HardwareModel.AMD_SEV
                        ? &quot;trusted-project&quot; : &quot;123456789&quot;),
                Set.of(&quot;us-central1-a&quot;),
                Set.of(model == GcpAttestationPolicy.HardwareModel.AMD_SEV
                        ? &quot;trusted-instance&quot; : &quot;987654321&quot;));
    }

    private String signedJwt(KeyPair keyPair) throws Exception {
        String header = base64(mapper.writeValueAsBytes(Map.of(&quot;alg&quot;, &quot;RS256&quot;, &quot;kid&quot;, &quot;gca-key&quot;)));
        String payload = base64(mapper.writeValueAsBytes(Map.of(
                &quot;iss&quot;, GcpGcaAttestationVerifier.GOOGLE_ISSUER,
                &quot;iat&quot;, NOW.getEpochSecond(),
                &quot;exp&quot;, NOW.plusSeconds(300).getEpochSecond())));
        String signingInput = header + &quot;.&quot; + payload;
        Signature signer = Signature.getInstance(&quot;SHA256withRSA&quot;);
        signer.initSign(keyPair.getPrivate());
        signer.update(signingInput.getBytes(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII));
        return signingInput + &quot;.&quot; + base64(signer.sign());
    }

    private static String base64Unsigned(java.math.BigInteger value) {
        byte[] bytes = value.toByteArray();
        if (bytes[0] == 0) {
            bytes = java.util.Arrays.copyOfRange(bytes, 1, bytes.length);
        }
        return base64(bytes);
    }

    private static String base64(byte[] value) {
        return Base64.getUrlEncoder().withoutPadding().encodeToString(value);
    }

    private static final class RecordingTransport implements GcpHttpTransport {
        private final ObjectMapper mapper;
        private final List&lt;Request&gt; requests = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;();
        private final AtomicReference&lt;String&gt; decodedForm = new AtomicReference&lt;&gt;();
        private final AtomicReference&lt;String&gt; authorization = new AtomicReference&lt;&gt;();

        private RecordingTransport(ObjectMapper mapper) {
            this.mapper = mapper;
        }

        @Override
        public HttpResponse postOrGet(
                String method,
                URI uri,
                Map&lt;String, String&gt; headers,
                byte[] body,
                Duration timeout,
                int maximumResponseBytes) {
            requests.add(new Request(method, uri, headers, body));
            if (uri.equals(URI.create(&quot;https://sts.googleapis.com/v1/token&quot;))) {
                String form = new String(body, StandardCharsets.UTF_8);
                decodedForm.set(URLDecoder.decode(form, StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
                return new HttpResponse(200, &quot;&quot;&quot;
                        {&quot;access_token&quot;:&quot;federated-access-token&quot;,&quot;token_type&quot;:&quot;Bearer&quot;,&quot;expires_in&quot;:3600}
                        &quot;&quot;&quot;.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            }
            if (uri.toString().startsWith(&quot;https://cloudkms.googleapis.com/v1/&quot;)) {
                authorization.set(headers.get(&quot;Authorization&quot;));
                try {
                    byte[] dek = new byte[32];
                    for (int i = 0; i &lt; dek.length; i++) {
                        dek[i] = (byte) i;
                    }
                    return new HttpResponse(200, mapper.writeValueAsBytes(
                            Map.of(&quot;plaintext&quot;, Base64.getEncoder().encodeToString(dek))));
                } catch (Exception e) {
                    throw new AssertionError(e);
                }
            }
            throw new AssertionError(&quot;Unexpected HTTP request: &quot; + uri);
        }
    }

    private record Request(String method, URI uri, Map&lt;String, String&gt; headers, byte[] body) {}
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/test/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/gcp/GoTpmGcpAttestationTokenSourceTest.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.gcp;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;

import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.List;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class GoTpmGcpAttestationTokenSourceTest {
    private static final String AUDIENCE = &quot;https://cjae.example/attestation&quot;;
    private static final String NONCE = &quot;0123456789abcdefghijklmnopqrstuv&quot;;
    private static final String TOKEN = &quot;header.payload.signature&quot;;
    private static final Path GOTPM = Path.of(System.getProperty(&quot;java.io.tmpdir&quot;), &quot;cjae&quot;, &quot;bin&quot;, &quot;gotpm&quot;)
            .toAbsolutePath();
    private static final Path EVENT_LOG = Path.of(System.getProperty(&quot;java.io.tmpdir&quot;), &quot;cjae&quot;, &quot;tpm&quot;, &quot;event.log&quot;)
            .toAbsolutePath();

    @Test
    void invokesGoogleToolWithAudienceNonceAndHardwareTechnology() {
        GoTpmGcpAttestationTokenSource source = new GoTpmGcpAttestationTokenSource(
                GOTPM,
                &quot;tdx&quot;,
                EVENT_LOG,
                Duration.ofSeconds(45),
                (command, timeout) -&gt; {
                    assertEquals(List.of(
                            GOTPM.toString(),
                            &quot;token&quot;,
                            &quot;--audience&quot;,
                            AUDIENCE,
                            &quot;--custom-nonce&quot;,
                            NONCE,
                            &quot;--event-log&quot;,
                            EVENT_LOG.toString(),
                            &quot;--tee-technology&quot;,
                            &quot;tdx&quot;), command);
                    assertEquals(Duration.ofSeconds(45), timeout);
                    return TOKEN;
                });

        assertEquals(TOKEN, source.fetch(AUDIENCE, NONCE));
    }

    @Test
    void usesVtpmEvidenceWithoutAnUnsupportedSevSnpQuote() {
        GoTpmGcpAttestationTokenSource source = new GoTpmGcpAttestationTokenSource(
                GOTPM,
                &quot;&quot;,
                EVENT_LOG,
                Duration.ofSeconds(45),
                (command, timeout) -&gt; {
                    assertEquals(List.of(
                            GOTPM.toString(),
                            &quot;token&quot;,
                            &quot;--audience&quot;,
                            AUDIENCE,
                            &quot;--custom-nonce&quot;,
                            NONCE,
                            &quot;--event-log&quot;,
                            EVENT_LOG.toString()), command);
                    return TOKEN;
                });

        assertEquals(TOKEN, source.fetch(AUDIENCE, NONCE));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsInvalidAudienceAndNonceBeforeInvokingGoogleTool() {
        GoTpmGcpAttestationTokenSource source = new GoTpmGcpAttestationTokenSource(
                GOTPM,
                &quot;&quot;,
                EVENT_LOG,
                Duration.ofSeconds(45),
                (command, timeout) -&gt; {
                    throw new AssertionError(&quot;Invalid input must not invoke gotpm.&quot;);
                });

        assertThrows(GcpAttestationException.class, () -&gt; source.fetch(&quot;bad audience&quot;, NONCE));
        assertThrows(GcpAttestationException.class, () -&gt; source.fetch(AUDIENCE, &quot;short&quot;));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsNonJwtOutputAndUnsupportedHardware() {
        GoTpmGcpAttestationTokenSource invalidOutput = new GoTpmGcpAttestationTokenSource(
                GOTPM,
                &quot;tdx&quot;,
                EVENT_LOG,
                Duration.ofSeconds(45),
                (command, timeout) -&gt; &quot;not-a-jwt&quot;);

        assertThrows(GcpAttestationException.class, () -&gt; invalidOutput.fetch(AUDIENCE, NONCE));
        assertThrows(
                IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -&gt; new GoTpmGcpAttestationTokenSource(
                        GOTPM,
                        &quot;unknown&quot;,
                        EVENT_LOG,
                        Duration.ofSeconds(45)));
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/test/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/onnx/BertWordPieceTokenizerTest.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.onnx;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;

import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.List;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;

class BertWordPieceTokenizerTest {
    @TempDir
    Path temporaryDirectory;

    @Test
    void tokenizesWordPiecesAndPreservesOriginalUtf16Offsets() throws Exception {
        BertWordPieceTokenizer tokenizer = createTokenizer(
                &quot;[PAD]\n[UNK]\n[CLS]\n[SEP]\nplay\n##ing\n!\n&quot;);

        List&lt;BertWordPieceTokenizer.Token&gt; tokens = tokenizer.tokenize(&quot;Playing!&quot;);

        assertEquals(List.of(
                new BertWordPieceTokenizer.Token(4, 0, 4, 0, 7),
                new BertWordPieceTokenizer.Token(5, 4, 7, 0, 7),
                new BertWordPieceTokenizer.Token(6, 7, 8, 7, 8)), tokens);
    }

    @Test
    void mapsUnrecognizedWordsToUnknownTokenWithoutLosingTheirOffsets() throws Exception {
        BertWordPieceTokenizer tokenizer = createTokenizer(
                &quot;[PAD]\n[UNK]\n[CLS]\n[SEP]\n!\n&quot;);

        assertEquals(List.of(
                new BertWordPieceTokenizer.Token(1, 0, 5),
                new BertWordPieceTokenizer.Token(4, 5, 6)),
                tokenizer.tokenize(&quot;Hello!&quot;));
    }

    @Test
    void normalizesUnicodeWhileKeepingOriginalOffsets() throws Exception {
        BertWordPieceTokenizer tokenizer = createTokenizer(
                &quot;[PAD]\n[UNK]\n[CLS]\n[SEP]\ncafe\n&quot;);

        assertEquals(List.of(new BertWordPieceTokenizer.Token(4, 0, 4)),
                tokenizer.tokenize(&quot;Caf\u00e9&quot;));
    }

    private BertWordPieceTokenizer createTokenizer(String vocabulary) throws Exception {
        Path vocabularyPath = temporaryDirectory.resolve(&quot;vocab.txt&quot;);
        Path specialTokensPath = temporaryDirectory.resolve(&quot;special_tokens_map.json&quot;);
        Files.writeString(vocabularyPath, vocabulary);
        Files.writeString(
                specialTokensPath,
                &quot;{\&quot;unk_token\&quot;:\&quot;[UNK]\&quot;,\&quot;cls_token\&quot;:\&quot;[CLS]\&quot;,&quot;
                        + &quot;\&quot;sep_token\&quot;:\&quot;[SEP]\&quot;,\&quot;pad_token\&quot;:\&quot;[PAD]\&quot;}&quot;);
        return new BertWordPieceTokenizer(
                vocabularyPath, specialTokensPath, true, new ObjectMapper());
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/test/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/onnx/OnnxPiiModelArtifactVerifierTest.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway.onnx;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;

import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.security.MessageDigest;
import java.util.HexFormat;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;

class OnnxPiiModelArtifactVerifierTest {
    @TempDir
    Path temporaryDirectory;

    @Test
    void verifiesModelDigestWithoutLoadingWholeFileIntoHeap() throws Exception {
        Path model = temporaryDirectory.resolve(&quot;model.onnx&quot;);
        byte[] contents = new byte[OnnxPiiModelArtifactVerifierTest.CHUNK_BYTES + 19];
        for (int i = 0; i &lt; contents.length; i++) {
            contents[i] = (byte) (i * 31);
        }
        Files.write(model, contents);
        String expectedDigest = HexFormat.of().formatHex(
                MessageDigest.getInstance(&quot;SHA-256&quot;).digest(contents));

        var verified = OnnxPiiModelArtifactVerifier.verify(model, expectedDigest);

        assertEquals(model.toAbsolutePath().normalize(), verified.path());
        assertEquals(expectedDigest, verified.sha256());
        assertEquals(contents.length, verified.sizeBytes());
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsDigestMismatchAndEmptyFile() throws Exception {
        Path model = temporaryDirectory.resolve(&quot;model.onnx&quot;);
        Files.writeString(model, &quot;not the pinned model&quot;);

        assertThrows(
                IllegalStateException.class,
                () -&gt; OnnxPiiModelArtifactVerifier.verify(model, &quot;0&quot;.repeat(64)));

        Files.write(model, new byte[0]);
        assertThrows(
                IllegalStateException.class,
                () -&gt; OnnxPiiModelArtifactVerifier.verify(
                        model, &quot;e3b0c44298fc1c149afbf4c8996fb92427ae41e4649b934ca495991b7852b855&quot;));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsMalformedExpectedDigest() {
        assertThrows(
                IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -&gt; OnnxPiiModelArtifactVerifier.verify(
                        temporaryDirectory.resolve(&quot;model.onnx&quot;), &quot;not-a-digest&quot;));
    }

    private static final int CHUNK_BYTES = 1024 * 1024;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

import java.security.SecureRandom;
import java.time.Clock;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.ZoneOffset;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;

import javax.crypto.KeyGenerator;
import javax.crypto.SecretKey;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

import com.fasterxml.jackson.databind.ObjectMapper;

class ConfidentialGatewayServiceTest {
    private static final String TENANT = &quot;tenant-a&quot;;
    private static final Instant NOW = Instant.parse(&quot;2026-01-01T00:00:00Z&quot;);

    @Test
    void sanitizesAndRestoresDetectedEntities() throws Exception {
        Fixture fixture = new Fixture(NOW);
        ConfidentialGatewayService service = fixture.service();

        ConfidentialGatewayService.SanitizeResult result = service.sanitize(
                TENANT, &quot;Contact Ada at ada@example.com or 212-555-0199.&quot;);

        assertFalse(result.sanitizedText().contains(&quot;ada@example.com&quot;));
        assertFalse(result.sanitizedText().contains(&quot;212-555-0199&quot;));
        assertTrue(result.secureContext().startsWith(&quot;v2.&quot;));
        assertFalse(result.secureContext().contains(&quot;ada@example.com&quot;));
        assertEquals(&quot;Contact Ada at ada@example.com or 212-555-0199.&quot;,
                service.desanitize(TENANT, result.sanitizedText(), result.secureContext()));
        assertEquals(&quot;prototype-rules-1&quot;, result.detectorVersion());
        assertEquals(NOW.plus(Duration.ofMinutes(10)), result.expiresAt());
    }

    @Test
    void sanitizesAndRestoresPiiFollowingSupplementaryUnicode() throws Exception {
        ConfidentialGatewayService service = new Fixture(NOW).service();
        String original = &quot;😀 Contact ada@example.com&quot;;

        var result = service.sanitize(TENANT, original);

        assertTrue(result.sanitizedText().startsWith(&quot;😀 Contact &quot;));
        assertFalse(result.sanitizedText().contains(&quot;ada@example.com&quot;));
        assertEquals(original,
                service.desanitize(TENANT, result.sanitizedText(), result.secureContext()));
    }

    @Test
    void sanitizesAndRestoresMultipleSegmentsWithOneContext() throws Exception {
        ConfidentialGatewayService service = new Fixture(NOW).service();

        var result = service.sanitizeSegments(
                TENANT,
                List.of(&quot;Email ada@example.com&quot;, &quot;Phone 212-555-0199&quot;));

        assertEquals(2, result.sanitizedSegments().size());
        assertFalse(result.sanitizedSegments().get(0).contains(&quot;ada@example.com&quot;));
        assertFalse(result.sanitizedSegments().get(1).contains(&quot;212-555-0199&quot;));
        assertEquals(
                List.of(&quot;Email ada@example.com&quot;, &quot;Phone 212-555-0199&quot;),
                service.desanitizeSegments(TENANT, result.sanitizedSegments(), result.secureContext()));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsEmptyAndOversizedBatches() throws Exception {
        ConfidentialGatewayService service = new Fixture(NOW).service();

        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -&gt; service.sanitizeSegments(TENANT, List.of()));
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -&gt; service.sanitizeSegments(TENANT, java.util.Collections.nCopies(101, &quot;x&quot;)));
    }

    @Test
    void leavesUnmatchedTextUnchangedWithValidEmptyContext() throws Exception {
        Fixture fixture = new Fixture(NOW);
        ConfidentialGatewayService service = fixture.service();

        ConfidentialGatewayService.SanitizeResult result =
                service.sanitize(TENANT, &quot;No supported entities here.&quot;);

        assertEquals(&quot;No supported entities here.&quot;, result.sanitizedText());
        assertEquals(result.sanitizedText(),
                service.desanitize(TENANT, result.sanitizedText(), result.secureContext()));
    }

    @Test
    void secureContextCannotBeUsedByAnotherTenant() throws Exception {
        Fixture fixture = new Fixture(NOW);
        ConfidentialGatewayService service = fixture.service();
        var result = service.sanitize(TENANT, &quot;ada@example.com&quot;);

        assertThrows(SecureContextException.class,
                () -&gt; service.desanitize(&quot;tenant-b&quot;, result.sanitizedText(), result.secureContext()));
    }

    @Test
    void tamperedSecureContextIsRejected() throws Exception {
        Fixture fixture = new Fixture(NOW);
        ConfidentialGatewayService service = fixture.service();
        var result = service.sanitize(TENANT, &quot;ada@example.com&quot;);
        String[] contextParts = result.secureContext().split(&quot;\\.&quot;, -1);
        contextParts[3] = (contextParts[3].startsWith(&quot;A&quot;) ? &quot;B&quot; : &quot;A&quot;)
                + contextParts[3].substring(1);
        String tampered = String.join(&quot;.&quot;, contextParts);

        assertThrows(SecureContextException.class,
                () -&gt; service.desanitize(TENANT, result.sanitizedText(), tampered));
    }

    @Test
    void expiredSecureContextIsRejected() throws Exception {
        Fixture fixture = new Fixture(NOW);
        ConfidentialGatewayService service = fixture.service();
        var result = service.sanitize(TENANT, &quot;ada@example.com&quot;);
        fixture.clock.advance(Duration.ofMinutes(10));

        assertThrows(SecureContextException.class,
                () -&gt; service.desanitize(TENANT, result.sanitizedText(), result.secureContext()));
    }

    @Test
    void secureContextCanBeOpenedAfterKeyRotation() throws Exception {
        Fixture fixture = new Fixture(NOW);
        ConfidentialGatewayService service = fixture.service();
        var result = service.sanitize(TENANT, &quot;ada@example.com&quot;);
        fixture.keyProvider.rotate(&quot;key-2&quot;, generateKey());

        assertEquals(&quot;ada@example.com&quot;,
                service.desanitize(TENANT, result.sanitizedText(), result.secureContext()));
        assertTrue(fixture.keyProvider.findKey(&quot;key-1&quot;).isPresent());
    }

    @Test
    void secureContextAuthenticatesItsKeyIdentifier() throws Exception {
        Fixture fixture = new Fixture(NOW);
        ConfidentialGatewayService service = fixture.service();
        var result = service.sanitize(TENANT, &quot;ada@example.com&quot;);
        fixture.keyProvider.rotate(&quot;key-alias&quot;, fixture.keyProvider.currentKey().key());
        String[] parts = result.secureContext().split(&quot;\\.&quot;, -1);
        parts[1] = &quot;key-alias&quot;;
        String tampered = String.join(&quot;.&quot;, parts);

        assertThrows(SecureContextException.class,
                () -&gt; service.desanitize(TENANT, result.sanitizedText(), tampered));
    }

    @Test
    void prototypeDetectorFindsOnlyItsDocumentedEntityTypes() {
        PrototypeRuleBasedPiiDetector detector = new PrototypeRuleBasedPiiDetector();

        assertEquals(2, detector.detect(&quot;ada@example.com 212-555-0199&quot;).size());
        assertTrue(detector.detect(&quot;Ada Lovelace, 123 Main Street&quot;).isEmpty());
    }

    private static SecretKey generateKey() throws Exception {
        KeyGenerator generator = KeyGenerator.getInstance(&quot;AES&quot;);
        generator.init(256);
        return generator.generateKey();
    }

    private static final class Fixture {
        private final TestKeyProvider keyProvider;
        private final MutableClock clock;

        private Fixture(Instant now) throws Exception {
            keyProvider = new TestKeyProvider(new SecureContextKey(&quot;key-1&quot;, generateKey()));
            clock = new MutableClock(now);
        }

        private ConfidentialGatewayService service() {
            AesGcmSecureContextCodec codec = new AesGcmSecureContextCodec(
                    keyProvider, new ObjectMapper(), new SecureRandom(), clock);
            return new ConfidentialGatewayService(
                    new PrototypeRuleBasedPiiDetector(),
                    codec,
                    Duration.ofMinutes(10),
                    new SecureRandom(),
                    clock);
        }
    }

    private static final class MutableClock extends Clock {
        private Instant instant;

        private MutableClock(Instant instant) {
            this.instant = instant;
        }

        private void advance(Duration duration) {
            instant = instant.plus(duration);
        }

        @Override
        public ZoneOffset getZone() {
            return ZoneOffset.UTC;
        }

        @Override
        public Clock withZone(java.time.ZoneId zone) {
            if (!ZoneOffset.UTC.equals(zone)) {
                throw new IllegalArgumentException(&quot;Test clock only supports UTC.&quot;);
            }
            return this;
        }

        @Override
        public Instant instant() {
            return instant;
        }
    }

    private static final class TestKeyProvider implements SecureContextKeyProvider {
        private final Map&lt;String, SecureContextKey&gt; keys = new HashMap&lt;&gt;();
        private SecureContextKey currentKey;

        private TestKeyProvider(SecureContextKey currentKey) {
            rotate(currentKey.id(), currentKey.key());
        }

        private void rotate(String id, SecretKey key) {
            currentKey = new SecureContextKey(id, key);
            keys.put(id, currentKey);
        }

        @Override
        public SecureContextKey currentKey() {
            return currentKey;
        }

        @Override
        public Optional&lt;SecureContextKey&gt; findKey(String id) {
            return Optional.ofNullable(keys.get(id));
        }
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/test/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/ConfidentialOperationGovernorTest.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNull;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch;
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class ConfidentialOperationGovernorTest {
    @Test
    void limitsConcurrentOperationsAndReleasesPermitAfterCompletion() throws Exception {
        ConfidentialOperationGovernor governor = new ConfidentialOperationGovernor(1);
        CountDownLatch secondAttempted = new CountDownLatch(1);
        CountDownLatch secondEntered = new CountDownLatch(1);
        CountDownLatch secondFinished = new CountDownLatch(1);
        AtomicReference&lt;Throwable&gt; secondFailure = new AtomicReference&lt;&gt;();

        governor.execute(() -&gt; {
            Thread second = Thread.startVirtualThread(() -&gt; {
                secondAttempted.countDown();
                try {
                    governor.execute(() -&gt; {
                        secondEntered.countDown();
                        return null;
                    });
                } catch (Throwable failure) {
                    secondFailure.set(failure);
                } finally {
                    secondFinished.countDown();
                }
            });

            await(secondAttempted);
            assertNotEntered(secondEntered);
            return null;
        });

        await(secondFinished);
        assertNull(secondFailure.get());
        assertEquals(1, governor.maximumConcurrentOperations());
    }

    @Test
    void restoresInterruptStatusWhenWaitingIsInterrupted() throws Exception {
        ConfidentialOperationGovernor governor = new ConfidentialOperationGovernor(1);
        CountDownLatch attempted = new CountDownLatch(1);
        CountDownLatch finished = new CountDownLatch(1);
        AtomicReference&lt;Boolean&gt; interruptRestored = new AtomicReference&lt;&gt;(false);

        governor.execute(() -&gt; {
            Thread waiter = Thread.startVirtualThread(() -&gt; {
                attempted.countDown();
                try {
                    governor.execute(() -&gt; null);
                } catch (IllegalStateException expected) {
                    interruptRestored.set(Thread.currentThread().isInterrupted());
                } finally {
                    finished.countDown();
                }
            });
            await(attempted);
            waiter.interrupt();
            await(finished);
            return null;
        });

        assertTrue(interruptRestored.get());
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsNonPositivePermitLimit() {
        assertThrows(
                IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -&gt; new ConfidentialOperationGovernor(0));
    }

    @Test
    void explicitPermitCanCoverRequestParsingAndCannotBeReusedAfterClose() {
        ConfidentialOperationGovernor governor = new ConfidentialOperationGovernor(1);
        ConfidentialOperationGovernor.Permit permit = governor.acquire();

        assertEquals(&quot;processed&quot;, governor.execute(permit, () -&gt; &quot;processed&quot;));
        permit.close();
        assertThrows(
                IllegalArgumentException.class,
                () -&gt; governor.execute(permit, () -&gt; &quot;should not run&quot;));
    }

    private static void await(CountDownLatch latch) {
        try {
            assertTrue(latch.await(Duration.ofSeconds(5).toMillis(), TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS));
        } catch (InterruptedException exception) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            throw new AssertionError(&quot;Test thread was interrupted.&quot;, exception);
        }
    }

    private static void assertNotEntered(CountDownLatch latch) {
        try {
            assertFalse(latch.await(100, TimeUnit.MILLISECONDS));
        } catch (InterruptedException exception) {
            Thread.currentThread().interrupt();
            throw new AssertionError(&quot;Test thread was interrupted.&quot;, exception);
        }
    }

                    () -&gt; new NativeUtf8CharSequence(segment));

}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/test/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/gateway/PiiDetectorEvaluationTest.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential.gateway;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

import java.lang.management.ManagementFactory;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Comparator;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Locale;
import java.util.Map;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class PiiDetectorEvaluationTest {
    private static final int WARMUP_ITERATIONS = 2_000;
    private static final int MEASURED_ITERATIONS = 10_000;

    private final PiiDetector detector = new PrototypeRuleBasedPiiDetector();
    private final List&lt;Fixture&gt; corpus = syntheticCorpus();

    @Test
    void reportsQualityCoverageLatencyAndAllocationForSyntheticCorpus() {
        Evaluation evaluation = evaluate();
        Performance performance = measurePerformance();

        assertEquals(16, evaluation.goldCount());
        assertEquals(4, evaluation.truePositives());
        assertEquals(5, evaluation.predictedCount());
        assertEquals(0.8, evaluation.precision(), 0.0001);
        assertEquals(0.25, evaluation.recall(), 0.0001);
        assertEquals(0.38095, evaluation.f1(), 0.0001);
        assertTrue(evaluation.falsePositives() &gt;= 0);
        assertTrue(evaluation.falseNegatives() &gt; 0,
                &quot;The corpus must keep unsupported entity categories visible.&quot;);
        assertTrue(performance.p95Nanos() &gt; 0);
        assertTrue(performance.allocatedBytesPerDetection() &gt;= -1);

        System.out.printf(Locale.ROOT,
                &quot;PII evaluation detector=%s corpus=%d gold=%d predicted=%d &quot;
                        + &quot;tp=%d fp=%d fn=%d precision=%.3f recall=%.3f f1=%.3f &quot;
                        + &quot;p50_ns=%d p95_ns=%d allocated_bytes_per_detection=%s%n&quot;,
                detector.version(),
                corpus.size(),
                evaluation.goldCount(),
                evaluation.predictedCount(),
                evaluation.truePositives(),
                evaluation.falsePositives(),
                evaluation.falseNegatives(),
                evaluation.precision(),
                evaluation.recall(),
                evaluation.f1(),
                performance.p50Nanos(),
                performance.p95Nanos(),
                performance.allocatedBytesPerDetection() &lt; 0
                        ? &quot;unavailable&quot;
                        : Long.toString(performance.allocatedBytesPerDetection()));
        evaluation.categoryRecall().forEach((category, recall) -&gt;
                System.out.printf(Locale.ROOT, &quot;  category=%s recall=%.3f%n&quot;, category, recall));
        evaluation.languageRecall().forEach((language, recall) -&gt;
                System.out.printf(Locale.ROOT, &quot;  language=%s recall=%.3f%n&quot;, language, recall));
    }

    private Evaluation evaluate() {
        int truePositives = 0;
        int predictedCount = 0;
        int goldCount = 0;
        Map&lt;String, int[]&gt; categoryCounts = new HashMap&lt;&gt;();
        Map&lt;String, int[]&gt; languageCounts = new HashMap&lt;&gt;();

        for (Fixture fixture : corpus) {
            List&lt;DetectedEntity&gt; predicted = detector.detect(fixture.text());
            predictedCount += predicted.size();
            goldCount += fixture.gold().size();
            boolean[] matchedGold = new boolean[fixture.gold().size()];
            for (DetectedEntity result : predicted) {
                int matchingIndex = exactGoldMatch(fixture.gold(), result);
                if (matchingIndex &gt;= 0 &amp;&amp; !matchedGold[matchingIndex]) {
                    matchedGold[matchingIndex] = true;
                    truePositives++;
                    GoldEntity gold = fixture.gold().get(matchingIndex);
                    categoryCounts.computeIfAbsent(gold.category(), ignored -&gt; new int[2])[0]++;
                    languageCounts.computeIfAbsent(fixture.language(), ignored -&gt; new int[2])[0]++;
                }
            }
            for (GoldEntity gold : fixture.gold()) {
                categoryCounts.computeIfAbsent(gold.category(), ignored -&gt; new int[2])[1]++;
                languageCounts.computeIfAbsent(fixture.language(), ignored -&gt; new int[2])[1]++;
            }
        }

        int falsePositives = predictedCount - truePositives;
        int falseNegatives = goldCount - truePositives;
        double precision = ratio(truePositives, predictedCount);
        double recall = ratio(truePositives, goldCount);
        double f1 = precision + recall == 0
                ? 0
                : 2 * precision * recall / (precision + recall);
        return new Evaluation(
                goldCount,
                predictedCount,
                truePositives,
                falsePositives,
                falseNegatives,
                precision,
                recall,
                f1,
                recalls(categoryCounts),
                recalls(languageCounts));
    }

    private Performance measurePerformance() {
        for (int i = 0; i &lt; WARMUP_ITERATIONS; i++) {
            detectCorpusOnce();
        }

        long threadId = Thread.currentThread().threadId();
        com.sun.management.ThreadMXBean allocationBean = allocationBean();
        long allocatedBefore = allocationBean == null
                ? -1
                : allocationBean.getThreadAllocatedBytes(threadId);
        long[] elapsedNanos = new long[MEASURED_ITERATIONS];
        for (int i = 0; i &lt; MEASURED_ITERATIONS; i++) {
            Fixture fixture = corpus.get(i % corpus.size());
            long start = System.nanoTime();
            detector.detect(fixture.text());
            elapsedNanos[i] = System.nanoTime() - start;
        }
        long allocatedAfter = allocationBean == null
                ? -1
                : allocationBean.getThreadAllocatedBytes(threadId);

        java.util.Arrays.sort(elapsedNanos);
        long allocatedBytesPerDetection =
                allocatedBeanAvailable(allocatedBefore, allocatedAfter)
                        ? Math.max(0, (allocatedAfter - allocatedBefore) / MEASURED_ITERATIONS)
                        : -1;
        return new Performance(
                percentile(elapsedNanos, 0.50),
                percentile(elapsedNanos, 0.95),
                allocatedBytesPerDetection);
    }

    private void detectCorpusOnce() {
        for (Fixture fixture : corpus) {
            detector.detect(fixture.text());
        }
    }

    private static com.sun.management.ThreadMXBean allocationBean() {
        var bean = ManagementFactory.getThreadMXBean();
        if (bean instanceof com.sun.management.ThreadMXBean allocationBean
                &amp;&amp; allocationBean.isThreadAllocatedMemorySupported()) {
            if (!allocationBean.isThreadAllocatedMemoryEnabled()) {
                allocationBean.setThreadAllocatedMemoryEnabled(true);
            }
            return allocationBean;
        }
        return null;
    }

    private static boolean allocatedBeanAvailable(long before, long after) {
        return before &gt;= 0 &amp;&amp; after &gt;= before;
    }

    private static int exactGoldMatch(
            List&lt;GoldEntity&gt; gold, DetectedEntity predicted) {
        for (int i = 0; i &lt; gold.size(); i++) {
            GoldEntity expected = gold.get(i);
            if (expected.start() == predicted.start()
                    &amp;&amp; expected.end() == predicted.end()
                    &amp;&amp; expected.detectorType().equals(predicted.type())) {
                return i;
            }
        }
        return -1;
    }

    private static Map&lt;String, Double&gt; recalls(Map&lt;String, int[]&gt; counts) {
        Map&lt;String, Double&gt; result = new java.util.TreeMap&lt;&gt;();
        counts.forEach((key, value) -&gt; result.put(key, ratio(value[0], value[1])));
        return Map.copyOf(result);
    }

    private static double ratio(int numerator, int denominator) {
        return denominator == 0 ? 0 : (double) numerator / denominator;
    }

    private static long percentile(long[] sorted, double percentile) {
        int index = Math.min(sorted.length - 1,
                (int) Math.ceil(percentile * sorted.length) - 1);
        return sorted[index];
    }

    private static List&lt;Fixture&gt; syntheticCorpus() {
        return List.of(
                fixture(&quot;en-email&quot;, &quot;en&quot;, &quot;Email ada@example.com for a reply.&quot;,
                        entity(&quot;EMAIL&quot;, &quot;ada@example.com&quot;)),
                fixture(&quot;en-phone&quot;, &quot;en&quot;, &quot;Call (212) 555-0199 after lunch.&quot;,
                        entity(&quot;PHONE_NUMBER&quot;, &quot;(212) 555-0199&quot;)),
                fixture(&quot;en-mixed&quot;, &quot;en&quot;, &quot;Reach bob.smith+work@example.org or 415-555-0132.&quot;,
                        entity(&quot;EMAIL&quot;, &quot;bob.smith+work@example.org&quot;),
                        entity(&quot;PHONE_NUMBER&quot;, &quot;415-555-0132&quot;)),
                fixture(&quot;en-negative&quot;, &quot;en&quot;, &quot;The example address is user at example dot com.&quot;),
                fixture(&quot;en-name&quot;, &quot;en&quot;, &quot;Ask Ada Lovelace to review the report.&quot;,
                        entity(&quot;PERSON_NAME&quot;, &quot;Ada Lovelace&quot;)),
                fixture(&quot;en-address&quot;, &quot;en&quot;, &quot;Ship it to 221B Baker Street, London.&quot;,
                        entity(&quot;POSTAL_ADDRESS&quot;, &quot;221B Baker Street, London&quot;)),
                fixture(&quot;en-payment&quot;, &quot;en&quot;, &quot;Test card 4111 1111 1111 1111.&quot;,
                        entity(&quot;PAYMENT_CARD&quot;, &quot;4111 1111 1111 1111&quot;)),
                fixture(&quot;fr-phone&quot;, &quot;fr&quot;, &quot;Appelez le +33 6 12 34 56 78 demain.&quot;,
                        entity(&quot;PHONE_INTERNATIONAL&quot;, &quot;+33 6 12 34 56 78&quot;)),
                fixture(&quot;fr-email-idn&quot;, &quot;fr&quot;, &quot;Écrivez à alice@bücher.de, merci.&quot;,
                        entity(&quot;EMAIL_IDN&quot;, &quot;alice@bücher.de&quot;)),
                fixture(&quot;es-phone&quot;, &quot;es&quot;, &quot;Llame al +34 612 345 678.&quot;,
                        entity(&quot;PHONE_INTERNATIONAL&quot;, &quot;+34 612 345 678&quot;)),
                fixture(&quot;es-name&quot;, &quot;es&quot;, &quot;Consulte a María-José Álvarez.&quot;,
                        entity(&quot;PERSON_NAME&quot;, &quot;María-José Álvarez&quot;)),
                fixture(&quot;de-address&quot;, &quot;de&quot;, &quot;Lieferung: Hauptstraße 5, 10115 Berlin.&quot;,
                        entity(&quot;POSTAL_ADDRESS&quot;, &quot;Hauptstraße 5, 10115 Berlin&quot;)),
                fixture(&quot;zh-phone&quot;, &quot;zh&quot;, &quot;联系电话 +86 138 0013 8000。&quot;,
                        entity(&quot;PHONE_INTERNATIONAL&quot;, &quot;+86 138 0013 8000&quot;)),
                fixture(&quot;ja-id&quot;, &quot;ja&quot;, &quot;個人番号は1234-5678-9012です。&quot;,
                        entity(&quot;GOVERNMENT_ID&quot;, &quot;1234-5678-9012&quot;)),
                fixture(&quot;en-health&quot;, &quot;en&quot;, &quot;Diagnosis: type 2 diabetes.&quot;,
                        entity(&quot;HEALTH_INFORMATION&quot;, &quot;type 2 diabetes&quot;)),
                fixture(&quot;en-ambiguous&quot;, &quot;en&quot;, &quot;The code 123-456-7890 is a ticket number.&quot;,
                        entity(&quot;TICKET_NUMBER&quot;, &quot;123-456-7890&quot;)));
    }

    private static Fixture fixture(String id, String language, String text, GoldEntity... values) {
        List&lt;GoldEntity&gt; gold = new ArrayList&lt;&gt;();
        for (GoldEntity value : values) {
            int start = text.indexOf(value.literal());
            if (start &lt; 0) {
                throw new IllegalStateException(&quot;Synthetic label is absent from fixture &quot; + id);
            }
            gold.add(new GoldEntity(
                    value.category(),
                    value.detectorType(),
                    value.literal(),
                    start,
                    start + value.literal().length()));
        }
        gold.sort(Comparator.comparingInt(GoldEntity::start));
        return new Fixture(id, language, text, List.copyOf(gold));
    }

    private static GoldEntity entity(String category, String literal) {
        String detectorType = category.equals(&quot;EMAIL&quot;)
                ? &quot;EMAIL&quot;
                : category.equals(&quot;PHONE_NUMBER&quot;) ? &quot;PHONE_NUMBER&quot; : &quot;&quot;;
        return new GoldEntity(category, detectorType, literal, -1, -1);
    }

    private record Fixture(String id, String language, String text, List&lt;GoldEntity&gt; gold) {
    }

    private record GoldEntity(
            String category,
            String detectorType,
            String literal,
            int start,
            int end) {
    }

    private record Evaluation(
            int goldCount,
            int predictedCount,
            int truePositives,
            int falsePositives,
            int falseNegatives,
            double precision,
            double recall,
            double f1,
            Map&lt;String, Double&gt; categoryRecall,
            Map&lt;String, Double&gt; languageRecall) {
    }

    private record Performance(
            long p50Nanos,
            long p95Nanos,
            long allocatedBytesPerDetection) {
    }

class ConfidentialAIAspectTest {
    @Test
    void proceedsWhenRuntimeVerifierApproves() throws Throwable {
        ConfidentialRuntimeVerifier verifier = mock(ConfidentialRuntimeVerifier.class);
        ProceedingJoinPoint joinPoint = mock(ProceedingJoinPoint.class);
        when(joinPoint.proceed()).thenReturn(&quot;completed&quot;);

        ConfidentialAIAspect aspect = new ConfidentialAIAspect(List.of(verifier));

        assertEquals(&quot;completed&quot;, aspect.requireConfidentialRuntime(joinPoint));

        ConfidentialRuntimeVerifier verifier = mock(ConfidentialRuntimeVerifier.class);
        ProceedingJoinPoint joinPoint = mock(ProceedingJoinPoint.class);
        doThrow(new ConfidentialExecutionDeniedException(&quot;Attestation rejected.&quot;))
                .when(verifier).verifyCurrentRuntime();

        ConfidentialAIAspect aspect = new ConfidentialAIAspect(List.of(verifier));

        assertThrows(
                ConfidentialExecutionDeniedException.class,
                () -&gt; aspect.requireConfidentialRuntime(joinPoint));
        verify(joinPoint, never()).proceed();
    }

    @Test

        ProceedingJoinPoint joinPoint = mock(ProceedingJoinPoint.class);

        assertThrows(
                ConfidentialExecutionDeniedException.class,
                () -&gt; aspect.requireConfidentialRuntime(joinPoint));
    }

    @Test

        ConfidentialAIAspect aspect = new ConfidentialAIAspect(
                List.of(mock(ConfidentialRuntimeVerifier.class),
                        mock(ConfidentialRuntimeVerifier.class)));
        ProceedingJoinPoint joinPoint = mock(ProceedingJoinPoint.class);

        assertThrows(
                ConfidentialExecutionDeniedException.class,
                () -&gt; aspect.requireConfidentialRuntime(joinPoint));
    }
}</file><file path="java/opentron-java/backend/src/test/java/org/opentron/backend/security/confidential/ConfidentialSegmentTest.java">package org.opentron.backend.security.confidential;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertArrayEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;

import java.lang.foreign.MemorySegment;
import java.lang.foreign.ValueLayout;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;

class ConfidentialSegmentTest {
    @Test
    void copiesUtf8IntoNativeMemoryAndReportsItsSize() {
        byte[] expected = &quot;A\u03a9&quot;.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

        try (ConfidentialSegment confidential = ConfidentialSegment.fromUtf8(&quot;A\u03a9&quot;)) {
            assertEquals(expected.length, confidential.byteSize());
            for (int i = 0; i &lt; expected.length; i++) {
                assertEquals(expected[i], confidential.memory().get(ValueLayout.JAVA_BYTE, i));
            }
        }
    }

    @Test
    void encodesUtf8DirectlyIntoNativeMemoryWithCompatibleMalformedSurrogateHandling() {
        String value = &quot;A\u03a9\ud83d\ude00\ud800&quot;;
        byte[] expected = value.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8);

        try (ConfidentialSegment confidential = ConfidentialSegment.fromUtf8(value)) {
            assertEquals(expected.length, confidential.byteSize());
            assertEquals(expected.length, ConfidentialSegment.utf8Length(value));
            assertArrayEquals(expected, confidential.memory().toArray(ValueLayout.JAVA_BYTE));
        }
    }

    @Test
    void zeroizeClearsTheOwnedMemory() {
        try (ConfidentialSegment confidential = ConfidentialSegment.fromUtf8(&quot;sensitive&quot;)) {
            MemorySegment memory = confidential.memory();
            confidential.zeroize();

            for (long i = 0; i &lt; confidential.byteSize(); i++) {
                assertEquals((byte) 0, memory.get(ValueLayout.JAVA_BYTE, i));
            }
        }
    }

    @Test
    void closeZeroizesAndInvalidatesPreviouslyObtainedMemory() {
        ConfidentialSegment confidential = ConfidentialSegment.fromUtf8(&quot;sensitive&quot;);
        MemorySegment memory = confidential.memory();

        confidential.close();
        confidential.close();

        assertThrows(IllegalStateException.class,
                () -&gt; memory.get(ValueLayout.JAVA_BYTE, 0));
        assertThrows(IllegalStateException.class, confidential::memory);
        assertThrows(IllegalStateException.class, confidential::zeroize);
        assertFalse(memory.scope().isAlive());
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsNullInput() {
        assertThrows(NullPointerException.class, () -&gt; ConfidentialSegment.fromUtf8(null));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsNegativeAllocationSize() {
        assertThrows(IllegalArgumentException.class, () -&gt; ConfidentialSegment.allocate(-1));
    }
